local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local guiParent
local ok = pcall(function() guiParent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
if not ok then guiParent = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end

do
    local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    if env.__GfxPreviewUnload then
        pcall(env.__GfxPreviewUnload)
        env.__GfxPreviewUnload = nil
    end
end

local cameraZoom = {
    Targets = {100000, 1000, 500, 400},
    Original = nil,
}

function cameraZoom.apply()
    for _, target in ipairs(cameraZoom.Targets) do
        if pcall(function() player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = target end) then return end
    end
end

function cameraZoom.restore()
    if cameraZoom.Original == nil then return end
    pcall(function() player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = cameraZoom.Original end)
end

pcall(function() cameraZoom.Original = player.CameraMaxZoomDistance end)
cameraZoom.apply()

local originalLighting = {
    GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
    Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
    OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
    EnvironmentDiffuseScale = Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale,
    EnvironmentSpecularScale = Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale,
    ShadowSoftness = Lighting.ShadowSoftness,
    LightingStyle = Lighting.LightingStyle,
    Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
    ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
    ColorShift_Top = Lighting.ColorShift_Top,
    ExposureCompensation = Lighting.ExposureCompensation,
}
local originalShadowColor
pcall(function() originalShadowColor = Lighting.ShadowColor end)

local COL = {
    bg1       = Color3.fromRGB(22, 19, 30),
    bg2       = Color3.fromRGB(27, 23, 37),
    row       = Color3.fromRGB(34, 29, 47),
    off       = Color3.fromRGB(42, 35, 58),
    stroke    = Color3.fromRGB(62, 52, 86),
    accent    = Color3.fromRGB(160, 100, 255),
    accentDim = Color3.fromRGB(90, 60, 150),
    pinkOn    = Color3.fromRGB(140, 78, 166),
    text      = Color3.fromRGB(228, 222, 240),
    textMuted = Color3.fromRGB(150, 140, 175),
    textDim   = Color3.fromRGB(115, 108, 140),
}

local function lighten(c, amt)
    return Color3.new(
        math.min(1, c.R + (1-c.R)*amt),
        math.min(1, c.G + (1-c.G)*amt),
        math.min(1, c.B + (1-c.B)*amt))
end
local function mutedAccent(t)
    t = t or 0.72
    local base = COL.off
    return Color3.new(
        base.R + (COL.accentDim.R - base.R) * (1-t),
        base.G + (COL.accentDim.G - base.G) * (1-t),
        base.B + (COL.accentDim.B - base.B) * (1-t))
end
local function mutedPink(t)
    t = t or 0.72
    local base = COL.off
    return Color3.new(
        base.R + (COL.pinkOn.R - base.R) * (1-t),
        base.G + (COL.pinkOn.G - base.G) * (1-t),
        base.B + (COL.pinkOn.B - base.B) * (1-t))
end

local function mk(class, props)
    local inst = Instance.new(class)
    for k, v in pairs(props) do
        if k ~= "Parent" then inst[k] = v end
    end
    if props.Parent then inst.Parent = props.Parent end
    return inst
end

    Name = "GfxPreview_" .. math.random(1000,9999),

local openBtns = {}

local openBtn = mk("TextButton", {Parent = ScreenGui, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0,0.5),
    Position = UDim2.new(0,8,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,86,0,36),

    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "🎨", TextSize = 15})
mk("TextLabel", {Parent = openBtn, Position = UDim2.new(0,28,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-34,1,0),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "Меню", TextSize = 12,
    TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
table.insert(openBtns, openBtn)

local closeCurrentPanel

local function openMenu()
    Root.Visible = true
    for _, b in ipairs(openBtns) do b.Visible = false end
    Root.GroupTransparency = 1
    RootStroke.Transparency = 1
    TweenService:Create(Root, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        GroupTransparency = 0,
    }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(RootStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Transparency = 0.1,
    }):Play()
end

local function closeMenu()
    if closeCurrentPanel then closeCurrentPanel() end
    TweenService:Create(RootStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Transparency = 1,
    }):Play()
    local tw = TweenService:Create(Root, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        GroupTransparency = 1,
    })
    tw:Play()
    local conn; conn = tw.Completed:Connect(function()
        conn:Disconnect()
        Root.Visible = false
        for _, b in ipairs(openBtns) do b.Visible = true end
        Root.GroupTransparency = 0
        RootStroke.Transparency = 0.1
    end)
end

local CloseBtn = mk("TextButton", {Parent = Header, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
    Position = UDim2.new(1,-12,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,28,0,28),
    BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
    Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = "X", TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = COL.text,
    AutoButtonColor = false})
mk("UICorner", {Parent = CloseBtn, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,8)})
CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() closeMenu() end)
do
    local dragging, dragStart, startPos, moved = false, nil, nil, false
    openBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging = true; dragStart = input.Position; startPos = openBtn.Position; moved = false
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            local d = input.Position - dragStart
            if math.abs(d.X) > 4 or math.abs(d.Y) > 4 then moved = true end
            openBtn.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            if dragging and not moved then openMenu() end
            dragging = false
        end
    end)
end

local dragZone = mk("Frame", {Parent = Header, Size = UDim2.new(1,-52,1,0),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1})

do
    local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
    dragZone.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging = true; dragStart = input.Position; startPos = Root.Position
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            local d = input.Position - dragStart
            Root.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then dragging = false end
    end)
end

local tabDefs = {
    {key="light", label="Освещение",   category="ГРАФИКА"},
    {key="fx",    label="Эффекты",     category="ГРАФИКА"},
    {key="atmo",  label="Атмосфера",   category="ГРАФИКА"},
    {key="terrain", label="Террейн и т.д", category="ГРАФИКА"},
    {key="scr",   label="Спецэффекты", category="ВИЗУАЛ"},
    {key="feel",  label="Утилиты",     category="ВИЗУАЛ"},
    {key="sound", label="Звук",        category="ЗВУК"},
    {key="cam",   label="Камера",      category="КАМЕРА"},
    {key="custom", label="Кастомизация плейса", category="ДРУГОЕ", fitSize=11},
    {key="favorites", label="Избранное", category="ДРУГОЕ"},
    {key="presets", label="Пресеты",  category="ДРУГОЕ"},
    {key="other", label="Другое",     category="ДРУГОЕ"},
}
local tabButtons = {}
local tabPages = {}
local activeTab = nil
local toggleRegistry = {}

local function makePage()
    local scroll = mk("ScrollingFrame", {
        Parent = ContentArea, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = COL.accentDim,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        Visible = false,
    })
    local layout = mk("UIListLayout", {Parent = scroll, Padding = UDim.new(0,8),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder})
    local pad = mk("UIPadding", {Parent = scroll,
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0,12), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0,12),
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,12), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,12)})
    return scroll
end

local function switchTab(key)
    if activeTab == key then return end
    activeTab = key
    for k, page in pairs(tabPages) do page.Visible = (k == key) end
    for _, def in ipairs(tabDefs) do
        local isActive = (def.key == key)
        local bd = tabButtons[def.key]
        TweenService:Create(bd.btn, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
            BackgroundColor3 = isActive and mutedAccent(0.5) or COL.bg2,
            TextColor3 = isActive and COL.text or COL.textMuted,
        }):Play()
        bd.stripe.BackgroundTransparency = isActive and 0 or 1
    end
end

local y = 14
local lastCategory = nil
for _, def in ipairs(tabDefs) do
    if def.category ~= lastCategory then
        lastCategory = def.category
        if y > 14 then y = y + 8 end
        mk("Frame", {Parent = SideBarScroll, Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,y), Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,1),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.stroke, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        y = y + 3
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = SideBarScroll, Position = UDim2.new(0,10,0,y), Size = UDim2.new(1,-20,0,16),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10,
            Text = def.category, TextColor3 = COL.textDim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center})
        y = y + 16 + 3
        mk("Frame", {Parent = SideBarScroll, Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,y), Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,1),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.stroke, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        y = y + 8
    end
    local btn = mk("TextButton", {
        Parent = SideBarScroll, Position = UDim2.new(0,8,0,y), Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,0,32),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.bg2, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 12, Text = def.label,
        TextColor3 = COL.textMuted, AutoButtonColor = false,
    })
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = btn, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,8)})
    if def.fitSize then
        btn.TextScaled = true
        mk("UITextSizeConstraint", {Parent = btn, MaxTextSize = def.fitSize})
    end
    local stripe = mk("Frame", {Parent = btn, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,3,0.6,0),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = stripe, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    tabButtons[def.key] = {btn = btn, stripe = stripe}
    tabPages[def.key] = makePage()
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if activeTab ~= def.key then toggleRegistry.Sounds.play("tab") end
        switchTab(def.key)
    end)
    y = y + 38
end

local function addSectionHeader(parent, text)
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Name = "Section_" .. text, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,24), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local stripe = mk("Frame", {Parent = row, Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,4),
        Size = UDim2.new(0,3,0,16), BackgroundColor3 = COL.accent, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = stripe, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,2)})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0,6,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-6,1,0),
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = text, TextSize = 12,
        TextColor3 = COL.textDim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    return row
end

local function addPanelHeader(parent, text)
    return mk("TextLabel", {Parent = parent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,14),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = text, TextSize = 11,
        TextColor3 = COL.textDim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
end

toggleRegistry.Layout = {Epoch = 0, PanelMargin = 10}
toggleRegistry.Icons = {
    StrokeColor = COL.stroke,
    StrokeThickness = 1.5,
    CrossColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
    CrossLength = 14,
    CrossThickness = 2,
    GearColor = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 135),
    GearHolder = 16,
    GearTeeth = 8,
    GearToothWidth = 4,
    GearToothLength = 4,
    GearRingSize = 6,
    GearRingThickness = 2,
    PieceCorner = 1,
    Style = "shape",
    Entries = {},
    GearGlyph = {Text = "⚙", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Color = COL.textMuted, Ratio = 0.46},
    HeaderCrossGlyph = {Text = "X", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Color = COL.text, Size = 13},
    PanelCrossGlyph = {Text = "X", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Color = COL.text, Size = 10},
    DeleteCrossGlyph = {Text = "X", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Color = COL.text, Size = 12, Nudge = 0.5},
}

function toggleRegistry.Icons.paint(entry)
    local icons = toggleRegistry.Icons
    local drawn = icons.Style == "shape"
    local button = entry.Button
    local glyph = entry.Glyph
    entry.Holder.Visible = drawn
    if glyph.Nudge and not entry.Pad then
        entry.Pad = mk("UIPadding", {Parent = button})
    end
    if entry.Pad then
        entry.Pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, drawn and 0 or glyph.Nudge * 2)
    end
    if drawn then
        button.Text = ""
        return
    end
    button.Font = glyph.Font
    button.TextColor3 = glyph.Color
    button.TextSize = glyph.Size or math.floor(math.min(button.Size.X.Offset, button.Size.Y.Offset) * glyph.Ratio + 0.5)
    button.Text = glyph.Text
end

function toggleRegistry.Icons.register(button, glyph)
    local icons = toggleRegistry.Icons
    local holder = mk("Frame", {Parent = button, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)})
    local entry = {Button = button, Glyph = glyph, Holder = holder}
    icons.Entries[entry] = true
    button.Destroying:Connect(function() icons.Entries[entry] = nil end)
    return entry
end

function toggleRegistry.Icons.setStyle(style)
    local icons = toggleRegistry.Icons
    icons.Style = style
    for entry in pairs(icons.Entries) do icons.paint(entry) end
end

function toggleRegistry.Icons.outline(button)
    local icons = toggleRegistry.Icons
    return mk("UIStroke", {Parent = button, Thickness = icons.StrokeThickness, Color = icons.StrokeColor,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
end

function toggleRegistry.Icons.drawBar(parent, color, width, length, rotation)
    local bar = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, width, 0, length),
        BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, Rotation = rotation})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = bar, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, toggleRegistry.Icons.PieceCorner)})
    return bar
end

function toggleRegistry.Icons.drawCross(button, glyph)
    local icons = toggleRegistry.Icons
    local entry = icons.register(button, glyph)
    icons.drawBar(entry.Holder, icons.CrossColor, icons.CrossThickness, icons.CrossLength, 45)
    icons.drawBar(entry.Holder, icons.CrossColor, icons.CrossThickness, icons.CrossLength, -45)
    icons.paint(entry)
end

function toggleRegistry.Icons.drawGear(button)
    local icons = toggleRegistry.Icons
    local entry = icons.register(button, icons.GearGlyph)
    for tooth = 0, icons.GearTeeth - 1 do
        local holder = mk("Frame", {Parent = entry.Holder, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, icons.GearHolder, 0, icons.GearHolder),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Rotation = tooth * 360 / icons.GearTeeth})
        local piece = mk("Frame", {Parent = holder, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, icons.GearToothWidth, 0, icons.GearToothLength),
            BackgroundColor3 = icons.GearColor, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = piece, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, icons.PieceCorner)})
    end
    local ring = mk("Frame", {Parent = entry.Holder, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, icons.GearRingSize, 0, icons.GearRingSize),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = ring, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)})
    mk("UIStroke", {Parent = ring, Thickness = icons.GearRingThickness, Color = icons.GearColor,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
    icons.paint(entry)
end

toggleRegistry.Sounds = {
    Volume = 0.5,
    Enabled = true,
    NamePrefix = "GraphicsMenuClick_",
    Kinds = {
        toggle = {Id = "rbxassetid://88442833509532", Speed = 1},
        gear = {Id = "rbxassetid://88442833509532", Speed = 0.8},
        close = {Id = "rbxassetid://8968249849", Speed = 0.8},
        select = {Id = "rbxassetid://74657965144290", Speed = 0.8},
        tab = {Id = "rbxassetid://74657965144290", Speed = 0.6},
        hotkey = {Id = "rbxassetid://108458528749106", Speed = 0.8},
        open = {Id = "rbxassetid://88442833509532", Speed = 1.3},
    },
    Instances = {},
}

function toggleRegistry.Sounds.build()
    local sounds = toggleRegistry.Sounds
    local loaded = {}
    for kind, def in pairs(sounds.Kinds) do
        local sound = mk("Sound", {Name = sounds.NamePrefix .. kind, SoundId = def.Id,
            PlaybackSpeed = def.Speed, Volume = sounds.Volume, Parent = SoundService})
        sounds.Instances[kind] = sound
        loaded[#loaded + 1] = sound
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function() game:GetService("ContentProvider"):PreloadAsync(loaded) end)
    end)
end

function toggleRegistry.Sounds.play(kind)
    local sounds = toggleRegistry.Sounds
    local sound = sounds.Instances[kind]
    if not sound or not sounds.Enabled then return end
    sound.TimePosition = 0
    sound:Play()
end

function toggleRegistry.Sounds.setVolume(value)
    local sounds = toggleRegistry.Sounds
    sounds.Volume = value
    for _, sound in pairs(sounds.Instances) do
        sound.Volume = value
    end
end

function toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(button, kind)
    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() toggleRegistry.Sounds.play(kind) end)
end

function toggleRegistry.Sounds.destroy()
    local sounds = toggleRegistry.Sounds
    for kind, sound in pairs(sounds.Instances) do
        pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end)
        sounds.Instances[kind] = nil
    end
end

toggleRegistry.Sounds.build()

Root:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
    if Root.Visible then toggleRegistry.Sounds.play("open") end
end)

addSectionHeader(tabPages["custom"], "КАСТОМИЗАЦИЯ ИГРЫ")
local openPanel = nil
local openPanelGearPaint = nil
local openPanelCloseFn = nil
closeCurrentPanel = function()
    if openPanel then
        if openPanelCloseFn then
            openPanelCloseFn()
        else
            openPanel.Visible = false
        end
        if openPanelGearPaint then openPanelGearPaint(false) end
        openPanel = nil
        openPanelGearPaint = nil
        openPanelCloseFn = nil
    end
end

function toggleRegistry.attachGear(parent, rightOffset, size, label, panelBuilder, panelHeight, openOverride)
    local rebuildPanel = nil
    local conns = {}
    local gb = mk("TextButton", {Parent = parent, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1,-rightOffset,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,size,0,size),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = gb, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,7)})
    toggleRegistry.Icons.outline(gb)
    toggleRegistry.Icons.drawGear(gb)
    toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(gb, "gear")
    if openOverride then
        gb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(openOverride)
        return gb
    end

    local gearButtons = {gb}
    local function paintGear(on)
        for _, button in ipairs(gearButtons) do
            if button.Parent then
                TweenService:Create(button, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                    BackgroundColor3 = on and COL.pinkOn or COL.off}):Play()
            end
        end
    end

    local SettingsPanel = mk("CanvasGroup", {Parent = ScreenGui, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,320,0,math.min(panelHeight or 240, 520)),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.bg1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 50, Active = true})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = SettingsPanel, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,18)})
    local SettingsPanelStroke = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = SettingsPanel, Thickness = 1, Color = COL.stroke, Transparency = 0.1,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round})
    local panelToken = 0
    local function openPanelAnim()
        panelToken = panelToken + 1
        SettingsPanel.Visible = true
        SettingsPanel.GroupTransparency = 1
        SettingsPanelStroke.Transparency = 1
        TweenService:Create(SettingsPanel, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            GroupTransparency = 0,
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(SettingsPanelStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Transparency = 0.1,
        }):Play()
    end
    local function closePanelAnim()
        panelToken = panelToken + 1
        local token = panelToken
        TweenService:Create(SettingsPanelStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Transparency = 1,
        }):Play()
        local tw = TweenService:Create(SettingsPanel, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            GroupTransparency = 1,
        })
        tw:Play()
        local conn; conn = tw.Completed:Connect(function()
            conn:Disconnect()
            if token ~= panelToken then return end
            SettingsPanel.Visible = false
            SettingsPanel.GroupTransparency = 0
            SettingsPanelStroke.Transparency = 0.1
        end)
    end
    local dragZone = mk("Frame", {Parent = SettingsPanel, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,38),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local spDot = mk("Frame", {Parent = SettingsPanel, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0,16,0,20), Size = UDim2.new(0,7,0,7),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.pinkOn, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = spDot, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = SettingsPanel, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0,30,0,10), Size = UDim2.new(1,-70,0,20),
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = label, TextSize = 13,
        TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    local SPClose = mk("TextButton", {Parent = SettingsPanel, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0),
        Position = UDim2.new(1,-12,0,10), Size = UDim2.new(0,22,0,22),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = ""})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = SPClose, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,7)})
    toggleRegistry.Icons.outline(SPClose)
    toggleRegistry.Icons.drawCross(SPClose, toggleRegistry.Icons.PanelCrossGlyph)
    toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(SPClose, "close")
    SPClose.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        closePanelAnim()
        paintGear(false)
        if openPanel == SettingsPanel then
            openPanel = nil; openPanelGearPaint = nil; openPanelCloseFn = nil
        end
    end)
    do
        local dragging = false
        local dragStart, startPos
        dragZone.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = SettingsPanel.Position
            end
        end)
        conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                local d = input.Position - dragStart
                SettingsPanel.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
            end
        end)
        conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then dragging = false end
        end)
    end

    local Body = mk("ScrollingFrame", {Parent = SettingsPanel, Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,40),
        Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,1,-52), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = COL.accentDim,
        VerticalScrollBarInset = Enum.ScrollBarInset.Always,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y})
    mk("UIListLayout", {Parent = Body, Padding = UDim.new(0,8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder})

    if panelBuilder then
        panelBuilder(Body)
        rebuildPanel = function()
            for _, child in ipairs(Body:GetChildren()) do
                if not child:IsA("UIListLayout") then child:Destroy() end
            end
            panelBuilder(Body)
        end
    else
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,14),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = "МЕТОДЫ", TextSize = 11,
            TextColor3 = COL.textDim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, LayoutOrder = -5})
        local methodsWrap = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,28),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = -4})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = methodsWrap, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        local indicator = mk("Frame", {Parent = methodsWrap, Position = UDim2.new(0,2,0,2),
            Size = UDim2.new(0.5,-4,1,-4), BackgroundColor3 = COL.pinkOn, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = indicator, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        local opt1 = mk("TextButton", {Parent = methodsWrap, Size = UDim2.new(0.5,0,1,0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Метод A", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = COL.text, AutoButtonColor = false})
        local opt2 = mk("TextButton", {Parent = methodsWrap, Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0,0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0.5,0,1,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Метод Б",
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted, AutoButtonColor = false})
        local curVariant = 1
        local function setVariant(v)
            curVariant = v
            TweenService:Create(indicator, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                Position = v==1 and UDim2.new(0,2,0,2) or UDim2.new(0.5,2,0,2)}):Play()
            opt1.TextColor3 = v==1 and COL.text or COL.textMuted
            opt2.TextColor3 = v==2 and COL.text or COL.textMuted
        end
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(opt1, "select")
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(opt2, "select")
        opt1.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setVariant(1) end)
        opt2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setVariant(2) end)

        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,14),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = "ЗНАЧЕНИЕ", TextSize = 11,
            TextColor3 = COL.textDim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, LayoutOrder = -3})
        local valDisplay = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,30),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "50", TextSize = 24,
            TextColor3 = COL.pinkOn, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, LayoutOrder = -2})
        local track = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,4),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = -1})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = track, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        local fill = mk("Frame", {Parent = track, Size = UDim2.new(0.5,0,1,0),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.pinkOn, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = fill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        local ctrlRow = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,26),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = 0})
        local minBtn = mk("TextButton", {Parent = ctrlRow, Size = UDim2.new(0,26,0,26),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = "−", TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = COL.text,
            AutoButtonColor = false})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = minBtn, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        local plusBtn = mk("TextButton", {Parent = ctrlRow, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0),
            Position = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,26,0,26),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = "+", TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = COL.text,
            AutoButtonColor = false})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = plusBtn, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        local curVal = 50
        local function setVal(n)
            curVal = math.max(0, n)
            valDisplay.Text = tostring(curVal)
            TweenService:Create(fill, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(curVal/100,0,1),0,1,0)}):Play()
        end
        minBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setVal(curVal - 5) end)
        plusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setVal(curVal + 5) end)
    end

    local positionedEpoch = nil
    local function togglePanel()
        if openPanel == SettingsPanel then
            closePanelAnim()
            paintGear(false)
            if openPanel == SettingsPanel then
                openPanel = nil; openPanelGearPaint = nil; openPanelCloseFn = nil
            end
        else
            if openPanel and openPanel ~= SettingsPanel then
                closeCurrentPanel()
            end
            if positionedEpoch ~= toggleRegistry.Layout.Epoch then
                positionedEpoch = toggleRegistry.Layout.Epoch
                local margin = toggleRegistry.Layout.PanelMargin
                local rootPos = Root.AbsolutePosition
                local rootSize = Root.AbsoluteSize
                local panelX = math.min(rootPos.X + rootSize.X + margin,
                    ScreenGui.AbsoluteSize.X - SettingsPanel.Size.X.Offset - margin)
                SettingsPanel.Position = UDim2.new(0, math.max(panelX, 0), 0, rootPos.Y + rootSize.Y/2)
            end
            openPanelAnim()
            paintGear(true)
            openPanel = SettingsPanel
            openPanelGearPaint = paintGear
            openPanelCloseFn = closePanelAnim
        end
    end
    gb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(togglePanel)

    local function addMirror(button)
        gearButtons[#gearButtons + 1] = button
        button.BackgroundColor3 = openPanel == SettingsPanel and COL.pinkOn or COL.off
        button.Destroying:Connect(function()
            local index = table.find(gearButtons, button)
            if index then table.remove(gearButtons, index) end
        end)
    end

    local function dispose()
        for _, conn in ipairs(conns) do conn:Disconnect() end
        conns = {}
        if openPanel == SettingsPanel then
            openPanel = nil; openPanelGearPaint = nil; openPanelCloseFn = nil
        end
        SettingsPanel:Destroy()
    end

    return gb, SettingsPanel, rebuildPanel, dispose, togglePanel, addMirror
end

toggleRegistry.Favorites = {
    File = "GraphicsMenu_Favorites.json",
    SectionTitle = "ИЗБРАННОЕ",
    HintText = "ты зажимая можешь передвигать функции вверх/вниз",
    HintTextSize = 11,
    HintGap = 8,
    HintLift = 1,
    HintColor = COL.textDim,
    StarOn = "★",
    StarOff = "☆",
    StarOnColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 80),
    StarX = 2,
    StarY = 1,
    StarSize = 16,
    StarTextSize = 12,
    Page = tabPages["favorites"],
    DragThreshold = 5,
    DragOutlineColor = COL.accent,
    DragOutlineThickness = 1.5,
    Moved = false,
    Drag = nil,
    Rows = {},
    Order = {},
}

function toggleRegistry.attachStar(parent, label)
    local favorites = toggleRegistry.Favorites
    local entry = toggleRegistry[label]
    local star = mk("TextButton", {Parent = parent, ZIndex = 3, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutoButtonColor = false,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, favorites.StarX, 0, favorites.StarY),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, favorites.StarSize, 0, favorites.StarSize), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        Text = favorites.StarOff, TextSize = favorites.StarTextSize, TextColor3 = COL.textDim})
    local function paint()
        local on = favorites.Rows[label] ~= nil
        star.Text = on and favorites.StarOn or favorites.StarOff
        star.TextColor3 = on and favorites.StarOnColor or COL.textDim
    end
    entry.Stars[paint] = true
    star.Destroying:Connect(function() entry.Stars[paint] = nil end)
    star.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() favorites.toggle(label) end)
    paint()
end

local function addToggleRow(parent, label, hasPanel, onToggle, panelBuilder, panelHeight, iconGlyph, onKeyButton, canEnable, hidePill, mirror)
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Name = "Row_" .. label, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})

    local PILL_W, PILL_H, GEAR_D, GAP, M, KEY_W = 62, 26, 26, 8, 10, 56
    local keyRE = M
    local pillRE = onKeyButton and (keyRE + KEY_W + GAP) or M
    local gearRE = hasPanel and (pillRE + (hidePill and 0 or PILL_W + GAP)) or 0
    local rightEdge = hasPanel and (gearRE + GEAR_D) or (pillRE + PILL_W)

    local clickZone = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1,-(rightEdge+GAP),1,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Visible = mirror or not hidePill})
    if iconGlyph then
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,16,1,0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = iconGlyph, TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(33,30,42), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
        mk("Frame", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0,0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0,32,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,1,0,16),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130,126,142), BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0,40,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-(40+rightEdge+GAP),1,0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = label, TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
    else
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-(14+rightEdge+GAP),1,0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = label, TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
    end

    local pillBtn = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1,-pillRE,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,PILL_W,0,PILL_H),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "ВЫКЛ", TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
        AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = pillBtn, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,9)})
    mk("UIStroke", {Parent = pillBtn, Thickness = 1.5, Color = COL.stroke,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
    pillBtn.Visible = not hidePill

    if onKeyButton then
        local keyBtn = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1,-keyRE,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,KEY_W,0,PILL_H),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "", TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
            AutoButtonColor = false})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = keyBtn, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,9)})
        mk("UIStroke", {Parent = keyBtn, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.fromRGB(48,42,64),
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
        onKeyButton(keyBtn)
    end

    local source = mirror and toggleRegistry[label] or nil
    local entry = source or {Listeners = {}, Stars = {}}
    local state = false
    local function paint(v)
        state = v
        TweenService:Create(pillBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            BackgroundColor3 = v and COL.pinkOn or COL.off,
            TextColor3 = v and COL.text or COL.textMuted,
        }):Play()
        pillBtn.Text = v and "ВКЛ" or "ВЫКЛ"
    end
    local function setState(v)
        if v and canEnable and not canEnable() then return end
        paint(v)
        if onToggle then onToggle(v) end
        for listener in pairs(entry.Listeners) do listener(v) end
    end
    local function press()
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.play("toggle")
        if source then source.setState(not source.isOn()) else setState(not state) end
    end
    clickZone.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if hidePill or (source and toggleRegistry.Favorites.Moved) then return end
        press()
    end)
    pillBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(press)

    if source then
        local function grab(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                toggleRegistry.Favorites.press(label, input)
            end
        end
        clickZone.InputBegan:Connect(grab)
        row.InputBegan:Connect(grab)
        if hasPanel then
            local mirrorGear = toggleRegistry.attachGear(row, gearRE, GEAR_D, label, nil, nil, source.openPanel)
            if source.gearMirror then source.gearMirror(mirrorGear) end
        end
        paint(source.isOn())
        source.Listeners[paint] = true
        row.Destroying:Connect(function() source.Listeners[paint] = nil end)
        toggleRegistry.attachStar(row, label)
        return row
    end

    local rebuildPanel, panelToggle, gearMirror = nil, nil, nil
    if hasPanel then
        local _, _, rebuild, _, toggle, addMirror = toggleRegistry.attachGear(row, gearRE, GEAR_D, label,
            panelBuilder, panelHeight)
        rebuildPanel, panelToggle, gearMirror = rebuild, toggle, addMirror
    end

    entry.setState = setState
    entry.isOn = function() return state end
    entry.rebuild = rebuildPanel
    entry.openPanel = panelToggle
    entry.gearMirror = gearMirror
    entry.Spec = {hasPanel = hasPanel, iconGlyph = iconGlyph, onKeyButton = onKeyButton, hidePill = hidePill}
    toggleRegistry[label] = entry
    toggleRegistry.attachStar(row, label)
    return row, setState
end

function toggleRegistry.Favorites.refreshStars(label)
    for paint in pairs(toggleRegistry[label].Stars) do paint() end
end

function toggleRegistry.Favorites.relayout()
    local favorites = toggleRegistry.Favorites
    for index, label in ipairs(favorites.Order) do
        favorites.Rows[label].LayoutOrder = index
    end
end

function toggleRegistry.Favorites.add(label)
    local favorites = toggleRegistry.Favorites
    local source = toggleRegistry[label]
    if type(source) ~= "table" or not source.Spec or favorites.Rows[label] then return end
    local spec = source.Spec
    if spec.mirror then
        favorites.Rows[label] = spec.mirror(favorites.Page)
    else
        favorites.Rows[label] = addToggleRow(favorites.Page, label, spec.hasPanel, nil, nil, nil, spec.iconGlyph,
            spec.onKeyButton, nil, spec.hidePill, true)
    end
    favorites.Order[#favorites.Order + 1] = label
    favorites.relayout()
    favorites.refreshStars(label)
end

function toggleRegistry.Favorites.remove(label)
    local favorites = toggleRegistry.Favorites
    local row = favorites.Rows[label]
    if not row then return end
    favorites.Rows[label] = nil
    local index = table.find(favorites.Order, label)
    if index then table.remove(favorites.Order, index) end
    row:Destroy()
    favorites.relayout()
    favorites.refreshStars(label)
end

function toggleRegistry.Favorites.press(label, input)
    local favorites = toggleRegistry.Favorites
    if favorites.Drag then favorites.Drag.finish() end
    favorites.Moved = false
    local row = favorites.Rows[label]
    local count = #favorites.Order
    if not row or count < 2 then return end
    local tops, bottoms, centers = {}, {}, {}
    for position, name in ipairs(favorites.Order) do
        local other = favorites.Rows[name]
        tops[position] = other.AbsolutePosition.Y
        bottoms[position] = tops[position] + other.AbsoluteSize.Y
        centers[position] = (tops[position] + bottoms[position]) / 2
    end
    local startIndex = table.find(favorites.Order, label)
    local startPointer = input.Position.Y
    local startCanvas = favorites.Page.CanvasPosition.Y
    local index = startIndex
    local outline = nil
    local moveConn, endConn
    local drag = {}
    function drag.finish()
        moveConn:Disconnect()
        endConn:Disconnect()
        if outline then outline:Destroy() end
        if favorites.Drag == drag then favorites.Drag = nil end
        if index ~= startIndex then favorites.save() end
    end
    moveConn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(change)
        if change.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then return end
        if favorites.Rows[label] ~= row then
            drag.finish()
            return
        end
        local delta = change.Position.Y - startPointer
        if not favorites.Moved then
            if math.abs(delta) < favorites.DragThreshold then return end
            favorites.Moved = true
            outline = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = row, Thickness = favorites.DragOutlineThickness,
                Color = favorites.DragOutlineColor, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
        end
        local shift = delta + favorites.Page.CanvasPosition.Y - startCanvas
        local target = startIndex
        for position = 1, count do
            if position < startIndex and tops[startIndex] + shift < centers[position] then
                target = math.min(target, position)
            elseif position > startIndex and bottoms[startIndex] + shift > centers[position] then
                target = math.max(target, position)
            end
        end
        if target ~= index then
            table.remove(favorites.Order, index)
            table.insert(favorites.Order, target, label)
            index = target
            favorites.relayout()
        end
    end)
    endConn = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(finished)
        if finished.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then drag.finish() end
    end)
    favorites.Drag = drag
end

function toggleRegistry.Favorites.save()
    local favorites = toggleRegistry.Favorites
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
    pcall(function()
        writefile(favorites.File, game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode({labels = favorites.Order}))
    end)
end

function toggleRegistry.Favorites.toggle(label)
    local favorites = toggleRegistry.Favorites
    if favorites.Rows[label] then favorites.remove(label) else favorites.add(label) end
    favorites.save()
end

function toggleRegistry.Favorites.load()
    local favorites = toggleRegistry.Favorites
    if type(readfile) ~= "function" or type(isfile) ~= "function" then return end
    pcall(function()
        if not isfile(favorites.File) then return end
        local decoded = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(readfile(favorites.File))
        if type(decoded) ~= "table" or type(decoded.labels) ~= "table" then return end
        for _, label in ipairs(decoded.labels) do
            if type(label) == "string" then favorites.add(label) end
        end
    end)
end

function toggleRegistry.Favorites.buildHeader()
    local favorites = toggleRegistry.Favorites
    local header = addSectionHeader(favorites.Page, favorites.SectionTitle)
    local title = header:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
    local holder = mk("Frame", {Parent = header, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 1, 0)})
    mk("UIListLayout", {Parent = holder, FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, favorites.HintGap), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center})
    title.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    title.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    title.LayoutOrder = 1
    title.Parent = holder
    local hint = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = holder, LayoutOrder = 2, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
        Text = favorites.HintText, TextSize = favorites.HintTextSize, TextColor3 = favorites.HintColor})
    mk("UIPadding", {Parent = hint, PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, favorites.HintLift * 2)})
end

toggleRegistry.Favorites.buildHeader()

local function addSimpleToggle(parent, label)
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-96,1,0),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = label, TextSize = 13,
        TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    local btn = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1,-10,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,68,0,28),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "ВЫКЛ", TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
        AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = btn, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,8)})
    local state = false
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        state = not state
        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            BackgroundColor3 = state and COL.pinkOn or COL.off,
            TextColor3 = state and COL.text or COL.textMuted,
        }):Play()
        btn.Text = state and "ВКЛ" or "ВЫКЛ"
    end)
    return row
end

local function addToggleCheckbox(parent, label)
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-50,1,0),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = label, TextSize = 13,
        TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    local box = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1,-14,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,22,0,22),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = box, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)})
    local boxStroke = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = box, Thickness = 1.5, Color = COL.stroke,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
    local check = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = box, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0),
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "✓", TextSize = 14,
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), TextTransparency = 1})
    local state = false
    box.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        state = not state
        TweenService:Create(box, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = state and COL.accent or COL.off}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(boxStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Transparency = state and 1 or 0}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(check, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextTransparency = state and 0 or 1}):Play()
    end)
    return row
end

local function addToggleRing(parent, label)
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-50,1,0),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = label, TextSize = 13,
        TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    local ring = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1,-14,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,22,0,22),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.bg1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = ring, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local ringStroke = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = ring, Thickness = 2, Color = COL.stroke,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
    local state = false
    ring.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        state = not state
        TweenService:Create(ring, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = state and COL.accent or COL.bg1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(ringStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            Color = state and lighten(COL.accent,0.3) or COL.stroke}):Play()
    end)
    return row
end

local function addColorPicker(parent, labelText, initialColor, callback)
    local headerRaise = 3
    local container = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,132),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = container, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
    local pad = mk("Frame", {Parent = container, Position = UDim2.new(0,10,0,8),
        Size = UDim2.new(1,-20,1,-16), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = pad, Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,-headerRaise),
        Size = UDim2.new(1,-104,0,16), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = labelText, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
    local field = mk("TextBox", {Parent = pad, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0),
        Position = UDim2.new(1,0,0,-headerRaise), Size = UDim2.new(0,98,0,16), BackgroundColor3 = COL.bg1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = COL.text,
        PlaceholderText = "R, G, B / #HEX", PlaceholderColor3 = COL.textDim,
        ClearTextOnFocus = false, Text = ""})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = field, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,5)})

    local h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(initialColor)

    local svHolder = mk("Frame", {Parent = pad, Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,20),
        Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,78), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local svSquare = mk("CanvasGroup", {Parent = svHolder, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(h,1,1), BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = svSquare, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)})
    local whiteLayer = mk("Frame", {Parent = svSquare, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1), BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UIGradient", {Parent = whiteLayer, Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 1)})
    local blackLayer = mk("Frame", {Parent = svSquare, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0), BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UIGradient", {Parent = blackLayer, Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1, 0)})
    local svDot = mk("Frame", {Parent = svHolder, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(s,0,1-v,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,10,0,10),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = svDot, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    mk("UIStroke", {Parent = svDot, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.new(0,0,0)})

    local hueStrip = mk("Frame", {Parent = pad, Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,104),
        Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,14), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1), BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = hueStrip, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)})
    mk("UIGradient", {Parent = hueStrip, Color = ColorSequence.new{
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromHSV(0,1,1)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1/6, Color3.fromHSV(1/6,1,1)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(2/6, Color3.fromHSV(2/6,1,1)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(3/6, Color3.fromHSV(3/6,1,1)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(4/6, Color3.fromHSV(4/6,1,1)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(5/6, Color3.fromHSV(5/6,1,1)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromHSV(1,1,1)),
    }})
    local hueDot = mk("Frame", {Parent = hueStrip, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(h,0,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,6,1.4,0),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = hueDot, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,3)})
    mk("UIStroke", {Parent = hueDot, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.new(0,0,0)})

    local function parseColor(text)
        local hex = text:match("^%s*#?(%x%x%x%x%x%x)%s*$")
        if hex then
            return Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(hex:sub(1,2), 16), tonumber(hex:sub(3,4), 16), tonumber(hex:sub(5,6), 16))
        end
        local short = text:match("^%s*#(%x%x%x)%s*$")
        if short then
            return Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(short:sub(1,1), 16) * 17, tonumber(short:sub(2,2), 16) * 17,
                tonumber(short:sub(3,3), 16) * 17)
        end
        if text:find("[^%d%s,;]") then return nil end
        local numbers = {}
        for token in text:gmatch("%d+") do
            numbers[#numbers + 1] = tonumber(token)
        end
        if #numbers == 3 and numbers[1] <= 255 and numbers[2] <= 255 and numbers[3] <= 255 then
            return Color3.fromRGB(numbers[1], numbers[2], numbers[3])
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function formatColor(color)
        return string.format("%d, %d, %d", math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5))
    end
    local function refreshInput()
        if not field:IsFocused() then field.Text = formatColor(Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v)) end
    end
    local function refreshVisuals()
        svSquare.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(h, 1, 1)
        svDot.Position = UDim2.new(s, 0, 1 - v, 0)
        hueDot.Position = UDim2.new(h, 0, 0.5, 0)
        refreshInput()
    end
    local function updateSV(inputPos)
        s = math.clamp((inputPos.X - svSquare.AbsolutePosition.X) / svSquare.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
        v = 1 - math.clamp((inputPos.Y - svSquare.AbsolutePosition.Y) / svSquare.AbsoluteSize.Y, 0, 1)
        refreshVisuals()
        callback(Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v))
    end
    local function updateHue(inputPos)
        h = math.clamp((inputPos.X - hueStrip.AbsolutePosition.X) / hueStrip.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
        refreshVisuals()
        callback(Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v))
    end
    refreshInput()

    local dragTarget = nil
    local function beginSV(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragTarget = "sv"; updateSV(input.Position)
        end
    end
    svSquare.InputBegan:Connect(beginSV)
    blackLayer.InputBegan:Connect(beginSV)
    hueStrip.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragTarget = "hue"; updateHue(input.Position)
        end
    end)
    field.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local color = parseColor(field.Text)
        if color then
            local newHue, newSat, newValue = Color3.toHSV(color)
            if newSat > 0 then h = newHue end
            s, v = newSat, newValue
            refreshVisuals()
            callback(color)
        else
            refreshInput()
        end
    end)
    local dragConnMove = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then return end
        if dragTarget == "sv" then updateSV(input.Position)
        elseif dragTarget == "hue" then updateHue(input.Position) end
    end)
    local dragConnEnd = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then dragTarget = nil end
    end)
    container.Destroying:Connect(function()
        dragConnMove:Disconnect()
        dragConnEnd:Disconnect()
    end)
    return container
end

local function addSlider(parent, labelText, minValue, maxValue, initialValue, decimals, callback, accentColor, logScale)
    accentColor = accentColor or COL.pinkOn
    local container = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,52),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = container, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
    local pad = mk("Frame", {Parent = container, Position = UDim2.new(0,10,0,8),
        Size = UDim2.new(1,-20,1,-16), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local function fmt(v)
        if decimals == 0 then return tostring(math.floor(v)) end
        return string.format("%."..decimals.."f", v)
    end
    local dot = mk("Frame", {Parent = pad, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,7), Size = UDim2.new(0,6,0,6),
        BackgroundColor3 = accentColor, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = dot, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = pad, Position = UDim2.new(0,12,0,0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0.6,-12,0,14), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = labelText, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    local valueLabel = mk("TextBox", {Parent = pad, Position = UDim2.new(0.6,0,0,0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0.4,0,0,14), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        Text = fmt(initialValue), TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = accentColor,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, ClearTextOnFocus = false})
    local hit = mk("Frame", {Parent = pad, Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,20),
        Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,16), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local track = mk("Frame", {Parent = hit, Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0.5,-2),
        Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,4), BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = track, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local function ratioOf(v)
        if logScale then
            return math.clamp(math.log(v / minValue) / math.log(maxValue / minValue), 0, 1)
        end
        return math.clamp((v - minValue) / (maxValue - minValue), 0, 1)
    end
    local function valueAt(r)
        if logScale then return minValue * (maxValue / minValue) ^ r end
        return minValue + (maxValue - minValue) * r
    end
    local r0 = ratioOf(initialValue)
    local fill = mk("Frame", {Parent = track, Size = UDim2.new(r0,0,1,0),
        BackgroundColor3 = accentColor, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = fill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local handle = mk("Frame", {Parent = track, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(r0,0,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,12,0,12),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230,225,238), BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = handle, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local currentVal = initialValue
    local function setDisplay(v)
        v = math.clamp(v, minValue, maxValue)
        currentVal = v
        local r = ratioOf(v)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(r,0,1,0)
        handle.Position = UDim2.new(r,0,0.5,0)
        valueLabel.Text = fmt(v)
        return v
    end
    local function setValue(v)
        callback(setDisplay(v))
    end
    local dragging = false
    local function update(inputPos)
        local r = math.clamp((inputPos.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X)/track.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
        setValue(valueAt(r))
    end
    valueLabel.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local n = tonumber(valueLabel.Text)
        if n then setValue(n) else valueLabel.Text = fmt(currentVal) end
    end)
    hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging = true
            update(input.Position)
        end
    end)
    local dragConnMove = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            update(input.Position)
        end
    end)
    local dragConnEnd = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then dragging = false end
    end)
    container.Destroying:Connect(function()
        dragConnMove:Disconnect()
        dragConnEnd:Disconnect()
    end)
    return container, setValue, setDisplay
end

local function addSliderCompact(parent, labelText, minValue, maxValue, initialValue, decimals, callback, accentColor)
    accentColor = accentColor or COL.pinkOn
    local container = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,26), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local function fmt(v)
        if decimals == 0 then return tostring(math.floor(v)) end
        return string.format("%."..decimals.."f", v)
    end
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = container, Size = UDim2.new(0,90,1,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = labelText, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    local valueLabel = mk("TextBox", {Parent = container, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1,0,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,32,1,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = fmt(initialValue), TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = accentColor,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, ClearTextOnFocus = false})
    local hit = mk("Frame", {Parent = container, Position = UDim2.new(0,96,0,0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1,-134,1,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local track = mk("Frame", {Parent = hit, Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0.5,-2),
        Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,4), BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = track, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local r0 = math.clamp((initialValue-minValue)/(maxValue-minValue), 0, 1)
    local fill = mk("Frame", {Parent = track, Size = UDim2.new(r0,0,1,0),
        BackgroundColor3 = accentColor, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = fill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local handle = mk("Frame", {Parent = track, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(r0,0,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,10,0,10),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230,225,238), BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = handle, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local currentVal = initialValue
    local function setValue(v)
        v = math.clamp(v, minValue, maxValue)
        currentVal = v
        local r = (v-minValue)/(maxValue-minValue)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(r,0,1,0)
        handle.Position = UDim2.new(r,0,0.5,0)
        valueLabel.Text = fmt(v)
        callback(v)
    end
    local dragging = false
    local function update(inputPos)
        local r = math.clamp((inputPos.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X)/track.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
        setValue(minValue + (maxValue-minValue)*r)
    end
    valueLabel.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local n = tonumber(valueLabel.Text)
        if n then setValue(n) else valueLabel.Text = fmt(currentVal) end
    end)
    hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging = true
            update(input.Position)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            update(input.Position)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then dragging = false end
    end)
    return container
end

local function addSliderStepper(parent, labelText, minValue, maxValue, initialValue, step, decimals, callback, accentColor)
    accentColor = accentColor or COL.pinkOn
    local container = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,30), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local function fmt(v)
        if decimals == 0 then return tostring(math.floor(v)) end
        return string.format("%."..decimals.."f", v)
    end
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = container, Size = UDim2.new(0,90,1,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = labelText, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    local currentVal = initialValue
    local minBtn = mk("TextButton", {Parent = container, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1,-72,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,22,0,22),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = "−", TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = COL.text,
        AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = minBtn, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)})
    local box = mk("TextBox", {Parent = container, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1,-32,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,38,0,22),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = fmt(initialValue), TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = accentColor,
        ClearTextOnFocus = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = box, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)})
    local plusBtn = mk("TextButton", {Parent = container, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1,0,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,22,0,22),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = "+", TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = COL.text,
        AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = plusBtn, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)})
    local function setValue(v)
        v = math.clamp(v, minValue, maxValue)
        currentVal = v
        box.Text = fmt(v)
        callback(v)
    end
    minBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setValue(currentVal - step) end)
    plusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setValue(currentVal + step) end)
    box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local n = tonumber(box.Text)
        if n then setValue(n) else box.Text = fmt(currentVal) end
    end)
    return container
end

local function addSliderPresets(parent, labelText, presetValues, initialValue, decimals, callback, accentColor)
    accentColor = accentColor or COL.pinkOn
    local container = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,40), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local function fmt(v)
        if decimals == 0 then return tostring(math.floor(v)) end
        return string.format("%."..decimals.."f", v)
    end
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = container, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,14), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = labelText, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = container, Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,18),
        Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,22), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local n = #presetValues
    local btns = {}
    local function setValue(v)
        for i, pv in ipairs(presetValues) do
            local on = math.abs(pv-v) < 0.001
            btns[i].BackgroundColor3 = on and accentColor or COL.off
            btns[i].TextColor3 = on and COL.text or COL.textMuted
        end
        callback(v)
    end
    for i, pv in ipairs(presetValues) do
        local btn = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, Position = UDim2.new((i-1)/n,2,0,0),
            Size = UDim2.new(1/n,-4,1,0), BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = fmt(pv), TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
            AutoButtonColor = false})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = btn, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)})
        btns[i] = btn
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(btn, "select")
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setValue(pv) end)
    end
    setValue(initialValue)
    return container
end

local function addSliderVertical(parent, labelText, minValue, maxValue, initialValue, decimals, callback, accentColor)
    accentColor = accentColor or COL.pinkOn
    local container = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,74), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local function fmt(v)
        if decimals == 0 then return tostring(math.floor(v)) end
        return string.format("%."..decimals.."f", v)
    end
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = container, Size = UDim2.new(0,90,0,14), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = labelText, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    local valueLabel = mk("TextBox", {Parent = container, Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,54),
        Size = UDim2.new(0,50,0,16), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = fmt(initialValue), TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = accentColor,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ClearTextOnFocus = false})
    local hit = mk("Frame", {Parent = container, Position = UDim2.new(0,100,0,0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0,16,1,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local track = mk("Frame", {Parent = hit, Position = UDim2.new(0.5,-2,0,0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0,4,1,0), BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = track, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local r0 = math.clamp((initialValue-minValue)/(maxValue-minValue), 0, 1)
    local fill = mk("Frame", {Parent = track, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0,1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0,0,1,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,0,r0,0),
        BackgroundColor3 = accentColor, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = fill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local handle = mk("Frame", {Parent = track, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,1-r0,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,12,0,12),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230,225,238), BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = handle, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local currentVal = initialValue
    local function setValue(v)
        v = math.clamp(v, minValue, maxValue)
        currentVal = v
        local r = (v-minValue)/(maxValue-minValue)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,r,0)
        handle.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,1-r,0)
        valueLabel.Text = fmt(v)
        callback(v)
    end
    local dragging = false
    local function update(inputPos)
        local r = 1 - math.clamp((inputPos.Y - track.AbsolutePosition.Y)/track.AbsoluteSize.Y, 0, 1)
        setValue(minValue + (maxValue-minValue)*r)
    end
    valueLabel.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local n = tonumber(valueLabel.Text)
        if n then setValue(n) else valueLabel.Text = fmt(currentVal) end
    end)
    hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging = true
            update(input.Position)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            update(input.Position)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then dragging = false end
    end)
    return container
end

local sliderSync = {}
local ToggleSetters = {}
local shadowsEnabled = false
local shadowDarkness = 0
local shadowIntensity = 0.1
local shadowSoftness = 0.5
local shadowSpecular = 0.5
local shadowTint = Color3.new(0,0,0)
local shadowDay = {
    Enabled = false,
    Amount = 25,
    Fade = 0.3,
}

function shadowDay.factor()
    local t = math.clamp(Lighting:GetSunDirection().Y / shadowDay.Fade, 0, 1)
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t)
end

local shadowsLoop = {
    StepName = "GraphicsMenuShadows",
    StepPriority = Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value + 10,
    Tolerance = 0.003,
    HasColor = false,
    BaseValue = 0.5,
    DarkRange = 0.4,
    MaxDarkness = 50,
    OutdoorDarkness = 0,
    Lift = nil,
    Release = nil,
}

local moonState = {
    EmitterName = "MoonlightEmitter",
    Enabled = false,
    Conn = nil,
    Strength = 0.35,
    Fade = 0.3,
    Color = Color3.fromRGB(120, 150, 255),
    LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(120, 150, 255),
    LightEnabled = true,
    LightBrightness = 3,
    LightRange = 60,
    LightHeight = 30,
    LightAngle = 180,
    LightShadows = true,
    Reach = 10000,
    Spacing = 30,
    Scatter = 0.9,
    Variation = 0,
    Drift = 0.05,
    MaxLights = 600,
    FarLights = 300,
    MinSurfaceUp = 0.7,
    CreatePerTick = 40,
    GroundPerTick = 12,
    RoofChar = nil,
    RoofParams = nil,
    Layers = {},
    Pool = {},
    SettingsKey = nil,
    PropsVersion = 0,
    RefreshAt = 0,
    LightAccum = 0,
    BaseAmbient = nil,
    BaseOutdoor = nil,
    WrittenAmbient = nil,
    WrittenOutdoor = nil,
}

function moonState.factor()
    local t = math.clamp(Lighting:GetMoonDirection().Y / moonState.Fade, 0, 1)
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t)
end

function moonState.mix(base, factor)
    local weight = moonState.Strength * (factor or moonState.factor())
    local c = moonState.Color
    return Color3.new(
        math.min(1, base.R + c.R * weight),
        math.min(1, base.G + c.G * weight),
        math.min(1, base.B + c.B * weight))
end

function shadowsLoop.same(current, value)
    local tolerance = shadowsLoop.Tolerance
    if typeof(value) == "Color3" then
        return math.abs(current.R - value.R) <= tolerance
            and math.abs(current.G - value.G) <= tolerance
            and math.abs(current.B - value.B) <= tolerance
    elseif type(value) == "number" then
        return math.abs(current - value) <= tolerance
    end
    return current == value
end

function shadowsLoop.write(property, value)
    if shadowsLoop.same(Lighting[property], value) then return end
    Lighting[property] = value
end

function shadowsLoop.ambient(darkness)
    local loop = shadowsLoop
    local hue, sat = Color3.toHSV(shadowTint)
    local color = Color3.fromHSV(hue, sat, loop.BaseValue - darkness / loop.MaxDarkness * loop.DarkRange)
    if moonState.Enabled then color = moonState.mix(color) end
    if loop.Lift then color = loop.Lift(color) end
    return color
end

function shadowsLoop.step()
    if not shadowsEnabled then return end
    local loop = shadowsLoop
    local write = loop.write
    local outdoorDarkness = loop.OutdoorDarkness
    if shadowDay.Enabled then
        outdoorDarkness = math.max(0, outdoorDarkness - shadowDay.Amount * shadowDay.factor())
    end
    write("GlobalShadows", true)
    write("Ambient", loop.ambient(shadowDarkness))
    write("OutdoorAmbient", loop.ambient(outdoorDarkness))
    write("EnvironmentDiffuseScale", 1.0 - shadowIntensity)
    write("EnvironmentSpecularScale", shadowSpecular)
    write("ShadowSoftness", shadowSoftness)
    if shadowsLoop.HasColor then pcall(write, "ShadowColor", shadowTint) end
end

function shadowsLoop.unbind()
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(shadowsLoop.StepName) end)
end

local function applyShadows(enabled)
    shadowsEnabled = enabled
    shadowsLoop.unbind()
    if enabled then
        if shadowsLoop.Release then shadowsLoop.Release() end
        shadowsLoop.HasColor = pcall(function() return Lighting.ShadowColor end)
        shadowsLoop.step()
        RunService:BindToRenderStep(shadowsLoop.StepName, shadowsLoop.StepPriority, shadowsLoop.step)
    else
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = originalLighting.GlobalShadows
        Lighting.Ambient = originalLighting.Ambient
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = originalLighting.OutdoorAmbient
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = originalLighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale
        Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = originalLighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale
        Lighting.ShadowSoftness = originalLighting.ShadowSoftness
        if originalShadowColor then
            pcall(function() Lighting.ShadowColor = originalShadowColor end)
        end
    end
end
Lighting.PrioritizeLightingQuality = true

addSectionHeader(tabPages["light"], "ОСВЕЩЕНИЕ")

        shadowSpecular = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(90,170,150)))
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ДНЁМ")
    do
        local dayRow = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})

        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(pill, "toggle")
        pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

    addSlider(Body, "Насколько снижать", 0, shadowsLoop.MaxDarkness, shadowDay.Amount, 0, function(v)
        shadowDay.Amount = math.floor(v)

    addPanelHeader(Body, "ЦВЕТ")

        shadowTint = c
