addSectionHeader(tabPages["fx"], "ЭФФЕКТЫ")

local effectPriority = {
    Interval = 0.2,
    Active = {},
    Pinned = {},
    Held = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
    Conn = nil,
    Next = 0,
}

function effectPriority.isOurs(instance)
    return instance.Name:match("^Custom.*Preview$") ~= nil
end

function effectPriority.sweep()
    local containers = {Lighting}
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then containers[2] = camera end
    for className in pairs(effectPriority.Active) do
        for _, container in ipairs(containers) do
            for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA(className) and not effectPriority.isOurs(child) and child.Enabled then
                    effectPriority.Held[child] = true
                    child.Enabled = false
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function effectPriority.step()
    local now = os.clock()
    if now < effectPriority.Next then return end
    effectPriority.Next = now + effectPriority.Interval
    effectPriority.sweep()
end

function effectPriority.set(className, on)
    on = on or effectPriority.Pinned[className]
    effectPriority.Active[className] = on or nil
    if on then
        effectPriority.sweep()
        if not effectPriority.Conn then
            effectPriority.Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(effectPriority.step)
        end
        return
    end
    for instance in pairs(effectPriority.Held) do
        if instance:IsA(className) then
            effectPriority.Held[instance] = nil
            pcall(function() instance.Enabled = true end)
        end
    end
    if next(effectPriority.Active) == nil and effectPriority.Conn then
        effectPriority.Conn:Disconnect()
        effectPriority.Conn = nil
    end
end

    end
end

local function applyBlur(enabled)

        if blurEffect then blurEffect:Destroy() end

        blurEffect.Name = "CustomBlurPreview"
        blurEffect.Size = blurSize
        blurEffect.Parent = Lighting
        effectPriority.set("BlurEffect", true)
    else
        if blurEffect then blurEffect:Destroy(); blurEffect = nil end
        effectPriority.set("BlurEffect", false)
    end
end

local clockTimeEnabled = false
local clockTimeValue = 12
local clockTimeConn = nil
local clockCycleEnabled = false
local clockCycleSpeed = 1
local clockRealEnabled = false
local clockTimeSliderSetValue = nil
local clockTimeReconnectLoopId = 0

local function applyClockTime(enabled)
    clockTimeEnabled = enabled
    clockTimeReconnectLoopId = clockTimeReconnectLoopId + 1
    local myLoopId = clockTimeReconnectLoopId
    if clockTimeConn then clockTimeConn:Disconnect(); clockTimeConn = nil end
    if enabled then
        Lighting.ClockTime = clockTimeValue
        local function enforce(dt)
            if not (clockTimeEnabled and clockTimeReconnectLoopId == myLoopId) then return end
            if clockRealEnabled then
                local now = os.date("*t")
                clockTimeValue = now.hour + now.min / 60 + now.sec / 3600
                if clockTimeSliderSetValue then clockTimeSliderSetValue(clockTimeValue) end
            elseif clockCycleEnabled then
                clockTimeValue = clockTimeValue + clockCycleSpeed * dt
                if clockTimeValue >= 24 then clockTimeValue = clockTimeValue - 24 end
                if clockTimeSliderSetValue then clockTimeSliderSetValue(clockTimeValue) end
            end
            Lighting.ClockTime = clockTimeValue
        end
        clockTimeConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(enforce)
        task.spawn(function()
            while clockTimeEnabled and clockTimeReconnectLoopId == myLoopId do
                task.wait(1)
                if clockTimeEnabled and clockTimeReconnectLoopId == myLoopId then
                    if clockTimeConn then clockTimeConn:Disconnect() end
                    clockTimeConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(enforce)
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        if clockTimeConn then clockTimeConn:Disconnect(); clockTimeConn = nil end
        Lighting.ClockTime = originalLighting.ClockTime
    end
end

addToggleRow(tabPages["light"], "ClockTime", true, applyClockTime, function(Body)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ОСНОВНОЕ")
    local _, timeSetValue = addSlider(Body, "Время", 0, 24, clockTimeValue, 2, function(v)
        clockTimeValue = v
        if clockTimeEnabled then Lighting.ClockTime = v end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(240,210,120))
    clockTimeSliderSetValue = timeSetValue
    local refreshCycle, refreshReal
    local function addClockPill(label, isOn, onClick)
        local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-70,1,0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = label, TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
        local pill = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1,-14,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,40,0,22),
            BackgroundColor3 = isOn() and COL.pinkOn or COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = pill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        mk("UIStroke", {Parent = pill, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.fromRGB(48,42,64),
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
        local thumb = mk("Frame", {Parent = pill, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
            Position = isOn() and UDim2.new(1,-11,0.5,0) or UDim2.new(0,11,0.5,0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0,16,0,16), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1), BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = thumb, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(pill, "toggle")
        pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(onClick)
        return function()
            TweenService:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundColor3 = isOn() and COL.pinkOn or COL.off}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(thumb, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                Position = isOn() and UDim2.new(1,-11,0.5,0) or UDim2.new(0,11,0.5,0)}):Play()
        end
    end
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ЦИКЛ ВРЕМЕНИ")
    refreshCycle = addClockPill("Цикл времени", function() return clockCycleEnabled end, function()
        clockCycleEnabled = not clockCycleEnabled
        if clockCycleEnabled then clockRealEnabled = false end
        refreshCycle()
        refreshReal()
    end)
    addSlider(Body, "Скорость цикла", 0, 24, clockCycleSpeed, 5, function(v)
        clockCycleSpeed = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(220,190,130))
    refreshReal = addClockPill("Время компьютера (os.date)", function() return clockRealEnabled end, function()
        clockRealEnabled = not clockRealEnabled
        if clockRealEnabled then clockCycleEnabled = false end
        refreshCycle()
        refreshReal()
    end)
end, 340, "🕛")

local existingAtmosphere = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
local originalFogDensity, originalFogOffset, originalFogHaze, originalFogColor
if existingAtmosphere then
    originalFogDensity = existingAtmosphere.Density
    originalFogOffset = existingAtmosphere.Offset
    originalFogHaze = existingAtmosphere.Haze
    originalFogColor = existingAtmosphere.Color
end

local fogAtmosphere = nil
local fogIsOwnInstance = false
local fogDensity = 0.4
local fogOffset = 0.25
local fogHaze = 1
local fogColor = Color3.new(1,1,1)

local fogConn = nil
local function applyFog(enabled)
    if enabled then
        if existingAtmosphere then
            fogAtmosphere = existingAtmosphere
            fogIsOwnInstance = false
        else
            fogAtmosphere = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
            fogAtmosphere.Name = "CustomFogPreview"
            fogAtmosphere.Parent = Lighting
            fogIsOwnInstance = true
        end
        local function enforce()
            fogAtmosphere.Density = fogDensity
            fogAtmosphere.Offset = fogOffset
            fogAtmosphere.Haze = fogHaze
            fogAtmosphere.Color = fogColor
        end
        enforce()
        fogConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(enforce)
    else
        if fogConn then fogConn:Disconnect(); fogConn = nil end
        if fogAtmosphere then
            if fogIsOwnInstance then
                fogAtmosphere:Destroy()
            else
                fogAtmosphere.Density = originalFogDensity
                fogAtmosphere.Offset = originalFogOffset
                fogAtmosphere.Haze = originalFogHaze
                fogAtmosphere.Color = originalFogColor
            end
            fogAtmosphere = nil
        end
    end
end

addSectionHeader(tabPages["atmo"], "АТМОСФЕРА")
ToggleSetters.atmoRow, ToggleSetters.atmo = addToggleRow(tabPages["atmo"], "Атмосфера", true, applyFog, function(Body)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ОСНОВНОЕ")
    addSlider(Body, "Плотность", 0, 1, fogDensity, 2, function(v)
        fogDensity = v
        if fogAtmosphere then fogAtmosphere.Density = v end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(160,180,200))
    addSlider(Body, "Смещение", 0, 1, fogOffset, 2, function(v)
        fogOffset = v
        if fogAtmosphere then fogAtmosphere.Offset = v end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(150,165,195))
    addSlider(Body, "Дымка", 0, 1, fogHaze, 2, function(v)
        fogHaze = v
        if fogAtmosphere then fogAtmosphere.Haze = v end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(140,150,190))
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ЦВЕТ")
    addColorPicker(Body, "Оттенок тумана", fogColor, function(c)
        fogColor = c
        if fogAtmosphere then fogAtmosphere.Color = c end
    end)
end, 300, "🌫️")

local existingClouds = workspace.Terrain:FindFirstChildOfClass("Clouds")
local originalCloudCover, originalCloudDensity, originalCloudColor, originalCloudEnabled
if existingClouds then
    originalCloudCover = existingClouds.Cover
    originalCloudDensity = existingClouds.Density
    originalCloudColor = existingClouds.Color
    originalCloudEnabled = existingClouds.Enabled
end

local cloudsInstance = nil
local cloudsIsOwnInstance = false
local cloudsSky = nil
local cloudCover = 0.5
local cloudDensity = 0.5
local cloudColor = Color3.new(1,1,1)
local cloudsConn = nil

local function enforceClouds()
    local terrain = workspace.Terrain
    local inst = cloudsInstance
    if not inst or inst.Parent ~= terrain then
        local found = terrain:FindFirstChildOfClass("Clouds")
        if found then
            local ours = found.Name == "CustomCloudsPreview"
            if not ours and found ~= existingClouds then
                existingClouds = found
                originalCloudCover = found.Cover
                originalCloudDensity = found.Density
                originalCloudColor = found.Color
                originalCloudEnabled = found.Enabled
            end
            inst = found
            cloudsIsOwnInstance = ours
        else
            inst = Instance.new("Clouds")
            inst.Name = "CustomCloudsPreview"
            inst.Parent = terrain
            cloudsIsOwnInstance = true
        end
        cloudsInstance = inst
    end
    if not Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky") then
        if cloudsSky then pcall(function() cloudsSky:Destroy() end) end
        cloudsSky = Instance.new("Sky")
        cloudsSky.Name = "CustomCloudsSkyPreview"
        cloudsSky.Parent = Lighting
    end
    inst.Enabled = true
    inst.Cover = cloudCover
    inst.Density = cloudDensity
    inst.Color = cloudColor
end

local function applyClouds(enabled)
    if cloudsConn then cloudsConn:Disconnect(); cloudsConn = nil end
    if enabled then
        enforceClouds()
        cloudsConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(enforceClouds)
    else
        local inst = cloudsInstance
        cloudsInstance = nil
        if inst then
            if cloudsIsOwnInstance then
                pcall(function() inst:Destroy() end)
            else
                pcall(function()
                    inst.Cover = originalCloudCover
                    inst.Density = originalCloudDensity
                    inst.Color = originalCloudColor
                    inst.Enabled = originalCloudEnabled
                end)
            end
        end
        if cloudsSky then
            pcall(function() cloudsSky:Destroy() end)
            cloudsSky = nil
        end
    end
end

addSectionHeader(tabPages["atmo"], "ПОГОДА")

local newFogEnabled = false
local newFogParts = {}
local newFogEmitters = {}
local newFogConn = nil
local newFogDensity = 20
local newFogColor = Color3.fromRGB(200,200,210)
local newFogGridSize = 10

local function rebuildNewFogGrid()
    for _, part in ipairs(newFogParts) do
        if part.Parent then part:Destroy() end
    end
    newFogParts = {}
    newFogEmitters = {}
    local half = (newFogGridSize - 1) / 2
    for gx = 0, newFogGridSize - 1 do
        for gz = 0, newFogGridSize - 1 do
            local tile = Instance.new("Part")
            tile.Name = "CustomBigFogPreview"
            tile.Size = Vector3.new(300, 1000, 300)
            tile.Anchored = true
            tile.CanCollide = false
            tile.CanQuery = false
            tile.Transparency = 1
            tile.Parent = workspace

            local em = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
            em.Parent = tile
            em.Texture = "rbxassetid://244514423"
            em.Rate = newFogDensity
            em.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(10, 15)
            em.Speed = NumberRange.new(1, 3)
            em.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
            em.Size = NumberSequence.new(80)
            em.Transparency = NumberSequence.new{
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.7),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.5),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
            }
            em.Color = ColorSequence.new(newFogColor)
            em.Shape = Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box
            em.ShapeStyle = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume

            table.insert(newFogParts, tile)
            table.insert(newFogEmitters, em)
        end
    end
end

local function applyNewFog(enabled)
    newFogEnabled = enabled
    if enabled then
        rebuildNewFogGrid()
        newFogConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not cam then return end
            local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
            local half = (newFogGridSize - 1) / 2
            local idx = 0
            for gx = 0, newFogGridSize - 1 do
                for gz = 0, newFogGridSize - 1 do
                    idx = idx + 1
                    local tile = newFogParts[idx]
                    if tile then
                        local offset = Vector3.new((gx - half) * 300, 0, (gz - half) * 300)
                        tile.CFrame = CFrame.new(camPos + offset)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        if newFogConn then newFogConn:Disconnect(); newFogConn = nil end
        for _, part in ipairs(newFogParts) do
            if part.Parent then part:Destroy() end
        end
        newFogParts = {}
        newFogEmitters = {}
    end
end

addToggleRow(tabPages["atmo"], "Туман", true, applyNewFog, function(Body)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ОСНОВНОЕ")
    addSlider(Body, "Плотность", 1, 100, newFogDensity, 0, function(v)
        local newDensity = math.floor(v)
        if newDensity == newFogDensity then return end
        newFogDensity = newDensity
        for _, em in ipairs(newFogEmitters) do em.Rate = newDensity end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(160,180,200))
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ЦВЕТ")
    addColorPicker(Body, "Цвет тумана", newFogColor, function(c)
        newFogColor = c
        for _, em in ipairs(newFogEmitters) do em.Color = ColorSequence.new(c) end
    end)
end, 300, "🌫️")

addToggleRow(tabPages["atmo"], "Облака", true, applyClouds, function(Body)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ОСНОВНОЕ")
    addSlider(Body, "Покрытие", 0, 1, cloudCover, 2, function(v)
        cloudCover = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(200,205,215))
    addSlider(Body, "Плотность", 0, 1, cloudDensity, 2, function(v)
        cloudDensity = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(190,195,210))
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ЦВЕТ")
    addColorPicker(Body, "Цвет облаков", cloudColor, function(c)
        cloudColor = c
    end)
end, 340, "⛅")

local skyState = {
    Enabled = false,
    Instance = nil,
    Hidden = {},
    Conn = nil,
    SunSize = 21,
    MoonSize = 11,
    StarCount = 3000,
    BodiesShown = true,
    Faces = {
        {Key = "SkyboxUp", Label = "Верх", Id = ""},
        {Key = "SkyboxDn", Label = "Низ", Id = ""},
        {Key = "SkyboxFt", Label = "Перед", Id = ""},
        {Key = "SkyboxBk", Label = "Зад", Id = ""},
        {Key = "SkyboxLf", Label = "Лево", Id = ""},
        {Key = "SkyboxRt", Label = "Право", Id = ""},
    },
    Defaults = {},
    ResetDelay = 1.5,
}

do
    local probe = Instance.new("Sky")
    for _, face in ipairs(skyState.Faces) do
        skyState.Defaults[face.Key] = probe[face.Key]
    end
    probe:Destroy()
end

do
    local found = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
    if found then
        skyState.SunSize = found.SunAngularSize
        skyState.MoonSize = found.MoonAngularSize
        skyState.StarCount = found.StarCount
        skyState.BodiesShown = found.CelestialBodiesShown
    end
end

function skyState.hideOthers()
    for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Sky") and child ~= skyState.Instance then
            skyState.Hidden[#skyState.Hidden + 1] = child
            child.Parent = nil
        end
    end
end

function skyState.resolve(text)
    local digits = string.match(tostring(text or ""), "%d+")
    if not digits then return nil end
    return "rbxassetid://" .. digits
end

function skyState.applyFaces()
    local sky = skyState.Instance
    if not sky then return end
    for _, face in ipairs(skyState.Faces) do
        if face.Resolved then
            if face.Applied ~= face.Resolved then
                sky[face.Key] = face.Resolved
                face.Applied = face.Resolved
            end
        elseif face.Applied then
            local default = skyState.Defaults[face.Key]
            if default then sky[face.Key] = default end
            face.Applied = nil
        end
    end
end

function skyState.commit()
    local valid = true
    for _, face in ipairs(skyState.Faces) do
        face.Resolved = skyState.resolve(face.Id)
        if face.Id ~= "" and not face.Resolved then valid = false end
    end
    skyState.applyFaces()
    return valid
end

function skyState.apply(enabled)
    if skyState.Conn then skyState.Conn:Disconnect(); skyState.Conn = nil end
    skyState.Enabled = enabled
    if enabled then
        if skyState.Instance then skyState.Instance:Destroy() end
        local sky = Instance.new("Sky")
        sky.Name = "CustomSkyPreview"
        sky.Parent = Lighting
        skyState.Instance = sky
        for _, face in ipairs(skyState.Faces) do face.Applied = nil end
        skyState.applyFaces()
        local function enforce()
            if sky.Parent ~= Lighting then sky.Parent = Lighting end
            skyState.hideOthers()
            if sky.SunAngularSize ~= skyState.SunSize then sky.SunAngularSize = skyState.SunSize end
            if sky.MoonAngularSize ~= skyState.MoonSize then sky.MoonAngularSize = skyState.MoonSize end
            if sky.StarCount ~= skyState.StarCount then sky.StarCount = skyState.StarCount end
            if sky.CelestialBodiesShown ~= skyState.BodiesShown then sky.CelestialBodiesShown = skyState.BodiesShown end
        end
        enforce()
        skyState.Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(enforce)
    else
        local sky = skyState.Instance
        skyState.Instance = nil
        if sky then sky:Destroy() end
        for _, child in ipairs(skyState.Hidden) do
            pcall(function() child.Parent = Lighting end)
        end
        skyState.Hidden = {}
    end
end

addToggleRow(tabPages["atmo"], "Небо", true, skyState.apply, function(Body)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ОСНОВНОЕ")
    addSlider(Body, "Размер солнца", 0, 60, skyState.SunSize, 0, function(v)
        skyState.SunSize = math.floor(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(240,200,120))
    addSlider(Body, "Размер луны", 0, 60, skyState.MoonSize, 0, function(v)
        skyState.MoonSize = math.floor(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(200,210,235))
    addSlider(Body, "Количество звёзд", 0, 5000, skyState.StarCount, 0, function(v)
        skyState.StarCount = math.floor(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(190,185,240))
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ОТОБРАЖЕНИЕ")
    do
        local bodiesRow = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = bodiesRow, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = bodiesRow, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-70,1,0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = "Солнце и луна", TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
        local pill = mk("TextButton", {Parent = bodiesRow, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1,-14,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,40,0,22),
            BackgroundColor3 = skyState.BodiesShown and COL.pinkOn or COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = pill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        mk("UIStroke", {Parent = pill, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.fromRGB(48,42,64),
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
        local thumb = mk("Frame", {Parent = pill, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
            Position = skyState.BodiesShown and UDim2.new(1,-11,0.5,0) or UDim2.new(0,11,0.5,0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0,16,0,16), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1), BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = thumb, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(pill, "toggle")
        pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            skyState.BodiesShown = not skyState.BodiesShown
            TweenService:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundColor3 = skyState.BodiesShown and COL.pinkOn or COL.off}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(thumb, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                Position = skyState.BodiesShown and UDim2.new(1,-11,0.5,0) or UDim2.new(0,11,0.5,0)}):Play()
        end)
    end
    addPanelHeader(Body, "КАСТОМНОЕ НЕБО")
    local inputs = {}
    for _, face in ipairs(skyState.Faces) do
        local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(0.35,-14,1,0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = face.Label, TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
        local box = mk("TextBox", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1,-10,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0.65,-10,0,28),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
            TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = COL.text, PlaceholderText = "ID картинки",
            PlaceholderColor3 = COL.textMuted, Text = face.Id, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = box, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,8)})
        mk("UIPadding", {Parent = box, PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,8), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,8)})
        inputs[face] = box
    end
    local applyButton = mk("TextButton", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,36),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.pinkOn, BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = COL.text, Text = "Применить"})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = applyButton, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
    applyButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        for face, box in pairs(inputs) do
            face.Id = string.match(box.Text, "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        end
        local valid = skyState.commit()
        applyButton.Text = valid and "Применено" or "Проверь айди"
        task.delay(skyState.ResetDelay, function()
            if applyButton.Parent then applyButton.Text = "Применить" end
        end)
    end)
end, 520, "🌌")


local function syncCharacterFilter(params, lastChar)
    local char = player.Character
    if char ~= lastChar then
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = char and {char} or {}
    end
    return char
end

local function isRoofAbove(position, baseParams, maxHops)
    maxHops = maxHops or 5
    local origin = position
    local remaining = 200
    local result = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, remaining, 0), baseParams)
    if not result then return false end
    if not (result.Instance and result.Instance.Transparency and result.Instance.Transparency >= 1) then
        return true
    end
    local extraIgnore = {}
    for _, v in ipairs(baseParams.FilterDescendantsInstances) do table.insert(extraIgnore, v) end
    table.insert(extraIgnore, result.Instance)
    local traveled = (result.Position - origin).Magnitude
    remaining = remaining - traveled - 0.1
    origin = result.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
    for _ = 2, maxHops do
        if remaining <= 0 then return false end
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = extraIgnore
        local r2 = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, remaining, 0), params)
        if not r2 then return false end
        if not (r2.Instance and r2.Instance.Transparency and r2.Instance.Transparency >= 1) then
            return true
        end
        table.insert(extraIgnore, r2.Instance)
        local traveled2 = (r2.Position - origin).Magnitude
        remaining = remaining - traveled2 - 0.1
        origin = r2.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
    end
    return false
end

function moonState.takeEntry()
    local entry = table.remove(moonState.Pool)
    if entry then return entry end
    local part = Instance.new("Part")
    part.Name = moonState.EmitterName
    part.Anchored = true
    part.CanCollide = false
    part.CanQuery = false
    part.CanTouch = false
    part.CastShadow = false
    part.Transparency = 1
    part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
    part.Parent = workspace
    local light = Instance.new("SpotLight")
    light.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
    light.Enabled = false
    light.Parent = part
    return {
        Part = part, Light = light, I = 0, J = 0, RX = 0, RZ = 0, RB = 0.5, RR = 1,
        GroundY = nil, RetryAt = 0, Dirty = true, On = false, Brightness = -1, PropsVersion = -1,
    }
end

function moonState.assignEntry(entry, layerIndex, i, j)
    local rng = Random.new((i * 73856093 + j * 19349663 + layerIndex * 83492791) % 2147483647)
    entry.I, entry.J = i, j
    entry.RX = (rng:NextNumber() - 0.5) * moonState.Scatter
    entry.RZ = (rng:NextNumber() - 0.5) * moonState.Scatter
    entry.RB = rng:NextNumber()
    entry.RR = 0.6 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.4
    entry.GroundY = nil
    entry.Dirty = true
    entry.Brightness = -1
    entry.PropsVersion = -1
    entry.RetryAt = 0
end

function moonState.findGround(x, z, referenceY)
    local base = moonState.RoofParams
    local origin = Vector3.new(x, referenceY + 500, z)
    local params = base
    local ignore = nil
    for _ = 1, 4 do
        local result = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -2000, 0), params)
        if not result then return false end
        if result.Instance.Transparency >= 1 then
            if not ignore then
                ignore = {table.unpack(base.FilterDescendantsInstances)}
                params = RaycastParams.new()
                params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                params.IgnoreWater = true
            end
            ignore[#ignore + 1] = result.Instance
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
        else
            if result.Normal.Y >= moonState.MinSurfaceUp then return result.Position.Y end
            return false
        end
    end
    return false
end

function moonState.retire(entry)
    entry.Light.Enabled = false
    entry.On = false
    moonState.Pool[#moonState.Pool + 1] = entry
end

function moonState.newLayer(index)
    return {
        Index = index, Cells = {}, Pending = {}, PendingHead = 1, BuildFocus = nil,
        Spacing = 0, Reach = 0, Max = 0, EffReach = 0, RefreshIndex = 0,
    }
end

function moonState.layerParams(layer)
    local reach = moonState.Reach
    local nearSpacing = moonState.Spacing
    if layer.Index == 1 then
        return nearSpacing, reach, moonState.MaxLights
    end
    local nearCoverage = nearSpacing * math.sqrt(moonState.MaxLights / math.pi)
    if reach <= nearCoverage then return nil end
    local spacing = math.max(nearSpacing * 4, reach * math.sqrt(math.pi / moonState.FarLights))
    return spacing, reach, moonState.FarLights
end

function moonState.clearLayer(layer)
    for _, entry in ipairs(layer.Cells) do moonState.retire(entry) end
    layer.Cells = {}
    layer.Pending = {}
    layer.PendingHead = 1
    layer.BuildFocus = nil
    layer.Spacing = 0
end

function moonState.rebuildLayer(layer, focus, spacing, reach, maxCount)
    if layer.Spacing ~= spacing then
        for _, entry in ipairs(layer.Cells) do moonState.retire(entry) end
        layer.Cells = {}
    end
    layer.Spacing, layer.Reach, layer.Max = spacing, reach, maxCount
    layer.BuildFocus = focus
    local capRadius = spacing * math.sqrt(maxCount / math.pi) * 1.25
    local limit = math.min(reach, capRadius) + spacing * 0.75
    local limitSq = limit * limit
    local candidates = {}
    for i = math.floor((focus.X - limit) / spacing), math.ceil((focus.X + limit) / spacing) do
        for j = math.floor((focus.Z - limit) / spacing), math.ceil((focus.Z + limit) / spacing) do
            local dx, dz = i * spacing - focus.X, j * spacing - focus.Z
            local distSq = dx * dx + dz * dz
            if distSq <= limitSq then
                candidates[#candidates + 1] = {i, j, distSq}
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(candidates, function(a, b) return a[3] < b[3] end)
    local count = math.min(#candidates, maxCount)
    local usedReach = reach
    if count > 0 and #candidates > count then
        usedReach = math.min(reach, math.sqrt(candidates[count][3]))
    end
    layer.EffReach = usedReach
    local wanted = {}
    for k = 1, count do
        local c = candidates[k]
        wanted[c[1] .. "," .. c[2]] = true
    end
    local kept, present = {}, {}
    for _, entry in ipairs(layer.Cells) do
        local key = entry.I .. "," .. entry.J
        if wanted[key] then
            kept[#kept + 1] = entry
            present[key] = true
        else
            moonState.retire(entry)
        end
    end
    local pending = {}
    for k = 1, count do
        local c = candidates[k]
        if not present[c[1] .. "," .. c[2]] then pending[#pending + 1] = c end
    end
    layer.Cells = kept
    layer.Pending = pending
    layer.PendingHead = 1
end

function moonState.hideLights()
    for _, layer in ipairs(moonState.Layers) do
        for _, entry in ipairs(layer.Cells) do
            if entry.On then
                entry.Light.Enabled = false
                entry.On = false
            end
        end
    end
end

function moonState.updateLight(factor, dt)
    if not (moonState.LightEnabled and factor > 0.01) then
        moonState.hideLights()
        return
    end
    local char = player.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local focus = root and root.Position or (camera and camera.CFrame.Position)
    if not focus then return end
    moonState.RoofChar = syncCharacterFilter(moonState.RoofParams, moonState.RoofChar)
    local now = os.clock()
    local created = 0
    for _, layer in ipairs(moonState.Layers) do
        local spacing, reach, maxCount = moonState.layerParams(layer)
        if not spacing then
            moonState.clearLayer(layer)
        else
            if not layer.BuildFocus
                or layer.Spacing ~= spacing
                or layer.Reach ~= reach
                or layer.Max ~= maxCount
                or (focus - layer.BuildFocus).Magnitude > spacing * 0.25 then
                moonState.rebuildLayer(layer, focus, spacing, reach, maxCount)
            end
            local pending = layer.Pending
            while created < moonState.CreatePerTick and layer.PendingHead <= #pending do
                local c = pending[layer.PendingHead]
                layer.PendingHead = layer.PendingHead + 1
                local entry = moonState.takeEntry()
                moonState.assignEntry(entry, layer.Index, c[1], c[2])
                layer.Cells[#layer.Cells + 1] = entry
                created = created + 1
            end
        end
    end
    local refresh = now >= moonState.RefreshAt
    if refresh then moonState.RefreshAt = now + 0.1 end
    local settingsKey = moonState.LightRange .. "|" .. moonState.LightHeight .. "|" .. moonState.LightAngle .. "|"
        .. tostring(moonState.LightShadows) .. "|" .. tostring(moonState.LightColor)
    if settingsKey ~= moonState.SettingsKey then
        moonState.SettingsKey = settingsKey
        moonState.PropsVersion = moonState.PropsVersion + 1
    end
    local drift = now * moonState.Drift
    local variation = moonState.Variation
    local groundBudget = moonState.GroundPerTick
    for _, layer in ipairs(moonState.Layers) do
        local spacing = layer.Spacing
        local cells = layer.Cells
        local effSq = layer.EffReach * layer.EffReach
        if refresh and #cells > 0 then
            layer.RefreshIndex = layer.RefreshIndex % #cells + 1
            local entry = cells[layer.RefreshIndex]
            local surfaceY = moonState.findGround((entry.I + entry.RX) * spacing, (entry.J + entry.RZ) * spacing, focus.Y)
            if surfaceY ~= entry.GroundY then
                entry.GroundY = surfaceY
                entry.Dirty = true
            end
        end
        for _, entry in ipairs(cells) do
            local px, pz = (entry.I + entry.RX) * spacing, (entry.J + entry.RZ) * spacing
            if groundBudget > 0 and (entry.GroundY == nil or (entry.GroundY == false and now >= entry.RetryAt)) then
                groundBudget = groundBudget - 1
                entry.GroundY = moonState.findGround(px, pz, focus.Y)
                entry.RetryAt = now + 3
                entry.Dirty = true
            end
            local surfaceY = entry.GroundY
            local brightness = 0
            if surfaceY then
                local dx, dz = px - focus.X, pz - focus.Z
                local distSq = dx * dx + dz * dz
                if distSq <= effSq then
                    local patch = 1
                    if variation > 0 then
                        local noise = math.clamp(math.noise(entry.I * 0.37 + 0.13, entry.J * 0.37 + 0.71, drift + 0.29) + 0.5, 0, 1)
                        patch = math.max(0, 1 + variation * (noise * 2 - 1)) * (1 + 0.5 * variation * (entry.RB * 2 - 1))
                    end
                    brightness = moonState.LightBrightness * factor * patch
                end
            end
            local light = entry.Light
            if brightness > 0.01 then
                local stale = entry.PropsVersion ~= moonState.PropsVersion
                if stale or entry.Dirty or not entry.On then
                    local source = Vector3.new(px, surfaceY + moonState.LightHeight, pz)
                    entry.Part.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(source, Vector3.new(px, surfaceY, pz), Vector3.zAxis)
                    entry.Dirty = false
                end
                if stale then
                    light.Range = moonState.LightRange * entry.RR
                    light.Angle = moonState.LightAngle
                    light.Color = moonState.LightColor
                    light.Shadows = moonState.LightShadows
                    entry.PropsVersion = moonState.PropsVersion
                end
                if math.abs(brightness - entry.Brightness) > 0.005 then
                    light.Brightness = brightness
                    entry.Brightness = brightness
                end
                if not entry.On then
                    light.Enabled = true
                    entry.On = true
                end
            elseif entry.On then
                light.Enabled = false
                entry.On = false
            end
        end
    end
end

function moonState.step(dt)
    local factor = moonState.factor()
    if shadowsEnabled then
        moonState.WrittenAmbient = nil
        moonState.WrittenOutdoor = nil
    else
        local ambient, outdoor = Lighting.Ambient, Lighting.OutdoorAmbient
        if ambient ~= moonState.WrittenAmbient then moonState.BaseAmbient = ambient end
        if outdoor ~= moonState.WrittenOutdoor then moonState.BaseOutdoor = outdoor end
        local targetAmbient = moonState.mix(moonState.BaseAmbient, factor)
        local targetOutdoor = moonState.mix(moonState.BaseOutdoor, factor)
        if shadowsLoop.Lift then
            targetAmbient = shadowsLoop.Lift(targetAmbient)
            targetOutdoor = shadowsLoop.Lift(targetOutdoor)
        end
        if ambient ~= targetAmbient then Lighting.Ambient = targetAmbient end
        if outdoor ~= targetOutdoor then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = targetOutdoor end
        moonState.WrittenAmbient = Lighting.Ambient
        moonState.WrittenOutdoor = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient
    end
    moonState.LightAccum = moonState.LightAccum + dt
    if moonState.LightAccum >= 0.05 then
        local elapsed = moonState.LightAccum
        moonState.LightAccum = 0
        moonState.updateLight(factor, elapsed)
    end
end

function moonState.apply(enabled)
    if moonState.Conn then moonState.Conn:Disconnect(); moonState.Conn = nil end
    moonState.Enabled = enabled
    for _, layer in ipairs(moonState.Layers) do
        for _, entry in ipairs(layer.Cells) do entry.Part:Destroy() end
    end
    for _, entry in ipairs(moonState.Pool) do entry.Part:Destroy() end
    moonState.Layers = {}
    moonState.Pool = {}
    if enabled then
        if shadowsLoop.Release then shadowsLoop.Release() end
        moonState.RoofChar = nil
        moonState.RoofParams = RaycastParams.new()
        moonState.RoofParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        moonState.RoofParams.IgnoreWater = true
        moonState.RefreshAt = 0
        moonState.LightAccum = 0
        moonState.SettingsKey = nil
        moonState.WrittenAmbient = nil
        moonState.WrittenOutdoor = nil
        moonState.Layers = {moonState.newLayer(1), moonState.newLayer(2)}
        moonState.Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(moonState.step)
    else
        if not shadowsEnabled then
            if moonState.BaseAmbient and moonState.WrittenAmbient and Lighting.Ambient == moonState.WrittenAmbient then
                Lighting.Ambient = moonState.BaseAmbient
            end
            if moonState.BaseOutdoor and moonState.WrittenOutdoor and Lighting.OutdoorAmbient == moonState.WrittenOutdoor then
                Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = moonState.BaseOutdoor
            end
        end
        moonState.BaseAmbient = nil
        moonState.BaseOutdoor = nil
        moonState.WrittenAmbient = nil
        moonState.WrittenOutdoor = nil
    end
end

addToggleRow(tabPages["atmo"], "Лунный свет", true, moonState.apply, function(Body)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ПОДСВЕТКА")
    addSlider(Body, "Яркость подсветки", 0, 1, moonState.Strength, 2, function(v)
        moonState.Strength = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(140,165,255))
    addColorPicker(Body, "Цвет подсветки", moonState.Color, function(c)
        moonState.Color = c
    end)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ПЯТНА СВЕТА")
    addSlider(Body, "Яркость пятен", 0, 10, moonState.LightBrightness, 2, function(v)
        moonState.LightBrightness = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(170,185,255))
    addSlider(Body, "Дальность пятен", 100, 100000, moonState.Reach, 0, function(v)
        moonState.Reach = math.floor(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(160,180,250), true)
    addColorPicker(Body, "Цвет пятен", moonState.LightColor, function(c)
        moonState.LightColor = c
    end)
end, 520, "🌙")

local windState = {
    Enabled = false,
    Strength = 15,
    Direction = 17,
    Color = Color3.new(1,1,1),
    Lifetime = 2,
    SpawnRate = 600,
    Sound = nil,
    SoundEnabled = true,
    SoundVolume = 1,
    LineQueue = {},
    LineAnchor = nil,
    LineConn = nil,
    SpawnAccum = 0,
    MaxDistance = 600,
    PassThroughWalls = false,
    ColorSeq = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1,1,1)),
    WidthScale = NumberSequence.new{
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.3),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2,1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8,1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0.3),
    },
    Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.7),
}

local function windDirVector()
    local rad = math.rad(windState.Direction)
    return Vector3.new(math.cos(rad), 0, math.sin(rad))
end

local function createWindLine()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam or not windState.LineAnchor then return end
    local position = (cam.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(
        math.rad(math.random(-30,70)), math.rad(math.random(-80,80)), 0
    ) * CFrame.new(0,0, math.random(200, windState.MaxDistance*10)*-0.1)).Position

    if not windState.PassThroughWalls then
        if isRoofAbove(position, windState.RoofParams) then return end
    end

    local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
    local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
    local trail = Instance.new("Trail")
    trail.Attachment0 = a0
    trail.Attachment1 = a1
    trail.WidthScale = windState.WidthScale
    trail.Transparency = windState.Transparency
    trail.Color = windState.ColorSeq
    trail.FaceCamera = true
    trail.Parent = a0

    a0.WorldPosition = position
    a1.WorldPosition = position + Vector3.new(0,0.1,0)
    a0.Parent = windState.LineAnchor
    a1.Parent = windState.LineAnchor

    local speed = math.max(0.1, windState.Strength + (math.random(-10,10)*0.1))
    table.insert(windState.LineQueue, {
        a0 = a0, a1 = a1, trail = trail,
        lifetime = windState.Lifetime + (math.random(-10,10)*0.1),
        position = position,
        speed = speed,
        startClock = os.clock(),
        seed = math.random(1,1000)*0.1,
    })
end

local function applyWind(enabled)
    windState.Enabled = enabled
    if enabled then
        windState.PrevGlobalWind = workspace.GlobalWind
        workspace.GlobalWind = windDirVector() * windState.Strength

        windState.LineAnchor = Instance.new("Part")
        windState.LineAnchor.Name = "CustomWindLinesAnchorPreview"
        windState.LineAnchor.Size = Vector3.new(1,1,1)
        windState.LineAnchor.Anchored = true
        windState.LineAnchor.CanCollide = false
        windState.LineAnchor.CanQuery = false
        windState.LineAnchor.Transparency = 1
        windState.LineAnchor.Parent = workspace

        windState.RoofParams = RaycastParams.new()
        windState.RoofParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        windState.RoofChar = nil

        windState.SpawnAccum = 0
        windState.LineConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            workspace.GlobalWind = windDirVector() * windState.Strength
            if windState.Strength <= 0 then return end
            local clock = os.clock()
            windState.RoofChar = syncCharacterFilter(windState.RoofParams, windState.RoofChar)
            windState.SpawnAccum = math.min(windState.SpawnAccum + dt * windState.SpawnRate, 400)
            local spawnedThisTick = 0
            while windState.SpawnAccum >= 1 and spawnedThisTick < 200 and #windState.LineQueue < 4000 do
                createWindLine()
                windState.SpawnAccum = windState.SpawnAccum - 1
                spawnedThisTick = spawnedThisTick + 1
            end
            local dir = windDirVector()
            for i = #windState.LineQueue, 1, -1 do
                local wl = windState.LineQueue[i]
                local aliveTime = clock - wl.startClock
                if aliveTime >= wl.lifetime then
                    wl.a0:Destroy()
                    wl.a1:Destroy()
                    wl.trail:Destroy()
                    local lastIdx = #windState.LineQueue
                    windState.LineQueue[i] = windState.LineQueue[lastIdx]
                    windState.LineQueue[lastIdx] = nil
                else
                    wl.trail.MaxLength = 20 - (20*(aliveTime/wl.lifetime))
                    local seededClock = (clock + wl.seed) * (wl.speed * 0.2)
                    local newPos = wl.position + dir * (wl.speed * aliveTime)
                        + Vector3.new(math.sin(seededClock)*0.5, math.sin(seededClock)*0.8, math.sin(seededClock)*0.5)
                    wl.a0.WorldPosition = newPos
                    wl.a1.WorldPosition = newPos + Vector3.new(0,0.1,0)
                end
            end
        end)

        windState.Sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        windState.Sound.Name = "CustomWindSoundPreview"
        windState.Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://97357579003831"
        windState.Sound.Looped = true
        windState.Sound.Volume = windState.SoundVolume
        windState.Sound.Parent = SoundService
        if windState.SoundEnabled then windState.Sound:Play() end
    else
        workspace.GlobalWind = windState.PrevGlobalWind or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        windState.PrevGlobalWind = nil
        if windState.LineConn then windState.LineConn:Disconnect(); windState.LineConn = nil end
        for _, wl in ipairs(windState.LineQueue) do
            wl.a0:Destroy()
            wl.a1:Destroy()
            wl.trail:Destroy()
        end
        windState.LineQueue = {}
        if windState.LineAnchor then windState.LineAnchor:Destroy(); windState.LineAnchor = nil end
        if windState.Sound then windState.Sound:Stop(); windState.Sound:Destroy(); windState.Sound = nil end
    end
end

addToggleRow(tabPages["atmo"], "Ветер", true, applyWind, function(Body)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ОСНОВНОЕ")
    addSlider(Body, "Скорость ветра", 0, 50, windState.Strength, 0, function(v)
        windState.Strength = v
        if windState.Enabled then workspace.GlobalWind = windDirVector() * v end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(180,200,220))
    addSlider(Body, "Тик ветров", 100, 3000, windState.SpawnRate, 0, function(v)
        windState.SpawnRate = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(170,190,215))
    addSlider(Body, "Время жизни ветра", 0.5, 10, windState.Lifetime, 1, function(v)
        windState.Lifetime = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(165,185,210))
    addSlider(Body, "Направление ветра", 0, 360, windState.Direction, 0, function(v)
        windState.Direction = v
        if windState.Enabled then workspace.GlobalWind = windDirVector() * windState.Strength end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(175,195,225))
    addSlider(Body, "Дальность ветра", 100, 3000, windState.MaxDistance, 0, function(v)
        windState.MaxDistance = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(170,185,215))
    do
        local wallsRow = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = wallsRow, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = wallsRow, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-70,1,0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = "Проходить сквозь стены", TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
        local pill = mk("TextButton", {Parent = wallsRow, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1,-14,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,40,0,22),
            BackgroundColor3 = windState.PassThroughWalls and COL.pinkOn or COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = pill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        mk("UIStroke", {Parent = pill, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.fromRGB(48,42,64),
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
        local thumb = mk("Frame", {Parent = pill, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
            Position = windState.PassThroughWalls and UDim2.new(1,-11,0.5,0) or UDim2.new(0,11,0.5,0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0,16,0,16), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1), BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = thumb, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(pill, "toggle")
        pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            windState.PassThroughWalls = not windState.PassThroughWalls
            TweenService:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundColor3 = windState.PassThroughWalls and COL.pinkOn or COL.off}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(thumb, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                Position = windState.PassThroughWalls and UDim2.new(1,-11,0.5,0) or UDim2.new(0,11,0.5,0)}):Play()
        end)
    end
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ЗВУК")
    do
        local soundRow = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = soundRow, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = soundRow, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-70,1,0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = "Звук ветра", TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
        local pill = mk("TextButton", {Parent = soundRow, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1,-14,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,40,0,22),
            BackgroundColor3 = windState.SoundEnabled and COL.pinkOn or COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = pill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        mk("UIStroke", {Parent = pill, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.fromRGB(48,42,64),
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
        local thumb = mk("Frame", {Parent = pill, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
            Position = windState.SoundEnabled and UDim2.new(1,-11,0.5,0) or UDim2.new(0,11,0.5,0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0,16,0,16), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1), BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = thumb, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(pill, "toggle")
        pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            windState.SoundEnabled = not windState.SoundEnabled
            TweenService:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundColor3 = windState.SoundEnabled and COL.pinkOn or COL.off}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(thumb, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                Position = windState.SoundEnabled and UDim2.new(1,-11,0.5,0) or UDim2.new(0,11,0.5,0)}):Play()
            if windState.Sound then
                if windState.SoundEnabled then windState.Sound:Play() else windState.Sound:Pause() end
            end
        end)
    end
    addSlider(Body, "Громкость ветра", 0, 10, windState.SoundVolume, 2, function(v)
        windState.SoundVolume = v
        if windState.Sound then windState.Sound.Volume = v end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(160,180,210))
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ЦВЕТ")
    addColorPicker(Body, "Цвет ветра", windState.Color, function(c)
        windState.Color = c
        windState.ColorSeq = ColorSequence.new(c)
    end)
end, 600, "🌬️")

local weatherGrid = {
    Layers = 3,
    NearRings = 3,
    DensityRadius = 4,
}

function weatherGrid.plan(radius)
    local plan = {}
    local half = (weatherGrid.Layers - 1) / 2
    local boost = (2 * math.min(radius, weatherGrid.DensityRadius) + 1) / weatherGrid.Layers
    for gx = -radius, radius do
        for gz = -radius, radius do
            local ring = math.max(math.abs(gx), math.abs(gz))
            if ring <= weatherGrid.NearRings then
                for gy = -half, half do
                    plan[#plan + 1] = {gx = gx, gy = gy, gz = gz, boost = boost, layers = weatherGrid.Layers}
                end
            else
                plan[#plan + 1] = {gx = gx, gy = 0, gz = gz, boost = boost * weatherGrid.Layers, layers = 1}
            end
        end
    end
    return plan
end

function weatherGrid.rate(entry, intensity)
    return intensity * entry.boost
end

rainState.SplashColor = rainState.ColorSeq

rainState.Variant3 = {
    Texture = "rbxassetid://3806148993",
    Lifetime = NumberRange.new(5, 10),
    Speed = 100,
    ReferenceReach = 130,
    Drag = 0.75,
    Size = NumberSequence.new(1),
    Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0),
    Squash = NumberSequence.new(1),
    Rotation = NumberRange.new(-90, -90),
    LightEmission = 0,
    LightInfluence = 0.3,
    WindAffectsDrag = true,
    ColumnRate = 81,
    BaseIntensity = 3,
}

function rainState.fall()
    return rainState.Up + rainState.Down
end

function rainState.reach()
    return rainState.fall() * rainState.SplashReach
end

function rainState.rate(entry)
    if rainState.Variant == 3 then
        local streak = rainState.Variant3
        return streak.ColumnRate * rainState.Intensity / streak.BaseIntensity / entry.layers
    end
    return weatherGrid.rate(entry, rainState.Intensity)
end

function rainState.style(emitter)
    local streak = rainState.Variant3
    local length = rainState.fall()
    if rainState.Variant == 3 then
        emitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(streak.Speed * length / streak.ReferenceReach)
    else
        emitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(length / rainState.DropFallSpeed)
    end
end
