function rainState.Puddles.cellKey(cx, cz)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    return (cx + puddles.CellOffset) * 65536 + (cz + puddles.CellOffset)
end

function rainState.Puddles.refreshFilter()
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local list = {puddles.Folder}
    for _, other in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if other.Character then list[#list + 1] = other.Character end
    end
    puddles.Params.FilterDescendantsInstances = list
end

function rainState.Puddles.imagesLoaded()
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = puddles.Name .. "Probe"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    local labels = {}
    for _, id in pairs(puddles.Maps) do
        labels[#labels + 1] = mk("ImageLabel", {Parent = gui, Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Image = id})
    end
    gui.Parent = guiParent
    local started = os.clock()
    local ready = false
    while os.clock() - started < puddles.LoadTimeout do
        ready = true
        for _, label in ipairs(labels) do
            if not label.IsLoaded then
                ready = false
                break
            end
        end
        if ready then break end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    gui:Destroy()
    return ready
end

function rainState.Puddles.load(variant)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    if puddles.Sets[variant] then return end
    local set = {Parts = {}, Ready = false, Failed = false}
    puddles.Sets[variant] = set
    local look = puddles.Looks[variant]
    local epoch = puddles.Epoch
    task.spawn(function()
        local meshes = {}
        local wantsMaps = false
        local failed = false
        for _, layer in ipairs(look.Layers) do
            if not meshes[layer.Mesh] then
                local okMesh, mesh = pcall(function()
                    return game:GetService("InsertService"):CreateMeshPartAsync(
                        layer.Mesh, Enum.CollisionFidelity.Default, Enum.RenderFidelity.Precise)
                end)
                if not okMesh or not mesh then
                    failed = true
                    break
                end
                meshes[layer.Mesh] = mesh
            end
            if layer.Maps then wantsMaps = true end
        end
        if not failed and wantsMaps then failed = not puddles.imagesLoaded() end
        if epoch ~= puddles.Epoch then
            for _, mesh in pairs(meshes) do mesh:Destroy() end
            return
        end
        if failed then
            set.Failed = true
        else
            for index, layer in ipairs(look.Layers) do
                local part = meshes[layer.Mesh]:Clone()
                part.Name = puddles.Name
                part.Anchored = true
                part.CanCollide = false
                part.CanQuery = false
                part.CanTouch = false
                part.CastShadow = false
                part.Material = layer.Material
                part.Reflectance = layer.Reflectance
                part.Transparency = 1
                if layer.Maps then
                    local appearance = Instance.new("SurfaceAppearance")
                    appearance.ColorMap = puddles.Maps.ColorMap
                    appearance.NormalMap = puddles.Maps.NormalMap
                    appearance.MetalnessMap = puddles.Maps.MetalnessMap
                    appearance.RoughnessMap = puddles.Maps.RoughnessMap
                    appearance.AlphaMode = Enum.AlphaMode.Transparency
                    appearance.Parent = part
                end
                set.Parts[index] = part
            end
        end
        for _, mesh in pairs(meshes) do mesh:Destroy() end
        set.Ready = true
    end)
end

function rainState.Puddles.ready()
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local set = puddles.Sets[puddles.Variant]
    return set ~= nil and set.Ready
end

function rainState.Puddles.syncTint()
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    if puddles.Tint ~= rainState.Color then
        puddles.Tint = rainState.Color
        puddles.TintVersion = puddles.TintVersion + 1
    end
end

function rainState.Puddles.paint(entry)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    entry.TintVersion = puddles.TintVersion
    for _, layer in ipairs(entry.Layers) do
        layer.Part.Color = puddles.Tint
        if layer.Skin then layer.Skin.Color = puddles.Tint end
    end
end

function rainState.Puddles.build(position, sizeX, sizeZ, yaw)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local look = puddles.Looks[puddles.Variant]
    local set = puddles.Sets[puddles.Variant]
    local layers = {}
    if set and set.Ready and not set.Failed then
        local scaleX = sizeX / look.Width
        local scaleZ = look.Stretch and sizeZ / look.Depth or scaleX
        local base = CFrame.new(position) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
        for index, layer in ipairs(look.Layers) do
            local part = set.Parts[index]:Clone()
            part.Size = Vector3.new(layer.Width * scaleX, layer.Height, layer.Depth * scaleZ)
            part.CFrame = base * CFrame.new(layer.X * scaleX, layer.Y, layer.Z * scaleZ)
                * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(layer.Yaw), 0)
            layers[index] = {Part = part, Skin = part:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceAppearance"),
                Target = layer.Transparency}
        end
    else
        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Name = puddles.Name
        part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanCollide = false
        part.CanQuery = false
        part.CanTouch = false
        part.CastShadow = false
        part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
        part.Reflectance = puddles.FallbackReflectance
        part.Size = Vector3.new(puddles.Thickness, sizeX, sizeZ)
        part.CFrame = CFrame.new(position + Vector3.new(0, puddles.Lift, 0))
            * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, math.pi / 2)
        layers[1] = {Part = part, Target = puddles.FallbackTransparency}
    end
    for _, layer in ipairs(layers) do
        layer.Part.Transparency = 1
        layer.Part.Color = puddles.Tint
        if layer.Skin then layer.Skin.Color = puddles.Tint end
    end
    local root = layers[1].Part
    if #layers > 1 then
        root = Instance.new("Model")
        root.Name = puddles.Name
        for _, layer in ipairs(layers) do layer.Part.Parent = root end
    end
    root.Parent = puddles.Folder
    return root, layers
end

function rainState.Puddles.surface(x, z, topY)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local origin = Vector3.new(x, topY, z)
    local distance = puddles.CastDistance
    local params = puddles.Params
    local skipped = nil
    for _ = 1, puddles.SeeThroughHops do
        local hit = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -distance, 0), params)
        if not hit then return nil end
        local instance = hit.Instance
        if instance:IsA("Terrain") then
            if hit.Material == Enum.Material.Water then return nil end
            return hit
        end
        if instance.Transparency >= 1 then
            skipped = skipped or table.clone(params.FilterDescendantsInstances)
            skipped[#skipped + 1] = instance
            puddles.Scratch.FilterDescendantsInstances = skipped
            params = puddles.Scratch
            distance = distance - (origin.Y - hit.Position.Y)
            origin = Vector3.new(x, hit.Position.Y, z)
        else
            if not instance.CanCollide or not instance.Anchored then return nil end
            local model = instance:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
            if model and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return nil end
            return hit
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function rainState.Puddles.crowded(position, radius)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local cell = puddles.CellSize
    local span = math.ceil((radius + puddles.Widest) * puddles.Spacing / cell)
    local cx, cz = math.floor(position.X / cell), math.floor(position.Z / cell)
    for dx = -span, span do
        for dz = -span, span do
            local bucket = puddles.Cells[puddles.cellKey(cx + dx, cz + dz)]
            if bucket then
                for _, entry in ipairs(bucket) do
                    local offsetX, offsetZ = entry.X - position.X, entry.Z - position.Z
                    local need = (radius + entry.Radius) * puddles.Spacing
                    if math.abs(entry.Y - position.Y) < puddles.NearHeight
                        and offsetX * offsetX + offsetZ * offsetZ < need * need then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

function rainState.Puddles.pickSize()
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local look = puddles.Looks[puddles.Variant]
    local low = math.min(puddles.SizeMin, puddles.SizeMax)
    local high = math.max(puddles.SizeMin, puddles.SizeMax)
    local width = low + math.random() * (high - low)
    if not look.Stretch then return width, width * look.Depth / look.Width end
    local depth = low + math.random() * (high - low)
    return width, math.clamp(depth, width / puddles.MaxAspect, width * puddles.MaxAspect)
end

function rainState.Puddles.candidate(camPos)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local angle = math.random() * math.pi * 2
    local radius = math.sqrt(math.random()) * puddles.Reach
    local x = camPos.X + math.cos(angle) * radius
    local z = camPos.Z + math.sin(angle) * radius
    local hit = puddles.surface(x, z, camPos.Y + puddles.StartHeight)
    if not hit or hit.Normal.Y < puddles.MinNormalY then return nil end
    local sizeX, sizeZ = puddles.pickSize()
    local yaw = math.random() * math.pi * 2
    local frame = CFrame.new(hit.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
    for _, signX in ipairs({-1, 1}) do
        for _, signZ in ipairs({-1, 1}) do
            local corner = frame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(
                signX * sizeX / 2 * puddles.CornerShare, puddles.CornerRise, signZ * sizeZ / 2 * puddles.CornerShare))
            local below = workspace:Raycast(corner, Vector3.new(0, -puddles.CornerDrop, 0), puddles.Params)
            if not below or math.abs(below.Position.Y - hit.Position.Y) > puddles.FlatTolerance then
                return nil
            end
        end
    end
    if puddles.crowded(hit.Position, (sizeX + sizeZ) / 4) then return nil end
    return hit.Position, sizeX, sizeZ, yaw
end

function rainState.Puddles.add(position, sizeX, sizeZ, yaw)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local cell = puddles.CellSize
    local root, layers = puddles.build(position, sizeX, sizeZ, yaw)
    local entry = {
        Root = root, Layers = layers, Variant = puddles.Variant,
        X = position.X, Y = position.Y, Z = position.Z,
        Yaw = yaw, SizeX = sizeX, SizeZ = sizeZ,
        Radius = (sizeX + sizeZ) / 4,
        Alpha = 0, Painted = 0, DryAt = nil, Revive = false, TintVersion = puddles.TintVersion,
    }
    entry.Key = puddles.cellKey(math.floor(entry.X / cell), math.floor(entry.Z / cell))
    local bucket = puddles.Cells[entry.Key]
    if not bucket then
        bucket = {}
        puddles.Cells[entry.Key] = bucket
    end
    bucket[#bucket + 1] = entry
    puddles.List[#puddles.List + 1] = entry
    puddles.Widest = math.max(puddles.Widest, entry.Radius)
end

function rainState.Puddles.refit(entry)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    entry.Root:Destroy()
    entry.Root, entry.Layers = puddles.build(Vector3.new(entry.X, entry.Y, entry.Z), entry.SizeX, entry.SizeZ, entry.Yaw)
    entry.Variant = puddles.Variant
    entry.TintVersion = puddles.TintVersion
    entry.Painted = -1
end

function rainState.Puddles.remove(index)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local list = puddles.List
    local entry = list[index]
    local bucket = puddles.Cells[entry.Key]
    if bucket then
        local at = table.find(bucket, entry)
        if at then table.remove(bucket, at) end
        if #bucket == 0 then puddles.Cells[entry.Key] = nil end
    end
    entry.Root:Destroy()
    local last = #list
    list[index] = list[last]
    list[last] = nil
    if last == 1 then puddles.Widest = 0 end
end

function rainState.Puddles.makeRoom(camPos, force)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local farthest, farthestDistance = nil, force and 0 or puddles.Reach * puddles.Reach
    for index, entry in ipairs(puddles.List) do
        local offsetX, offsetZ = entry.X - camPos.X, entry.Z - camPos.Z
        local distance = offsetX * offsetX + offsetZ * offsetZ
        if distance > farthestDistance then farthest, farthestDistance = index, distance end
    end
    if not farthest then return false end
    puddles.remove(farthest)
    return true
end

function rainState.Puddles.valid(entry, camPos)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local offsetX, offsetZ = entry.X - camPos.X, entry.Z - camPos.Z
    local distance = math.sqrt(offsetX * offsetX + offsetZ * offsetZ)
    if distance > puddles.Reach + puddles.CheckMargin then return true end
    local origin = Vector3.new(entry.X, entry.Y + puddles.CornerRise, entry.Z)
    local hit = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -puddles.CornerDrop, 0), puddles.Params)
    return hit ~= nil and math.abs(hit.Position.Y - entry.Y) <= puddles.FlatTolerance + puddles.Lift
end

function rainState.Puddles.step(dt)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local list = puddles.List
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    puddles.syncTint()
    if camera then
        local camPos = camera.CFrame.Position
        puddles.FilterTimer = puddles.FilterTimer + dt
        if puddles.FilterTimer >= puddles.FilterInterval then
            puddles.FilterTimer = 0
            puddles.refreshFilter()
        end
        local trimmed = 0
        while #list > puddles.Limit and trimmed < puddles.TrimPerFrame do
            puddles.makeRoom(camPos, true)
            trimmed = trimmed + 1
        end
        local ready = puddles.ready()
        if puddles.Raining and ready then
            puddles.Accum = math.min(puddles.Accum + dt * puddles.Rate, puddles.AccumCap)
            local spawned = 0
            while puddles.Accum >= 1 and spawned < puddles.SpawnsPerFrame do
                puddles.Accum = puddles.Accum - 1
                spawned = spawned + 1
                if #list >= puddles.Limit then puddles.makeRoom(camPos, false) end
                if #list < puddles.Limit then
                    for _ = 1, puddles.CandidateTries do
                        local position, sizeX, sizeZ, yaw = puddles.candidate(camPos)
                        if position then
                            puddles.add(position, sizeX, sizeZ, yaw)
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for _ = 1, puddles.ChecksPerFrame do
            local count = #list
            if count == 0 then break end
            puddles.CheckIndex = puddles.CheckIndex % count + 1
            local entry = list[puddles.CheckIndex]
            if not puddles.valid(entry, camPos) then
                puddles.remove(puddles.CheckIndex)
            elseif ready and entry.Variant ~= puddles.Variant then
                puddles.refit(entry)
            end
        end
        for _ = 1, puddles.TintPerFrame do
            local count = #list
            if count == 0 then break end
            puddles.TintIndex = puddles.TintIndex % count + 1
            local entry = list[puddles.TintIndex]
            if entry.TintVersion ~= puddles.TintVersion then puddles.paint(entry) end
        end
    end
    if puddles.Faded ~= puddles.Transparency then
        puddles.Faded = puddles.Transparency
        for _, entry in ipairs(list) do entry.Painted = -1 end
    end
    local visible = 1 - math.clamp(puddles.Transparency, 0, 1)
    local now = os.clock()
    local rise = dt / puddles.FadeIn
    local fall = dt / (puddles.dryShape())
    for index = #list, 1, -1 do
        local entry = list[index]
        local alpha = entry.Alpha
        if puddles.Raining then
            entry.DryAt = nil
            entry.Revive = false
            alpha = math.min(1, alpha + rise)
        elseif entry.DryAt and now >= entry.DryAt then
            alpha = alpha - fall
        elseif entry.Revive then
            alpha = math.min(1, alpha + rise)
            if alpha >= 1 then entry.Revive = false end
        end
        entry.Alpha = alpha
        if alpha <= 0 and not puddles.Raining then
            puddles.remove(index)
        elseif alpha ~= entry.Painted then
            entry.Painted = alpha
            for _, layer in ipairs(entry.Layers) do
                layer.Part.Transparency = 1 - (1 - layer.Target) * alpha * visible
            end
        end
    end
    if not puddles.Raining and #list == 0 and puddles.Conn then
        puddles.Conn:Disconnect()
        puddles.Conn = nil
    end
end

function rainState.Puddles.clear()
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    for _, entry in ipairs(puddles.List) do
        pcall(function() entry.Root:Destroy() end)
    end
    puddles.List = {}
    puddles.Cells = {}
    puddles.Widest = 0
    puddles.Accum = 0
    puddles.CheckIndex = 0
    puddles.TintIndex = 0
end

function rainState.Puddles.start()
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    if not puddles.Folder or puddles.Folder.Parent ~= workspace then
        puddles.clear()
        puddles.Folder = Instance.new("Folder")
        puddles.Folder.Name = puddles.FolderName
        puddles.Folder.Parent = workspace
    end
    if not puddles.Params then
        puddles.Params = RaycastParams.new()
        puddles.Params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        puddles.Scratch = RaycastParams.new()
        puddles.Scratch.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    end
    puddles.syncTint()
    puddles.refreshFilter()
    puddles.load(puddles.Variant)
    if not puddles.Conn then
        puddles.Conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(puddles.step)
    end
end

function rainState.Puddles.setVariant(variant)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    puddles.Variant = variant
    if puddles.Conn then puddles.load(variant) end
end

function rainState.Puddles.dryShape()
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local scale = math.min(1, puddles.Lifetime / (puddles.DryTime + puddles.DryJitter))
    return puddles.DryTime * scale, puddles.DryJitter * scale
end

function rainState.Puddles.schedule(entry, now)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    local fade, spread = puddles.dryShape()
    entry.DryAt = now + puddles.Lifetime - fade - spread + math.random() * spread
end

function rainState.Puddles.setLifetime(seconds)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    puddles.Lifetime = seconds
    if puddles.Raining then return end
    local now = os.clock()
    for _, entry in ipairs(puddles.List) do
        puddles.schedule(entry, now)
        entry.Revive = entry.Alpha < 1
    end
end

function rainState.Puddles.apply(enabled)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    if enabled and puddles.Enabled then
        puddles.Raining = true
        puddles.start()
    elseif puddles.Raining then
        puddles.Raining = false
        local now = os.clock()
        for _, entry in ipairs(puddles.List) do
            puddles.schedule(entry, now)
        end
    end
end

function rainState.Puddles.destroy()
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    puddles.Raining = false
    puddles.Epoch = puddles.Epoch + 1
    if puddles.Conn then
        puddles.Conn:Disconnect()
        puddles.Conn = nil
    end
    puddles.clear()
    if puddles.Folder then
        puddles.Folder:Destroy()
        puddles.Folder = nil
    end
    for _, set in pairs(puddles.Sets) do
        for _, part in pairs(set.Parts) do
            pcall(function() part:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    puddles.Sets = {}
end

local function rebuildRainGrid()
    for _, tile in ipairs(rainState.Parts) do
        if tile.Parent then tile:Destroy() end
    end
    rainState.Parts = {}
    rainState.Emitters = {}
    rainState.RoofState = {}
    rainState.Plan = weatherGrid.plan(rainState.GridRadius)
    for _, entry in ipairs(rainState.Plan) do
        local tile = Instance.new("Part")
        tile.Name = "CustomRainPreview"
        tile.Size = Vector3.new(20,1,20)
        tile.Anchored = true
        tile.CanCollide = false
        tile.CanQuery = false
        tile.Transparency = 1
        tile.Parent = workspace

        local em = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        em.Parent = tile
        em.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
        em.Rate = rainState.rate(entry)
        em.Color = ColorSequence.new(rainState.Color)
        if rainState.Variant == 3 then
            local streak = rainState.Variant3
            em.Texture = streak.Texture
            em.Lifetime = streak.Lifetime
            em.Drag = streak.Drag
            em.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            em.Size = streak.Size
            em.Transparency = streak.Transparency
            em.Squash = streak.Squash
            em.Rotation = streak.Rotation
            em.Orientation = Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel
            em.LightEmission = streak.LightEmission
            em.LightInfluence = streak.LightInfluence
            em.WindAffectsDrag = streak.WindAffectsDrag
        else
            em.Texture = "rbxassetid://1822883048"
            em.Speed = NumberRange.new(60, 70)
            em.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(2,2)
            em.Size = NumberSequence.new(10)
            em.Transparency = NumberSequence.new{
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.4),
            }
            em.LightEmission = 0.6
        end
        rainState.style(em)

        table.insert(rainState.Parts, tile)
        table.insert(rainState.Emitters, em)
        table.insert(rainState.RoofState, false)
    end
end

local function spawnSplash(position)
    local index = rainState.SplashNext % rainState.SplashPoolSize + 1
    rainState.SplashNext = index
    local slot = rainState.SplashPool[index]
    if not slot then
        local baseSize = 1 + math.random() * 2.5
        local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
        attachment.Name = "CustomRainSplashPreview"
        local emitter = mk("ParticleEmitter", {
            Texture = "rbxassetid://1822856633",
            EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Top,
            Rate = 0,
            Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.1, 0.15),
            Speed = NumberRange.new(0),
            Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360),
            RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(0),
            SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(0, 0),
            Squash = rainState.SplashSquash,
            ZOffset = 0,
            Orientation = Enum.ParticleOrientation.FacingCamera,
            LightEmission = 0.05,
            LightInfluence = 0.9,
            Size = NumberSequence.new(baseSize * rainState.SplashSize),
            Transparency = rainState.SplashTransparency,
            Color = rainState.SplashColor,
            Parent = attachment,
        })
        attachment.Parent = workspace.Terrain
        slot = {Attachment = attachment, Emitter = emitter, Base = baseSize}
        rainState.SplashPool[index] = slot
    end
    slot.Attachment.WorldPosition = position
    slot.Emitter:Emit(1)
end

local function applyRainInner(enabled)
    if enabled then
        rainState.Sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        rainState.Sound.Name = "CustomRainSoundPreview"
        rainState.Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://246259756"
        rainState.Sound.Looped = true
        rainState.Sound.Volume = rainState.SoundVolume
        rainState.Sound.Parent = SoundService
        if rainState.SoundEnabled then rainState.Sound:Play() end

        if rainState.Variant ~= 2 then
            rebuildRainGrid()

            local rainCycleIdx = 0
            local roofParams = RaycastParams.new()
            roofParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            local roofChar = nil
            rainState.Conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if not cam then return end
                local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
                local basePos = camPos + Vector3.new(0, rainState.Up, 0)
                rainCycleIdx = rainCycleIdx + 1
                roofChar = syncCharacterFilter(roofParams, roofChar)
                for idx, entry in ipairs(rainState.Plan) do
                    local tile = rainState.Parts[idx]
                    local em = rainState.Emitters[idx]
                    if tile then
                        local tileOffset = Vector3.new(entry.gx*20, entry.gy*20, entry.gz*20)
                        tile.CFrame = CFrame.new(basePos + tileOffset)
                        local distSq = entry.gx*entry.gx + entry.gy*entry.gy + entry.gz*entry.gz
                        local interval = distSq <= 1 and 1 or (distSq <= 4 and 3 or (distSq <= 36 and 6 or 24))
                        if em and (rainCycleIdx % interval) == (idx % interval) then
                            local checkPos = camPos + Vector3.new(tileOffset.X, 0, tileOffset.Z)
                            local hasRoof = isRoofAbove(checkPos, roofParams)
                            if hasRoof ~= rainState.RoofState[idx] then
                                em.Rate = hasRoof and 0 or rainState.rate(entry)
                                rainState.RoofState[idx] = hasRoof
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)

            local splashAccum = 0
            local splashRoofParams = RaycastParams.new()
            splashRoofParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            local splashRoofChar = nil
            local splashRayParams = RaycastParams.new()
            splashRayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            rainState.DropSpawnConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
                if not rainState.SplashesEnabled then
                    splashAccum = 0
                    return
                end
                local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if not cam then return end
                splashAccum = math.min(splashAccum + dt * rainState.DropsPerTick / 0.033, rainState.SplashAccumCap)
                if splashAccum < 1 then return end
                local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
                local coverage = (rainState.GridRadius*2+1)*20
                local fallVector = Vector3.new(0, -rainState.reach(), 0)
                local startOffset = Vector3.new(0, rainState.Up, 0)
                splashRoofChar = syncCharacterFilter(splashRoofParams, splashRoofChar)
                local attempts, emitted = 0, 0
                while splashAccum >= 1 and attempts < rainState.SplashAttemptMax and emitted < rainState.SplashPoolSize do
                    splashAccum = splashAccum - 1
                    attempts = attempts + 1
                    local checkPos = camPos + Vector3.new((math.random()-0.5)*coverage, 0, (math.random()-0.5)*coverage)
                    if not isRoofAbove(checkPos, splashRoofParams) then
                        local hit = workspace:Raycast(checkPos + startOffset, fallVector, splashRayParams)
                        if hit then
                            spawnSplash(hit.Position)
                            emitted = emitted + 1
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            rainState.TrailAnchor = Instance.new("Part")
            rainState.TrailAnchor.Name = "CustomRainTrailAnchorPreview"
            rainState.TrailAnchor.Size = Vector3.new(1,1,1)
            rainState.TrailAnchor.Transparency = 1
            rainState.TrailAnchor.Anchored = true
            rainState.TrailAnchor.CanCollide = false
            rainState.TrailAnchor.CanQuery = false
            rainState.TrailAnchor.Parent = workspace

            local trailSpawnAccum = 0
            local roofParams = RaycastParams.new()
            roofParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            local roofChar = nil
            local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
            rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            rainState.TrailConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
                local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if cam then
                    roofChar = syncCharacterFilter(roofParams, roofChar)
                    local targetRate = (rainState.DropsPerTick * rainState.Intensity / 3) / 0.033
                    trailSpawnAccum = math.min(trailSpawnAccum + dt * targetRate, 800)
                    local coverage = (rainState.GridRadius*2+1)*20
                    local fallVector = Vector3.new(0, -rainState.reach(), 0)
                    local fallTime = rainState.reach() / rainState.DropFallSpeed
                    local spawnedThisFrame = 0
                    while trailSpawnAccum >= 1 and spawnedThisFrame < 800 do
                        trailSpawnAccum = trailSpawnAccum - 1
                        spawnedThisFrame = spawnedThisFrame + 1
                        local offset = Vector3.new((math.random()-0.5)*coverage, 0, (math.random()-0.5)*coverage)
                        local checkPos = cam.CFrame.Position + offset
                        local hasRoof = isRoofAbove(checkPos, roofParams)
                        if not hasRoof then
                            local startPos = checkPos + Vector3.new(0, rainState.Up, 0)
                            local hit = workspace:Raycast(startPos, fallVector, rayParams)
                            rainState.TrailDropIdCounter = rainState.TrailDropIdCounter + 1
                            local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
                            local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
                            local trail = Instance.new("Trail")
                            trail.Lifetime = 0.06
                            trail.MinLength = 0
                            trail.WidthScale = rainState.TrailWidthScale
                            trail.Transparency = rainState.TrailTransparency
                            trail.Color = rainState.ColorSeq
                            trail.FaceCamera = true
                            trail.Attachment0 = a0
                            trail.Attachment1 = a1
                            trail.Parent = a0
                            a0.WorldPosition = startPos
                            a1.WorldPosition = startPos + Vector3.new(0.08,0,0)
                            a0.Parent = rainState.TrailAnchor
                            a1.Parent = rainState.TrailAnchor
                            rainState.TrailDrops[rainState.TrailDropIdCounter] = {
                                x = startPos.X, y = startPos.Y, z = startPos.Z, age = 0,
                                life = hit and hit.Distance / rainState.DropFallSpeed or fallTime,
                                landing = hit and hit.Position,
                                a0 = a0, a1 = a1, trail = trail,
                            }
                        end
                    end
                end
                local splashesLeft = rainState.SplashPoolSize
                for dropId, info in pairs(rainState.TrailDrops) do
                    local age = info.age + dt
                    info.age = age
                    if age >= info.life then
                        if info.landing and rainState.SplashesEnabled and splashesLeft > 0 then
                            splashesLeft = splashesLeft - 1
                            spawnSplash(info.landing)
                        end
                        info.a0:Destroy(); info.a1:Destroy(); info.trail:Destroy()
                        rainState.TrailDrops[dropId] = nil
                    else
                        local y = info.y - rainState.DropFallSpeed * age
                        info.a0.WorldPosition = Vector3.new(info.x, y, info.z)
                        info.a1.WorldPosition = Vector3.new(info.x + 0.08, y, info.z)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    else
        if rainState.Conn then rainState.Conn:Disconnect(); rainState.Conn = nil end
        for _, tile in ipairs(rainState.Parts) do
            if tile.Parent then tile:Destroy() end
        end
        rainState.Parts = {}
        rainState.Emitters = {}
        if rainState.DropSpawnConn then rainState.DropSpawnConn:Disconnect(); rainState.DropSpawnConn = nil end
        for _, slot in ipairs(rainState.SplashPool) do
            slot.Attachment:Destroy()
        end
        rainState.SplashPool = {}
        rainState.SplashNext = 0
        if rainState.TrailConn then rainState.TrailConn:Disconnect(); rainState.TrailConn = nil end
        for _, info in pairs(rainState.TrailDrops) do
            info.a0:Destroy(); info.a1:Destroy(); info.trail:Destroy()
        end
        rainState.TrailDrops = {}
        if rainState.TrailAnchor then rainState.TrailAnchor:Destroy(); rainState.TrailAnchor = nil end
        if rainState.Sound then rainState.Sound:Stop(); rainState.Sound:Destroy(); rainState.Sound = nil end
    end
end

local function applyRain(enabled)
    pcall(applyRainInner, enabled)
end

ToggleSetters.rainRow, ToggleSetters.rain = addToggleRow(tabPages["atmo"], "Дождь", true, function(enabled)
    rainState.EnabledState = enabled
    applyRain(enabled)
    rainState.Puddles.apply(enabled)
end, function(Body)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ДОЖДЬ")
    local methodsWrap = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,28),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = methodsWrap, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local indicator = mk("Frame", {Parent = methodsWrap,
        Position = UDim2.new((rainState.Variant - 1) / 3, 2, 0, 2),
        Size = UDim2.new(1/3,-4,1,-4), BackgroundColor3 = COL.pinkOn, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = indicator, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local variantButtons = {}
    local function setRainVariant(v)
        rainState.Variant = v
        TweenService:Create(indicator, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
            Position = UDim2.new((v - 1) / 3, 2, 0, 2)}):Play()
        for index, button in ipairs(variantButtons) do
            button.TextColor3 = index == v and COL.text or COL.textMuted
        end
        if rainState.EnabledState and ToggleSetters.rain then
            ToggleSetters.rain(false)
            ToggleSetters.rain(true)
        end
    end
    for index, title in ipairs({"Emitter", "Trail", "Emitter 2"}) do
        local button = mk("TextButton", {Parent = methodsWrap, Position = UDim2.new((index - 1) / 3,0,0,0),
            Size = UDim2.new(1/3,0,1,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = title,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 10,
            TextColor3 = rainState.Variant == index and COL.text or COL.textMuted, AutoButtonColor = false})
        variantButtons[index] = button
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(button, "select")
        button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setRainVariant(index) end)
    end
    addSlider(Body, "Дальность дождя", 1, 40, rainState.GridRadius, 0, function(v)
        local newRadius = math.floor(v)
        if newRadius == rainState.GridRadius then return end
        rainState.GridRadius = newRadius
        if rainState.EnabledState and rainState.Variant ~= 2 then
            rainState.GridRebuildToken = (rainState.GridRebuildToken or 0) + 1
            local myToken = rainState.GridRebuildToken
            task.delay(0.15, function()
                if myToken == rainState.GridRebuildToken and rainState.EnabledState and rainState.Variant ~= 2 then
                    rebuildRainGrid()
                end
            end)
        end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(150,180,210))
    addSlider(Body, "Дальность вверх", rainState.MinReach, rainState.MaxReach, rainState.Up, 0, function(v)
        local value = math.floor(v + 0.5)
        if value == rainState.Up then return end
        rainState.Up = value
        for _, em in ipairs(rainState.Emitters) do rainState.style(em) end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(150,180,210))
    addSlider(Body, "Дальность вниз", rainState.MinReach, rainState.MaxReach, rainState.Down, 0, function(v)
        local value = math.floor(v + 0.5)
        if value == rainState.Down then return end
        rainState.Down = value
        for _, em in ipairs(rainState.Emitters) do rainState.style(em) end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(150,180,210))
    addSlider(Body, "Интенсивность", rainState.MinIntensity, rainState.MaxIntensity, rainState.Intensity, 1, function(v)
        local newIntensity = math.floor(v * 10 + 0.5) / 10
        if newIntensity == rainState.Intensity then return end
        rainState.Intensity = newIntensity
        for idx, em in ipairs(rainState.Emitters) do
            if not rainState.RoofState[idx] then
                em.Rate = rainState.rate(rainState.Plan[idx])
            end
        end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(140,170,200))
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ЗВУК")
    do
        local soundRow = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = soundRow, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = soundRow, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-70,1,0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = "Звук дождя", TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
        local pill = mk("TextButton", {Parent = soundRow, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1,-14,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,40,0,22),
            BackgroundColor3 = rainState.SoundEnabled and COL.pinkOn or COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = pill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        mk("UIStroke", {Parent = pill, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.fromRGB(48,42,64),

        local thumb = mk("Frame", {Parent = pill, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),

    addPanelHeader(Body, "БРЫЗГИ")

            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
        local pill = mk("TextButton", {Parent = splashRow, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1,-14,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,40,0,22),
            BackgroundColor3 = rainState.SplashesEnabled and COL.pinkOn or COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = pill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        mk("UIStroke", {Parent = pill, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.fromRGB(48,42,64),
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
        local thumb = mk("Frame", {Parent = pill, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
            Position = rainState.SplashesEnabled and UDim2.new(1,-11,0.5,0) or UDim2.new(0,11,0.5,0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0,16,0,16), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1), BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = thumb, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(pill, "toggle")
        pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            rainState.SplashesEnabled = not rainState.SplashesEnabled
            TweenService:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundColor3 = rainState.SplashesEnabled and COL.pinkOn or COL.off}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(thumb, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {

        end)

    addSlider(Body, "Брызгов в тик", 10, 100, rainState.DropsPerTick, 0, function(v)
        rainState.DropsPerTick = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(140,170,200))
    addSlider(Body, "Размер", rainState.MinSplashSize, rainState.MaxSplashSize, rainState.SplashSize, 1, function(v)
        rainState.SplashSize = math.floor(v * 10 + 0.5) / 10
        for _, slot in ipairs(rainState.SplashPool) do
            slot.Emitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(slot.Base * rainState.SplashSize)
        end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(140,170,200), true)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ЦВЕТ")
    addColorPicker(Body, "Цвет дождя", rainState.Color, function(c)
        rainState.Color = c
        rainState.ColorSeq = ColorSequence.new(c)
        rainState.SplashColor = rainState.ColorSeq
        for _, em in ipairs(rainState.Emitters) do
            em.Color = ColorSequence.new(c)
        end
        for _, slot in ipairs(rainState.SplashPool) do
            slot.Emitter.Color = rainState.SplashColor
        end
    end)
    local puddles = rainState.Puddles
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ЛУЖИ")
    do
        local puddleRow = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})

        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = puddleRow, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-70,1,0),

        local pill = mk("TextButton", {Parent = puddleRow, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),

            Size = UDim2.new(0,16,0,16), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1), BorderSizePixel = 0})

        pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            puddles.Enabled = not puddles.Enabled

                Position = puddles.Enabled and UDim2.new(1,-11,0.5,0) or UDim2.new(0,11,0.5,0)}):Play()
            puddles.apply(rainState.EnabledState)

    do
        local looksWrap = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,28),

        local looksCount = #puddles.Looks
        local lookIndicator = mk("Frame", {Parent = looksWrap,
            Position = UDim2.new((puddles.Variant - 1) / looksCount, 2, 0, 2),

    addSlider(Body, "Количество", puddles.MinLimit, puddles.MaxLimit, puddles.Limit, 0, function(v)

    end, puddles.AccentColor, true)

local snowState = {
    Parts = {},
    RoofState = {},
    Plan = {},
    Emitters = {},
    Conn = nil,
    GridRadius = 1,
    Intensity = 1,
    FallSpeed = 12,
    SizeScale = 1,
    SpeedSpread = 0.3,
    Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
    Sound = nil,
    SoundEnabled = true,
    SoundVolume = 1,
    EnabledState = false,
    Texture = "rbxassetid://178107563",
    TileSize = 20,
    ColumnBottom = -40,
    ColumnTop = 80,
    NearRings = 2,
    NearFlakes = 900,
    FarFlakes = 40,
    NearSize = 0.35,
    SizePerStud = 0.004,
    RefreshDelay = 0.15,
    RefreshToken = 0,
    WarmFraction = 0.5,
}

function snowState.plan(radius)
    local plan = {}
    for gx = -radius, radius do
        for gz = -radius, radius do
            local near = math.max(math.abs(gx), math.abs(gz)) <= snowState.NearRings
            local distance = math.sqrt(gx * gx + gz * gz) * snowState.TileSize
            plan[#plan + 1] = {
                gx = gx,
                gz = gz,
                flakes = near and snowState.NearFlakes or snowState.FarFlakes,
                size = math.max(snowState.NearSize, distance * snowState.SizePerStud),
            }
        end
    end
    return plan
end

function snowState.lifetime()
    return (snowState.ColumnTop - snowState.ColumnBottom) / snowState.FallSpeed
end

function snowState.speed()
    return NumberRange.new(snowState.FallSpeed * (1 - snowState.SpeedSpread), snowState.FallSpeed * (1 + snowState.SpeedSpread))
end

function snowState.rate(entry)
    return entry.flakes * snowState.Intensity / snowState.lifetime()
end

function snowState.refresh()
    local lifetime = NumberRange.new(snowState.lifetime())
    local speed = snowState.speed()
    for idx, em in ipairs(snowState.Emitters) do
        local entry = snowState.Plan[idx]
        em.Lifetime = lifetime
        em.Speed = speed
        em.Size = NumberSequence.new(entry.size * snowState.SizeScale)
        em:Clear()
        if not snowState.RoofState[idx] then
            em.Rate = snowState.rate(entry)
            em:Emit(math.floor(entry.flakes * snowState.Intensity * snowState.WarmFraction))
        end
    end
end

function snowState.refreshSoon()
    snowState.RefreshToken = snowState.RefreshToken + 1
    local myToken = snowState.RefreshToken
    task.delay(snowState.RefreshDelay, function()
        if myToken == snowState.RefreshToken and snowState.EnabledState then
            snowState.refresh()
        end
    end)
end

local function rebuildSnowGrid()
    for _, tile in ipairs(snowState.Parts) do
        if tile.Parent then tile:Destroy() end
    end
    snowState.Parts = {}
    snowState.Emitters = {}
    snowState.RoofState = {}
    snowState.Plan = snowState.plan(snowState.GridRadius)
    local height = snowState.ColumnTop - snowState.ColumnBottom
    for _, entry in ipairs(snowState.Plan) do
        local tile = Instance.new("Part")
        tile.Name = "CustomSnowPreview"
        tile.Size = Vector3.new(snowState.TileSize, height, snowState.TileSize)
        tile.Anchored = true
        tile.CanCollide = false
        tile.CanQuery = false
        tile.Transparency = 1
        tile.Parent = workspace

        local em = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        em.Parent = tile
        em.Shape = Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box
        em.ShapeStyle = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume
        em.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
        em.Texture = snowState.Texture
        em.Rate = snowState.rate(entry)
        em.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(snowState.lifetime())
        em.Speed = snowState.speed()
        em.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(3,3)
        em.Size = NumberSequence.new(entry.size * snowState.SizeScale)
        em.Transparency = NumberSequence.new{
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, 0.2),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
        }
        em.Color = ColorSequence.new(snowState.Color)
        em.LightEmission = 0.3
        em.Drag = 0.05
        em.WindAffectsDrag = true

        table.insert(snowState.Parts, tile)
        table.insert(snowState.Emitters, em)
        table.insert(snowState.RoofState, false)
    end
end

local function applySnow(enabled)
    snowState.EnabledState = enabled
    if enabled then
        rebuildSnowGrid()

        snowState.Sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        snowState.Sound.Name = "CustomSnowSoundPreview"
        snowState.Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://551546110"
        snowState.Sound.Looped = true
        snowState.Sound.Volume = snowState.SoundVolume
        snowState.Sound.Parent = SoundService
        if snowState.SoundEnabled then snowState.Sound:Play() end

        local snowCycleIdx = 0
        local snowRoofParams = RaycastParams.new()
        snowRoofParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        local snowRoofChar = nil
        snowState.Conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not cam then return end
            local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
            local center = camPos + Vector3.new(0, (snowState.ColumnTop + snowState.ColumnBottom) / 2, 0)
            local size = snowState.TileSize
            snowCycleIdx = snowCycleIdx + 1
            snowRoofChar = syncCharacterFilter(snowRoofParams, snowRoofChar)
            for idx, entry in ipairs(snowState.Plan) do
                local tile = snowState.Parts[idx]
                local em = snowState.Emitters[idx]
                if tile then
                    local offsetX = entry.gx * size
                    local offsetZ = entry.gz * size
                    tile.CFrame = CFrame.new(center.X + offsetX, center.Y, center.Z + offsetZ)
                    local distSq = entry.gx * entry.gx + entry.gz * entry.gz
                    local interval = distSq <= 1 and 1 or (distSq <= 4 and 3 or (distSq <= 36 and 6 or 24))
                    if em and (snowCycleIdx % interval) == (idx % interval) then
                        local checkPos = camPos + Vector3.new(offsetX, 0, offsetZ)
                        local hasRoof = isRoofAbove(checkPos, snowRoofParams)
                        if hasRoof ~= snowState.RoofState[idx] then
                            em.Rate = hasRoof and 0 or snowState.rate(entry)
                            if hasRoof then
                                em:Clear()
                            end
                            snowState.RoofState[idx] = hasRoof
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        if snowState.Conn then snowState.Conn:Disconnect(); snowState.Conn = nil end
        for _, tile in ipairs(snowState.Parts) do
            if tile.Parent then tile:Destroy() end
        end
        snowState.Parts = {}
        snowState.Emitters = {}
        if snowState.Sound then snowState.Sound:Stop(); snowState.Sound:Destroy(); snowState.Sound = nil end
    end
end

local lightningState = {
    Enabled = false,
    Frequency = 8,
    LoopId = 0,
    Sound = nil,
    ThunderSound = nil,
    DeepThunderSound = nil,
    SoundEnabled = true,
    SoundVolume = 5,
    BoltDistancePerSecond = 200,
    BoltHeight = 900,
    BoltMaxAngle = 75,
    BoltWidthPerDistance = 0.005,
    BoltMinWidth = 1,
    BoltGlowScale = 6,
    BoltGlowTransparency = 0.8,
    BoltIterations = 5,
    BoltBranches = 3,
    BoltBranchIterations = 3,
    BoltColor = Color3.fromRGB(210,225,255),
    Bolt = nil,
}

function lightningState.bendPath(points, iterations, offset)
    for _ = 1, iterations do
        local nextPoints = {points[1]}
        for i = 1, #points - 1 do
            local a, b = points[i], points[i + 1]
            local mid = (a + b) / 2
            local perp = (b - a).Unit:Cross(Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, math.random() - 0.5, math.random() - 0.5))
            if perp.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                mid = mid + perp.Unit * offset * (math.random() * 2 - 1)
            end
            nextPoints[#nextPoints + 1] = mid
            nextPoints[#nextPoints + 1] = b
        end
        points = nextPoints
        offset = offset * 0.55
    end
    return points
end

function lightningState.strikePosition(distance)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local origin = cam and cam.CFrame.Position or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    local look = cam and cam.CFrame.LookVector or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
    local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
    if flat.Magnitude < 0.01 then flat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
    local angle = math.rad((math.random() * 2 - 1) * lightningState.BoltMaxAngle)
    local direction = CFrame.Angles(0, angle, 0):VectorToWorldSpace(flat.Unit)
    local target = origin + direction * distance
    local ignore = {}
    if player.Character then ignore[1] = player.Character end
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
    local from = Vector3.new(target.X, origin.Y + 3000, target.Z)
    for _ = 1, 8 do
        local hit = workspace:Raycast(from, Vector3.new(0, -6000, 0), params)
        if not hit then break end
        if hit.Instance:IsA("Terrain") or hit.Instance.Transparency < 1 then return hit.Position end
        ignore[#ignore + 1] = hit.Instance
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
    end
    return Vector3.new(target.X, origin.Y - 3, target.Z)
end

function lightningState.buildBolt(strikePos, distance)
    local anchor = Instance.new("Part")
    anchor.Name = "CustomLightningBoltPreview"
    anchor.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
    anchor.Transparency = 1
    anchor.Anchored = true
    anchor.CanCollide = false
    anchor.CanQuery = false
    anchor.CanTouch = false
    anchor.Position = strikePos
    anchor.Parent = workspace

    local beams = {}
    local width = math.max(lightningState.BoltMinWidth, distance * lightningState.BoltWidthPerDistance)
    local colorSeq = ColorSequence.new(lightningState.BoltColor)

    local function addPath(points, baseWidth, taper)
        local attachments = {}
        for i, p in ipairs(points) do
            local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
            att.Parent = anchor
            att.WorldPosition = p
            attachments[i] = att
        end
        local count = #points - 1
        for i = 1, count do
            for layer = 1, 2 do
                local scale = layer == 1 and 1 or lightningState.BoltGlowScale
                local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
                beam.Attachment0 = attachments[i]
                beam.Attachment1 = attachments[i + 1]
                beam.Segments = 1
                beam.FaceCamera = true
                beam.LightEmission = 1
                beam.LightInfluence = 0
                beam.Color = colorSeq
                beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(layer == 1 and 0 or lightningState.BoltGlowTransparency)
                beam.Width0 = baseWidth * scale * (1 - taper * (i - 1) / count)
                beam.Width1 = baseWidth * scale * (1 - taper * i / count)
                beam.Enabled = false
                beam.Parent = anchor
                beams[#beams + 1] = beam
            end
        end
    end

    local height = lightningState.BoltHeight
    local top = strikePos + Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * height * 0.5, height, (math.random() - 0.5) * height * 0.5)
    local main = lightningState.bendPath({top, strikePos}, lightningState.BoltIterations, (top - strikePos).Magnitude * 0.1)
    addPath(main, width, 0)
    for _ = 1, lightningState.BoltBranches do
        local origin = main[math.random(math.floor(#main * 0.2), math.floor(#main * 0.8))]
        local length = height * (0.15 + math.random() * 0.15)
        local side = length * 0.6
        local tip = origin + Vector3.new((math.random() * 2 - 1) * side, -length, (math.random() * 2 - 1) * side)
        tip = Vector3.new(tip.X, math.max(tip.Y, strikePos.Y), tip.Z)
        addPath(lightningState.bendPath({origin, tip}, lightningState.BoltBranchIterations, length * 0.1), width * 0.5, 0.7)
    end

    return {Anchor = anchor, Beams = beams}
end

function lightningState.showBolt(bolt, visible)
    if not bolt then return end
    for _, beam in ipairs(bolt.Beams) do
        beam.Enabled = visible
    end
end

function lightningState.clearBolt(bolt)
    if bolt and bolt.Anchor then bolt.Anchor:Destroy() end
end

local function setLightningFlash(active, restoreValue)
    lightningFlashBoost = (active and brightnessEnabled) and (100 - brightnessValue) or 0
    if not brightnessEnabled then
        Lighting.Brightness = active and 100 or restoreValue
    end
end

local function doFlash(restoreValue, bolt)
    setLightningFlash(true, restoreValue)
    lightningState.showBolt(bolt, true)
    task.wait(0.2)
    setLightningFlash(false, restoreValue)
    lightningState.showBolt(bolt, false)
    task.wait()
end

local function applyLightning(enabled)
    lightningState.Enabled = enabled
    lightningState.LoopId = lightningState.LoopId + 1
    local myId = lightningState.LoopId
    if enabled then
        local function addAtmosphere(snd)
            local echo = Instance.new("EchoSoundEffect")
            echo.Delay = 0.12
            echo.Feedback = 0.65
            echo.WetLevel = 0
            echo.Parent = snd
            return echo
        end

        local function randomizeEcho(echo)
            echo.Delay = 0.05 + math.random() * 0.35
            echo.Feedback = 0.3 + math.random() * 0.5
        end

        lightningState.Sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        lightningState.Sound.Name = "CustomLightningSoundPreview"
        lightningState.Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://12222019"
        lightningState.Sound.PlaybackSpeed = 2
        lightningState.Sound.Parent = SoundService

        lightningState.ThunderSound = Instance.new("Sound")
        lightningState.ThunderSound.Name = "CustomThunderSoundPreview"
        lightningState.ThunderSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://12222030"
        lightningState.ThunderSound.PlaybackSpeed = 2
        lightningState.ThunderSound.Parent = SoundService

        lightningState.DeepThunderSound = Instance.new("Sound")
        lightningState.DeepThunderSound.Name = "CustomDeepThunderSoundPreview"
        lightningState.DeepThunderSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://12222019"
        lightningState.DeepThunderSound.PlaybackSpeed = 0.5
        lightningState.DeepThunderSound.Parent = SoundService
        local deepThunderEcho = addAtmosphere(lightningState.DeepThunderSound)

        task.spawn(function()
            local progress, goal = 0, 0.5 + math.random()
            while lightningState.Enabled and lightningState.LoopId == myId do
                progress = progress + task.wait() * lightningState.Frequency / 300
                if progress >= goal and lightningState.Enabled and lightningState.LoopId == myId then
                    progress, goal = 0, 0.5 + math.random()
                    local restoreValue = Lighting.Brightness
                    local isFar = math.random(1,3) <= 2
                    local delayTime = isFar and math.random(5,8) or math.random(1,4)
                    local distance = delayTime * lightningState.BoltDistancePerSecond
                    local boltOk, bolt = pcall(function()
                        return lightningState.buildBolt(lightningState.strikePosition(distance), distance)
                    end)
                    if not boltOk then
                        bolt = nil
                    end
                    lightningState.Bolt = bolt
                    for i = 1, math.random(1,5) do
                        if not (lightningState.Enabled and lightningState.LoopId == myId) then break end
                        doFlash(restoreValue, bolt)
                    end
                    lightningState.clearBolt(bolt)
                    if lightningState.Bolt == bolt then lightningState.Bolt = nil end
                    task.wait(delayTime)
                    if lightningState.Enabled and lightningState.LoopId == myId and lightningState.SoundEnabled then
                        local vol = (1/delayTime) * lightningState.SoundVolume
                        lightningState.Sound.Volume = vol
                        lightningState.ThunderSound.Volume = vol
                        if not isFar then
                            lightningState.Sound:Play()
                            lightningState.ThunderSound:Play()
                        else
                            randomizeEcho(deepThunderEcho)
                            lightningState.DeepThunderSound:Play()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        lightningFlashBoost = 0
        if not brightnessEnabled then Lighting.Brightness = originalLighting.Brightness end
        lightningState.clearBolt(lightningState.Bolt)
        lightningState.Bolt = nil
        if lightningState.Sound then lightningState.Sound:Destroy(); lightningState.Sound = nil end
        if lightningState.ThunderSound then lightningState.ThunderSound:Destroy(); lightningState.ThunderSound = nil end
        if lightningState.DeepThunderSound then lightningState.DeepThunderSound:Destroy(); lightningState.DeepThunderSound = nil end
    end
end

local auroraState = {

    Speed = 1,
    NightOnly = true,
    Size = 3,
    Distance = 1000,
    BaseSpan = 2000,
    Follow = true,
    Angle = 0,
    Heading = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),
    Origin = nil,
    Height = 360,
    ReferenceSize = 3,
    Roll = 0,
    CurveSize0 = 500,
    CurveSize1 = 260,
    Segments = 250,
    CheckInterval = 0.1,
    Layers = {
        {
            Texture = "rbxassetid://4553095322", Length = 800, Speed = -0.015, Width = 700,
            Color = {
                {0, 0.752941, 0.258824, 1},

    Accum = 0,
    AppliedSize = nil,
    AppliedSpeed = nil,

function auroraState.direction()
    local heading = auroraState.Heading
    local radians = math.rad(auroraState.Angle)
    local right = Vector3.new(-heading.Z, 0, heading.X)
    return heading * math.cos(radians) + right * math.sin(radians)
end

function auroraState.frame(center, direction)
    local yaw = math.atan2(-direction.Z, direction.X)
    return CFrame.new(center) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(auroraState.Roll))
end

function auroraState.place()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera or not auroraState.Anchor then return end
    if auroraState.Follow or not auroraState.Origin then
        auroraState.Origin = camera.CFrame.Position
    end
    local direction = auroraState.direction()
    local ground = Vector3.new(0, auroraState.Height, 0)
    auroraState.Anchor.CFrame = auroraState.frame(auroraState.Origin + direction * auroraState.Distance + ground, direction)
end

function auroraState.fit()
    local size = auroraState.Size
    local half = auroraState.BaseSpan * size / 2
    local bend = (auroraState.CurveSize0 - auroraState.CurveSize1) / 4 * (size - auroraState.ReferenceSize)
    auroraState.One.Position = Vector3.new(-bend, 0, -half)
    auroraState.Two.Position = Vector3.new(-bend, 0, half)
    for index, beam in ipairs(auroraState.Beams) do
        local layer = auroraState.Layers[index]
        beam.Width0 = layer.Width * size
        beam.Width1 = layer.Width * size
        beam.TextureLength = layer.Length * size
        beam.CurveSize0 = auroraState.CurveSize0 * size
        beam.CurveSize1 = auroraState.CurveSize1 * size
    end
    auroraState.AppliedSize = size
end

function auroraState.build()
    local anchor = Instance.new("Part")
    anchor.Name = "CustomAuroraAnchorPreview"
    anchor.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
    anchor.Anchored = true
    anchor.CanCollide = false
    anchor.CanQuery = false
    anchor.CanTouch = false
    anchor.CastShadow = false
    anchor.Transparency = 1
    anchor.Parent = workspace

    local one = Instance.new("Attachment")
    one.Name = "One"
    one.Parent = anchor
    local two = Instance.new("Attachment")
    two.Name = "Two"
    two.Parent = anchor

    local beams = {}
    for index, layer in ipairs(auroraState.Layers) do
        local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
        beam.Name = "AuroraBeam" .. index
        beam.Attachment0 = one
        beam.Attachment1 = two
        beam.Texture = layer.Texture
        beam.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Static
        beam.TextureSpeed = layer.Speed * auroraState.Speed
        beam.Segments = auroraState.Segments
        beam.LightEmission = 1
        beam.LightInfluence = 0
        beam.FaceCamera = false
        beam.ZOffset = 0
        local colorKeys = table.create(#layer.Color)
        for i, point in ipairs(layer.Color) do
            colorKeys[i] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(point[1], Color3.new(point[2], point[3], point[4]))
        end
        beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(colorKeys)
        beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1)
        beam.Enabled = false
        beam.Parent = anchor
        beams[index] = beam
    end
    auroraState.Anchor = anchor
    auroraState.Beams = beams
    auroraState.One = one
    auroraState.Two = two
    auroraState.AppliedSpeed = auroraState.Speed
    auroraState.fit()
    auroraState.place()
end

function auroraState.step(dt)
    auroraState.place()
    auroraState.Accum = auroraState.Accum + dt
    if auroraState.Accum < auroraState.CheckInterval then return end
    auroraState.Accum = 0

    if auroraState.AppliedSize ~= auroraState.Size then auroraState.fit() end

    local night = 1
    if auroraState.NightOnly then
        night = math.clamp((0.1 - Lighting:GetSunDirection().Y) / 0.25, 0, 1)
    end
    local master = auroraState.Intensity * night
    local visible = master > 0.01
    local speedChanged = auroraState.AppliedSpeed ~= auroraState.Speed

    for index, beam in ipairs(auroraState.Beams) do
        local layer = auroraState.Layers[index]
        if visible then
            local keys = table.create(#layer.Alpha)
            for i, point in ipairs(layer.Alpha) do
                keys[i] = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(point[1], math.clamp(1 - (1 - point[2]) * master, 0, 1))
            end
            beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(keys)
        end
        if speedChanged then beam.TextureSpeed = layer.Speed * auroraState.Speed end
        if beam.Enabled ~= visible then beam.Enabled = visible end
    end
    auroraState.AppliedSpeed = auroraState.Speed
end

function auroraState.apply(enabled)
    if auroraState.Conn then auroraState.Conn:Disconnect(); auroraState.Conn = nil end
    auroraState.Enabled = enabled
    if auroraState.Anchor then auroraState.Anchor:Destroy(); auroraState.Anchor = nil end
    auroraState.Beams = nil
    if enabled then
        auroraState.Origin = nil
        auroraState.Accum = math.huge
        auroraState.build()
        auroraState.Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(auroraState.step)
    end
end

addToggleRow(tabPages["atmo"], "Северное сияние", true, auroraState.apply, function(Body)

        end)
    end

    addPanelHeader(Body, "РАЗМЕР И ПОЛОЖЕНИЕ")
    addSlider(Body, "Размер", 0.5, 10, auroraState.Size, 1, function(v)
        auroraState.Size = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(130,220,190))
    addSlider(Body, "Дальность", 100, 10000, auroraState.Distance, 0, function(v)
        auroraState.Distance = math.floor(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(140,200,215), true)
    addSlider(Body, "Высота", 0, 3000, auroraState.Height, 0, function(v)
        auroraState.Height = math.floor(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(145,205,205))
    addSlider(Body, "Сторона", 0, 360, auroraState.Angle, 0, function(v)
        auroraState.Angle = math.floor(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(150,190,225))
    pillRow("Смещение (отходит от тебя)", function() return auroraState.Follow end, function(v)
        auroraState.Follow = v
    end)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ОТОБРАЖЕНИЕ")
    pillRow("Только ночью", function() return auroraState.NightOnly end, function(v)
        auroraState.NightOnly = v
    end)
end, 520, "🌌")

local meteorState = {
    Enabled = false,
    Rate = 8,
    Speed = 1,
    Size = 1,
    NightOnly = true,
    Distance = 2500,
    MaxAngle = 60,
    MinElevation = 15,
    MaxElevation = 65,
    Tilt = 50,
    EndElevation = -3,
    AngularSpeed = 35,
    TailArc = 5,
    FadeIn = 0.15,
    HeadWidthPerDistance = 0.0026,
    GlowScale = 4,
    FirstDelay = 1.5,
    Palettes = {
        {{0, 255, 255, 255}, {0.4, 210, 230, 255}, {1, 140, 170, 255}},
        {{0, 255, 255, 255}, {0.4, 215, 255, 225}, {1, 120, 230, 170}},
        {{0, 255, 255, 240}, {0.4, 255, 225, 170}, {1, 255, 150, 90}},
    },
    Anchor = nil,
    Active = {},
    Conn = nil,
    NextSpawn = 0,
}

function meteorState.isNight()
    return math.clamp((0.1 - Lighting:GetSunDirection().Y) / 0.25, 0, 1) > 0.3
end

function meteorState.spawn()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera or not meteorState.Anchor then return end
    local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector
    local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
    if flat.Magnitude < 0.01 then flat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
    local yaw = math.rad((math.random() * 2 - 1) * meteorState.MaxAngle)
    local horizontal = CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0):VectorToWorldSpace(flat.Unit)
    local elevation = math.rad(meteorState.MinElevation + math.random() * (meteorState.MaxElevation - meteorState.MinElevation))
    local start = (horizontal * math.cos(elevation) + Vector3.new(0, math.sin(elevation), 0)).Unit
    local fall = (Vector3.new(0, -1, 0) + start * start.Y).Unit
    local side = start:Cross(fall)
    local tilt = math.rad((math.random() * 2 - 1) * meteorState.Tilt)
    local tangent = fall * math.cos(tilt) + side * math.sin(tilt)
    local radius = math.sqrt(start.Y * start.Y + tangent.Y * tangent.Y)
    local endSin = math.sin(math.rad(meteorState.EndElevation))
    local total = math.atan2(tangent.Y, start.Y) + math.acos(math.clamp(endSin / radius, -1, 1))
    local tailArc = math.rad(meteorState.TailArc) * meteorState.Size
    local angularSpeed = math.rad(meteorState.AngularSpeed) * meteorState.Speed * (0.8 + math.random() * 0.4)

    local head = Instance.new("Attachment")
    head.Parent = meteorState.Anchor
    local tail = Instance.new("Attachment")
    tail.Parent = meteorState.Anchor

    local palette = meteorState.Palettes[math.random(#meteorState.Palettes)]
    local colorKeys = {}
    for i, point in ipairs(palette) do
        colorKeys[i] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(point[1], Color3.fromRGB(point[2], point[3], point[4]))
    end
    local color = ColorSequence.new(colorKeys)
    local coreAlpha = NumberSequence.new{
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, 0.2),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    }
    local glowAlpha = NumberSequence.new{
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.8),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    }
    local beams = {}
    for layer = 1, 2 do
        local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
        beam.Attachment0 = head
        beam.Attachment1 = tail
        beam.Segments = 1
        beam.FaceCamera = true
        beam.LightEmission = 1
        beam.LightInfluence = 0
        beam.Color = color
        beam.Transparency = layer == 1 and coreAlpha or glowAlpha
        beam.Width0 = 0
        beam.Width1 = 0
        beam.Parent = meteorState.Anchor
        beams[layer] = beam
    end

    meteorState.Active[#meteorState.Active + 1] = {
        Head = head,
        Tail = tail,
        Beams = beams,
        Start = os.clock(),
        Life = (total + tailArc) / angularSpeed,
        S = start,
        T = tangent,
        Arc = total,
        TailArc = tailArc,
        Reach = total / (total + tailArc),
        Width = meteorState.Distance * meteorState.HeadWidthPerDistance * meteorState.Size,
    }
end

function meteorState.step()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end
    local now = os.clock()
    if now >= meteorState.NextSpawn then
        meteorState.NextSpawn = now + (60 / meteorState.Rate) * (0.5 + math.random())
        if not meteorState.NightOnly or meteorState.isNight() then
            pcall(meteorState.spawn)
        end
    end
    local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
    local distance = meteorState.Distance
    local active = meteorState.Active
    for i = #active, 1, -1 do
        local m = active[i]
        local u = (now - m.Start) / m.Life
        if u >= 1 then
            m.Head:Destroy()
            m.Tail:Destroy()
            for _, beam in ipairs(m.Beams) do beam:Destroy() end
            table.remove(active, i)
        else
            local travelled = u * (m.Arc + m.TailArc)
            local a = math.min(travelled, m.Arc)
            local b = math.min(travelled - m.TailArc, m.Arc)
            m.Head.WorldPosition = origin + (m.S * math.cos(a) + m.T * math.sin(a)) * distance
            m.Tail.WorldPosition = origin + (m.S * math.cos(b) + m.T * math.sin(b)) * distance
            local fade = math.min(1, u / meteorState.FadeIn)
            if u > m.Reach then fade = fade * (1 - u) / (1 - m.Reach) end
            m.Beams[1].Width0 = m.Width * fade
            m.Beams[2].Width0 = m.Width * meteorState.GlowScale * fade
        end
    end
end

function meteorState.apply(enabled)
    if meteorState.Conn then meteorState.Conn:Disconnect(); meteorState.Conn = nil end
    meteorState.Enabled = enabled
    meteorState.Active = {}
    if meteorState.Anchor then meteorState.Anchor:Destroy(); meteorState.Anchor = nil end
    if enabled then
        local anchor = Instance.new("Part")
        anchor.Name = "CustomMeteorAnchorPreview"
        anchor.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
        anchor.Anchored = true
        anchor.CanCollide = false
        anchor.CanQuery = false
        anchor.CanTouch = false
        anchor.CastShadow = false
        anchor.Transparency = 1
        anchor.Parent = workspace
        meteorState.Anchor = anchor
        meteorState.NextSpawn = os.clock() + meteorState.FirstDelay
        meteorState.Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(meteorState.step)
    end
end

    addPanelHeader(Body, "ОСНОВНОЕ")

        meteorState.Rate = math.floor(v)

        meteorState.Speed = v
