end, 340, "🌠")

local rainbowState = {
    Enabled = false,
    Brightness = 0.7,
    Width = 6,
    Distance = 2500,
    Height = 0,
    Angle = 0,
    Heading = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),
    Radius = 41,
    ArcSpan = 90,
    PieceAngle = 30,
    PieceSegments = 40,
    FadeSamples = 12,
    LightEmission = 0.2,
    HorizonFadeStart = 0,
    HorizonFadeLength = 0.12,
    Layers = {
        {Scale = 2.6, Opacity = 0.12},
        {Scale = 2.0, Opacity = 0.18},
        {Scale = 1.5, Opacity = 0.26},
        {Scale = 1.1, Opacity = 0.38},
        {Scale = 0.8, Opacity = 0.45},
    },
    Colors = {
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70),
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 55),
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 90),
        Color3.fromRGB(100, 225, 115),
        Color3.fromRGB(80, 195, 255),
        Color3.fromRGB(90, 115, 255),
        Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 240),
    },
    Anchor = nil,
    Conn = nil,
    Bands = {},
}

function rainbowState.layout()
    local bandCount = #rainbowState.Colors
    local bandAngle = rainbowState.Width / bandCount
    local bandWidth = rainbowState.Distance * math.rad(bandAngle)
    local startAngle = -math.rad(rainbowState.ArcSpan)
    local step = math.rad(rainbowState.PieceAngle)
    local handle = 4 / 3 * math.tan(step / 4)
    local samples = rainbowState.FadeSamples
    for k, band in ipairs(rainbowState.Bands) do
        local radius = math.rad(rainbowState.Radius + rainbowState.Width / 2 - (k - 0.5) * bandAngle)
        local sinR = math.sin(radius)
        local planar = sinR * rainbowState.Distance
        local depth = -math.cos(radius) * rainbowState.Distance
        for index, track in ipairs(band.Tracks) do
            local layer = rainbowState.Layers[index]
            local opacity = math.clamp(layer.Opacity * rainbowState.Brightness, 0, 1)
            local trackStart = startAngle - math.rad(track.Shift)
            for j = 0, track.Steps do
                local phi = trackStart + j * step
                local sinP, cosP = math.sin(phi), math.cos(phi)
                track.Attachments[j].CFrame = CFrame.fromMatrix(
                    Vector3.new(planar * sinP, planar * cosP, depth),
                    Vector3.new(cosP, -sinP, 0),
                    Vector3.new(sinP, cosP, 0)
                )
            end
            for j = 1, track.Steps do
                local beam = track.Beams[j]
                beam.Width0 = bandWidth * layer.Scale
                beam.Width1 = bandWidth * layer.Scale
                beam.CurveSize0 = planar * handle
                beam.CurveSize1 = planar * handle
                local from = trackStart + (j - 1) * step
                local keys = {}
                for m = 0, samples do
                    local phi = from + step * m / samples
                    local visible = math.clamp((sinR * math.cos(phi) - rainbowState.HorizonFadeStart) / rainbowState.HorizonFadeLength, 0, 1)
                    keys[m + 1] = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(m / samples, 1 - opacity * visible)
                end
                beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(keys)
            end
        end
    end
end

function rainbowState.build()
    local anchor = rainbowState.Anchor
    rainbowState.Bands = {}
    for k, color in ipairs(rainbowState.Colors) do
        local tracks = {}
        for index in ipairs(rainbowState.Layers) do
            local shift = ((k * 0.618034 + index * 0.37) % 1) * rainbowState.PieceAngle
            local count = math.ceil((2 * rainbowState.ArcSpan + shift) / rainbowState.PieceAngle)
            local attachments, beams = {}, {}
            for j = 0, count do
                local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
                attachment.Parent = anchor
                attachments[j] = attachment
                if j > 0 then
                    local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
                    beam.Attachment0 = attachments[j - 1]
                    beam.Attachment1 = attachment
                    beam.Segments = rainbowState.PieceSegments
                    beam.FaceCamera = true
                    beam.LightEmission = rainbowState.LightEmission
                    beam.LightInfluence = 0
                    beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(color)
                    beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1)
                    beam.Parent = anchor
                    beams[j] = beam
                end
            end
            tracks[index] = {Steps = count, Shift = shift, Attachments = attachments, Beams = beams}
        end
        rainbowState.Bands[k] = {Tracks = tracks}
    end
    rainbowState.layout()
end

function rainbowState.step()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local anchor = rainbowState.Anchor
    if not camera or not anchor then return end
    local origin = camera.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, rainbowState.Height, 0)
    local sun = Lighting:GetSunDirection()
    local flat = Vector3.new(-sun.X, 0, -sun.Z)
    if flat.Magnitude > 0.05 then rainbowState.Heading = flat.Unit end
    local direction = CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(rainbowState.Angle), 0):VectorToWorldSpace(rainbowState.Heading)
    anchor.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(origin, origin + direction)
end

function rainbowState.apply(enabled)
    if rainbowState.Conn then rainbowState.Conn:Disconnect(); rainbowState.Conn = nil end
    rainbowState.Enabled = enabled
    if rainbowState.Anchor then rainbowState.Anchor:Destroy(); rainbowState.Anchor = nil end
    rainbowState.Bands = {}
    if enabled then
        local anchor = Instance.new("Part")
        anchor.Name = "CustomRainbowAnchorPreview"
        anchor.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
        anchor.Anchored = true
        anchor.CanCollide = false
        anchor.CanQuery = false
        anchor.CanTouch = false
        anchor.CastShadow = false
        anchor.Transparency = 1
        anchor.Parent = workspace
        rainbowState.Anchor = anchor
        pcall(rainbowState.build)
        rainbowState.Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(rainbowState.step)
    end
end

addToggleRow(tabPages["atmo"], "Радуга", true, rainbowState.apply, function(Body)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ОСНОВНОЕ")
    addSlider(Body, "Яркость", 0.1, 1, rainbowState.Brightness, 2, function(v)
        rainbowState.Brightness = v
        if rainbowState.Anchor then pcall(rainbowState.layout) end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 120))
    addSlider(Body, "Ширина", 2, 15, rainbowState.Width, 1, function(v)
        rainbowState.Width = v
        if rainbowState.Anchor then pcall(rainbowState.layout) end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 170))
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ПОЛОЖЕНИЕ")
    addSlider(Body, "Дальность", 100, 10000, rainbowState.Distance, 0, function(v)
        rainbowState.Distance = math.floor(v)
        if rainbowState.Anchor then pcall(rainbowState.layout) end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 160), true)
    addSlider(Body, "Высота", -1500, 2000, rainbowState.Height, 0, function(v)
        rainbowState.Height = math.floor(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 140))
    addSlider(Body, "Сторона", 0, 360, rainbowState.Angle, 0, function(v)
        rainbowState.Angle = math.floor(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 190))
end, 440, "🌈")

local fireflyState = {
    Enabled = false,
    Count = 25,
    Size = 1,
    Transparency = 0,
    NightOnly = true,
    Radius = 45,
    MinHeight = 0.8,
    MaxHeight = 9,
    SphereSize = 0.35,
    Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 60),
    ReferenceColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 60),
    MinSpeed = 1.2,
    MaxSpeed = 3,
    Turn = 1.5,
    RetargetMin = 3,
    RetargetMax = 6,
    ArriveDistance = 2,
    RespawnFactor = 1.4,
    PassThroughWalls = false,
    LowHeight = 9,
    LowShare = 0.5,
    WallMargin = 0.8,
    WallLookAhead = 3,
    WallCheckInterval = 0.2,
    AimAttempts = 3,
    AimCooldown = 0.5,
    ScatterDelay = 0.15,
    ScatterToken = 0,
    GroundRay = 100,
    GroundOffset = 3,
    CheckInterval = 0.3,
    Texture = "rbxassetid://75202463",
    PoisonRate = 4,
    PoisonSize = 0.1,
    PoisonColorStart = Color3.fromRGB(255, 203, 46),
    PoisonColorEnd = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
    ShineRate = 1,
    ShineSize = 4,
    ShineColorStart = Color3.fromRGB(250, 200, 150),
    ShineColorEnd = Color3.fromRGB(255, 246, 178),
    ShineTransparency = {{0, 1}, {0.05, 0.98}, {0.15, 0.93}, {0.769, 0.93}, {0.848, 0.98}, {1, 1}},
    Folder = nil,
    List = {},
    Palette = nil,
    Sequences = nil,
    Faded = -1,
    Conn = nil,
    RayParams = nil,
    GroundY = 0,
    NextCheck = 0,
    Night = 1,
}

function fireflyState.tint(source)
    local reference = fireflyState.ReferenceColor
    local color = fireflyState.Color
    return Color3.new(
        math.clamp(source.R * color.R / reference.R, 0, 1),
        math.clamp(source.G * color.G / reference.G, 0, 1),
        math.clamp(source.B * color.B / reference.B, 0, 1)
    )
end

function fireflyState.palette()
    local tint = fireflyState.tint
    fireflyState.Palette = {
        Sphere = fireflyState.Color,
        Poison = ColorSequence.new(tint(fireflyState.PoisonColorStart), tint(fireflyState.PoisonColorEnd)),
        Shine = ColorSequence.new(tint(fireflyState.ShineColorStart), tint(fireflyState.ShineColorEnd)),
    }
end

function fireflyState.paint(firefly)
    local palette = fireflyState.Palette
    firefly.Part.Color = palette.Sphere
    firefly.Poison.Color = palette.Poison
    firefly.Shine.Color = palette.Shine
end

function fireflyState.recolor()
    if not fireflyState.Folder then return end
    fireflyState.palette()
    for _, firefly in ipairs(fireflyState.List) do
        fireflyState.paint(firefly)
    end
end

function fireflyState.fade()
    local fireflies = fireflyState
    local visible = 1 - math.clamp(fireflies.Transparency, 0, 1)
    local keys = table.create(#fireflies.ShineTransparency)
    for i, point in ipairs(fireflies.ShineTransparency) do
        keys[i] = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(point[1], 1 - (1 - point[2]) * visible)
    end
    fireflies.Sequences = {
        Poison = NumberSequence.new(1 - visible),
        Shine = NumberSequence.new(keys),
    }
    fireflies.Faded = fireflies.Transparency
    for _, firefly in ipairs(fireflies.List) do
        firefly.Poison.Transparency = fireflies.Sequences.Poison
        firefly.Shine.Transparency = fireflies.Sequences.Shine
        firefly.Part.Transparency = 1 - fireflies.Night * visible
    end
end

function fireflyState.ground()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local character = player.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then
        return (camera and camera.CFrame.Position.Y or 0) - 5
    end
    fireflyState.RayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character}
    local result = workspace:Raycast(root.Position, Vector3.new(0, -fireflyState.GroundRay, 0), fireflyState.RayParams)
    if result then return result.Position.Y end
    return root.Position.Y - fireflyState.GroundOffset
end

function fireflyState.randomPoint(center, ceiling)
    local angle = math.random() * math.pi * 2
    local distance = math.sqrt(math.random()) * fireflyState.Radius
    local height = fireflyState.MinHeight + math.random() * (ceiling - fireflyState.MinHeight)
    return Vector3.new(
        center.X + math.cos(angle) * distance,
        fireflyState.GroundY + height,
        center.Z + math.sin(angle) * distance
    )
end

function fireflyState.retarget()
    return os.clock() + fireflyState.RetargetMin + math.random() * (fireflyState.RetargetMax - fireflyState.RetargetMin)
end

function fireflyState.pickCeiling()
    if math.random() < fireflyState.LowShare then
        return math.min(fireflyState.LowHeight, fireflyState.MaxHeight)
    end
    return fireflyState.MaxHeight
end

function fireflyState.clip(from, to)
    if fireflyState.PassThroughWalls then return to end
    local offset = to - from
    if offset.Magnitude < 0.001 then return to end
    local result = workspace:Raycast(from, offset, fireflyState.RayParams)
    if not result then return to end
    return from + offset.Unit * math.max(result.Distance - fireflyState.WallMargin, 0)
end

function fireflyState.spawnPoint(center, ceiling)
    return fireflyState.clip(center, fireflyState.randomPoint(center, ceiling))
end

function fireflyState.aim(firefly, center)
    local target = firefly.Pos
    for _ = 1, fireflyState.AimAttempts do
        target = fireflyState.clip(firefly.Pos, fireflyState.randomPoint(center, firefly.Ceiling))
        if (target - firefly.Pos).Magnitude >= fireflyState.ArriveDistance * 2 then break end
    end
    firefly.Target = target
    firefly.NextTarget = fireflyState.retarget()
    firefly.Cooldown = os.clock() + fireflyState.AimCooldown
end

function fireflyState.avoid(firefly, center)
    local heading = firefly.Vel
    if heading.Magnitude < 0.05 then heading = firefly.Target - firefly.Pos end
    if heading.Magnitude < 0.001 then return end
    local result = workspace:Raycast(firefly.Pos, heading.Unit * fireflyState.WallLookAhead, fireflyState.RayParams)
    if not result then return end
    local normal = result.Normal
    firefly.Vel = firefly.Vel - normal * firefly.Vel:Dot(normal)
    if result.Distance < fireflyState.WallMargin then
        firefly.Pos = firefly.Pos + normal * (fireflyState.WallMargin - result.Distance)
    end
    fireflyState.aim(firefly, center)
end

function fireflyState.newEmitter(part, name)
    local emitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
    emitter.Name = name
    emitter.Texture = fireflyState.Texture
    emitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(5, 10)
    emitter.Acceleration = Vector3.zero
    emitter.Drag = 0
    emitter.VelocityInheritance = 0
    emitter.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Top
    emitter.Shape = Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box
    emitter.ShapeStyle = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume
    emitter.ShapeInOut = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeInOut.Outward
    emitter.ShapePartial = 1
    emitter.Squash = NumberSequence.new(0)
    emitter.LightEmission = 1
    emitter.LightInfluence = 0
    emitter.Brightness = 1
    emitter.Orientation = Enum.ParticleOrientation.FacingCamera
    emitter.Rotation = NumberRange.new(-100, 100)
    emitter.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(0)
    emitter.TimeScale = 1
    emitter.WindAffectsDrag = false
    emitter.Rate = 0
    emitter.Parent = part
    return emitter
end

function fireflyState.look(firefly)
    local night = fireflyState.Night
    local scale = fireflyState.Size
    firefly.Part.Size = Vector3.one * fireflyState.SphereSize * scale
    firefly.Part.Transparency = 1 - night * (1 - math.clamp(fireflyState.Transparency, 0, 1))
    firefly.Poison.Size = NumberSequence.new(fireflyState.PoisonSize * scale)
    firefly.Poison.Rate = fireflyState.PoisonRate * night
    firefly.Poison.Enabled = night > 0.01
    firefly.Shine.Size = NumberSequence.new(fireflyState.ShineSize * scale)
    firefly.Shine.Rate = fireflyState.ShineRate * night
    firefly.Shine.Enabled = night > 0.01
end

function fireflyState.create(center)
    if fireflyState.Faded ~= fireflyState.Transparency then fireflyState.fade() end
    local part = Instance.new("Part")
    part.Name = "CustomFirefly"
    part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
    part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
    part.Anchored = true
    part.CanCollide = false
    part.CanQuery = false
    part.CanTouch = false
    part.CastShadow = false

    local poison = fireflyState.newEmitter(part, "Poison")
    poison.Speed = NumberRange.new(0.2)
    poison.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(100, 100)
    poison.LockedToPart = false
    poison.ZOffset = 0
    poison.Transparency = fireflyState.Sequences.Poison

    local shine = fireflyState.newEmitter(part, "Shine")
    shine.Speed = NumberRange.new(0.05)
    shine.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    shine.LockedToPart = true
    shine.ZOffset = 2
    shine.Transparency = fireflyState.Sequences.Shine

    local ceiling = fireflyState.pickCeiling()
    local position = fireflyState.spawnPoint(center, ceiling)
    part.Position = position

    local firefly = {
        Part = part,
        Poison = poison,
        Shine = shine,
        Ceiling = ceiling,
        Pos = position,
        Vel = Vector3.zero,
        Target = position,
        NextTarget = 0,
        Cooldown = 0,
        NextWall = os.clock() + math.random() * fireflyState.WallCheckInterval,
        Speed = fireflyState.MinSpeed + math.random() * (fireflyState.MaxSpeed - fireflyState.MinSpeed),
    }
    fireflyState.aim(firefly, center)
    fireflyState.paint(firefly)
    fireflyState.look(firefly)
    part.Parent = fireflyState.Folder
    return firefly
end

function fireflyState.setCount(count)
    if not fireflyState.Folder then return end
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local center = camera and camera.CFrame.Position or Vector3.zero
    local list = fireflyState.List
    while #list < count do
        list[#list + 1] = fireflyState.create(center)
    end
    while #list > count do
        list[#list].Part:Destroy()
        list[#list] = nil
    end
end

function fireflyState.scatter()
    if not fireflyState.Folder then return end
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local center = camera and camera.CFrame.Position or Vector3.zero
    for _, firefly in ipairs(fireflyState.List) do
        firefly.Ceiling = fireflyState.pickCeiling()
        firefly.Pos = fireflyState.spawnPoint(center, firefly.Ceiling)
        firefly.Vel = Vector3.zero
        fireflyState.aim(firefly, center)
        firefly.Part.Position = firefly.Pos
    end
end

function fireflyState.scatterSoon()
    fireflyState.ScatterToken = fireflyState.ScatterToken + 1
    local token = fireflyState.ScatterToken
    task.delay(fireflyState.ScatterDelay, function()
        if token == fireflyState.ScatterToken then fireflyState.scatter() end
    end)
end

function fireflyState.refresh()
    if not fireflyState.Folder then return end
    fireflyState.Night = 1
    if fireflyState.NightOnly then
        fireflyState.Night = math.clamp((0.1 - Lighting:GetSunDirection().Y) / 0.25, 0, 1)
    end
    if fireflyState.Faded ~= fireflyState.Transparency then fireflyState.fade() end
    for _, firefly in ipairs(fireflyState.List) do
        fireflyState.look(firefly)
    end
end

function fireflyState.step(dt)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera or not fireflyState.Folder then return end
    local now = os.clock()
    local center = camera.CFrame.Position
    if now >= fireflyState.NextCheck then
        fireflyState.NextCheck = now + fireflyState.CheckInterval
        local ok, result = pcall(fireflyState.ground)
        if ok then
            fireflyState.GroundY = result
        end
        fireflyState.refresh()
    end
    local reach = fireflyState.Radius * fireflyState.RespawnFactor
    for _, firefly in ipairs(fireflyState.List) do
        local flat = Vector3.new(firefly.Pos.X - center.X, 0, firefly.Pos.Z - center.Z)
        if flat.Magnitude > reach then
            firefly.Pos = fireflyState.spawnPoint(center, firefly.Ceiling)
            firefly.Vel = Vector3.zero
            fireflyState.aim(firefly, center)
        else
            local toTarget = firefly.Target - firefly.Pos
            if now >= firefly.NextTarget or (toTarget.Magnitude < fireflyState.ArriveDistance and now >= firefly.Cooldown) then
                fireflyState.aim(firefly, center)
                toTarget = firefly.Target - firefly.Pos
            end
            if not fireflyState.PassThroughWalls and now >= firefly.NextWall then
                firefly.NextWall = now + fireflyState.WallCheckInterval
                fireflyState.avoid(firefly, center)
                toTarget = firefly.Target - firefly.Pos
            end
            local desired = Vector3.zero
            if toTarget.Magnitude > 0.001 then desired = toTarget.Unit * firefly.Speed end
            firefly.Vel = firefly.Vel:Lerp(desired, math.clamp(dt * fireflyState.Turn, 0, 1))
            firefly.Pos = firefly.Pos + firefly.Vel * dt
        end
        firefly.Part.Position = firefly.Pos
    end
end

function fireflyState.apply(enabled)
    if fireflyState.Conn then fireflyState.Conn:Disconnect(); fireflyState.Conn = nil end
    fireflyState.Enabled = enabled
    if fireflyState.Folder then fireflyState.Folder:Destroy(); fireflyState.Folder = nil end
    fireflyState.List = {}
    if enabled then
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.IgnoreWater = true
        pcall(function() params.RespectCanCollide = true end)
        fireflyState.RayParams = params

        local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
        folder.Name = "CustomFirefliesPreview"
        folder.Parent = workspace
        fireflyState.Folder = folder

        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        fireflyState.GroundY = (camera and camera.CFrame.Position.Y or 0) - 5
        local ok, result = pcall(fireflyState.ground)
        if ok then fireflyState.GroundY = result end
        fireflyState.Night = 1
        if fireflyState.NightOnly then
            fireflyState.Night = math.clamp((0.1 - Lighting:GetSunDirection().Y) / 0.25, 0, 1)
        end
        fireflyState.NextCheck = 0
        fireflyState.palette()
        pcall(fireflyState.setCount, fireflyState.Count)
        fireflyState.Conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(fireflyState.step)
    end
end

function terrainState.read(name, fallback)
    local ok, value = pcall(function() return terrainState.Terrain[name] end)
    if ok and value ~= nil then return value end
    return fallback
end

function terrainState.write(name, value)
    local terrain = terrainState.Terrain
    local ok = pcall(function() terrain[name] = value end)
    return ok
end

function terrainState.same(first, second)
    if typeof(first) == "number" and typeof(second) == "number" then
        return math.abs(first - second) < 0.0001
    end
    return first == second
end

function terrainState.Water.values()
    local water = terrainState.Water
    return {
        WaterColor = water.Color,
        WaterTransparency = water.Transparency,
        WaterReflectance = water.Reflectance,
        WaterWaveSize = water.WaveSize,
        WaterWaveSpeed = water.WaveSpeed,
    }
end

do
    local water = terrainState.Water
    water.Color = terrainState.read("WaterColor", water.Color)
    water.Transparency = terrainState.read("WaterTransparency", water.Transparency)
    water.Reflectance = terrainState.read("WaterReflectance", water.Reflectance)
    water.WaveSize = terrainState.read("WaterWaveSize", water.WaveSize)
    water.WaveSpeed = terrainState.read("WaterWaveSpeed", water.WaveSpeed)
end

function terrainState.apply(group, enabled)
    if group.Conn then group.Conn:Disconnect(); group.Conn = nil end
    group.Enabled = enabled
    if enabled then
        if not group.Saved then
            group.Saved = {}
            for name in pairs(group.values()) do group.Saved[name] = terrainState.read(name) end
        end
        local function enforce()
            for name, value in pairs(group.values()) do
                if not terrainState.same(terrainState.read(name), value) then
                    terrainState.write(name, value)
                end
            end
        end
        enforce()
        group.Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(enforce)
    elseif group.Saved then
        for name, value in pairs(group.Saved) do
            terrainState.write(name, value)
        end
        group.Saved = nil
    end
end

function terrainState.Materials.read(entry)
    local ok, value = pcall(function() return terrainState.Terrain:GetMaterialColor(entry.Enum) end)
    if ok and typeof(value) == "Color3" then return value end
    return nil
end

function terrainState.Materials.write(entry, color)
    pcall(function() terrainState.Terrain:SetMaterialColor(entry.Enum, color) end)
end

function terrainState.Materials.close(first, second)
    local tolerance = terrainState.Materials.Tolerance
    return math.abs(first.R - second.R) < tolerance
        and math.abs(first.G - second.G) < tolerance
        and math.abs(first.B - second.B) < tolerance
end

function terrainState.Materials.enforce()
    local materials = terrainState.Materials
    for _, entry in ipairs(materials.List) do
        if not materials.close(entry.Color, entry.Original) then
            local current = materials.read(entry)
            if not current or not materials.close(current, entry.Color) then
                materials.write(entry, entry.Color)
            end
        end
    end
end

function terrainState.Materials.apply(enabled)
    local materials = terrainState.Materials
    if materials.Conn then materials.Conn:Disconnect(); materials.Conn = nil end
    materials.Enabled = enabled
    if enabled then
        materials.enforce()
        materials.Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(materials.enforce)
    else
        for _, entry in ipairs(materials.List) do
            if entry.Known and not materials.close(entry.Color, entry.Original) then
                materials.write(entry, entry.Original)
            end
        end
    end
end

do
    local materials = terrainState.Materials
    local available = {}
    for _, entry in ipairs(materials.List) do
        local ok, enum = pcall(function() return Enum.Material[entry.Name] end)
        if ok and enum then
            entry.Enum = enum
            local original = materials.read(entry)
            entry.Known = original ~= nil
            entry.Original = original or materials.FallbackColor
            entry.Color = entry.Original
            available[#available + 1] = entry
        end
    end
    materials.List = available
end

function terrainState.Pack.read()
    local pack = terrainState.Pack
    if not pack.Service then return nil end
    local ok, value = pcall(function() return pack.Service.Use2022Materials end)
    if ok and type(value) == "boolean" then return value end
    return nil
end

function terrainState.Pack.write(value)
    local pack = terrainState.Pack
    if not pack.Service then return false end
    if pack.read() == value then return true end
    if pcall(function() pack.Service.Use2022Materials = value end) then return true end
    return false
end

function terrainState.Pack.apply(enabled)
    local pack = terrainState.Pack
    pack.Enabled = enabled
    if enabled then
        if pack.Original == nil then pack.Original = pack.read() end
        pack.write(pack.Values[pack.Choice])
    elseif pack.Original ~= nil then
        pack.write(pack.Original)
        pack.Original = nil
    end
end

function terrainState.Pack.set(choice)
    local pack = terrainState.Pack
    pack.Choice = choice
    if pack.Enabled then pack.write(pack.Values[choice]) end
end

do
    local pack = terrainState.Pack
    pcall(function() pack.Service = game:GetService("MaterialService") end)
end

function terrainState.Underwater.sample()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return false end
    local position = camera.CFrame.Position
    local size = under.VoxelSize
    local corner = Vector3.new(
        math.floor(position.X / size) * size,
        math.floor(position.Y / size) * size,
        math.floor(position.Z / size) * size)
    local ok, data = pcall(function()
        return terrainState.Terrain:ReadVoxelChannels(
            Region3.new(corner, corner + Vector3.new(size, size, size)), size, {"LiquidOccupancy"})
    end)
    if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" then
        return false
    end
    local grid = data.LiquidOccupancy
    local occupancy = grid and grid[1] and grid[1][1] and grid[1][1][1]
    if type(occupancy) ~= "number" or occupancy <= 0 then return false end
    return position.Y < corner.Y + occupancy * size
end

function terrainState.Underwater.ensureEffects()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    if not under.ColorEffect then
        under.ColorEffect = mk("ColorCorrectionEffect", {Name = under.TintName, Enabled = false, Parent = Lighting})
    end
    if not under.BlurEffect then
        under.BlurEffect = mk("BlurEffect", {Name = under.BlurName, Enabled = false, Size = 0, Parent = Lighting})
    end
    if under.ColorEffect.Parent ~= Lighting then under.ColorEffect.Parent = Lighting end
    if under.BlurEffect.Parent ~= Lighting then under.BlurEffect.Parent = Lighting end
end

function terrainState.Underwater.removeEffects()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    if under.ColorEffect then
        local effect = under.ColorEffect
        under.ColorEffect = nil
        pcall(function() effect:Destroy() end)
    end
    if under.BlurEffect then
        local effect = under.BlurEffect
        under.BlurEffect = nil
        pcall(function() effect:Destroy() end)
    end
end

function terrainState.Underwater.tintColor()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    local color = terrainState.Water.Color
    if under.TintNormalize then
        local hue, saturation = color:ToHSV()
        return Color3.fromHSV(hue, saturation, 1)
    end
    return color
end

function terrainState.Underwater.curve(level, normalValue, capValue)
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    if level <= 1 then return normalValue * level end
    local span = math.max(under.IntensityMax - 1, 0.001)
    local extra = math.min((level - 1) / span, 1)
    return normalValue + (capValue - normalValue) * extra
end

function terrainState.Underwater.applyTint()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    local blend = under.Alpha * under.TintIntensity
    if blend <= 0.001 and not under.ColorEffect then return end
    under.ensureEffects()
    local active = blend > 0.001
    under.ColorEffect.TintColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1):Lerp(under.tintColor(), math.min(blend, 1))
    under.BlurEffect.Size = under.curve(blend, under.BlurMax, under.BlurCap)
    if under.ColorEffect.Enabled ~= active then under.ColorEffect.Enabled = active end
    if under.BlurEffect.Enabled ~= active then under.BlurEffect.Enabled = active end
end

function terrainState.Underwater.setMuffle(effect)
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    local level = under.Alpha * under.SoundIntensity
    effect.HighGain = -under.curve(level, under.MuffleHighDb, under.MuffleCapDb)
    effect.MidGain = -under.curve(level, under.MuffleMidDb, under.MuffleCapDb)
end

function terrainState.Underwater.addMuffle(sound)
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    if under.Effects[sound] or sound == under.AmbientSound then return end
    local ok, effect = pcall(function()
        return mk("EqualizerSoundEffect", {Name = under.MuffleName, Parent = sound})
    end)
    if ok and effect then
        under.Effects[sound] = effect
        under.setMuffle(effect)
    end
end

function terrainState.Underwater.soundRoots()
    local roots = {workspace, SoundService}
    local gui = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if gui then roots[#roots + 1] = gui end
    return roots
end

function terrainState.Underwater.muffleOn()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    if under.SoundActive then return end
    under.SoundActive = true
    under.AppliedLevel = -1
    for _, root in ipairs(under.soundRoots()) do
        for _, item in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            if item:IsA("Sound") then under.addMuffle(item) end
        end
        under.SoundConns[#under.SoundConns + 1] = root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(item)
            if item:IsA("Sound") then under.addMuffle(item) end
        end)
    end
end

function terrainState.Underwater.muffleOff()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    for _, connection in ipairs(under.SoundConns) do connection:Disconnect() end
    under.SoundConns = {}
    for _, effect in pairs(under.Effects) do
        pcall(function() effect:Destroy() end)
    end
    under.Effects = {}
    under.SoundActive = false
    under.AppliedLevel = -1
end

function terrainState.Underwater.updateMuffle()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    local level = under.Alpha * under.SoundIntensity
    if math.abs(level - under.AppliedLevel) < 0.001 then return end
    under.AppliedLevel = level
    for sound, effect in pairs(under.Effects) do
        if effect.Parent then
            under.setMuffle(effect)
        else
            under.Effects[sound] = nil
        end
    end
end

function terrainState.Underwater.applySound()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    if under.Sound and (under.Inside or under.Alpha > 0.001) then
        if not under.SoundActive then under.muffleOn() end
        under.updateMuffle()
    elseif under.SoundActive then
        under.muffleOff()
    end
end

function terrainState.loopSound(name, id)
    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.Name = name
    sound.SoundId = id
    sound.Looped = true
    sound.Volume = 0
    return sound
end

function terrainState.Underwater.ensureAmbient()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    local sound = under.AmbientSound
    if sound and sound.Parent then return sound end
    if sound then pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end) end
    sound = terrainState.loopSound(under.AmbientName, under.AmbientId)
    under.AmbientSound = sound
    sound.Parent = SoundService
    return sound
end

function terrainState.Underwater.destroyAmbient()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    local sound = under.AmbientSound
    under.AmbientSound = nil
    if sound then pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end) end
end

function terrainState.Underwater.applyAmbient()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    if under.AmbientOn and (under.Inside or under.Alpha > 0.001) then
        local sound = under.ensureAmbient()
        sound.Volume = under.AmbientVolume * under.Alpha
        if not sound.IsPlaying then sound:Play() end
    elseif under.AmbientSound and under.AmbientSound.IsPlaying then
        under.AmbientSound:Stop()
    end
end

function terrainState.Shore.origin()
    local character = player.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if root then return root.Position end
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    return camera and camera.CFrame.Position
end

function terrainState.Shore.nearest(grid, low, origin)
    local shore = terrainState.Shore
    local size = shore.VoxelSize
    local best = math.huge
    for ix = 1, #grid do
        local plane = grid[ix]
        local dx = low.X + (ix - 0.5) * size - origin.X
        for iy = 1, #plane do
            local column = plane[iy]
            local dy = low.Y + (iy - 0.5) * size - origin.Y
            for iz = 1, #column do
                if column[iz] > shore.Threshold then
                    local dz = low.Z + (iz - 0.5) * size - origin.Z
                    local distance = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
                    if distance < best then best = distance end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

function terrainState.Shore.start(origin)
    local shore = terrainState.Shore
    local size = shore.VoxelSize
    shore.Scan = {
        origin = origin,
        radius = shore.Radius,
        cursor = math.floor((origin.X - shore.Radius) / size) * size,
        stop = math.ceil((origin.X + shore.Radius) / size) * size,
        lowY = math.floor((origin.Y - shore.Down) / size) * size,
        highY = math.ceil((origin.Y + shore.Up) / size) * size,
        lowZ = math.floor((origin.Z - shore.Radius) / size) * size,
        highZ = math.ceil((origin.Z + shore.Radius) / size) * size,
        best = math.huge,
    }
end

function terrainState.Shore.advance()
    local shore = terrainState.Shore
    local scan = shore.Scan
    local size = shore.VoxelSize
    local from = scan.cursor
    local to = math.min(from + shore.SliceVoxels * size, scan.stop)
    local low = Vector3.new(from, scan.lowY, scan.lowZ)
    local high = Vector3.new(to, scan.highY, scan.highZ)
    local ok, data = pcall(function()
        return terrainState.Terrain:ReadVoxelChannels(Region3.new(low, high), size, {"LiquidOccupancy"})
    end)
    if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" or type(data.LiquidOccupancy) ~= "table" then
        shore.Scan = nil
        shore.Target = 0
        return
    end
    local best = shore.nearest(data.LiquidOccupancy, low, scan.origin)
    if best < scan.best then scan.best = best end
    scan.cursor = to
    if to >= scan.stop then
        shore.Scan = nil
        shore.Target = scan.best == math.huge and 0 or 1 - math.clamp(scan.best / scan.radius, 0, 1)
    end
end

function terrainState.Shore.ensure()
    local shore = terrainState.Shore
    local sound = shore.Sound
    if sound and sound.Parent then return sound end
    if sound then pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end) end
    sound = terrainState.loopSound(shore.SoundName, shore.SoundId)
    shore.Sound = sound
    sound.Parent = SoundService
    return sound
end

function terrainState.Shore.destroy()
    local shore = terrainState.Shore
    local sound = shore.Sound
    shore.Sound = nil
    shore.Scan = nil
    shore.Target = 0
    shore.Level = 0
    if sound then pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end) end
end

function terrainState.Shore.step(dt)
    local shore = terrainState.Shore
    local inside = terrainState.Underwater.Inside
    if shore.Scan then
        shore.advance()
    else
        shore.Elapsed = shore.Elapsed + dt
        if shore.Elapsed >= shore.CheckInterval then
            shore.Elapsed = 0
            local origin = shore.origin()
            if origin and not inside then
                shore.start(origin)
                shore.advance()
            else
                shore.Target = 0
            end
        end
    end
    local target = inside and 0 or shore.Target
    shore.Level = shore.Level + (target - shore.Level) * math.min(1, dt * shore.FadeSpeed)
    if math.abs(target - shore.Level) < 0.002 then shore.Level = target end
    if shore.Level > 0.001 then
        local sound = shore.ensure()
        sound.Volume = shore.Volume * shore.Level ^ shore.Curve
        if not sound.IsPlaying then sound:Play() end
    elseif shore.Sound and shore.Sound.IsPlaying then
        shore.Sound:Stop()
    end
end

function terrainState.Underwater.step(dt)
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    under.Elapsed = under.Elapsed + dt
    if under.Elapsed >= under.CheckInterval then
        under.Elapsed = 0
        under.Inside = under.sample()
    end
    local target = under.Inside and 1 or 0
    under.Alpha = under.Alpha + (target - under.Alpha) * math.min(1, dt * under.FadeSpeed)
    if math.abs(target - under.Alpha) < 0.002 then under.Alpha = target end
    if under.Tint then under.applyTint() end
    under.applySound()
    under.applyAmbient()
    if terrainState.Shore.Enabled then terrainState.Shore.step(dt) end
end

function terrainState.Underwater.refresh()
    local under = terrainState.Underwater
    local shore = terrainState.Shore
    local needed = terrainState.Water.Enabled and (under.Tint or under.Sound or under.AmbientOn or shore.Enabled)
    if needed then
        if not under.Conn then
            under.Elapsed = under.CheckInterval
            shore.Elapsed = shore.CheckInterval
            under.Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(under.step)
        end
        if not under.Tint then under.removeEffects() end
        if not under.Sound then under.muffleOff() end
        if not under.AmbientOn then under.destroyAmbient() end
        if not shore.Enabled then shore.destroy() end
    else
        if under.Conn then under.Conn:Disconnect(); under.Conn = nil end
        under.removeEffects()
        under.muffleOff()
        under.destroyAmbient()
        shore.destroy()
        under.Inside = false
        under.Alpha = 0
    end
end

function terrainState.addPill(parent, label, get, set)
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, 0),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = label, TextSize = 13,
        TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
    local pill = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 22),
        BackgroundColor3 = get() and COL.pinkOn or COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = pill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)})
    mk("UIStroke", {Parent = pill, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.fromRGB(48, 42, 64),
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
    local thumb = mk("Frame", {Parent = pill, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = get() and UDim2.new(1, -11, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 11, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = thumb, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)})
    local function refresh()
        TweenService:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            BackgroundColor3 = get() and COL.pinkOn or COL.off}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(thumb, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            Position = get() and UDim2.new(1, -11, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 11, 0.5, 0)}):Play()
    end
    toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(pill, "toggle")
    pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        set(not get())
        refresh()
    end)
    return row, refresh
end
