    end, Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 150), true)
    addSlider(Body, "Конец бликов", indoor.MinDistance, indoor.MaxDistance, indoor.Near, 0, function(v)
        indoor.Near = math.floor(v + 0.5)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(255, 195, 140), true)
    addSlider(Body, "Размер по базе", indoor.MinSize, indoor.MaxSize, indoor.Size, 1, function(v)
        indoor.Size = math.floor(v * 10 + 0.5) / 10
    end, Color3.fromRGB(255, 185, 130), true)
    addSlider(Body, "Яркость", indoor.MinBrightness, indoor.MaxBrightness, indoor.Brightness, 1, function(v)
        indoor.Brightness = math.floor(v * 10 + 0.5) / 10
    end, Color3.fromRGB(255, 175, 120), true)
end, 520, "💡")
terrainState.Glare.Row.LayoutOrder = 1
for _, row in ipairs({ToggleSetters.ccRow, ToggleSetters.blurRow, ToggleSetters.distBlurRow}) do
    row.LayoutOrder = 2
end

addToggleRow(tabPages["terrain"], "Цвет террейна", true, function(enabled)
    terrainState.Materials.apply(enabled)
end, function(Body)
    local materials = terrainState.Materials
    if #materials.List == 0 then return end
    local rows = math.ceil(#materials.List / materials.Columns)
    local chips = {}
    local picker = nil

    local title = addPanelHeader(Body, "")
    title.LayoutOrder = 1

    local function showPicker()
        local index = materials.Selected
        local entry = materials.List[index]
        title.Text = "ЦВЕТ · " .. entry.Label
        if picker then picker:Destroy() end
        picker = addColorPicker(Body, entry.Label, entry.Color, function(color)
            entry.Color = color
            chips[index].swatch.BackgroundColor3 = color
        end)
        picker.LayoutOrder = 2
    end

    local header = addPanelHeader(Body, "МАТЕРИАЛЫ")
    header.LayoutOrder = 3

    local inset = materials.StrokeInset
    local gridHeight = rows * materials.ChipHeight + (rows - 1) * materials.ChipGap
    local gridWrap = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, LayoutOrder = 4, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, gridHeight + inset * 2)})
    local grid = mk("Frame", {Parent = gridWrap, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, inset, 0, inset), Size = UDim2.new(1, -inset * 2, 1, -inset * 2)})
    mk("UIGridLayout", {Parent = grid, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        CellSize = UDim2.new(1 / materials.Columns, -6, 0, materials.ChipHeight),
        CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, materials.ChipGap)})

    for index, entry in ipairs(materials.List) do
        local chip = mk("TextButton", {Parent = grid, LayoutOrder = index, BackgroundColor3 = COL.row,
            BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, Text = ""})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = chip, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)})
        local stroke = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = chip, Thickness = 1.5, Color = COL.pinkOn,
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
            Transparency = index == materials.Selected and 0 or 1})
        local swatch = mk("Frame", {Parent = chip, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10),
            BackgroundColor3 = entry.Color, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = swatch, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)})
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = chip, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 23, 0, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -27, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = entry.Label, TextSize = 10,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
        chips[index] = {stroke = stroke, swatch = swatch}
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(chip, "select")
        chip.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            materials.Selected = index
            for chipIndex, chipInfo in ipairs(chips) do
                chipInfo.stroke.Transparency = chipIndex == index and 0 or 1
            end
            showPicker()
        end)
    end

    showPicker()
end, 480, "🎨")

addToggleRow(tabPages["terrain"], "Стиль материалов", true, function(enabled)
    terrainState.Pack.apply(enabled)
end, function(Body)
    local pack = terrainState.Pack
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ВАРИАЦИИ")
    local methodsWrap = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,28),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = methodsWrap, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local indicator = mk("Frame", {Parent = methodsWrap,
        Position = pack.Choice==1 and UDim2.new(0,2,0,2) or UDim2.new(0.5,2,0,2),
        Size = UDim2.new(0.5,-4,1,-4), BackgroundColor3 = COL.pinkOn, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = indicator, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local opt1 = mk("TextButton", {Parent = methodsWrap, Size = UDim2.new(0.5,0,1,0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = pack.Labels[1], Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
        TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = pack.Choice==1 and COL.text or COL.textMuted,
        AutoButtonColor = false})
    local opt2 = mk("TextButton", {Parent = methodsWrap, Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0,0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0.5,0,1,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = pack.Labels[2],
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 10,
        TextColor3 = pack.Choice==2 and COL.text or COL.textMuted, AutoButtonColor = false})
    local function setChoice(v)
        pack.set(v)
        TweenService:Create(indicator, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
            Position = v==1 and UDim2.new(0,2,0,2) or UDim2.new(0.5,2,0,2)}):Play()
        opt1.TextColor3 = v==1 and COL.text or COL.textMuted
        opt2.TextColor3 = v==2 and COL.text or COL.textMuted
    end
    toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(opt1, "select")
    toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(opt2, "select")
    opt1.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setChoice(1) end)
    opt2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setChoice(2) end)
end, 120, "🧱")

addSectionHeader(tabPages["terrain"], "МАТЕРИАЛЫ")

function terrainState.Fire.rise()
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    return math.max(fire.Rise, fire.Height / fire.MaxLife)
end

function terrainState.Fire.life(kind)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local config = fire.Config[kind]
    if not config.Plume then return config.LifeMin, config.LifeMax end
    local top = fire.Height / fire.rise()
    return top * config.LifeMin / config.LifeMax, top
end

function terrainState.Fire.speed(kind, scale)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local config = fire.Config[kind]
    if config.Plume then
        local value = fire.rise()
        return value, value
    end
    local factor = math.clamp(scale, 0.7, 2)
    return config.SpeedMin * factor, config.SpeedMax * factor
end

function terrainState.Fire.push(kind)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local config = fire.Config[kind]
    if not config.Plume then return config.Acceleration end
    if fire.Lean <= 0 then return Vector3.zero end
    local _, top = fire.life(kind)
    local strength = 2 * math.tan(math.rad(fire.Lean)) * fire.Height / (top * top)
    local heading = math.rad(fire.Direction)
    return Vector3.new(math.cos(heading), 0, math.sin(heading)) * strength
end

function terrainState.Fire.shape(emitter, kind, scale)
    local config = terrainState.Fire.Config[kind]
    if config.SizeKeys then
        local stretch = 1
        if config.Plume then
            local _, top = terrainState.Fire.life(kind)
            stretch = math.max(top / config.LifeMax, 1)
        end
        local keys = {}
        for index, key in ipairs(config.SizeKeys) do
            keys[index] = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(key[1] / stretch, key[2] * scale)
        end
        if stretch > 1 then
            keys[#keys + 1] = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, config.SizeKeys[#config.SizeKeys][2] * scale)
        end
        emitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(keys)
    else
        emitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(config.SizeStart * scale, config.SizeEnd * scale)
    end
    emitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(terrainState.Fire.speed(kind, scale))
end

function terrainState.Fire.rate(kind, scale, cover)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local config = fire.Config[kind]
    local value = (config.Rate + config.RatePerScale * scale) * fire.Intensity[kind] * cover
    return math.min(value, fire.MaxRate)
end

function terrainState.Fire.face(part)
    local frame = part.CFrame
    local size = part.Size
    local up, right, look = frame.UpVector.Y, frame.RightVector.Y, frame.LookVector.Y
    local absUp, absRight, absLook = math.abs(up), math.abs(right), math.abs(look)
    if absUp >= absRight and absUp >= absLook then
        return up >= 0 and Enum.NormalId.Top or Enum.NormalId.Bottom, size.X * size.Z
    elseif absRight >= absLook then
        return right >= 0 and Enum.NormalId.Right or Enum.NormalId.Left, size.Y * size.Z
    end
    return look >= 0 and Enum.NormalId.Front or Enum.NormalId.Back, size.X * size.Y
end

function terrainState.Fire.hides(record)
    if record.Kind == "Smoke" then return true end
    return terrainState.Fire.Flames
end

function terrainState.Fire.fresh(kind)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local saved = fire.Fresh[kind]
    if saved then return saved end
    local probe = Instance.new(kind)
    saved = {Color = probe.Color}
    if kind == "Fire" then saved.SecondaryColor = probe.SecondaryColor end
    probe:Destroy()
    fire.Fresh[kind] = saved
    return saved
end

function terrainState.Fire.custom(record)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local source = record.Source
    local fresh = fire.fresh(record.Kind)
    if source.Color ~= fresh.Color then return true end
    return record.Kind == "Fire" and source.SecondaryColor ~= fresh.SecondaryColor
end

function terrainState.Fire.palette(kind, record)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local config = fire.Config[kind]
    if not fire.custom(record) then return config.Colors end
    local source = record.Source
    local colors = {}
    for index, shade in ipairs(config.Shades) do
        local color = source[shade[1]]
        if shade[2] > 0 then
            color = color:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), shade[2])
        elseif shade[2] < 0 then
            color = color:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), -shade[2])
        end
        colors[index] = color
    end
    return colors
end

function terrainState.Fire.same(first, second)
    if first == second then return true end
    if not first or not second or #first ~= #second then return false end
    for index = 1, #first do
        if first[index] ~= second[index] then return false end
    end
    return true
end

function terrainState.Fire.sequence(colors)
    local keys = {}
    for index, color in ipairs(colors) do
        keys[index] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new((index - 1) / (#colors - 1), color)
    end
    return ColorSequence.new(keys)
end

function terrainState.Fire.build(kind, parent, scale, colors)
    local config = terrainState.Fire.Config[kind]
    local emitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
    emitter.Name = config.Name
    if config.Texture then emitter.Texture = config.Texture end
    emitter.Color = terrainState.Fire.sequence(colors)
    local alphas = {}
    if config.Flicker then
        for index, key in ipairs(terrainState.Fire.FlickerKeys) do
            alphas[index] = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(key[1], key[2])
        end
    else
        for index, value in ipairs(config.Transparency) do
            alphas[index] = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new((index - 1) / (#config.Transparency - 1), value)
        end
    end
    emitter.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(alphas)
    emitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(terrainState.Fire.life(kind))
    emitter.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(config.Spread, config.Spread)
    emitter.Acceleration = terrainState.Fire.push(kind)
    emitter.Drag = config.Drag
    emitter.WindAffectsDrag = true
    emitter.LightEmission = config.LightEmission
    emitter.LightInfluence = config.LightInfluence
    local rotation = config.Rotation or {0, 360}
    emitter.Rotation = NumberRange.new(rotation[1], rotation[2])
    emitter.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(config.RotMin, config.RotMax)
    emitter.ZOffset = config.ZOffset or 0
    terrainState.Fire.shape(emitter, kind, scale)
    emitter.Rate = 0
    emitter.Parent = parent
    return emitter
end

function terrainState.Fire.locate(source)
    local parent = source.Parent
    if not parent then return nil end
    if parent:IsA("BasePart") then return parent, parent.Position end
    if parent:IsA("Attachment") and parent.Parent and parent.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
        return parent.Parent, parent.WorldPosition
    end
    return nil
end

function terrainState.Fire.alive(record)
    if record.BaseEnabled ~= nil then return record.BaseEnabled end
    return record.Source.Enabled
end

function terrainState.Fire.mute(record)
    local source = record.Source
    if not source.Enabled then return end
    record.Muting = true
    source.Enabled = false
    record.Muting = false
end

function terrainState.Fire.hideFlame(record, hide)
    local source = record.Source
    if hide then
        if record.BaseEnabled == nil then
            record.BaseEnabled = source.Enabled
            record.EnabledConn = source:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
                if record.Muting or not source.Enabled then return end
                record.BaseEnabled = true
                pcall(terrainState.Fire.mute, record)
            end)
        end
        terrainState.Fire.mute(record)
    elseif record.BaseEnabled ~= nil then
        local saved = record.BaseEnabled
        record.BaseEnabled = nil
        if record.EnabledConn then
            record.EnabledConn:Disconnect()
            record.EnabledConn = nil
        end
        pcall(function() source.Enabled = saved end)
    end
end

function terrainState.Fire.hideBase(record, hide)
    if record.Kind == "Fire" then
        terrainState.Fire.hideFlame(record, hide)
        return
    end
    local source = record.Source
    if hide then
        if record.BaseOpacity == nil then record.BaseOpacity = source.Opacity end
        if source.Opacity ~= 0 then source.Opacity = 0 end
    elseif record.BaseOpacity ~= nil then
        local saved = record.BaseOpacity
        record.BaseOpacity = nil
        pcall(function() source.Opacity = saved end)
    end
end

function terrainState.Fire.free(record)
    for kind, emitter in pairs(record.Emitters) do
        record.Emitters[kind] = nil
        pcall(function() emitter:Destroy() end)
    end
    if next(record.Scales) then record.Scales = {} end
    if next(record.Palettes) then record.Palettes = {} end
    if next(record.Rates) then record.Rates = {} end
    if next(record.Carry) then record.Carry = {} end
    if record.Anchor then
        local anchor = record.Anchor
        record.Anchor = nil
        pcall(function() anchor:Destroy() end)
    end
end

function terrainState.Fire.release(record)
    terrainState.Fire.free(record)
    terrainState.Fire.hideBase(record, false)
end

function terrainState.Fire.anchor(record, part, position)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local anchor = record.Anchor
    if anchor and anchor.Parent == part then return anchor end
    if anchor then pcall(function() anchor:Destroy() end) end
    anchor = Instance.new("Attachment")
    anchor.Name = fire.AnchorName
    anchor.Parent = part
    anchor.WorldCFrame = CFrame.new(position)
    record.Anchor = anchor
    return anchor
end

function terrainState.Fire.forget(source)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local record = fire.Sources[source]
    if not record then return end
    fire.Sources[source] = nil
    fire.release(record)
    fire.silence(record)
end

function terrainState.Fire.silence(record)
    if record.Sound then
        local sound = record.Sound
        record.Sound = nil
        pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end)
    end
end

function terrainState.Fire.sound(record)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local source = record.Source
    local id = source.Size <= fire.SoundSplit and fire.SoundSmall or fire.SoundLarge
    local sound = record.Sound
    if sound and (sound.Parent ~= source.Parent or record.SoundIdUsed ~= id) then
        fire.silence(record)
        sound = nil
    end
    if not sound then
        sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        sound.Name = fire.SoundName
        sound.SoundId = id
        sound.Looped = true
        sound.RollOffMode = fire.SoundRollOff
        sound.RollOffMinDistance = fire.SoundMinDistance
        sound.Parent = source.Parent
        record.Sound = sound
        record.SoundIdUsed = id
        record.SoundSeeded = false
        record.SoundBorn = os.clock()
    end
    sound.RollOffMaxDistance = fire.SoundRange
    sound.Volume = fire.SoundVolume
    if fire.alive(record) then
        if not record.SoundSeeded then
            if sound.IsLoaded and sound.TimeLength > 0 then
                sound.TimePosition = math.random() * sound.TimeLength
                record.SoundSeeded = true
                sound:Play()
            elseif os.clock() - record.SoundBorn > fire.SeedWait then
                record.SoundSeeded = true
                sound:Play()
            end
        elseif not sound.IsPlaying then
            sound:Play()
        end
    else
        if sound.IsPlaying then sound:Stop() end
        record.SoundSeeded = false
        record.SoundBorn = os.clock()
    end
end

function terrainState.Fire.remember(record, position)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local cell = fire.StreamCell
    local key = record.Kind .. math.floor(position.X / cell) .. ":" .. math.floor(position.Y / cell) .. ":" .. math.floor(position.Z / cell)
    local entry = fire.Known[key]
    if not entry then
        entry = {Kind = record.Kind, Last = 0}
        fire.Known[key] = entry
    end
    entry.Position = position
    entry.Alive = true
end

function terrainState.Fire.prune(origin)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    if not origin then return end
    for key, entry in pairs(fire.Known) do
        if not entry.Alive and (entry.Position - origin).Magnitude < fire.StreamNear then
            fire.Known[key] = nil
        end
    end
end

function terrainState.Fire.stream(origin)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    if fire.StreamBusy or not origin then return end
    local now = os.clock()
    local pending = {}
    for _, entry in pairs(fire.Known) do
        if not entry.Alive and now - entry.Last >= fire.StreamInterval
            and (entry.Position - origin).Magnitude <= fire.StreamRange then
            pending[#pending + 1] = entry
        end
    end
    if #pending == 0 then return end
    table.sort(pending, function(first, second) return first.Last < second.Last end)
    fire.StreamBusy = true
    task.spawn(function()
        for index = 1, math.min(#pending, fire.StreamLimit) do
            local entry = pending[index]
            entry.Last = os.clock()
            pcall(function() player:RequestStreamAroundAsync(entry.Position, fire.StreamTimeout) end)
        end
        fire.StreamBusy = false
    end)
end

function terrainState.Fire.register(item)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    if fire.Sources[item] then return end
    local kind = nil
    if item:IsA("Fire") then
        kind = "Fire"
    elseif item:IsA("Smoke") then
        kind = "Smoke"
    end
    if kind and fire.Active[kind] then
        local record = {Source = item, Kind = kind, Emitters = {}, Scales = {}, Palettes = {}, Rates = {}, Carry = {}}
        fire.Sources[item] = record
        pcall(fire.hideBase, record, fire.hides(record))
    end
end

function terrainState.Fire.sync(record, part, position)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local source = record.Source
    local wanted = {}
    for _, kind in ipairs(fire.Kinds[record.Kind]) do
        local flag = fire.Flags[kind]
        if not flag or fire[flag] then wanted[#wanted + 1] = kind end
    end
    if #wanted == 0 then
        fire.release(record)
        return
    end
    local covers = source.Parent == part
    local face, area = Enum.NormalId.Top, 0
    if covers then face, area = fire.face(part) end
    local scale = math.clamp(source.Size / fire.SizeRef, fire.MinScale, fire.MaxScale)
    local used = {}
    local alive = fire.alive(record)
    for _, kind in ipairs(wanted) do
        used[kind] = true
        local config = fire.Config[kind]
        local spread = covers and config.Cover == true
        local parent = part
        if not spread then parent = fire.anchor(record, part, position) end
        local power = 1
        if spread then power = math.clamp(area / fire.CoverRef, 1, config.CoverMax or fire.CoverMax) end
        local emitter = record.Emitters[kind]
        if emitter and emitter.Parent ~= parent then
            record.Emitters[kind] = nil
            pcall(function() emitter:Destroy() end)
            emitter = nil
        end
        local colors = fire.palette(kind, record)
        if not emitter then
            emitter = fire.build(kind, parent, scale, colors)
            record.Emitters[kind] = emitter
            record.Scales[kind] = scale
            record.Palettes[kind] = colors
        elseif math.abs(scale / record.Scales[kind] - 1) > 0.05 then
            fire.shape(emitter, kind, scale)
            record.Scales[kind] = scale
        end
        if not fire.same(record.Palettes[kind], colors) then
            emitter.Color = fire.sequence(colors)
            record.Palettes[kind] = colors
        end
        if config.Plume then
            local lifeLow, lifeHigh = fire.life(kind)
            if math.abs(emitter.Lifetime.Max - lifeHigh) > 0.01 then
                emitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(lifeLow, lifeHigh)
                fire.shape(emitter, kind, scale)
                record.Scales[kind] = scale
            end
            local low, high = fire.speed(kind, scale)
            if math.abs(emitter.Speed.Min - low) > 0.01 then emitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(low, high) end
            local push = fire.push(kind)
            if (emitter.Acceleration - push).Magnitude > 0.001 then emitter.Acceleration = push end
        end
        if spread and emitter.EmissionDirection ~= face then emitter.EmissionDirection = face end
        record.Rates[kind] = fire.rate(kind, scale, power)
        if emitter.Enabled ~= alive then emitter.Enabled = alive end
    end
    for kind, emitter in pairs(record.Emitters) do
        if not used[kind] then
            record.Emitters[kind] = nil
            record.Scales[kind] = nil
            record.Palettes[kind] = nil
            record.Rates[kind] = nil
            record.Carry[kind] = nil
            pcall(function() emitter:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    fire.hideBase(record, fire.hides(record))
end

function terrainState.Fire.tick()
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local eye = camera and camera.CFrame.Position
    local character = player.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local body = root and root.Position
    local streaming = workspace.StreamingEnabled
    if streaming then
        for _, entry in pairs(fire.Known) do entry.Alive = false end
    end
    for source, record in pairs(fire.Sources) do
        local part, position = fire.locate(source)
        if not part or not source:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
            fire.forget(source)
        else
            if streaming then fire.remember(record, position) end
            pcall(fire.sync, record, part, position)
            local near = eye ~= nil and (position - eye).Magnitude <= fire.SoundRange
            if fire.SoundOn and record.Kind == "Fire" and near then
                pcall(fire.sound, record)
            else
                fire.silence(record)
            end
        end
    end
    if streaming then
        fire.prune(body)
        fire.stream(body or eye)
    end
end

function terrainState.Fire.emit(dt)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    local span = math.min(dt, fire.EmitSpan)
    for _, record in pairs(fire.Sources) do
        for kind, emitter in pairs(record.Emitters) do
            local rate = record.Rates[kind]
            if rate and emitter.Enabled and emitter.Parent then
                local owed = (record.Carry[kind] or 0) + rate * span
                local count = math.floor(owed)
                record.Carry[kind] = owed - count
                if count > 0 then emitter:Emit(count) end
            end
        end
    end
end

function terrainState.Fire.step(dt)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    fire.emit(dt)
    fire.Elapsed = fire.Elapsed + dt
    if fire.Elapsed < fire.TickInterval then return end
    fire.Elapsed = 0
    fire.tick()
end

function terrainState.Fire.apply(kind, enabled)
    local fire = terrainState.Fire
    fire.Active[kind] = enabled
    if not enabled then
        for source, record in pairs(fire.Sources) do
            if record.Kind == kind then fire.forget(source) end
        end
        for key, entry in pairs(fire.Known) do
            if entry.Kind == kind then fire.Known[key] = nil end
        end
    end
    local running = fire.Active.Fire or fire.Active.Smoke
    fire.Enabled = running
    if not running then
        if fire.Conn then fire.Conn:Disconnect(); fire.Conn = nil end
        if fire.AddedConn then fire.AddedConn:Disconnect(); fire.AddedConn = nil end
        return
    end
    if not fire.Conn then
        fire.AddedConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(fire.register)
        fire.Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(fire.step)
    end
    if enabled then
        for _, item in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do fire.register(item) end
    end
    fire.Elapsed = fire.TickInterval
end

function terrainState.Glare.kind(item)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    if item:IsA("Light") then return "Lamp" end
    if glare.Active.Neon and item:IsA("BasePart") and item.Material == Enum.Material.Neon then
        return "Neon"
    end
    return nil
end

function terrainState.Glare.excluded(item)
    local host = item.Parent
    return host ~= nil and host.Name == moonState.EmitterName
end

function terrainState.Glare.invisible(host)
    local part = host:IsA("Attachment") and host.Parent or host
    return part ~= nil and part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Transparency >= 1
end

function terrainState.Glare.register(item)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    if glare.Sources[item] or glare.excluded(item) then return end
    local kind = glare.kind(item)
    if kind and glare.Active[kind] then
        glare.Sources[item] = {Source = item, Kind = kind, Checked = 0}
    end
end

function terrainState.Glare.locate(record)
    local host = record.Source
    if record.Kind == "Lamp" then host = host.Parent end
    if not host then return nil end
    if host:IsA("BasePart") then return host, host.Position end
    if host:IsA("Attachment") then return host, host.WorldPosition end
    return nil
end

function terrainState.Glare.shines(record)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    local source = record.Source
    if record.Kind == "Lamp" then
        return source.Enabled and source.Brightness > 0
    end
    local size = source.Size
    return source.Material == Enum.Material.Neon
        and source.Transparency < glare.NeonOpacity
        and math.max(size.X, size.Y, size.Z) <= glare.NeonMaxSize
end

function terrainState.Glare.owned(host)
    local character = player.Character
    return character ~= nil and host:IsDescendantOf(character)
end

function terrainState.Glare.build(host, color)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    local gui = mk("BillboardGui", {Name = glare.Name, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), AlwaysOnTop = false,
        LightInfluence = 0, Brightness = glare.Brightness, MaxDistance = math.huge, Active = false})
    local frame = mk("Frame", {Parent = gui, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0})
    local labels = {}
    for index, layer in ipairs(glare.Layers) do
        labels[index] = mk("ImageLabel", {Parent = frame, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(layer.Width, layer.Height),
            Rotation = layer.Rotation, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Image = glare.Image, ImageColor3 = color, ImageTransparency = layer.Transparency})
    end
    gui.Parent = host
    return gui, labels
end

function terrainState.Glare.hide(record)
    local gui = record.Gui
    if gui then
        record.Gui = nil
        record.Labels = nil
        record.Pixels = nil
        pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
    end
    local beam = record.Beam
    if beam then
        local pieces = {beam, record.Top, record.Bottom}
        record.Beam = nil
        record.Top = nil
        record.Bottom = nil
        record.Scale = nil
        record.Fade = nil
        record.Tint = nil
        record.Power = nil
        for _, piece in ipairs(pieces) do
            pcall(function() piece:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
end

function terrainState.Glare.show(record, host, color, pixels)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    if record.Beam then glare.hide(record) end
    if record.Gui and record.Gui.Parent ~= host then glare.hide(record) end
    if not record.Gui then
        record.Gui, record.Labels = glare.build(host, color)
        record.Color = color
    end
    local gui = record.Gui
    if record.Color ~= color then
        record.Color = color
        for _, label in ipairs(record.Labels) do label.ImageColor3 = color end
    end
    if gui.Brightness ~= glare.Brightness then gui.Brightness = glare.Brightness end
    if record.Pixels == nil or math.abs(record.Pixels - pixels) >= 1 then
        record.Pixels = pixels
        gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(pixels, pixels)
    end
end

function terrainState.Glare.buildBeam(part, color)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    local indoor = glare.Indoor
    local top = mk("Attachment", {Name = glare.Name, Visible = false, Parent = part})
    local bottom = mk("Attachment", {Name = glare.Name, Visible = false, Parent = part})
    local beam = mk("Beam", {Name = glare.Name, Attachment0 = top, Attachment1 = bottom,
        Texture = indoor.Texture, TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Stretch, TextureLength = 1,
        TextureSpeed = 0, FaceCamera = true, LightEmission = indoor.LightEmission, LightInfluence = 0,
        Segments = 1, CurveSize0 = 0, CurveSize1 = 0, ZOffset = indoor.ZOffset,
        Color = ColorSequence.new(color), Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1), Parent = part})
    return beam, top, bottom
end

function terrainState.Glare.glow(record, host, position, color, fade)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    local indoor = glare.Indoor
    local part = host:IsA("Attachment") and host.Parent or host
    if record.Gui then glare.hide(record) end
    if record.Beam and record.Beam.Parent ~= part then glare.hide(record) end
    if not record.Beam then
        record.Beam, record.Top, record.Bottom = glare.buildBeam(part, color)
        record.Tint = color
    end
    if record.Tint ~= color then
        record.Tint = color
        record.Beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(color)
    end
    local scale = indoor.Size * glare.reach(record)
    if record.Scale == nil or math.abs(record.Scale - scale) >= 0.01 then
        record.Scale = scale
        record.Top.WorldPosition = position + Vector3.new(0, indoor.Up * scale, 0)
        record.Bottom.WorldPosition = position - Vector3.new(0, indoor.Down * scale, 0)
        record.Beam.Width0 = indoor.Width * scale
        record.Beam.Width1 = indoor.Width * scale
    end
    if record.Fade == nil or math.abs(record.Fade - fade) >= 0.02 then
        record.Fade = fade
        record.Beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1 - (1 - indoor.Transparency) * fade)
    end
    if record.Power ~= indoor.Brightness then
        record.Power = indoor.Brightness
        record.Beam.Brightness = indoor.Brightness
    end
end

function terrainState.Glare.forget(source)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    local record = glare.Sources[source]
    if not record then return end
    glare.Sources[source] = nil
    glare.hide(record)
end

function terrainState.Glare.solid(record, origin, direction)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    local host = record.Host
    local ignore = {host:IsA("Attachment") and host.Parent or host}
    local character = player.Character
    if character then ignore[#ignore + 1] = character end
    local params = glare.Params
    for _ = 1, glare.SkipLimit do
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
        local hit = workspace:Raycast(origin, direction, params)
        if not hit then return false end
        local object = hit.Instance
        if object:IsA("Terrain") then return true end
        local clear = object.Transparency >= glare.SeeThrough
        local fixture = object.Size.Magnitude <= glare.FixtureSize
            and (object.Position - record.Position).Magnitude <= glare.FixtureReach
        if not clear and not fixture then return true end
        ignore[#ignore + 1] = object
    end
    return false
end

function terrainState.Glare.blocked(record, origin)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    local offset = record.Position - origin
    local length = offset.Magnitude - glare.Margin
    if length <= 0 then return false end
    return glare.solid(record, origin, offset.Unit * length)
end

function terrainState.Glare.covered(record)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    return glare.solid(record, record.Position, Vector3.new(0, glare.Indoor.RoofReach, 0))
end

function terrainState.Glare.reach(record)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    if record.Kind ~= "Lamp" then return 1 end
    return math.clamp(record.Source.Range / glare.RangeRef, glare.MinReach, glare.MaxReach)
end

function terrainState.Glare.tick()
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    local indoor = glare.Indoor
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end
    local eye = camera.CFrame.Position
    local visible = {}
    for source, record in pairs(glare.Sources) do
        local host, position = glare.locate(record)
        if not host or not host:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
            glare.forget(source)
        else
            local distance = (position - eye).Magnitude
            record.Outer = glare.Street and distance > glare.Near and distance < glare.Far
            record.Inner = indoor.Enabled and distance >= indoor.Near and distance < indoor.Far
            if (record.Outer or record.Inner) and glare.shines(record) and not glare.owned(host)
                and (glare.Invisible or not glare.invisible(host)) then
                record.Host = host
                record.Position = position
                record.Distance = distance
                visible[#visible + 1] = record
            else
                glare.hide(record)
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(visible, function(first, second) return first.Distance < second.Distance end)
    local now = os.clock()
    local due = {}
    for _, record in ipairs(visible) do
        if record.Open == nil or now - record.Checked >= glare.Recheck then
            due[#due + 1] = record
        end
    end
    table.sort(due, function(first, second) return first.Checked < second.Checked end)
    for index = 1, math.min(#due, glare.MaxChecks) do
        local record = due[index]
        record.Covered = indoor.Enabled and glare.covered(record)
        record.Open = record.Covered or not glare.blocked(record, eye)
        record.Checked = now
    end
    local shown = 0
    local shownInside = 0
    for _, record in ipairs(visible) do
        if not record.Open then
            glare.hide(record)
        elseif record.Covered then
            if record.Inner and shownInside < indoor.MaxCount then
                shownInside = shownInside + 1
                local lower = 1
                if indoor.Near > indoor.MinDistance then
                    lower = (record.Distance - indoor.Near) / indoor.Ramp
                end
                local fade = math.clamp(math.min(lower, (indoor.Far - record.Distance) / indoor.Ramp), 0, 1)
                pcall(glare.glow, record, record.Host, record.Position, record.Source.Color, fade)
            else
                glare.hide(record)
            end
        elseif record.Outer and shown < glare.MaxCount then
            shown = shown + 1
            local grow = math.clamp(math.min(record.Distance - glare.Near, glare.Far - record.Distance) / glare.Grow, 0, 1)
            local pixels = math.floor(glare.Pixels * glare.Size * glare.reach(record) * grow + 0.5)
            pcall(glare.show, record, record.Host, record.Source.Color, pixels)
        else
            glare.hide(record)
        end
    end
end

function terrainState.Glare.step(dt)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    glare.Elapsed = glare.Elapsed + dt
    if glare.Elapsed < glare.TickInterval then return end
    glare.Elapsed = 0
    glare.tick()
end

function terrainState.Glare.scan()
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    glare.Token = glare.Token + 1
    local token = glare.Token
    task.spawn(function()
        local count = 0
        for _, item in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if token ~= glare.Token or not glare.Enabled then return end
            glare.register(item)
            count = count + 1
            if count % glare.ScanBatch == 0 then task.wait() end
        end
    end)
end

function terrainState.Glare.use(kind, enabled)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    glare.Active[kind] = enabled
    if not enabled then
        for source, record in pairs(glare.Sources) do
            if record.Kind == kind then glare.forget(source) end
        end
    elseif glare.Enabled then
        glare.scan()
    end
end

function terrainState.Glare.setIndoor(enabled)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    glare.Indoor.Enabled = enabled
    for _, record in pairs(glare.Sources) do
        record.Open = nil
        record.Covered = false
    end
end

function terrainState.Glare.apply(enabled)
    local glare = terrainState.Glare
    glare.Enabled = enabled
    glare.Token = glare.Token + 1
    if not enabled then
        for source in pairs(glare.Sources) do glare.forget(source) end
        if glare.Conn then glare.Conn:Disconnect(); glare.Conn = nil end
        if glare.AddedConn then glare.AddedConn:Disconnect(); glare.AddedConn = nil end
        return
    end
    if not glare.Params then
        glare.Params = RaycastParams.new()
        glare.Params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        glare.Params.IgnoreWater = true
    end
    if not glare.Conn then
        glare.AddedConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(glare.register)
        glare.Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(glare.step)
    end
    glare.Elapsed = glare.TickInterval
    glare.scan()
end

local weatherCycle = {
    Enabled = false,
    StartDelay = 0.3,
    TickSeconds = 60,
    RainChance = 0.20,
    SnowChance = 0.10,
    ThunderWithRainChance = 0.05,
    FogWithRainChance = 0.05,
    FogAloneChance = 0.10,
    PrecipMinutes = 3,
    PrecipExtendChance = 0.80,
    PauseMinutesMin = 5,
    PauseMinutesMax = 10,
    FogMinutes = 6,
    FogExtendChance = 0.50,
    ExtendChanceStep = 0.05,
    ExtendMinutes = 1,
    GustChance = 0.30,
    GustMinutesMin = 1,
    GustMinutesMax = 3,
    FogDensityMin = 5,
    FogDensityMax = 50,
    RainRadius = 15,
    RainIntensityMin = 1,
    RainIntensityMax = 5,
    RainSplashes = false,
    SnowRadius = 15,
    WindBaseSpeed = 5,
    WindBaseRate = 100,
    WindBoostSpeedMin = 15,
    WindBoostSpeedMax = 25,
    WindBoostRateMin = 600,
    WindBoostRateMax = 1500,
    WindLifetime = 2,
    WindDistance = 3000,
    WindPassThroughWalls = false,
    SoundEnabled = true,
    Labels = {"Туман", "Дождь", "Снег", "Молния", "Ветер"},
    Fields = {
        {rainState, {"GridRadius", "Intensity", "SplashesEnabled", "SoundEnabled"}},
        {snowState, {"GridRadius", "SoundEnabled"}},
        {lightningState, {"SoundEnabled"}},
        {windState, {"Strength", "Direction", "SpawnRate", "Lifetime", "MaxDistance", "PassThroughWalls", "SoundEnabled"}},
    },
    Token = 0,
    Saved = nil,
    Precip = false,
    PrecipLeft = 0,
    PrecipExtends = 0,
    PauseLeft = 0,
    Thunder = false,
    Fog = false,
    FogLeft = 0,
    FogExtends = 0,
    GustLeft = 0,
    Boosted = false,
}

function weatherCycle.switch(label, on)
    local registry = toggleRegistry[label]
    if registry and registry.isOn() ~= on then registry.setState(on) end
end

function weatherCycle.refreshPanels()
    for _, label in ipairs(weatherCycle.Labels) do
        local registry = toggleRegistry[label]
        if registry and registry.rebuild then registry.rebuild() end
    end
end

function weatherCycle.save()
    local saved = {Fog = newFogDensity, Values = {}, Toggles = {}}
    for index, entry in ipairs(weatherCycle.Fields) do
        local values = {}
        for _, key in ipairs(entry[2]) do values[key] = entry[1][key] end
        saved.Values[index] = values
    end
    for _, label in ipairs(weatherCycle.Labels) do
        local registry = toggleRegistry[label]
        saved.Toggles[label] = registry ~= nil and registry.isOn()
    end
    weatherCycle.Saved = saved
end

function weatherCycle.restore()
    local saved = weatherCycle.Saved
    weatherCycle.Saved = nil
    if not saved then return end
    for _, label in ipairs(weatherCycle.Labels) do
        weatherCycle.switch(label, false)
    end
    newFogDensity = saved.Fog
    for index, entry in ipairs(weatherCycle.Fields) do
        for key, value in pairs(saved.Values[index]) do entry[1][key] = value end
    end
    for _, label in ipairs(weatherCycle.Labels) do
        weatherCycle.switch(label, saved.Toggles[label])
    end
    weatherCycle.refreshPanels()
end

function weatherCycle.setup()
    local cycle = weatherCycle
    rainState.GridRadius = cycle.RainRadius
    rainState.SplashesEnabled = cycle.RainSplashes
    rainState.SoundEnabled = cycle.SoundEnabled
    snowState.GridRadius = cycle.SnowRadius
    snowState.SoundEnabled = cycle.SoundEnabled
    lightningState.SoundEnabled = cycle.SoundEnabled
    windState.Lifetime = cycle.WindLifetime
    windState.MaxDistance = cycle.WindDistance
    windState.PassThroughWalls = cycle.WindPassThroughWalls
    windState.SoundEnabled = cycle.SoundEnabled
end

function weatherCycle.tryExtend(baseChance, extends)
    local chance = baseChance - weatherCycle.ExtendChanceStep * extends
    return chance > 0 and math.random() < chance
end

function weatherCycle.startPrecip(kind)
    local cycle = weatherCycle
    cycle.Precip = kind
    cycle.PrecipLeft = cycle.PrecipMinutes
    cycle.PrecipExtends = 0
    cycle.Thunder = false
    if kind == "rain" then
        rainState.Intensity = math.random(cycle.RainIntensityMin, cycle.RainIntensityMax)
        for index, emitter in ipairs(rainState.Emitters) do
            if not rainState.RoofState[index] then
                emitter.Rate = rainState.rate(rainState.Plan[index])
            end
        end
    end
end

function weatherCycle.startFog()
    local cycle = weatherCycle
    cycle.Fog = true
    cycle.FogLeft = cycle.FogMinutes
    cycle.FogExtends = 0
    newFogDensity = math.random(cycle.FogDensityMin, cycle.FogDensityMax)
    for _, emitter in ipairs(newFogEmitters) do emitter.Rate = newFogDensity end
end

function weatherCycle.advance()
    local cycle = weatherCycle
    local ended = false

    if cycle.PauseLeft > 0 then cycle.PauseLeft = cycle.PauseLeft - 1 end

    if cycle.Precip then
        cycle.PrecipLeft = cycle.PrecipLeft - 1
        if cycle.PrecipLeft <= 0 then
            if cycle.tryExtend(cycle.PrecipExtendChance, cycle.PrecipExtends) then
                cycle.PrecipLeft = cycle.ExtendMinutes
                cycle.PrecipExtends = cycle.PrecipExtends + 1
            else
                cycle.Precip = false
                cycle.Thunder = false
                cycle.PauseLeft = math.random(cycle.PauseMinutesMin, cycle.PauseMinutesMax)
                ended = true
            end
        end
    end

    if cycle.Fog then
        cycle.FogLeft = cycle.FogLeft - 1
        if cycle.FogLeft <= 0 then
            if cycle.tryExtend(cycle.FogExtendChance, cycle.FogExtends) then
                cycle.FogLeft = cycle.ExtendMinutes
                cycle.FogExtends = cycle.FogExtends + 1
            else
                cycle.Fog = false
                ended = true
            end
        end
    end

    if cycle.GustLeft > 0 then
        cycle.GustLeft = cycle.GustLeft - 1
        if cycle.GustLeft <= 0 then ended = true end
    end

    return ended
end

function weatherCycle.roll()
    local cycle = weatherCycle
    local event = false

    if not cycle.Precip and cycle.PauseLeft <= 0 then
        local value = math.random()
        if value < cycle.RainChance then
            cycle.startPrecip("rain")
            event = true
        elseif value < cycle.RainChance + cycle.SnowChance then
            cycle.startPrecip("snow")
            event = true
        end
    end

    if cycle.Precip == "rain" then
        if not cycle.Thunder and math.random() < cycle.ThunderWithRainChance then
            cycle.Thunder = true
            event = true
        end
        if not cycle.Fog and math.random() < cycle.FogWithRainChance then
            cycle.startFog()
            event = true
        end
    elseif not cycle.Precip and not cycle.Fog then
        if math.random() < cycle.FogAloneChance then
            cycle.startFog()
            event = true
        end
    end

    if not cycle.Precip and not cycle.Fog and cycle.GustLeft <= 0 then
        if math.random() < cycle.GustChance then
            cycle.GustLeft = math.random(cycle.GustMinutesMin, cycle.GustMinutesMax)
            event = true
        end
    end

    return event
end

function weatherCycle.applyWeather()
    local cycle = weatherCycle
    cycle.switch("Дождь", cycle.Precip == "rain")
    cycle.switch("Снег", cycle.Precip == "snow")
    cycle.switch("Молния", cycle.Thunder)
    cycle.switch("Туман", cycle.Fog)
end

function weatherCycle.applyWind(event)
    local cycle = weatherCycle
    local boosted = cycle.Precip ~= false or cycle.Fog or cycle.GustLeft > 0
    if boosted then
        if event or not cycle.Boosted then
            windState.Strength = math.random(cycle.WindBoostSpeedMin, cycle.WindBoostSpeedMax)
            windState.SpawnRate = math.random(cycle.WindBoostRateMin, cycle.WindBoostRateMax)
            windState.Direction = math.random(0, 360)
        end
    else
        windState.Strength = cycle.WindBaseSpeed
        windState.SpawnRate = cycle.WindBaseRate
    end
    cycle.Boosted = boosted
    if windState.Enabled then workspace.GlobalWind = windDirVector() * windState.Strength end
    cycle.switch("Ветер", true)
end

function weatherCycle.tick()
    local cycle = weatherCycle
    if not cycle.Enabled then return end
    local ended = cycle.advance()
    local event = cycle.roll()
    cycle.applyWeather()
    cycle.applyWind(event)
    if event or ended then cycle.refreshPanels() end
end

function weatherCycle.schedule()
    local cycle = weatherCycle
    cycle.Token = cycle.Token + 1
    local token = cycle.Token
    task.delay(cycle.TickSeconds, function()
        if cycle.Enabled and cycle.Token == token then cycle.step() end
    end)
end

function weatherCycle.step()
    pcall(weatherCycle.tick)
    weatherCycle.schedule()
end

function weatherCycle.start()
    local cycle = weatherCycle
    cycle.save()
    for _, label in ipairs(cycle.Labels) do cycle.switch(label, false) end
    cycle.setup()
    cycle.Precip = false
    cycle.PrecipLeft = 0
    cycle.PrecipExtends = 0
    cycle.PauseLeft = 0
    cycle.Thunder = false
    cycle.Fog = false
    cycle.FogLeft = 0
    cycle.FogExtends = 0
    cycle.GustLeft = 0
    cycle.Boosted = false
    windState.Direction = math.random(0, 360)
    cycle.step()
    cycle.refreshPanels()
end

function weatherCycle.apply(enabled)
    local cycle = weatherCycle
    cycle.Enabled = enabled
    cycle.Token = cycle.Token + 1
    local token = cycle.Token
    if enabled then
        task.delay(cycle.StartDelay, function()
            if cycle.Enabled and cycle.Token == token then
                pcall(cycle.start)
            end
        end)
    else
        cycle.restore()
    end
end

function weatherCycle.buildRow()
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = tabPages["atmo"], Name = "Row_Цикл погоды", Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    mk("UIListLayout", {Parent = row, FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
        Padding = UDim.new(0,8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder})
    local tile = mk("Frame", {Parent = row, Size = UDim2.new(1/3,-16/3,1,0),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = 1})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = tile, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)})
    local clickZone = mk("TextButton", {Parent = tile, Size = UDim2.new(1,-46,1,0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = tile, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0,8,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,16,1,0),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = "🌦️", TextSize = 13,
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(33,30,42), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = tile, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0,28,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,-74,1,0),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = "Цикл погоды", TextSize = 11,
        TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, TextWrapped = true})
    local pill = mk("TextButton", {Parent = tile, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1,-8,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,34,0,18),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = pill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    mk("UIStroke", {Parent = pill, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.fromRGB(48,42,64),
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
    local thumb = mk("Frame", {Parent = pill, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0,9,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,12,0,12),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1), BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = thumb, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)})
    local state = false
    local entry = {Listeners = {}, Stars = {}, Spec = {hasPanel = false, iconGlyph = "🌦️"}}
    local function setState(on)
        state = on
        TweenService:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            BackgroundColor3 = on and COL.pinkOn or COL.off}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(thumb, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            Position = on and UDim2.new(1,-9,0.5,0) or UDim2.new(0,9,0.5,0)}):Play()
        weatherCycle.apply(on)
        for listener in pairs(entry.Listeners) do listener(on) end
    end
    pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.play("toggle")
        setState(not state)
    end)
    clickZone.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.play("toggle")
        setState(not state)
    end)
    entry.setState = setState
    entry.isOn = function() return state end
    toggleRegistry["Цикл погоды"] = entry
    toggleRegistry.attachStar(tile, "Цикл погоды")
end

    local page = tabPages["atmo"]
    addSectionHeader(page, "ЭФФЕКТЫ")
    local order = {
        "Row_Цикл погоды", "Section_АТМОСФЕРА", "Row_Атмосфера",
        "Section_ПОГОДА", "Row_Облака", "Row_Небо", "Row_Туман", "Row_Ветер", "Row_Дождь", "Row_Молния", "Row_Снег",
        "Section_ЭФФЕКТЫ", "Row_Лунный свет", "Row_Светлячки", "Row_Северное сияние", "Row_Радуга", "Row_Падающие звёзды",
    }
    for index, name in ipairs(order) do
        local item = page:FindFirstChild(name)
        if item then item.LayoutOrder = index end
    end
end)()

local keyBinds = {
    ClearKey = Enum.KeyCode.Delete,
    CancelKey = Enum.KeyCode.Escape,
    ListenSeconds = 6,
    WaitColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 80),
    NameLength = 6,
    NoKeyText = "NONE",
    WaitKeyText = "...",
    List = {},
    Cleanups = {},
    Active = nil,
    Token = 0,
    Conn = nil,
}

function keyBinds.new(title, onChange, onPress)
    local bind = {Title = title, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, OnChange = onChange, OnPress = onPress, Buttons = {}}
    keyBinds.List[#keyBinds.List + 1] = bind
    return bind
end

function keyBinds.isBound(keyCode)
    for _, bind in ipairs(keyBinds.List) do
        if bind.Key == keyCode then return true end
    end
    return false
end

function keyBinds.text(bind)
    if bind.Key == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return keyBinds.NoKeyText end
    if bind.FullName then return bind.Key.Name end
    return "[" .. string.sub(bind.Key.Name, 1, keyBinds.NameLength) .. "]"
end

function keyBinds.paint(bind)
    for _, button in ipairs(bind.Buttons) do
        if button.Parent then
            if keyBinds.Active == bind then
                button.Text = keyBinds.WaitKeyText
                button.TextColor3 = keyBinds.WaitColor
            else
                button.Text = keyBinds.text(bind)
                button.TextColor3 = bind.Key == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and COL.textMuted or (bind.BoundColor or COL.text)
            end
        end
    end
end

function keyBinds.stop()
    local previous = keyBinds.Active
    keyBinds.Active = nil
    keyBinds.Token = keyBinds.Token + 1
    if previous then keyBinds.paint(previous) end
end

function keyBinds.start(bind)
    keyBinds.stop()
    keyBinds.Active = bind
    local token = keyBinds.Token
    keyBinds.paint(bind)
    task.delay(keyBinds.ListenSeconds, function()
        if keyBinds.Active == bind and keyBinds.Token == token then keyBinds.stop() end
    end)
end

function keyBinds.attacher(bind)
    return function(button)
        bind.Buttons[#bind.Buttons + 1] = button
        button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if keyBinds.Active == bind then keyBinds.stop() else keyBinds.start(bind) end
        end)
        button.Destroying:Connect(function()
            local index = table.find(bind.Buttons, button)
            if index then table.remove(bind.Buttons, index) end
            if #bind.Buttons == 0 and keyBinds.Active == bind then keyBinds.stop() end
        end)
        keyBinds.paint(bind)
    end
end

function keyBinds.bindInput()
    keyBinds.Conn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
        local active = keyBinds.Active
        if active then
            if input.KeyCode ~= keyBinds.CancelKey then
                if input.KeyCode == keyBinds.ClearKey then
                    active.Key = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
                else
                    active.Key = input.KeyCode
                    toggleRegistry.Sounds.play("hotkey")
                end
            end
            keyBinds.stop()
            if active.OnChange then active.OnChange() end
            return
        end
        if processed then return end
        for _, bind in ipairs(keyBinds.List) do
            if bind.Key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and bind.Key == input.KeyCode then
                if bind.OnPress then
                    bind.OnPress()
                else
                    local registry = toggleRegistry[bind.Title]
                    if registry then registry.setState(not registry.isOn()) end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function keyBinds.destroy()
    for _, cleanup in ipairs(keyBinds.Cleanups) do pcall(cleanup) end
    if keyBinds.Conn then
        keyBinds.Conn:Disconnect()
        keyBinds.Conn = nil
    end
    keyBinds.Active = nil
end

local hideState = {
    Enabled = false,
    HideGame = true,
    HideRoblox = true,
    HideOurs = true,
    HideMenu = false,
    HideCursor = false,
    OptionButtonWidth = 68,
    RowHeight = 44,
    PanelHeight = 326,
    TopbarFolder = "TopBarApp",
    PanelTitle = "Скрыть гуи",
    SectionTitle = "ДРУГОЕ",
    OptionsHeader = "ЧТО СКРЫВАТЬ",
    OnText = "ВКЛ",
    OffText = "ВЫКЛ",
    WarnText = "СНАЧАЛО ПОСТАВЬ ГОРЯЧУЮ КЛАВИШУ ДУРАЧОК!",
    WarnSeconds = 2,
    WarnFadeSeconds = 1,
    WarnFadeStyle = Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,
    WarnDisplayOrder = 100,
    WarnGuiName = "HideKeyWarning",
    WarnFont = Enum.Font.GothamBlack,
    WarnMaxTextSize = 100,
    WarnWidth = 0.92,
    WarnHeight = 0.6,
    WarnTextColor = COL.accent,
    WarnShadeColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
    WarnShadeTransparency = 0.45,
    WarnStrokeTransparency = 0.3,
    Options = {
        {Field = "HideGame", Label = "Гуи плейса"},
        {Field = "HideRoblox", Label = "Гуи Роблокса"},
        {Field = "HideOurs", Label = "Наши гуи (фильтр, плашки)"},
        {Field = "HideMenu", Label = "Кнопка «Меню»"},
        {Field = "HideCursor", Label = "Курсор мыши"},
    },
    ToolSlotCount = 10,
    ToolCheckDelay = 0.08,
    ToolLearnDelay = 0.4,
    ToolPollSeconds = 0.5,
    ToolWatch = false,
    ToolSlots = {
        [Enum.KeyCode.One] = 1, [Enum.KeyCode.Two] = 2, [Enum.KeyCode.Three] = 3,
        [Enum.KeyCode.Four] = 4, [Enum.KeyCode.Five] = 5, [Enum.KeyCode.Six] = 6,
        [Enum.KeyCode.Seven] = 7, [Enum.KeyCode.Eight] = 8, [Enum.KeyCode.Nine] = 9,
        [Enum.KeyCode.Zero] = 10,
    },
    ToolKeysOn = false,
    BackpackOff = false,
    Slots = {},
    Tracked = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
    SavedGame = {},
    SavedCore = {},
    TopbarSaved = {},
    TopbarHeld = false,
    GameActive = false,
    GameGuards = {},
    TopbarGuards = {},
    CoreConn = nil,
    TopbarConn = nil,
    CursorOwner = "hide",
    CursorOwners = {},
    CursorBase = true,
    CursorHidden = false,
    CursorConn = nil,
    WarnGui = nil,
    WarnToken = 0,
    GameConn = nil,
    ToolConn = nil,
}

hideState.Bind = keyBinds.new(hideState.PanelTitle, function() hideState.refresh() end)

function hideState.isOurs(gui)
    return gui == ScreenGui or hideState.Tracked[gui] == true
end

function hideState.track(gui)
    local state = hideState
    state.Tracked[gui] = true
    gui.Enabled = not (state.Enabled and state.HideOurs)
end

function hideState.lockGui(gui, saved, guards, flag)
    if guards[gui] then return end
    local okEnabled, enabledConn = pcall(function()
        return gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
            if not hideState[flag] then return end
            local okNow, now = pcall(function() return gui.Enabled end)
            if okNow and now then
                saved[gui] = true
                pcall(function() gui.Enabled = false end)
            end
        end)
    end)
    if not okEnabled then return end
    local okDying, dyingConn = pcall(function()
        return gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
            saved[gui] = nil
            guards[gui] = nil
        end)
    end)
    guards[gui] = {enabledConn, okDying and dyingConn or nil}
end

function hideState.unlockGuis(saved, guards)
    for _, pair in pairs(guards) do
        pair[1]:Disconnect()
        if pair[2] then pair[2]:Disconnect() end
    end
    table.clear(guards)
    for gui, was in pairs(saved) do
        if gui.Parent then pcall(function() gui.Enabled = was end) end
    end
    table.clear(saved)
end

function hideState.hideGameGui(gui)
    local state = hideState
    if not gui:IsA("ScreenGui") or state.isOurs(gui) then return end
    if state.SavedGame[gui] == nil then state.SavedGame[gui] = gui.Enabled end
    gui.Enabled = false
    state.lockGui(gui, state.SavedGame, state.GameGuards, "GameActive")
end

function hideState.applyGame(hide)
    local state = hideState
    if state.GameConn then
        state.GameConn:Disconnect()
        state.GameConn = nil
    end
    state.GameActive = hide
    if hide then
        local playerGui = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if not playerGui then return end
        for _, gui in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
            state.hideGameGui(gui)
        end
        state.GameConn = playerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(state.hideGameGui)
    else
        state.unlockGuis(state.SavedGame, state.GameGuards)
    end
end

function hideState.topbarGuis()
    local list = {}
    local okRoot, root = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild(hideState.TopbarFolder)
    end)
    if not okRoot or not root then return list end
    if root:IsA("ScreenGui") then list[#list + 1] = root end
    for _, item in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        if item:IsA("ScreenGui") then list[#list + 1] = item end
    end
    return list
end

function hideState.hideTopbarGui(gui)
    local state = hideState
    if state.TopbarSaved[gui] == nil then
        local okGet, was = pcall(function() return gui.Enabled end)
        if not okGet or not pcall(function() gui.Enabled = false end) then return end
        state.TopbarSaved[gui] = was
    else
        pcall(function() gui.Enabled = false end)
    end
    state.lockGui(gui, state.TopbarSaved, state.TopbarGuards, "TopbarHeld")
end

function hideState.hideTopbar()
    local state = hideState
    for _, gui in ipairs(state.topbarGuis()) do
        state.hideTopbarGui(gui)
    end
end

function hideState.showTopbar()
    hideState.unlockGuis(hideState.TopbarSaved, hideState.TopbarGuards)
end

function hideState.onCoreAdded(item)
    local state = hideState
    if not state.TopbarHeld or not item:IsA("ScreenGui") then return end
    if item.Name == state.TopbarFolder or item:FindFirstAncestor(state.TopbarFolder) then
        state.hideTopbarGui(item)
    end
end

function hideState.relockCore(item)
    local state = hideState
    if item == Enum.CoreGuiType.All then return end
    state.SavedCore[item] = true
    local starterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
    pcall(starterGui.SetCoreGuiEnabled, starterGui, item, false)
    if item == Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack and not state.BackpackOff then
        state.BackpackOff = true
        state.applyTools(true)
    end
end

function hideState.onCoreChanged(item, enabled)
    local state = hideState
    if not state.TopbarHeld or not enabled then return end
    if item == Enum.CoreGuiType.All then
        for _, entry in ipairs(Enum.CoreGuiType:GetEnumItems()) do
            state.relockCore(entry)
        end
    else
        state.relockCore(item)
    end
end

function hideState.applyRoblox(hide)
    local state = hideState
    local starterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
    if hide then
        for _, item in ipairs(Enum.CoreGuiType:GetEnumItems()) do
            if item ~= Enum.CoreGuiType.All and state.SavedCore[item] == nil then
                local okGet, was = pcall(starterGui.GetCoreGuiEnabled, starterGui, item)
                if okGet then
                    state.SavedCore[item] = was
                    local okSet = pcall(starterGui.SetCoreGuiEnabled, starterGui, item, false)
                    if okSet and was and item == Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack then
                        state.BackpackOff = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if not state.TopbarHeld then
            state.TopbarHeld = true
            state.hideTopbar()
        end
        if not state.CoreConn then
            local okSignal, conn = pcall(function()
                return starterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal:Connect(state.onCoreChanged)
            end)
            if okSignal then state.CoreConn = conn end
        end
        if not state.TopbarConn then
            local okAdded, conn = pcall(function()
                return game:GetService("CoreGui").DescendantAdded:Connect(state.onCoreAdded)
            end)
            if okAdded then state.TopbarConn = conn end
        end
    else
        local wasHeld = state.TopbarHeld
        state.TopbarHeld = false
        if state.CoreConn then
            state.CoreConn:Disconnect()
            state.CoreConn = nil
        end
        if state.TopbarConn then
            state.TopbarConn:Disconnect()
            state.TopbarConn = nil
        end
        for item, was in pairs(state.SavedCore) do
            pcall(starterGui.SetCoreGuiEnabled, starterGui, item, was)
        end
        table.clear(state.SavedCore)
        state.BackpackOff = false
        if wasHeld then state.showTopbar() end
    end
end

function hideState.applyOurs(hide)
    for gui in pairs(hideState.Tracked) do
        if gui.Parent then gui.Enabled = not hide end
    end
end

function hideState.applyMenu(hide)
    if hide then
        openBtn.Parent = nil
    else
        openBtn.Parent = ScreenGui
    end
end

function hideState.refreshCursor()
    local state = hideState
    local registered = false
    local hidden = false
    for _, wantsHidden in pairs(state.CursorOwners) do
        registered = true
        if wantsHidden then hidden = true end
    end
    if hidden and not state.CursorConn then
        state.CursorConn = UserInputService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("MouseIconEnabled"):Connect(function()
            if UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled then
                pcall(function() UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = false end)
            end
        end)
    elseif not hidden and state.CursorConn then
        state.CursorConn:Disconnect()
        state.CursorConn = nil
    end
    state.CursorHidden = hidden
    local wanted = state.CursorBase
    if registered then wanted = not hidden end
    if UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled ~= wanted then
        pcall(function() UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = wanted end)
    end
end

function hideState.setCursor(owner, hidden)
    local state = hideState
    if next(state.CursorOwners) == nil then state.CursorBase = UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled end
    state.CursorOwners[owner] = hidden
    state.refreshCursor()
end

function hideState.clearCursor(owner)
    local state = hideState
    if state.CursorOwners[owner] == nil then return end
    state.CursorOwners[owner] = nil
    state.refreshCursor()
end

function hideState.applyCursor(hide)
    if hide then
        hideState.setCursor(hideState.CursorOwner, true)
    else
        hideState.clearCursor(hideState.CursorOwner)
    end
end

function hideState.hideWarning()
    local state = hideState
    state.WarnToken = state.WarnToken + 1
    if state.WarnGui then
        state.WarnGui:Destroy()
        state.WarnGui = nil
    end
end

function hideState.showWarning()
    local state = hideState
    state.hideWarning()
    local token = state.WarnToken
    local gui = mk("ScreenGui", {Name = state.WarnGuiName, ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
        DisplayOrder = state.WarnDisplayOrder, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling})
    local shade = mk("Frame", {Parent = gui, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = state.WarnShadeColor, BackgroundTransparency = state.WarnShadeTransparency,
        BorderSizePixel = 0})
    local label = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = shade, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(state.WarnWidth, 0, state.WarnHeight, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = state.WarnFont, Text = state.WarnText, TextScaled = true,
        TextWrapped = true, TextColor3 = state.WarnTextColor, TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
        TextStrokeTransparency = state.WarnStrokeTransparency})
    mk("UITextSizeConstraint", {Parent = label, MaxTextSize = state.WarnMaxTextSize})
    state.WarnGui = gui
    gui.Parent = guiParent
    task.delay(state.WarnSeconds, function()
        if token ~= state.WarnToken then return end
        local info = TweenInfo.new(state.WarnFadeSeconds, state.WarnFadeStyle, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
        TweenService:Create(shade, info, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(label, info, {TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1}):Play()
        task.delay(state.WarnFadeSeconds, function()
            if token == state.WarnToken then state.hideWarning() end
        end)
    end)
end

function hideState.canEnable()
    if hideState.Bind.Key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return true end
    hideState.showWarning()
    return false
end

function hideState.refresh()
    local state = hideState
    local on = state.Enabled
    state.applyGame(on and state.HideGame)
    state.applyRoblox(on and state.HideRoblox)
    state.applyOurs(on and state.HideOurs)
    state.applyMenu(on and state.HideMenu and state.Bind.Key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown)
    state.applyCursor(on and state.HideCursor)
    state.applyTools(on and (state.HideGame or state.BackpackOff))
end

function hideState.applyTools(active)
    local state = hideState
    state.ToolKeysOn = active
    if active then state.refreshSlots() end
end

function hideState.apply(enabled)
    hideState.Enabled = enabled
    hideState.refresh()
end

function hideState.collectTools()
    local tools = {}
    local backpack = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if backpack then
        for _, item in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
            if item:IsA("Tool") then tools[#tools + 1] = item end
        end
    end
    local character = player.Character
    if character then
        for _, item in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if item:IsA("Tool") then tools[#tools + 1] = item end
        end
    end
    return tools
end

function hideState.refreshSlots()
    local state = hideState
    local tools = state.collectTools()
    local present = {}
    for _, tool in ipairs(tools) do present[tool] = true end
    local assigned = {}
    for slot = 1, state.ToolSlotCount do
        local tool = state.Slots[slot]
        if tool then
            if present[tool] then
                assigned[tool] = true
            else
                state.Slots[slot] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    for _, tool in ipairs(tools) do
        if not assigned[tool] then
            for slot = 1, state.ToolSlotCount do
                if not state.Slots[slot] then
                    state.Slots[slot] = tool
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function hideState.assignSlot(slot, tool)
    local state = hideState
    for index = 1, state.ToolSlotCount do
        if state.Slots[index] == tool then state.Slots[index] = nil end
    end
    state.Slots[slot] = tool
end

function hideState.equippedTool()
    local character = player.Character
    return character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or nil
end

function hideState.pressSlot(slot)
    local state = hideState
    local before = state.equippedTool()
    task.wait(state.ToolCheckDelay)
    if not state.ToolKeysOn or state.equippedTool() ~= before then return end
    state.refreshSlots()
    local tool = state.Slots[slot]
    local character = player.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not tool or not humanoid then return end
    if tool.Parent == character then
        humanoid:UnequipTools()
    else
        humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
    end
end

function hideState.learnSlot(slot)
    local state = hideState
    state.refreshSlots()
    local before = state.equippedTool()
    task.wait(state.ToolLearnDelay)
    local after = state.equippedTool()
    if after == before then return end
    local tool = after or before
    if tool then state.assignSlot(slot, tool) end
end

function hideState.bindToolInput()
    local state = hideState
    state.ToolWatch = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while state.ToolWatch do
            state.refreshSlots()
            task.wait(state.ToolPollSeconds)
        end
    end)
    state.ToolConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
        if processed then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
        if keyBinds.Active or keyBinds.isBound(input.KeyCode) then return end
        local slot = state.ToolSlots[input.KeyCode]
        if not slot then return end
        if state.ToolKeysOn then state.pressSlot(slot) else state.learnSlot(slot) end
    end)
end

function hideState.destroy()
    local state = hideState
    state.hideWarning()
    state.ToolWatch = false
    if state.ToolConn then
        state.ToolConn:Disconnect()
        state.ToolConn = nil
    end
    if state.Enabled then
        state.Enabled = false
        state.refresh()
    end
end

function hideState.addOption(Body, option)
    local state = hideState
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, state.RowHeight),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -(state.OptionButtonWidth + 28), 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
        Text = option.Label, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = COL.text,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
    local button = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, state.OptionButtonWidth, 0, 28),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        TextSize = 11, AutoButtonColor = false, Text = ""})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = button, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)})
    local function paint()
        local on = state[option.Field]
        button.BackgroundColor3 = on and COL.pinkOn or COL.off
        button.TextColor3 = on and COL.text or COL.textMuted
        button.Text = on and state.OnText or state.OffText
    end
    paint()
    toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(button, "toggle")
    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        state[option.Field] = not state[option.Field]
        paint()
        state.refresh()
    end)
end

local tvState = {
    Enabled = false,
    PanelTitle = "Фильтр телевизора",
    Variant = 1,
    Static = 0.5,
    Flicker = 0.5,
    Darkness = 0,
    Stripes = 360,
    Grain = 0.5,
    SweepBrightness = 1,
    SweepSpeed = 0.22,
    SweepCount = 1,
    MinStatic = 0,
    MaxStatic = 1,
    MinFlicker = 0,
    MaxFlicker = 1,
    MinDarkness = -0.5,
    MaxDarkness = 0.9,
    MinStripes = 0,
    MaxStripes = 600,
    MinGrain = 0,
    MaxGrain = 1,
    MinSweepBrightness = 0,
    MaxSweepBrightness = 10,
    MinSweepSpeed = 0.02,
    MaxSweepSpeed = 1,
    MinSweepCount = 1,
    MaxSweepCount = 6,
    Variants = {
        {
            Label = "Современная",
            Saturation = -0.3,
            Contrast = 0.18,
            BaseBrightness = -0.05,
            Tint = Color3.fromRGB(195, 215, 245),
            LineColor = Color3.fromRGB(200, 215, 240),
            LightLineColor = Color3.fromRGB(235, 245, 255),
            SweepColor = Color3.fromRGB(200, 215, 240),
            StripeTransparency = 0.75,
            FlickerFrequency = 25,
            FlickerStrength = 0.08,
            StaticMode = "Horizontal",
            Grain = false,
            Vignette = false,
            Dust = false,
        },
        {
            Label = "Пленка",
            Saturation = -0.25,
            Contrast = -0.08,
            BaseBrightness = -0.02,
            Tint = Color3.fromRGB(248, 230, 195),
            LineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 200),
            LightLineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 250, 235),
            SweepColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 210),
            StripeTransparency = 0.9,
            FlickerFrequency = 9,
            FlickerStrength = 0.14,
            StaticMode = "Vertical",
            Grain = true,
            Vignette = true,
            Dust = true,
        },
    },
    SweepGradient = {{0, 1}, {0.3, 0.4}, {0.5, 0}, {0.7, 0.4}, {1, 1}},
    SweepHeight = 0.06,
    SweepBaseOpacity = 0.032,
    SweepPulse = 0.375,
    StaticLineCount = 8,
    StaticInterval = 0.08,
    StaticChance = 0.5,
    DustCount = 12,
    DustDark = Color3.fromRGB(35, 28, 22),
    GrainTextures = {268592485, 268592462, 268592427, 268590007},
    GrainInterval = 0.06,
    GrainTileMin = 400,
    GrainTileMax = 600,
    VignetteTransparency = 0.55,
    VignetteGradient = {{0, 0}, {0.55, 0.6}, {1, 1}},
    VignetteEdges = {
        {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.45, 0), Rotation = 90},
        {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.55, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.45, 0), Rotation = 270},
        {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 1, 0), Rotation = 0},
        {Position = UDim2.new(0.7, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 1, 0), Rotation = 180},
    },
    FlickerThreshold = 0.1,
    DisplayOrder = -1,
    ColorName = "CustomTVColorPreview",
