    Sweeps = {},
    GrainRow = nil,
    StripeLines = {},
    StaticLines = {},
    DustSpots = {},
    GrainFrames = {},
    Conn = nil,
}

function tvState.destroy()
    if tvState.Conn then
        tvState.Conn:Disconnect()
        tvState.Conn = nil
    end
    if tvState.ColorEffect then
        tvState.ColorEffect:Destroy()
        tvState.ColorEffect = nil
    end
    if tvState.Gui then
        tvState.Gui:Destroy()
        tvState.Gui = nil
    end
    tvState.ScanOverlay = nil
    tvState.Sweeps = {}
    tvState.StripeLines = {}
    tvState.StaticLines = {}
    tvState.DustSpots = {}
    tvState.GrainFrames = {}
end

function tvState.layoutStripes()
    local count = math.floor(tvState.Stripes + 0.5)
    for index, line in ipairs(tvState.StripeLines) do
        if index <= count then
            line.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, (index - 1) / count, 0)
            line.Visible = true
        else
            line.Visible = false
        end
    end
end

function tvState.sequence(points)
    local keypoints = {}
    for index, point in ipairs(points) do
        keypoints[index] = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(point[1], point[2])
    end
    return NumberSequence.new(keypoints)
end

function tvState.build()
    local look = tvState.Look
    local gui = mk("ScreenGui", {
        Name = tvState.GuiName, ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
        DisplayOrder = tvState.DisplayOrder, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling})
    hideState.track(gui)
    local container = mk("Frame", {Parent = gui, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    if look.Vignette then
        local sequence = tvState.sequence(tvState.VignetteGradient)
        for _, edge in ipairs(tvState.VignetteEdges) do
            local frame = mk("Frame", {Parent = container, Position = edge.Position, Size = edge.Size,
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = tvState.VignetteTransparency,
                BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1})
            mk("UIGradient", {Parent = frame, Rotation = edge.Rotation, Transparency = sequence})
        end
    end
    local grain = {}
    if look.Grain then
        for index, id in ipairs(tvState.GrainTextures) do
            grain[index] = mk("ImageLabel", {Parent = container, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Image = "rbxassetid://" .. id,
                ImageTransparency = 1, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Tile,
                TileSize = UDim2.new(0, tvState.GrainTileMax, 0, tvState.GrainTileMax),
                Visible = false, ZIndex = 2})
        end
    end
    local scan = mk("Frame", {Parent = container, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 3})
    local holder = mk("Frame", {Parent = scan, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3})
    local stripes = {}
    for index = 1, tvState.MaxStripes do
        stripes[index] = mk("Frame", {Parent = holder, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = look.StripeTransparency,
            BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3, Visible = false})
    end
    local sweeps = {}
    local sweepSequence = tvState.sequence(tvState.SweepGradient)
    for index = 1, tvState.MaxSweepCount do
        sweeps[index] = mk("Frame", {Parent = container, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, tvState.SweepHeight, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, -tvState.SweepHeight, 0), BackgroundColor3 = look.SweepColor,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4})
        mk("UIGradient", {Parent = sweeps[index], Rotation = 90, Transparency = sweepSequence})
    end
    local lines = {}
    for index = 1, tvState.StaticLineCount do
        lines[index] = mk("Frame", {Parent = container,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.random(1, 2)),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, index / (tvState.StaticLineCount + 1), 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = look.LineColor, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5})
    end
    local dust = {}
    if look.Dust then
        for index = 1, tvState.DustCount do
            dust[index] = mk("Frame", {Parent = container, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5})
        end
    end
    tvState.ScanOverlay = scan
    tvState.Sweeps = sweeps
    tvState.StripeLines = stripes
    tvState.StaticLines = lines
    tvState.DustSpots = dust
    tvState.GrainFrames = grain
    tvState.Gui = gui
    tvState.layoutStripes()
    gui.Parent = guiParent
end

function tvState.stepStatic(look)
    local chance = tvState.Static * tvState.StaticChance
    if look.StaticMode == "Vertical" then
        for _, line in ipairs(tvState.StaticLines) do
            if math.random() < chance then
                line.Position = UDim2.new(math.random(), 0, math.random() * 0.6, 0)
                line.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.random(1, 2), math.random(30, 100) / 100, 0)
                line.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3 + math.random() * 0.5
                line.BackgroundColor3 = math.random() > 0.4 and look.LineColor or look.LightLineColor
            else
                line.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            end
        end
        for _, spot in ipairs(tvState.DustSpots) do
            if math.random() < chance then
                local size = math.random(2, 5)
                spot.Position = UDim2.new(math.random(), 0, math.random(), 0)
                spot.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
                spot.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 + math.random() * 0.4
                spot.BackgroundColor3 = math.random() > 0.5 and tvState.DustDark or look.LightLineColor
            else
                spot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            end
        end
        return
    end
    for _, line in ipairs(tvState.StaticLines) do
        if math.random() < chance then
            line.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, math.random() * 0.95, 0)
            line.Size = UDim2.new(math.random(50, 100) / 100, 0, 0, math.random(1, 3))
            line.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25 + math.random() * 0.45
            line.BackgroundColor3 = math.random() > 0.4 and look.LineColor or look.LightLineColor
        else
            line.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        end
    end
end

function tvState.stepGrain()
    local frames = tvState.GrainFrames
    for _, frame in ipairs(frames) do
        frame.Visible = false
    end
    local frame = frames[tvState.GrainIndex]
    frame.ImageTransparency = 1 - tvState.Grain
    frame.TileSize = UDim2.new(0, math.random(tvState.GrainTileMin, tvState.GrainTileMax),
        0, math.random(tvState.GrainTileMin, tvState.GrainTileMax))
    frame.Visible = true
    tvState.GrainIndex = tvState.GrainIndex % #frames + 1
end

function tvState.step(dt)
    local look = tvState.Look
    local cycle = 1 + 2 * tvState.SweepHeight
    tvState.SweepPos = tvState.SweepPos + dt * tvState.SweepSpeed
    if tvState.SweepPos >= 1 + tvState.SweepHeight then
        tvState.SweepPos = -tvState.SweepHeight
    end
    local sweepCount = math.floor(tvState.SweepCount + 0.5)
    for index, sweep in ipairs(tvState.Sweeps) do
        if index <= sweepCount then
            local pos = (tvState.SweepPos + tvState.SweepHeight + (index - 1) * cycle / sweepCount) % cycle
                - tvState.SweepHeight
            sweep.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, pos, 0)
            if pos > 0 and pos < 1 then
                local opacity = tvState.SweepBrightness * tvState.SweepBaseOpacity
                    * (1 + math.sin(pos * math.pi) * tvState.SweepPulse)
                sweep.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - math.min(1, opacity)
            else
                sweep.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            end
        else
            sweep.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        end
    end

    tvState.StaticTimer = tvState.StaticTimer + dt
    if tvState.StaticTimer >= tvState.StaticInterval then
        tvState.StaticTimer = 0
        tvState.stepStatic(look)
    end

    if look.Grain then
        tvState.GrainTimer = tvState.GrainTimer + dt
        if tvState.GrainTimer >= tvState.GrainInterval then
            tvState.GrainTimer = 0
            tvState.stepGrain()
        end
    end

    tvState.FlickerTime = tvState.FlickerTime + dt
    if tvState.Flicker > tvState.FlickerThreshold then
        local wave = math.sin(tvState.FlickerTime * look.FlickerFrequency * 2 * math.pi) * 0.5 + 0.5
        tvState.ScanOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - wave * tvState.Flicker * look.FlickerStrength
    else
        tvState.ScanOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    end
end

function tvState.apply(enabled)
    tvState.destroy()
    tvState.Enabled = enabled
    if not enabled then return end
    tvState.Look = tvState.Variants[tvState.Variant]
    local look = tvState.Look
    tvState.ColorEffect = mk("ColorCorrectionEffect", {
        Name = tvState.ColorName, Contrast = look.Contrast, Saturation = look.Saturation,
        Brightness = look.BaseBrightness - tvState.Darkness, TintColor = look.Tint,
        Parent = Lighting})
    tvState.build()
    tvState.SweepPos = -tvState.SweepHeight
    tvState.StaticTimer = 0
    tvState.FlickerTime = 0
    tvState.GrainTimer = 0
    tvState.GrainIndex = 1
    tvState.Conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(tvState.step)
end

function tvState.setVariant(index)
    tvState.Variant = index
    if tvState.GrainRow then
        tvState.GrainRow.Visible = tvState.Variants[index].Grain
    end
    if tvState.Enabled then
        tvState.apply(true)
    end
end

function tvState.addSwitch(Body, labels, current, onChange)
    local count = #labels
    local wrap = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = wrap, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)})
    local indicator = mk("Frame", {Parent = wrap,
        Position = UDim2.new((current - 1) / count, 2, 0, 2),
        Size = UDim2.new(1 / count, -4, 1, -4), BackgroundColor3 = COL.pinkOn, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = indicator, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)})
    local buttons = {}
    for index, label in ipairs(labels) do
        buttons[index] = mk("TextButton", {Parent = wrap,
            Position = UDim2.new((index - 1) / count, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1 / count, 0, 1, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = label, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 10,
            TextColor3 = index == current and COL.text or COL.textMuted, AutoButtonColor = false})
    end
    for index, button in ipairs(buttons) do
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(button, "select")
        button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            onChange(index)
            TweenService:Create(indicator, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                Position = UDim2.new((index - 1) / count, 2, 0, 2)}):Play()
            for other, otherButton in ipairs(buttons) do
                otherButton.TextColor3 = other == index and COL.text or COL.textMuted
            end
        end)
    end
end

function tvState.addInputRow(Body, label, placeholder, value, onCommit)
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.35, -14, 1, 0),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = label, TextSize = 13,
        TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    local box = mk("TextBox", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.65, -10, 0, 28),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
        TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = COL.text, PlaceholderText = placeholder,
        PlaceholderColor3 = COL.textMuted, Text = value, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = box, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)})
    mk("UIPadding", {Parent = box, PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)})
    box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local cleaned = onCommit(box.Text)
        if cleaned then box.Text = cleaned end
    end)
    return row, box
end

addSectionHeader(tabPages["scr"], "ТЕЛЕВИДЕНИЕ")

tvState.Bind = keyBinds.new(tvState.PanelTitle)

addToggleRow(tabPages["scr"], tvState.PanelTitle, true, tvState.apply, function(Body)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ВАРИАЦИЯ")
    local variantLabels = {}
    for index, look in ipairs(tvState.Variants) do
        variantLabels[index] = look.Label
    end
    tvState.addSwitch(Body, variantLabels, tvState.Variant, tvState.setVariant)
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ОСНОВНОЕ")

        tvState.Static = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(110, 170, 255))
    addSlider(Body, "Мигание", tvState.MinFlicker, tvState.MaxFlicker, tvState.Flicker, 2, function(v)
        tvState.Flicker = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(150, 170, 205))
    addSlider(Body, "Темнота", tvState.MinDarkness, tvState.MaxDarkness, tvState.Darkness, 2, function(v)
        tvState.Darkness = v
        if tvState.ColorEffect then
            tvState.ColorEffect.Brightness = tvState.Look.BaseBrightness - v
        end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(120, 130, 170))
    addSlider(Body, "Полоски", tvState.MinStripes, tvState.MaxStripes, tvState.Stripes, 0, function(v)
        tvState.Stripes = v
        if tvState.Gui then tvState.layoutStripes() end
    end, Color3.fromRGB(140, 150, 190))
    tvState.GrainRow = addSlider(Body, "Зернистость", tvState.MinGrain, tvState.MaxGrain, tvState.Grain, 2, function(v)
        tvState.Grain = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(210, 180, 130))
    tvState.GrainRow.Visible = tvState.Variants[tvState.Variant].Grain
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ЛИНИЯ")
    addSlider(Body, "Яркость", tvState.MinSweepBrightness, tvState.MaxSweepBrightness, tvState.SweepBrightness, 2, function(v)
        tvState.SweepBrightness = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(190, 215, 250))
    addSlider(Body, "Скорость", tvState.MinSweepSpeed, tvState.MaxSweepSpeed, tvState.SweepSpeed, 2, function(v)
        tvState.SweepSpeed = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(130, 190, 240))
    addSlider(Body, "Количество", tvState.MinSweepCount, tvState.MaxSweepCount, tvState.SweepCount, 0, function(v)
        tvState.SweepCount = v
    end, Color3.fromRGB(170, 200, 245))
end, 520, "📺", keyBinds.attacher(tvState.Bind))

tvState.Logo = {
    Enabled = false,
    Plates = {},
    Selected = 0,
    Defaults = {
        Variant = 1,
        Text = "ПРЯМОЙ ЭФИР",
        Picture = "",
        PosX = 0.985,
        PosY = 0.04,
        Color = Color3.fromRGB(215, 30, 45),
        TextColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        TextSize = 20,
        PlateTransparency = 0,
        TextTransparency = 0,
        Sizes = {{Width = 200, Height = 36}, {Width = 140, Height = 140}},
    },
    PosStepY = 0.09,
    PosStepCount = 10,
    MinWidth = 20,
    MaxWidth = 1000,
    MinHeight = 10,
    MaxHeight = 600,
    MinTextSize = 8,
    MaxTextSize = 300,
    NativeTextSize = 100,
    MaxTextLength = 60,
    Variants = {"Текст", "Картинка"},
    TextFont = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
    TextWrap = true,
    PaddingX = 14,
    PaddingY = 6,
    PlateCorner = 4,
    DisplayOrder = -2,
    GuiName = "CustomTVLogoPreview",
    RowHeight = 34,
    StrokeThickness = 2,
    RowCorner = 10,
    ButtonHeight = 36,
    ConfirmSeconds = 2,
    PanelTitle = "Плашка канала",
    ListHeader = "ПЛАШКИ",
    AddText = "+ Плашка",
    DeleteText = "Удалить",
    ConfirmText = "Точно?",
    EmptyText = "пусто",
    PictureWord = "картинка",
    NoPictureText = "нет айди",
    Hint = "Перетаскивай плашки мышкой прямо на экране.",
    Gui = nil,
    Container = nil,
    DragPlate = nil,
    DragPoint = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
    DragX = 0,
    DragY = 0,
    Conns = {},
}

function tvState.Logo.clean(text)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    local trimmed = string.match(tostring(text or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    local okCut, cut = pcall(utf8.offset, trimmed, logo.MaxTextLength + 1)
    if okCut and cut then trimmed = string.sub(trimmed, 1, cut - 1) end
    return trimmed
end

function tvState.Logo.number(value, fallback)
    if type(value) == "number" and value == value then return value end
    return fallback
end

function tvState.Logo.color(raw, fallback)
    if type(raw) == "table" and type(raw[1]) == "number" and type(raw[2]) == "number"
        and type(raw[3]) == "number" then
        return Color3.new(math.clamp(raw[1], 0, 1), math.clamp(raw[2], 0, 1), math.clamp(raw[3], 0, 1))
    end
    return fallback
end

function tvState.Logo.newPlate(raw)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    local defaults = logo.Defaults
    if type(raw) ~= "table" then raw = {} end
    local variant = raw.Variant
    if variant ~= 1 and variant ~= 2 then variant = defaults.Variant end
    local sizes = {}
    for index = 1, #logo.Variants do
        local rawSize = type(raw.Sizes) == "table" and type(raw.Sizes[index]) == "table" and raw.Sizes[index] or {}
        local base = defaults.Sizes[index]
        sizes[index] = {
            Width = math.clamp(logo.number(rawSize.Width, base.Width), logo.MinWidth, logo.MaxWidth),
            Height = math.clamp(logo.number(rawSize.Height, base.Height), logo.MinHeight, logo.MaxHeight),
        }
    end
    return {
        Variant = variant,
        Text = type(raw.Text) == "string" and logo.clean(raw.Text) or defaults.Text,
        Picture = type(raw.Picture) == "string" and logo.clean(raw.Picture) or defaults.Picture,
        Color = logo.color(raw.Color, defaults.Color),
        TextColor = logo.color(raw.TextColor, defaults.TextColor),
        TextSize = math.clamp(logo.number(raw.TextSize, defaults.TextSize), logo.MinTextSize, logo.MaxTextSize),
        PlateTransparency = math.clamp(logo.number(raw.PlateTransparency, defaults.PlateTransparency), 0, 1),
        TextTransparency = math.clamp(logo.number(raw.TextTransparency, defaults.TextTransparency), 0, 1),
        Sizes = sizes,
        PosX = math.clamp(logo.number(raw.PosX, defaults.PosX), 0, 1),
        PosY = math.clamp(logo.number(raw.PosY, defaults.PosY), 0, 1),
        Root = nil,
        RowButton = nil,
    }
end

function tvState.Logo.export()
    local list = {}
    for index, plate in ipairs(tvState.Logo.Plates) do
        local sizes = {}
        for variant, size in ipairs(plate.Sizes) do
            sizes[variant] = {Width = size.Width, Height = size.Height}
        end
        list[index] = {
            Variant = plate.Variant, Text = plate.Text, Picture = plate.Picture,
            Color = {plate.Color.R, plate.Color.G, plate.Color.B},
            TextColor = {plate.TextColor.R, plate.TextColor.G, plate.TextColor.B},
            TextSize = plate.TextSize, PlateTransparency = plate.PlateTransparency,
            TextTransparency = plate.TextTransparency,
            Sizes = sizes, PosX = plate.PosX, PosY = plate.PosY,
        }
    end
    return list
end

function tvState.Logo.import(list)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    local plates = {}
    for _, raw in ipairs(list) do
        if type(raw) == "table" then plates[#plates + 1] = logo.newPlate(raw) end
    end
    logo.Plates = plates
    logo.Selected = #plates > 0 and math.clamp(logo.Selected, 1, #plates) or 0
end

function tvState.Logo.isPress(input)
    return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end

function tvState.Logo.destroy()
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    for _, conn in ipairs(logo.Conns) do
        conn:Disconnect()
    end
    logo.Conns = {}
    if logo.Gui then
        logo.Gui:Destroy()
        logo.Gui = nil
    end
    logo.Container = nil
    logo.DragPlate = nil
    for _, plate in ipairs(logo.Plates) do
        plate.Root = nil
    end
end

function tvState.Logo.content(plate)
    if not plate.Root then return nil end
    return plate.Root:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("GuiObject")
end

function tvState.Logo.applySize(plate)
    if not plate.Root then return end
    local size = plate.Sizes[plate.Variant]
    plate.Root.Size = UDim2.new(0, size.Width, 0, size.Height)
end

function tvState.Logo.applyColors(plate)
    local content = tvState.Logo.content(plate)
    if not content or plate.Variant ~= 1 then return end
    content.BackgroundColor3 = plate.Color
    local label = content:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
    if label then label.TextColor3 = plate.TextColor end
end

function tvState.Logo.applyTextSize(plate)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    local content = logo.content(plate)
    if not content or plate.Variant ~= 1 then return end
    local label = content:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
    if not label then return end
    local scale = math.max(1, plate.TextSize / logo.NativeTextSize)
    label.TextSize = math.min(plate.TextSize, logo.NativeTextSize)
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1 / scale, 0, 1 / scale, 0)
    local scaler = label:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIScale")
    if scaler then scaler.Scale = scale end
end

function tvState.Logo.applyTransparency(plate)
    local content = tvState.Logo.content(plate)
    if not content or plate.Variant ~= 1 then return end
    content.BackgroundTransparency = plate.PlateTransparency
    local label = content:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
    if label then label.TextTransparency = plate.TextTransparency end
end

function tvState.Logo.refresh(plate)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    if not plate.Root then return end
    for _, child in ipairs(plate.Root:GetChildren()) do
        child:Destroy()
    end
    logo.applySize(plate)
    if plate.Variant == 1 then
        if plate.Text == "" then
            plate.Root.Visible = false
            return
        end
        local body = mk("Frame", {Parent = plate.Root, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = plate.Color, BackgroundTransparency = plate.PlateTransparency,
            BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = body, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, logo.PlateCorner)})
        mk("UIPadding", {Parent = body, PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, logo.PaddingX),
            PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, logo.PaddingX), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, logo.PaddingY),
            PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, logo.PaddingY)})
        local label = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = body, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Font = logo.TextFont, TextSize = logo.MinTextSize, TextWrapped = logo.TextWrap,
            TextColor3 = plate.TextColor, TextTransparency = plate.TextTransparency,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
            Text = plate.Text})
        mk("UIScale", {Parent = label, Scale = 1})
        logo.applyTextSize(plate)
    else
        local image = skyState.resolve(plate.Picture)
        if not image then
            plate.Root.Visible = false
            return
        end
        mk("ImageLabel", {Parent = plate.Root, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Image = image, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Stretch})
    end
    plate.Root.Visible = true
end

function tvState.Logo.buildPlate(plate)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    local root = mk("Frame", {Parent = logo.Container, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(plate.PosX, 0, plate.PosY, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Active = true, Visible = false})
    plate.Root = root
    root.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if logo.isPress(input) then
            logo.DragPlate = plate
            logo.DragPoint = input.Position
            logo.DragX = plate.PosX
            logo.DragY = plate.PosY
        end
    end)
    logo.refresh(plate)
end

function tvState.Logo.bindDrag()
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    logo.Conns[#logo.Conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        local plate = logo.DragPlate
        if not plate or not plate.Root or not logo.Container then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            return
        end
        local screen = logo.Container.AbsoluteSize
        if screen.X <= 0 or screen.Y <= 0 then return end
        local size = plate.Root.AbsoluteSize
        local delta = input.Position - logo.DragPoint
        plate.PosX = math.clamp(logo.DragX + delta.X / screen.X, math.min(1, size.X / screen.X), 1)
        plate.PosY = math.clamp(logo.DragY + delta.Y / screen.Y, 0, math.max(0, 1 - size.Y / screen.Y))
        plate.Root.Position = UDim2.new(plate.PosX, 0, plate.PosY, 0)
    end)
    logo.Conns[#logo.Conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if logo.isPress(input) then logo.DragPlate = nil end
    end)
end

function tvState.Logo.build()
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    local gui = mk("ScreenGui", {
        Name = logo.GuiName, ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
        DisplayOrder = logo.DisplayOrder, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling})
    hideState.track(gui)
    logo.Container = mk("Frame", {Parent = gui, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    logo.Gui = gui
    for _, plate in ipairs(logo.Plates) do
        logo.buildPlate(plate)
    end
    logo.bindDrag()
    gui.Parent = guiParent
end

function tvState.Logo.apply(enabled)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    logo.destroy()
    logo.Enabled = enabled
    if enabled then logo.build() end
end

function tvState.Logo.refreshPanel()
    local registry = toggleRegistry[tvState.Logo.PanelTitle]
    if registry and registry.rebuild then registry.rebuild() end
end

function tvState.Logo.add()
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    local count = #logo.Plates
    local plate = logo.newPlate({PosY = logo.Defaults.PosY + (count % logo.PosStepCount) * logo.PosStepY})
    logo.Plates[count + 1] = plate
    logo.Selected = count + 1
    if logo.Container then logo.buildPlate(plate) end
    logo.refreshPanel()
end

function tvState.Logo.remove(index)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    local plate = logo.Plates[index]
    if not plate then return end
    if plate.Root then plate.Root:Destroy() end
    if logo.DragPlate == plate then logo.DragPlate = nil end
    table.remove(logo.Plates, index)
    logo.Selected = math.min(index, #logo.Plates)
    logo.refreshPanel()
end

function tvState.Logo.describe(index, plate)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    if plate.Variant == 1 then
        return index .. "  ·  " .. (plate.Text ~= "" and plate.Text or logo.EmptyText)
    end
    return index .. "  ·  " .. logo.PictureWord .. ": " .. (plate.Picture ~= "" and plate.Picture or logo.NoPictureText)
end

function tvState.Logo.updateRow(plate)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    local index = table.find(logo.Plates, plate)
    if index and plate.RowButton and plate.RowButton.Parent then
        plate.RowButton.Text = logo.describe(index, plate)
    end
end

function tvState.Logo.addListRow(Body, index, plate)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    local selected = index == logo.Selected
    local inset = logo.StrokeThickness
    local holder = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, logo.RowHeight + inset * 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.pinkOn, BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0 or 1, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = holder, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, logo.RowCorner + inset)})
    local row = mk("TextButton", {Parent = holder, Position = UDim2.new(0, inset, 0, inset),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -inset * 2, 1, -inset * 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = selected and COL.text or COL.textMuted,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        Text = logo.describe(index, plate)})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, logo.RowCorner)})
    mk("UIPadding", {Parent = row, PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 14), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 14)})
    plate.RowButton = row
    toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(row, "select")
    row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if logo.Selected ~= index then
            logo.Selected = index
            logo.refreshPanel()
        end
    end)
end

function tvState.Logo.addButton(Body, text, color, onClick)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    local button = mk("TextButton", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, logo.ButtonHeight),
        BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = COL.text, Text = text})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = button, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(onClick)
    return button
end

function tvState.Logo.buildEditor(Body)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    local index = logo.Selected
    local plate = logo.Plates[index]
    if not plate then return end
    addPanelHeader(Body, "ПЛАШКА " .. index)
    local textRow
    local pictureRow
    local plateColorRow
    local textColorRow
    local textSizeRow
    local plateAlphaRow
    local textAlphaRow
    local setWidth
    local setHeight
    tvState.addSwitch(Body, logo.Variants, plate.Variant, function(variant)
        plate.Variant = variant
        textRow.Visible = variant == 1
        pictureRow.Visible = variant == 2
        textSizeRow.Visible = variant == 1
        plateAlphaRow.Visible = variant == 1
        textAlphaRow.Visible = variant == 1
        plateColorRow.Visible = variant == 1
        textColorRow.Visible = variant == 1
        setWidth(plate.Sizes[variant].Width)
        setHeight(plate.Sizes[variant].Height)
        logo.refresh(plate)
        logo.updateRow(plate)
    end)
    textRow = tvState.addInputRow(Body, "Текст", "Текст плашки", plate.Text, function(text)
        plate.Text = logo.clean(text)
        logo.refresh(plate)
        logo.updateRow(plate)
        return plate.Text
    end)
    pictureRow = tvState.addInputRow(Body, "Картинка", "ID картинки", plate.Picture, function(text)
        plate.Picture = logo.clean(text)
        logo.refresh(plate)
        logo.updateRow(plate)
        return plate.Picture
    end)
    local _, _, heightDisplay = addSlider(Body, "Высота плашки", logo.MinHeight, logo.MaxHeight,
        plate.Sizes[plate.Variant].Height, 0, function(v)
            plate.Sizes[plate.Variant].Height = v
            logo.applySize(plate)
        end, Color3.fromRGB(230, 150, 120))
    local _, _, widthDisplay = addSlider(Body, "Длина плашки", logo.MinWidth, logo.MaxWidth,
        plate.Sizes[plate.Variant].Width, 0, function(v)
            plate.Sizes[plate.Variant].Width = v
            logo.applySize(plate)
        end, Color3.fromRGB(230, 120, 130))
    setWidth = widthDisplay
    setHeight = heightDisplay
    plateAlphaRow = addSlider(Body, "Прозрачность плашки", 0, 1, plate.PlateTransparency, 2, function(v)
        plate.PlateTransparency = math.floor(v * 100 + 0.5) / 100
        logo.applyTransparency(plate)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(200, 130, 190))
    textSizeRow = addSlider(Body, "Размер текста", logo.MinTextSize, logo.MaxTextSize,
        plate.TextSize, 0, function(v)
            plate.TextSize = math.floor(v)
            logo.applyTextSize(plate)
        end, Color3.fromRGB(230, 170, 120))
    textAlphaRow = addSlider(Body, "Прозрачность текста", 0, 1, plate.TextTransparency, 2, function(v)
        plate.TextTransparency = math.floor(v * 100 + 0.5) / 100
        logo.applyTransparency(plate)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(180, 140, 210))
    plateColorRow = addColorPicker(Body, "Цвет плашки", plate.Color, function(color)
        plate.Color = color
        logo.applyColors(plate)
    end)
    textColorRow = addColorPicker(Body, "Цвет текста", plate.TextColor, function(color)
        plate.TextColor = color
        logo.applyColors(plate)
    end)
    textRow.Visible = plate.Variant == 1
    pictureRow.Visible = plate.Variant == 2
    textSizeRow.Visible = plate.Variant == 1
    plateAlphaRow.Visible = plate.Variant == 1
    textAlphaRow.Visible = plate.Variant == 1
    plateColorRow.Visible = plate.Variant == 1
    textColorRow.Visible = plate.Variant == 1
    local armed = false
    local deleteButton
    deleteButton = logo.addButton(Body, logo.DeleteText, COL.off, function()
        if armed then
            logo.remove(index)
            return
        end
        armed = true
        deleteButton.Text = logo.ConfirmText
        task.delay(logo.ConfirmSeconds, function()
            if armed and deleteButton.Parent then
                armed = false
                deleteButton.Text = logo.DeleteText
            end
        end)
    end)
end

tvState.Logo.Plates[1] = tvState.Logo.newPlate({})
tvState.Logo.Selected = 1

tvState.Logo.Bind = keyBinds.new(tvState.Logo.PanelTitle)

addToggleRow(tabPages["scr"], tvState.Logo.PanelTitle, true, tvState.Logo.apply, function(Body)
    local logo = tvState.Logo
    addPanelHeader(Body, logo.ListHeader)
    for index, plate in ipairs(logo.Plates) do
        logo.addListRow(Body, index, plate)
    end
    logo.addButton(Body, logo.AddText, COL.pinkOn, logo.add)
    logo.buildEditor(Body)
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = logo.Hint, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
        TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
end, 520, "📡", keyBinds.attacher(tvState.Logo.Bind))

function tvState.News.clean(text)
    local news = tvState.News
    local flat = string.gsub(tostring(text or ""), "[\r\n]+", " ")
    local trimmed = string.match(flat, "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    local okCut, cut = pcall(utf8.offset, trimmed, news.MaxTextLength + 1)
    if okCut and cut then trimmed = string.sub(trimmed, 1, cut - 1) end
    return trimmed
end

function tvState.News.upper(text)
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        local out = {}
        for _, code in utf8.codes(text) do
            if code >= 97 and code <= 122 then
                code = code - 32
            elseif code >= 0x430 and code <= 0x44F then
                code = code - 32
            elseif code >= 0x450 and code <= 0x45F then
                code = code - 80
            elseif code == 0x491 then
                code = 0x490
            end
            out[#out + 1] = utf8.char(code)
        end
        return table.concat(out)
    end)
    if ok then return result end
    return text
end

function tvState.News.shown()
    local news = tvState.News
    local text = news.Items[news.Current] or ""
    if news.UpperCase then text = news.upper(text) end
    return text
end

function tvState.News.refreshText()
    local news = tvState.News
    if news.HeadlineLabel then news.HeadlineLabel.Text = news.shown() end
end

function tvState.News.clock()
    local news = tvState.News
    if not news.TimeLabel then return end
    local total = math.floor((Lighting.ClockTime % 24) * 60)
    if total ~= news.LastMinute then
        news.LastMinute = total
        news.TimeLabel.Text = string.format(news.TimeFormat, math.floor(total / 60) % 24, total % 60)
    end
end

function tvState.News.describe(index)
    local news = tvState.News
    local text = news.Items[index]
    return index .. "  ·  " .. ((text and text ~= "") and text or news.EmptyText)
end

function tvState.News.updateRow(index)
    local news = tvState.News
    local row = news.Rows[index]
    if row and row.Parent then row.Text = news.describe(index) end
end

function tvState.News.timeGradient()
    local news = tvState.News
    return ColorSequence.new(
        news.TimeColor:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), news.TimeShadeUp),
        news.TimeColor:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), news.TimeShadeDown))
end

function tvState.News.applyColors()
    local news = tvState.News
    if news.TimeGradient then news.TimeGradient.Color = news.timeGradient() end
    if news.TimeLabel then news.TimeLabel.TextColor3 = news.TimeTextColor end
    if news.Strip then
        news.Strip.BackgroundColor3 = news.StripColor
        for _, child in ipairs(news.Strip:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("TextLabel") then child.TextColor3 = news.TextColor end
        end
    end
    if news.Ribbon then
        for _, child in ipairs(news.Ribbon:GetChildren()) do
            child.TextColor3 = news.TextColor
        end
    end
end

function tvState.News.slideSeconds()
    local news = tvState.News
    return news.Interval * news.SlideShare
end

function tvState.News.scaled(value)
    return math.floor(value * tvState.News.Size + 0.5)
end

function tvState.News.makeLabel(position)
    local news = tvState.News
    local label = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = news.Strip, Position = position, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = news.TextFont, TextScaled = true, TextWrapped = true,
        TextColor3 = news.TextColor, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, Text = ""})
    mk("UITextSizeConstraint", {Parent = label, MaxTextSize = math.max(1, news.scaled(news.TextSize)),
        MinTextSize = math.max(1, news.scaled(news.MinTextSize))})
    return label
end

function tvState.News.applyLayout()
    local news = tvState.News
    if not news.Bar then return end
    news.Bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, news.scaled(news.Height))
    news.TimeBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, news.scaled(news.TimeWidth), 1, 0)
    news.TimeLabel.TextSize = math.max(1, news.scaled(news.TimeTextSize))
    news.Strip.Position = UDim2.new(0, news.scaled(news.TimeWidth), 0, 0)
    news.Strip.Size = UDim2.new(1, -news.scaled(news.TimeWidth), 1, 0)
    news.Padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, news.scaled(news.PaddingX))
    news.Padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, news.scaled(news.PaddingX))
    news.Padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, news.scaled(news.PaddingY))
    news.Padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, news.scaled(news.PaddingY))
    news.touchRibbon()
    for _, child in ipairs(news.Strip:GetChildren()) do
        local limit = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("UITextSizeConstraint")
        if limit then
            limit.MinTextSize = 1
            limit.MaxTextSize = math.max(1, news.scaled(news.TextSize))
            limit.MinTextSize = math.max(1, news.scaled(news.MinTextSize))
        end
    end
end

function tvState.News.clearRibbon()
    local news = tvState.News
    if news.Ribbon then
        news.Ribbon:Destroy()
        news.Ribbon = nil
    end
    news.Period = 0
end

function tvState.News.touchRibbon()
    local news = tvState.News
    if news.Ticker then news.RibbonDirty = true end
end

function tvState.News.measure(text, size)
    local news = tvState.News
    local ok, bounds = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("TextService"):GetTextSize(text, size, news.TextFont, Vector2.new(100000, size * 2))
    end)
    if ok then return math.ceil(bounds.X) end
    return math.ceil(#text * size * 0.6)
end

function tvState.News.buildRibbon()
    local news = tvState.News
    news.clearRibbon()
    news.RibbonDirty = false
    news.RibbonBuiltAt = os.clock()
    if not news.Strip then return end
    local stripWidth = news.Strip.AbsoluteSize.X
    news.RibbonStrip = stripWidth
    local size = math.max(1, news.scaled(news.TextSize))
    local gap = math.max(1, news.scaled(news.TickerGap))
    local texts = {}
    local widths = {}
    local period = 0
    for _, text in ipairs(news.Items) do
        if text ~= "" then
            local shown = news.UpperCase and news.upper(text) or text
            texts[#texts + 1] = shown
            widths[#widths + 1] = news.measure(shown, size)
            period = period + widths[#widths] + gap
        end
    end
    if period <= 0 then return end
    local pad = news.scaled(news.PaddingX)
    local reps = math.ceil((stripWidth + pad * 2) / period) + 1
    local ribbon = mk("Frame", {Parent = news.Strip, Size = UDim2.new(0, period * reps, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    local x = 0
    for _ = 1, reps do
        for index, text in ipairs(texts) do
            mk("TextLabel", {Parent = ribbon, Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, widths[index], 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = news.TextFont,
                TextSize = size, TextColor3 = news.TextColor, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, Text = text})
            x = x + widths[index]
            mk("TextLabel", {Parent = ribbon, Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, gap, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = news.TextFont,
                TextSize = size, TextColor3 = news.TextColor, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, Text = news.TickerBullet})
            x = x + gap
        end
    end
    news.Ribbon = ribbon
    news.Period = period
end

function tvState.News.stepTicker(dt)
    local news = tvState.News
    local stripWidth = news.Strip.AbsoluteSize.X
    local due = os.clock() - news.RibbonBuiltAt >= news.RibbonDelay
    if (news.RibbonDirty and due) or math.abs(stripWidth - news.RibbonStrip) > 1 then
        news.buildRibbon()
    end
    if not news.Ribbon or news.Period <= 0 then return end
    local speed = news.scaled(news.TickerSpeed)
    news.TickerOffset = (news.TickerOffset + speed * dt) % news.Period
    news.Ribbon.Position = UDim2.new(0, news.TickerOffset - news.Period - news.scaled(news.PaddingX), 0, 0)
end

function tvState.News.applyMode()
    local news = tvState.News
    if not news.Strip then return end
    for _, child in ipairs(news.Strip:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("TextLabel") then child:Destroy() end
    end
    news.HeadlineLabel = nil
    news.clearRibbon()
    if news.Ticker then
        news.TickerOffset = 0
        news.buildRibbon()
    else
        news.HeadlineLabel = news.makeLabel(UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0))
        news.refreshText()
    end
end

function tvState.News.setTicker(value)
    local news = tvState.News
    news.Ticker = value
    if value then
        news.Auto = false
        news.Smooth = false
    end
    news.applyMode()
end

function tvState.News.setAuto(value)
    local news = tvState.News
    news.Auto = value
    news.Elapsed = 0
    if value and news.Ticker then
        news.Ticker = false
        news.applyMode()
    end
end

function tvState.News.setSmooth(value)
    local news = tvState.News
    news.Smooth = value
    if value and news.Ticker then
        news.Ticker = false
        news.applyMode()
    end
end

function tvState.News.swap()
    local news = tvState.News
    if not news.Strip then return end
    if not news.Smooth or not news.HeadlineLabel then
        news.refreshText()
        return
    end
    local old = news.HeadlineLabel
    local incoming = news.makeLabel(UDim2.new(-1, -news.scaled(news.PaddingX), 0, 0))
    incoming.Text = news.shown()
    news.HeadlineLabel = incoming
    local info = TweenInfo.new(news.slideSeconds(), news.SlideStyle, news.SlideDirection)
    local leaving = TweenService:Create(old, info, {Position = UDim2.new(1, news.scaled(news.PaddingX), 0, 0)})
    leaving.Completed:Connect(function(state)
        if state == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then old:Destroy() end
    end)
    leaving:Play()
    TweenService:Create(incoming, info, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)}):Play()
end

function tvState.News.show(index)
    local news = tvState.News
    local previous = news.Current
    news.Current = index
    news.Elapsed = 0
    if previous ~= index then
        news.swap()
    else
        news.refreshText()
    end
end

function tvState.News.setCurrent(index)
    local news = tvState.News
    local count = #news.Items
    news.Current = count > 0 and math.clamp(index, 1, count) or 0
end

function tvState.News.export()
    local list = {}
    for index, text in ipairs(tvState.News.Items) do
        list[index] = text
    end
    return list
end

function tvState.News.import(list)
    local news = tvState.News
    local items = {}
    for _, raw in ipairs(list) do
        if type(raw) == "string" and #items < news.MaxItems then
            items[#items + 1] = news.clean(raw)
        end
    end
    news.Items = items
    news.setCurrent(news.Current)
    local count = #items
    news.Selected = count > 0 and math.clamp(news.Selected, 1, count) or 0
end

function tvState.News.step(dt)
    local news = tvState.News
    if not news.Gui then return end
    news.clock()
    if news.Ticker then
        news.stepTicker(dt)
        return
    end
    if news.Auto and #news.Items > 1 then
        news.Elapsed = news.Elapsed + dt
        if news.Elapsed >= news.Interval then
            news.show(news.Current % #news.Items + 1)
        end
    end
end

function tvState.News.destroy()
    local news = tvState.News
    if news.Conn then
        news.Conn:Disconnect()
        news.Conn = nil
    end
    if news.Gui then
        news.Gui:Destroy()
        news.Gui = nil
    end
    news.Strip = nil
    news.Bar = nil
    news.TimeBox = nil
    news.Padding = nil
    news.TimeGradient = nil
    news.TimeLabel = nil
    news.HeadlineLabel = nil
    news.Ribbon = nil
    news.Period = 0
    news.RibbonDirty = false
    news.RibbonStrip = 0
end

function tvState.News.build()
    local news = tvState.News
    local gui = mk("ScreenGui", {
        Name = news.GuiName, ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
        DisplayOrder = news.DisplayOrder, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling})
    hideState.track(gui)
    local bar = mk("Frame", {Parent = gui, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -news.BottomMargin), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, news.scaled(news.Height)),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    local timeBox = mk("Frame", {Parent = bar, Size = UDim2.new(0, news.scaled(news.TimeWidth), 1, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0})
    news.TimeGradient = mk("UIGradient", {Parent = timeBox, Rotation = 90, Color = news.timeGradient()})
    news.TimeLabel = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = timeBox, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = news.TextFont, TextSize = math.max(1, news.scaled(news.TimeTextSize)),
        TextColor3 = news.TimeTextColor, Text = ""})
    local strip = mk("Frame", {Parent = bar, Position = UDim2.new(0, news.scaled(news.TimeWidth), 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -news.scaled(news.TimeWidth), 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = news.StripColor,
        BackgroundTransparency = news.StripTransparency, BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true})
    news.Padding = mk("UIPadding", {Parent = strip, PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, news.scaled(news.PaddingX)),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, news.scaled(news.PaddingX)), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, news.scaled(news.PaddingY)),
        PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, news.scaled(news.PaddingY))})
    news.Bar = bar
    news.TimeBox = timeBox
    news.Strip = strip
    news.Gui = gui
    news.LastMinute = -1
    news.Elapsed = 0
    news.TickerOffset = 0
    news.applyMode()
    news.clock()
    news.Conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(news.step)
    gui.Parent = guiParent
end

function tvState.News.apply(enabled)
    local news = tvState.News
    news.destroy()
    news.Enabled = enabled
    if enabled then news.build() end
end

function tvState.News.refreshPanel()
    local registry = toggleRegistry[tvState.News.PanelTitle]
    if registry and registry.rebuild then registry.rebuild() end
end

function tvState.News.add()
    local news = tvState.News
    local count = #news.Items
    if count >= news.MaxItems then return end
    news.Items[count + 1] = news.NewText
    news.Selected = count + 1
    news.show(count + 1)
    news.touchRibbon()
    news.refreshPanel()
end

function tvState.News.remove(index)
    local news = tvState.News
    if not news.Items[index] then return end
    local replaced = index == news.Current
    table.remove(news.Items, index)
    local count = #news.Items
    if index < news.Current then
        news.Current = news.Current - 1
    elseif replaced then
        news.Current = math.min(index, count)
    end
    news.Selected = math.min(index, count)
    news.Elapsed = 0
    if replaced then
        news.swap()
    else
        news.refreshText()
    end
    news.touchRibbon()
    news.refreshPanel()
end

function tvState.News.addListRow(Body, index)
    local news = tvState.News
    local selected = index == news.Selected
    local inset = news.StrokeThickness
    local holder = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, news.RowHeight + inset * 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.pinkOn, BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0 or 1, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = holder, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, news.RowCorner + inset)})
    local row = mk("TextButton", {Parent = holder, Position = UDim2.new(0, inset, 0, inset),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -inset * 2, 1, -inset * 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = selected and COL.text or COL.textMuted,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        Text = news.describe(index)})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, news.RowCorner)})
    mk("UIPadding", {Parent = row, PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 14), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 14)})
    news.Rows[index] = row
    toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(row, "select")
    row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local changed = news.Selected ~= index
        news.Selected = index
        news.show(index)
        if changed then news.refreshPanel() end
    end)
end

function tvState.News.addTextEditor(Body, index)
    local news = tvState.News
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, news.EditorHeight),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, news.RowCorner)})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 8),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 18), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = news.TextLabel, TextSize = 13,
        TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    local box = mk("TextBox", {Parent = row, Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 30),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -40), BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = COL.text,
        PlaceholderText = news.TextPlaceholder, PlaceholderColor3 = COL.textMuted,
        Text = news.Items[index], ClearTextOnFocus = false, MultiLine = true, TextWrapped = true,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = box, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)})
    mk("UIPadding", {Parent = box, PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8),
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)})
    box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        if string.find(box.Text, "[\r\n]") then
            box.Text = (string.gsub(box.Text, "[\r\n]+", ""))
            box:ReleaseFocus()
            return
        end
        if not news.Items[index] then return end
        news.Items[index] = news.clean(box.Text)
        if news.Current == index then news.refreshText() end
        news.touchRibbon()
        news.updateRow(index)
    end)
    box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        if news.Items[index] then box.Text = news.Items[index] end
    end)
end

function tvState.News.addDeleteButton(Body, index)
    local news = tvState.News
    local armed = false
    local button
    button = tvState.Logo.addButton(Body, news.DeleteText, COL.off, function()
        if armed then
            news.remove(index)
            return
        end
        armed = true
        button.Text = news.ConfirmText
        task.delay(news.ConfirmSeconds, function()
            if armed and button.Parent then
                armed = false
                button.Text = news.DeleteText
            end
        end)
    end)
end

function tvState.News.buildPanel(Body)
    local news = tvState.News
    news.Rows = {}
    addPanelHeader(Body, news.ListHeader)
    for index = 1, #news.Items do
        news.addListRow(Body, index)
    end
    tvState.Logo.addButton(Body, news.AddText, COL.pinkOn, news.add)
    local index = news.Selected
    if news.Items[index] then
        addPanelHeader(Body, news.EditorHeader .. index)
        news.addTextEditor(Body, index)
        news.addDeleteButton(Body, index)
    end
    addPanelHeader(Body, news.AutoHeader)
    local syncs = {}
    local function addModePill(label, get, set)
        local _, sync = terrainState.addPill(Body, label, get, function(v)
            set(v)
            for _, other in ipairs(syncs) do other() end
        end)
        syncs[#syncs + 1] = sync
    end
    addModePill(news.AutoLabel, function() return news.Auto end, news.setAuto)
    addModePill(news.SmoothLabel, function() return news.Smooth end, news.setSmooth)
    addSlider(Body, news.IntervalLabel, news.MinInterval, news.MaxInterval, news.Interval, 0, function(v)
        news.Interval = math.floor(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235))
    addModePill(news.TickerLabel, function() return news.Ticker end, news.setTicker)
    addSlider(Body, news.TickerSpeedLabel, news.MinTickerSpeed, news.MaxTickerSpeed, news.TickerSpeed, 0, function(v)
        news.TickerSpeed = math.floor(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235))
    addPanelHeader(Body, news.SizeHeader)
    addSlider(Body, news.SizeLabel, news.MinSize, news.MaxSize, news.Size, 2, function(v)
        news.Size = math.floor(v * 100 + 0.5) / 100
        news.applyLayout()
    end, Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235))
    addPanelHeader(Body, news.ColorsHeader)
    addColorPicker(Body, news.TimeColorLabel, news.TimeColor, function(color)
        news.TimeColor = color
        news.applyColors()
    end)
    addColorPicker(Body, news.StripColorLabel, news.StripColor, function(color)
        news.StripColor = color
        news.applyColors()
    end)
    addColorPicker(Body, news.TimeTextColorLabel, news.TimeTextColor, function(color)
        news.TimeTextColor = color
        news.applyColors()
    end)
    addColorPicker(Body, news.TextColorLabel, news.TextColor, function(color)
        news.TextColor = color
        news.applyColors()
    end)
end

tvState.News.Bind = keyBinds.new(tvState.News.PanelTitle)

addToggleRow(tabPages["scr"], tvState.News.PanelTitle, true, tvState.News.apply, tvState.News.buildPanel, 520, "📰",
    keyBinds.attacher(tvState.News.Bind))
