function uavState.setText(label, text)
    if label and label.Text ~= text then label.Text = text end
end

function uavState.setNumber(value)
    local state = uavState
    state.Number = math.clamp(math.floor(value), state.MinNumber, state.MaxNumber)
    state.setText(state.NumberLabel, tostring(state.Number))
end

function uavState.heatSource(humanoid)
    local state = uavState
    local model = humanoid.Parent
    if not model or not model:IsA("Model") or state.ThermalSources[model] then return end
    local highlight = mk("Highlight", {Name = state.ThermalName, FillColor = state.ThermalFill,
        FillTransparency = state.ThermalFillTransparency, OutlineTransparency = state.ThermalOutlineTransparency,
        DepthMode = state.ThermalDepthMode, Adornee = model, Parent = model})
    state.ThermalSources[model] = highlight
    highlight.Destroying:Connect(function()
        if state.ThermalSources[model] == highlight then state.ThermalSources[model] = nil end
    end)
end

function uavState.clearThermal()
    local state = uavState
    if state.ThermalConn then
        state.ThermalConn:Disconnect()
        state.ThermalConn = nil
    end
    if state.ThermalEffect then
        state.ThermalEffect:Destroy()
        state.ThermalEffect = nil
    end
    local sources = state.ThermalSources
    state.ThermalSources = {}
    for _, highlight in pairs(sources) do
        highlight:Destroy()
    end
end

function uavState.applyThermal()
    local state = uavState
    state.clearThermal()
    if not state.Enabled or not state.Thermal then return end
    state.ThermalEffect = mk("ColorCorrectionEffect", {Name = state.ThermalName,
        Saturation = state.ThermalSaturation, Contrast = state.ThermalContrast,
        Brightness = state.ThermalBrightness + state.ThermalLevel * state.ThermalLevelStep,
        TintColor = state.ThermalTint, Parent = Lighting})
    for _, object in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if object:IsA("Humanoid") then state.heatSource(object) end
    end
    state.ThermalConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
        if object:IsA("Humanoid") then state.heatSource(object) end
    end)
end

function uavState.setThermalLevel(value)
    local state = uavState
    state.ThermalLevel = math.clamp(math.floor(value), state.MinThermalLevel, state.MaxThermalLevel)
    if state.ThermalEffect then
        state.ThermalEffect.Brightness = state.ThermalBrightness + state.ThermalLevel * state.ThermalLevelStep
    end
end

function uavState.setThermal(value)
    local state = uavState
    state.Thermal = value
    state.applyThermal()
end

function uavState.pressThermal()
    local state = uavState
    if not state.Enabled then return end
    state.setThermal(not state.Thermal)
    if state.ThermalRefresh and state.ThermalRow and state.ThermalRow.Parent then state.ThermalRefresh() end
end

function uavState.capAvailable()
    return type(setfpscap) == "function"
end

function uavState.setFpsCap(value)
    if not uavState.capAvailable() then return false end
    return (pcall(setfpscap, value))
end

function uavState.applyFpsLimit()
    local state = uavState
    local target = nil
    if state.Enabled and state.FpsLimit < state.MaxFpsLimit then target = state.FpsLimit end
    if target == state.FpsCapValue then return end
    if state.setFpsCap(target or state.MaxFpsLimit) then state.FpsCapValue = target end
end

function uavState.unlockFps()
    local state = uavState
    if state.FpsUnlockOnStart then state.setFpsCap(state.MaxFpsLimit) end
end

function uavState.setFpsLimit(value)
    local state = uavState
    state.FpsLimit = math.clamp(math.floor(value), state.MinFpsLimit, state.MaxFpsLimit)
    state.applyFpsLimit()
end

function uavState.readPing()
    local state = uavState
    local okRead, value = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem[state.PingStatsItem]:GetValue()
    end)
    if okRead and type(value) == "number" then return value end
    return nil
end

function uavState.setFpsCounter(value)
    local state = uavState
    state.FpsCounter = value
    state.FpsFrames = 0
    state.FpsTime = 0
    if state.FpsHolder then state.FpsHolder.Visible = value end
end

function uavState.stepFps(dt)
    local state = uavState
    if not state.FpsHolder or not state.FpsCounter then return end
    state.FpsFrames = state.FpsFrames + 1
    state.FpsTime = state.FpsTime + dt
    if state.FpsTime < state.FpsInterval then return end
    state.setText(state.FpsLabel, string.format(state.FpsFormat, math.floor(state.FpsFrames / state.FpsTime + 0.5)))
    local ping = state.readPing()
    state.setText(state.PingLabel, ping and string.format(state.PingFormat, math.floor(ping + 0.5))
        or state.PingMissingText)
    state.setText(state.FrameLabel, string.format(state.FrameFormat, state.FpsTime / state.FpsFrames * 1000))
    state.FpsFrames = 0
    state.FpsTime = 0
end

function uavState.track(object, property)
    local state = uavState
    state.Painted[#state.Painted + 1] = {Object = object, Property = property}
    object[property] = state.Color
    return object
end

function uavState.paint()
    local state = uavState
    for _, entry in ipairs(state.Painted) do
        entry.Object[entry.Property] = state.Color
    end
end

function uavState.addMark(parent, onRight, y, length)
    local state = uavState
    local x = onRight and state.TickInset or (1 - state.TickInset - length)
    local mark = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(x, 0, y, 0), Size = UDim2.new(length, 0, 0, state.TickThickness),
        BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3})
    state.track(mark, "BackgroundColor3")
    return mark
end

function uavState.labelEdge()
    local state = uavState
    return state.TickInset + state.TickMajor + state.LabelGap
end

function uavState.addLabel(parent, onRight, y)
    local state = uavState
    local edge = state.labelEdge()
    local label = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = parent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(onRight and 0 or 1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(onRight and edge or 1 - edge, 0, y, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(state.LabelWidth, 0, 0, state.LabelTextSize + 6),
        Font = state.TextFont, TextSize = state.LabelTextSize, Text = "", ZIndex = 3,
        TextXAlignment = onRight and Enum.TextXAlignment.Left or Enum.TextXAlignment.Right})
    state.track(label, "TextColor3")
    return label
end

function uavState.addBox(parent, x)
    local state = uavState
    local box = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, state.BoxWidth, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = state.PanelColor,
        BackgroundTransparency = state.BoxTransparency, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4})
    local stroke = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = box, Thickness = state.BoxStroke,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
    state.track(stroke, "Color")
    local label = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = box, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Font = state.TextFont, TextSize = state.BoxTextSize, Text = "", ZIndex = 5})
    state.track(label, "TextColor3")
    return label
end

function uavState.addStatBox(parent, row)
    local state = uavState
    local box = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -row * (state.BoxHeight + state.FpsBoxGap)),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, state.BoxHeight), BackgroundColor3 = state.PanelColor,
        BackgroundTransparency = state.BoxTransparency, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4})
    local stroke = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = box, Thickness = state.BoxStroke,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
    state.track(stroke, "Color")
    local label = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = box, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Font = state.TextFont, TextSize = state.BoxTextSize, Text = "", ZIndex = 5})
    state.track(label, "TextColor3")
    return box, label
end

function uavState.addArrow(parent)
    local state = uavState
    local width = state.PointerWidth
    local height = state.ArrowHeight
    local holder = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, height)})
    for column = 0, width - 1 do
        local part = math.max(1, math.floor(height * (column + 0.5) / width + 0.5))
        state.track(mk("Frame", {Parent = holder, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, column, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, part),
            BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4}), "BackgroundColor3")
    end
    return holder
end

function uavState.pointerSpan()
    local state = uavState
    return state.PointerWidth + state.PointerGap
end

function uavState.addPointer(parent, onRight)
    local state = uavState
    if onRight then
        local group = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1 - state.PanelWidth, -state.RightBoxGap, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, state.BoxWidth, 0, state.BoxHeight)})
        return group, state.addBox(group, 0)
    end
    local group = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(state.PanelWidth, -state.pointerSpan(), 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, state.pointerSpan() + state.BoxWidth, 0, state.BoxHeight)})
    state.addArrow(group)
    return group, state.addBox(group, state.pointerSpan())
end

function uavState.placeSegment(frame, from, to, thickness)
    local delta = to - from
    local middle = (from + to) / 2
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(delta.Magnitude + 0.5), thickness)
    frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(middle.X + 0.5), math.floor(middle.Y + 0.5))
    frame.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(delta.Y, delta.X))
end

function uavState.addSegment(parent, from, to, thickness)
    local state = uavState
    local line = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4})
    state.placeSegment(line, from, to, thickness)
    state.track(line, "BackgroundColor3")
    return line
end

function uavState.addSideLine(parent)
    local state = uavState
    local glow = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundTransparency = state.SideGlowTransparency, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3})
    state.track(glow, "BackgroundColor3")
    local core = mk("Frame", {Parent = glow, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, state.SideThickness),
        BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4})
    state.track(core, "BackgroundColor3")
    return glow
end

function uavState.layout()
    local state = uavState
    local size = state.Root.AbsoluteSize
    local width, height = size.X, size.Y
    if width <= 0 or height <= 0 then return false end
    if width == state.LayoutW and height == state.LayoutH then return true end
    state.LayoutW = width
    state.LayoutH = height
    if state.ClockLabel then
        local clockSize = math.max(state.ClockMinSize, math.floor(state.PanelWidth * width * state.ClockRatio))
        state.ClockLabel.TextSize = clockSize
        local arc = state.ArcSize * state.PanelWidth * width
        local textWidth = state.measureClock(clockSize)
        local gap = state.ClockGap
        local floorLift = clockSize / 2 + gap + state.ArcDrop * height
        local diagonal = state.ArcClockRadius * arc * math.sqrt(0.5)
        local inset = math.max(diagonal, textWidth / 2 + gap)
        local lift = math.max(diagonal, floorLift)
        local corner = math.sqrt((inset + textWidth / 2) ^ 2 + (lift + clockSize / 2) ^ 2)
        if corner > arc - gap then
            inset = textWidth / 2 + gap
            local reach = math.sqrt(math.max(0, (arc - gap) ^ 2 - (inset + textWidth / 2) ^ 2)) - clockSize / 2
            lift = math.max(reach, floorLift)
        end
        state.ClockLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, textWidth + 2 * gap, 0, clockSize + state.ClockPadding)
        state.ClockLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -inset, 1, -lift)
    end
    if state.FpsHolder then
        local fpsSize = math.max(state.FpsMinSize, math.floor(state.PanelWidth * width * state.FpsWidthRatio
            / (state.FpsGlyphs * state.FpsGlyphWidth)))
        local boxHeight = fpsSize + state.FpsPadding
        state.FpsHolder.Size = UDim2.new(state.FpsBoxRatio, 0, 0,
            state.StatRows * boxHeight + (state.StatRows - 1) * state.FpsBoxGap)
        local rows = {{state.FrameBox, state.FrameLabel}, {state.PingBox, state.PingLabel},
            {state.FpsBox, state.FpsLabel}}
        for row, entry in ipairs(rows) do
            entry[1].Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, boxHeight)
            entry[1].Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -(row - 1) * (boxHeight + state.FpsBoxGap))
            entry[2].TextSize = fpsSize
        end
    end
    local centerX = width / 2
    local centerY = height / 2
    local run = centerY / math.tan(math.rad(state.GuideAngle))
    state.placeSegment(state.Guides[1], Vector2.new(centerX - run, 0), Vector2.new(centerX + run, height),
        state.ReticleThickness)
    state.placeSegment(state.Guides[2], Vector2.new(centerX + run, 0), Vector2.new(centerX - run, height),
        state.ReticleThickness)
    local half = state.ReticleSize * height / 2
    local start = half / math.tan(math.rad(state.GuideAngle))
    for index = 0, 3 do
        local signX = index % 2 == 0 and -1 or 1
        local signY = index < 2 and -1 or 1
        state.placeSegment(state.Rays[index + 1],
            Vector2.new(centerX + signX * start, centerY + signY * half),
            Vector2.new(centerX + signX * run, centerY + signY * centerY), state.ReticleThickness)
    end
    local length = math.floor(state.SideLength * height + 0.5)
    local reach = math.floor((state.SideGap + state.SideLength / 2) * height + 0.5)
    state.SideHolder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(centerX, centerY)
    state.SideHolder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2 * reach + length, state.SideGlow)
    state.SideLeft.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(length, state.SideGlow)
    state.SideRight.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(length, state.SideGlow)
    state.SideLeft.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -reach, 0.5, 0)
    state.SideRight.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, reach, 0.5, 0)
    return true
end

function uavState.stepBank(look, dt)
    local state = uavState
    local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
    local usable = flat.Magnitude > state.BankMinFlat
    local target = 0
    local last = state.LastLook
    state.Clock = state.Clock + dt
    if usable then
        if last then
            state.Yaw = state.Yaw + math.deg(math.atan2(last.X * flat.Z - last.Z * flat.X,
                last.X * flat.X + last.Z * flat.Z))
        end
        local times = state.YawTimes
        local angles = state.YawAngles
        times[#times + 1] = state.Clock
        angles[#angles + 1] = state.Yaw
        while #times > 2 and state.Clock - times[2] >= state.BankWindow do
            table.remove(times, 1)
            table.remove(angles, 1)
        end
        local span = state.Clock - times[1]
        if span > 0 then
            local drive = (state.Yaw - angles[1]) / span * state.BankGain / state.BankMax
            target = state.BankMax * math.tanh(drive)
        end
    else
        state.YawTimes = {}
        state.YawAngles = {}
    end
    state.LastLook = usable and flat or nil
    local omega = state.BankSpring
    local offset = state.Bank - target
    local carry = (state.BankSpeed + omega * offset) * dt
    local decay = math.exp(-omega * dt)
    state.Bank = target + (offset + carry) * decay
    state.BankSpeed = (state.BankSpeed - omega * carry) * decay
    return state.Bank * state.BankSign
end

function uavState.stepSides(look, dt)
    local state = uavState
    state.SideHolder.Rotation = state.stepBank(look, dt)
end

function uavState.addTriangle(parent, pointsUp)
    local state = uavState
    local width = state.TriangleWidth
    local height = state.TriangleHeight
    local thickness = state.TriangleThickness
    local holder = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, pointsUp and 1 or 0),
        Position = pointsUp and UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -state.TriangleGap) or UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, state.TriangleGap),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, height), Visible = false})
    for row = 0, height - 1 do
        local fraction = (row + 0.5) / height
        local rowWidth = math.max(1, math.floor(width * (pointsUp and fraction or 1 - fraction) + 0.5))
        mk("Frame", {Parent = holder, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, row),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(rowWidth, 1), BackgroundColor3 = state.PanelColor,
            BackgroundTransparency = state.BoxTransparency, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3})
    end
    local baseY = pointsUp and height or 0
    local tip = Vector2.new(width / 2, pointsUp and 0 or height)
    state.addSegment(holder, Vector2.new(0, baseY), Vector2.new(width, baseY), thickness)
    state.addSegment(holder, Vector2.new(0, baseY), tip, thickness)
    state.addSegment(holder, Vector2.new(width, baseY), tip, thickness)
    return holder
end

function uavState.stepTrend(height, dt)
    local state = uavState
    local last = state.LastHeight
    state.LastHeight = height
    if not last or dt <= 0 then return end
    local speed = (height - last) / dt
    if math.abs(speed) > state.TeleportSpeed then
        state.Rate = 0
        state.Trend = 0
    else
        state.Rate = state.Rate + (speed - state.Rate) * math.min(1, dt * state.RateSmooth)
    end
    local limit = state.Trend == 0 and state.RateOn or state.RateOff
    if state.Rate > limit then
        state.Trend = 1
    elseif state.Rate < -limit then
        state.Trend = -1
    else
        state.Trend = 0
    end
    state.UpTriangle.Visible = state.Trend > 0
    state.DownTriangle.Visible = state.Trend < 0
end

function uavState.buildReticle(parent)
    local state = uavState
    local frame = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), SizeConstraint = Enum.SizeConstraint.RelativeYY,
        Size = UDim2.new(state.ReticleSize, 0, state.ReticleSize, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3})
    local count = state.ReticleDashes
    local length = 1 / (2 * count - 1)
    local thickness = state.ReticleThickness
    for index = 0, count - 1 do
        local at = index * 2 * length
        state.track(mk("Frame", {Parent = frame, Position = UDim2.new(at, 0, 0, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(length, 0, 0, thickness), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3}), "BackgroundColor3")
        state.track(mk("Frame", {Parent = frame, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
            Position = UDim2.new(at, 0, 1, 0), Size = UDim2.new(length, 0, 0, thickness),
            BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3}), "BackgroundColor3")
        state.track(mk("Frame", {Parent = frame, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, at, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, thickness, length, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3}), "BackgroundColor3")
        state.track(mk("Frame", {Parent = frame, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, at, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, thickness, length, 0),
            BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3}), "BackgroundColor3")
    end
    local inner = mk("Frame", {Parent = frame, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(state.InnerSquare, 0, state.InnerSquare, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3})
    state.track(mk("UIStroke", {Parent = inner, Thickness = thickness,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}), "Color")
    state.InnerFrame = inner
end

function uavState.applyVariant()
    local state = uavState
    local full = state.Variant == 1
    for _, guide in ipairs(state.Guides) do guide.Visible = full end
    for _, ray in ipairs(state.Rays) do ray.Visible = not full end
    if state.InnerFrame then state.InnerFrame.Visible = full end
end

function uavState.buildArc(panel)
    local state = uavState
    local clip = mk("Frame", {Parent = panel, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1 + state.ArcDrop, 0),
        SizeConstraint = Enum.SizeConstraint.RelativeXX,
        Size = UDim2.new(state.ArcSize, state.ArcMargin, state.ArcSize, state.ArcMargin),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 3})
    local holder = mk("Frame", {Parent = clip, Position = UDim2.new(0, state.ArcMargin, 0, state.ArcMargin),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -state.ArcMargin, 1, -state.ArcMargin), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3})
    local ring = mk("Frame", {Parent = holder, Size = UDim2.new(2, 0, 2, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = ring, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.5, 0)})
    local stroke = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = ring, Thickness = state.ArcThickness,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
    state.track(stroke, "Color")
    state.ClockLabel = state.track(mk("TextLabel", {Parent = clip, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, state.ClockMinSize + state.ClockPadding),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = state.TextFont, TextSize = state.ClockMinSize,
        Text = "", ZIndex = 4}), "TextColor3")
end

function uavState.measureClock(size)
    local state = uavState
    local sample = string.format(state.ClockFormat, 0, 0, 0)
    local okMeasure, bounds = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("TextService"):GetTextSize(sample, size, state.TextFont, Vector2.new(10000, 10000))
    end)
    if okMeasure and bounds then return bounds.X end
    return #sample * state.ClockGlyphWidth * size
end

function uavState.clockText()
    local state = uavState
    local total = math.floor(Lighting.ClockTime * 3600) % 86400
    return string.format(state.ClockFormat, math.floor(total / 3600), math.floor(total % 3600 / 60), total % 60)
end

function uavState.stepClock()
    local state = uavState
    state.setText(state.ClockLabel, state.clockText())
end

function uavState.fovFor(zoom, base)
    local state = uavState
    local half = math.rad((base or state.BaseFov) / 2)
    local fov = math.deg(2 * math.atan(math.tan(half) / zoom))
    return math.clamp(fov, state.MinFov, state.MaxFov)
end

function uavState.zoomedFov(base)
    local state = uavState
    if not state.Enabled or state.Zoom <= 1 + state.ZoomEpsilon then return base end
    return state.fovFor(state.Zoom, base)
end

function uavState.applyZoom()
    local state = uavState
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera or not state.Enabled or state.ExternalCamera then return end
    if state.Zoom > 1 + state.ZoomEpsilon then
        camera.FieldOfView = state.fovFor(state.Zoom)
        state.ZoomApplied = true
    elseif state.ZoomApplied then
        camera.FieldOfView = state.BaseFov
        state.ZoomApplied = false
    end
end

function uavState.stepTape(height)
    local state = uavState
    local step = state.TapeStep
    local half = state.TapeHalf
    local nearest = math.floor(height / step + 0.5)
    local reach = (state.ScaleBottom - state.ScaleTop) / 2
    for slot, entry in ipairs(state.Tape) do
        local index = nearest + slot - (half + 2)
        local y = 0.5 - (index * step - height) / (half * step) * reach
        local inside = y >= state.ScaleTop - 0.001 and y <= state.ScaleBottom + 0.001
        local major = index % state.TapeLabelEvery == 0
        entry.Mark.Visible = inside
        entry.Label.Visible = inside and major
        if inside then
            entry.Mark.Position = UDim2.new(state.TickInset, 0, y, 0)
            entry.Mark.Size = UDim2.new(major and state.TickMajor or state.TickMinor, 0, 0, state.TickThickness)
            entry.Label.Position = UDim2.new(entry.LabelX, 0, y, 0)
            if major then
                state.setText(entry.Label, string.format("%d", index * step))
            end
        end
    end
end

function uavState.stepNoise(dt)
    local state = uavState
    local frames = state.NoiseFrames
    if #frames == 0 then return end
    if state.Noise <= 0 then
        for _, frame in ipairs(frames) do
            frame.Visible = false
        end
        return
    end
    state.NoiseTimer = state.NoiseTimer + dt
    if state.NoiseTimer < state.NoiseInterval then return end
    state.NoiseTimer = 0
    for _, frame in ipairs(frames) do
        frame.Visible = false
    end
    local frame = frames[state.NoiseIndex]
    frame.ImageTransparency = 1 - state.Noise
    frame.TileSize = UDim2.new(0, math.random(state.NoiseTileMin, state.NoiseTileMax),
        0, math.random(state.NoiseTileMin, state.NoiseTileMax))
    frame.Visible = true
    state.NoiseIndex = state.NoiseIndex % #frames + 1
end

function uavState.firstPerson()
    local state = uavState
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return false end
    return (camera.CFrame.Position - camera.Focus.Position).Magnitude < state.FirstPersonDistance
end

function uavState.zoomContext()
    local state = uavState
    return state.ExternalCamera or state.firstPerson()
end

function uavState.stepZoom(dt)
    local state = uavState
    if not state.zoomContext() then state.ZoomTarget = state.MinZoom end
    if state.Zoom == state.ZoomTarget then return end
    local gap = math.log(state.ZoomTarget) - math.log(state.Zoom)
    if math.abs(gap) < state.ZoomEpsilon then
        state.Zoom = state.ZoomTarget
    else
        state.Zoom = state.Zoom * math.exp(gap * (1 - math.exp(-state.ZoomSmooth * dt)))
    end
    state.applyZoom()
end

function uavState.zoomCeiling()
    local state = uavState
    local ratio = math.tan(math.rad(state.BaseFov / 2)) / math.tan(math.rad(state.MinFov / 2))
    return math.max(state.MinZoom, math.min(state.MaxZoom, ratio))
end

function uavState.setMaxZoom(value)
    local state = uavState
    state.MaxZoom = math.clamp(math.floor(value), state.MinZoom, state.MaxZoomLimit)
    state.ZoomTarget = math.min(state.ZoomTarget, state.zoomCeiling())
end

function uavState.onWheelAction(_, inputState, input)
    local state = uavState
    local delta = input.Position.Z
    if not state.zoomContext() then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass end
    if not state.ExternalCamera and delta < 0 and state.ZoomTarget <= state.MinZoom then
        return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
    end
    if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Change then
        state.ZoomTarget = math.clamp(state.ZoomTarget * state.WheelStep ^ delta, state.MinZoom, state.zoomCeiling())
    end
    return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
end

function uavState.refreshWheel()
    local state = uavState
    local actions = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
    local wanted = state.Enabled and state.WheelZoom
    if wanted and not state.WheelBound then
        state.WheelBound = pcall(function()
            actions:BindActionAtPriority(state.WheelAction, state.onWheelAction, false,
                state.WheelPriority, Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel)
        end)
    elseif not wanted and state.WheelBound then
        pcall(function() actions:UnbindAction(state.WheelAction) end)
        state.WheelBound = false
    end
    if not wanted then state.ZoomTarget = state.MinZoom end
end

function uavState.step(dt)
    local state = uavState
    if not state.Gui then return end
    state.stepFps(dt)
    state.stepClock()
    if not state.layout() then return end
    state.stepZoom(dt)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        local cframe = camera.CFrame
        local pitch = math.deg(math.asin(math.clamp(cframe.LookVector.Y, -1, 1)))
        local limit = state.PitchLimit
        local clamped = math.clamp(pitch, -limit, limit)
        local span = state.ScaleBottom - state.ScaleTop
        local pointerY = state.ScaleTop + (limit - clamped) / (2 * limit) * span
        state.PitchPointer.Position = UDim2.new(state.PanelWidth, -state.pointerSpan(), pointerY, 0)
        state.setText(state.PitchValue, string.format("%d", math.floor(pitch + 0.5)))
        local height = cframe.Position.Y
        state.stepTape(height)
        state.setText(state.HeightValue, string.format("%d", math.floor(height + 0.5)))
        state.stepTrend(height, dt)
        state.stepSides(cframe.LookVector, dt)
        if state.Zoom > 1 + state.ZoomEpsilon and not state.ExternalCamera then
            camera.FieldOfView = state.fovFor(state.Zoom)
        end
    end
    state.setText(state.ZoomValue, string.format(
        state.Zoom >= state.ZoomWideFrom and state.ZoomFormatWide or state.ZoomFormat, state.Zoom))
    state.stepNoise(dt)
end

function uavState.destroy()
    local state = uavState
    state.clearThermal()
    if state.Conn then
        state.Conn:Disconnect()
        state.Conn = nil
    end
    if state.ZoomApplied then
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then camera.FieldOfView = state.BaseFov end
        state.ZoomApplied = false
    end
    state.Zoom = state.MinZoom
    state.ZoomTarget = state.MinZoom
    if state.Gui then
        state.Gui:Destroy()
        state.Gui = nil
    end
    state.Root = nil
    state.PitchPointer = nil
    state.PitchValue = nil
    state.HeightValue = nil
    state.ZoomValue = nil
    state.NumberLabel = nil
    state.FpsHolder = nil
    state.FpsBox = nil
    state.PingBox = nil
    state.FrameBox = nil
    state.FpsLabel = nil
    state.PingLabel = nil
    state.FrameLabel = nil
    state.ClockLabel = nil
    state.FpsFrames = 0
    state.FpsTime = 0
    state.UpTriangle = nil
    state.DownTriangle = nil
    state.LastHeight = nil
    state.Rate = 0
    state.Trend = 0
    state.Guides = {}
    state.Rays = {}
    state.InnerFrame = nil
    state.SideLeft = nil
    state.SideRight = nil
    state.SideHolder = nil
    state.Bank = 0
    state.BankSpeed = 0
    state.Yaw = 0
    state.Clock = 0
    state.YawTimes = {}
    state.YawAngles = {}
    state.LastLook = nil
    state.LayoutW = 0
    state.LayoutH = 0
    state.Tape = {}
    state.Painted = {}
    state.NoiseFrames = {}
end

function uavState.build()
    local state = uavState
    local gui = mk("ScreenGui", {
        Name = state.GuiName, ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
        DisplayOrder = state.DisplayOrder, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling})
    hideState.track(gui)
    local root = mk("Frame", {Parent = gui, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    local noise = {}
    for index, id in ipairs(state.NoiseTextures) do
        noise[index] = mk("ImageLabel", {Parent = root, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Image = "rbxassetid://" .. id,
            ImageTransparency = 1, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Tile,
            TileSize = UDim2.new(0, state.NoiseTileMax, 0, state.NoiseTileMax), Visible = false, ZIndex = 1})
    end
    state.NoiseFrames = noise
    state.NoiseTimer = 0
    state.NoiseIndex = 1
    local left = mk("Frame", {Parent = root, Size = UDim2.new(state.PanelWidth, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = state.PanelColor, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2})
    local right = mk("Frame", {Parent = root, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(state.PanelWidth, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = state.PanelColor,
        BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2})
    state.track(mk("Frame", {Parent = left, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, state.EdgeThickness, 1, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3}), "BackgroundColor3")
    state.track(mk("Frame", {Parent = right, Size = UDim2.new(0, state.EdgeThickness, 1, 0),
        BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3}), "BackgroundColor3")
    local limit = state.PitchLimit
    local span = state.ScaleBottom - state.ScaleTop
    local steps = math.floor(2 * limit / state.PitchStep + 0.5)
    for index = 0, steps do
        local value = limit - index * state.PitchStep
        local y = state.ScaleTop + index / steps * span
        local major = index % state.PitchLabelEvery == 0
        state.addMark(left, false, y, major and state.TickMajor or state.TickMinor)
        if major then
            state.addLabel(left, false, y).Text = string.format("%d", value)
        end
    end
    local tape = {}
    for slot = 1, 2 * state.TapeHalf + 3 do
        tape[slot] = {
            Mark = state.addMark(right, true, 0.5, state.TickMajor),
            Label = state.addLabel(right, true, 0.5),
            LabelX = state.labelEdge(),
        }
    end
    state.Tape = tape
    state.Root = root
    state.Guides = {}
    for index = 1, 2 do
        state.Guides[index] = state.track(mk("Frame", {Parent = root, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3}), "BackgroundColor3")
    end
    state.Rays = {}
    for index = 1, 4 do
        state.Rays[index] = state.track(mk("Frame", {Parent = root, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3}), "BackgroundColor3")
    end
    state.SideHolder = mk("Frame", {Parent = root, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3})
    state.SideLeft = state.addSideLine(state.SideHolder)
    state.SideRight = state.addSideLine(state.SideHolder)
    state.buildReticle(root)
    state.applyVariant()
    state.buildArc(left)
    state.PitchPointer, state.PitchValue = state.addPointer(root, false)
    local heightGroup, heightValue = state.addPointer(root, true)
    state.HeightValue = heightValue
    state.UpTriangle = state.addTriangle(heightGroup, true)
    state.DownTriangle = state.addTriangle(heightGroup, false)
    local zoomHolder = mk("Frame", {Parent = right, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, state.ZoomBoxTop, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, state.BoxWidth, 0, state.BoxHeight),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4})
    state.ZoomValue = state.addBox(zoomHolder, 0)
    state.FpsHolder = mk("Frame", {Parent = right, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, (state.ScaleBottom + 1) / 2, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(state.FpsBoxRatio, 0, 0,
            state.StatRows * state.BoxHeight + (state.StatRows - 1) * state.FpsBoxGap),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = state.FpsCounter, ZIndex = 4})
    state.FrameBox, state.FrameLabel = state.addStatBox(state.FpsHolder, 0)
    state.PingBox, state.PingLabel = state.addStatBox(state.FpsHolder, 1)
    state.FpsBox, state.FpsLabel = state.addStatBox(state.FpsHolder, 2)
    state.FpsFrames = 0
    state.FpsTime = 0
    state.NumberLabel = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = left, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(state.UavNumberX, 0, 0, state.UavNumberTop), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, state.UavNumberSize),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = state.TextFont, TextSize = state.UavNumberSize,
        TextColor3 = state.UavNumberColor, Text = tostring(state.Number), ZIndex = 4})
    state.Gui = gui
    gui.Parent = guiParent
end

function uavState.apply(enabled)
    local state = uavState
    state.destroy()
    state.Enabled = enabled
    state.refreshWheel()
    state.applyFpsLimit()
    if not enabled then return end
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    state.BaseFov = state.FovSource and state.FovSource() or (camera and camera.FieldOfView or state.DefaultFov)
    state.ZoomApplied = false
    state.build()
    state.applyZoom()
    state.applyThermal()
    state.Conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(state.step)
end

function uavState.buildPanel(Body)
    local state = uavState
    addPanelHeader(Body, state.VariantHeader)
    tvState.addSwitch(Body, state.Variants, state.Variant, function(index)
        state.Variant = index
        state.applyVariant()
    end)
    addPanelHeader(Body, state.ViewHeader)
    addSlider(Body, state.NoiseText, state.MinNoise, state.MaxNoise, state.Noise, 2, function(v)
        state.Noise = math.floor(v * 100 + 0.5) / 100
    end, Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235))
    addSlider(Body, state.NumberText, state.MinNumber, state.MaxNumber, state.Number, 0, function(v)
        state.setNumber(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235))
    terrainState.addPill(Body, state.WheelText, function() return state.WheelZoom end, function(value)
        state.WheelZoom = value
        state.refreshWheel()
    end)
    addSlider(Body, state.MaxZoomText, state.MinZoom, state.MaxZoomLimit, state.MaxZoom, 0, function(v)
        state.setMaxZoom(v)
    end, Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235), true)
    local thermalRow, thermalRefresh = terrainState.addPill(Body, state.ThermalText,
        function() return state.Thermal end, function(value)
            state.setThermal(value)
        end)
    state.ThermalRow = thermalRow
    state.ThermalRefresh = thermalRefresh
    local thermalPill = thermalRow:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextButton")
    if thermalPill then
        thermalPill.Position = UDim2.new(1, -(state.KeyRightInset + state.KeyWidth + state.KeyGap), 0.5, 0)
    end
    local keyButton = mk("TextButton", {Parent = thermalRow, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -state.KeyRightInset, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, state.KeyWidth, 0, state.KeyHeight),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "", TextSize = 11,
        TextColor3 = COL.textMuted, AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = keyButton, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)})
    mk("UIStroke", {Parent = keyButton, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.fromRGB(48, 42, 64),
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
    keyBinds.attacher(state.ThermalBind)(keyButton)
    addSlider(Body, state.ThermalLevelText, state.MinThermalLevel, state.MaxThermalLevel, state.ThermalLevel, 0,
        function(v) state.setThermalLevel(v) end, Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235))
    terrainState.addPill(Body, state.FpsText, function() return state.FpsCounter end, function(value)
        state.setFpsCounter(value)
    end)
    addSlider(Body, state.capAvailable() and state.FpsLimitText or state.FpsLimitMissingText,
        state.MinFpsLimit, state.MaxFpsLimit, state.FpsLimit, 0,
        function(v) state.setFpsLimit(v) end, Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235), true)
    addPanelHeader(Body, state.ColorsHeader)
    addColorPicker(Body, state.ColorText, state.Color, function(color)
        state.Color = color
        state.paint()
    end)
end

addSectionHeader(tabPages["scr"], uavState.SectionTitle)

uavState.Bind = keyBinds.new(uavState.PanelTitle)

uavState.ThermalBind = keyBinds.new(uavState.ThermalText, nil, uavState.pressThermal)

addToggleRow(tabPages["scr"], uavState.PanelTitle, true, uavState.apply, uavState.buildPanel, uavState.PanelHeight, "📹",
    keyBinds.attacher(uavState.Bind))

uavState.unlockFps()

local freecamState = {
    Enabled = false,
    PanelTitle = "Фри кам",
    PanelHeight = 508,
    ControlHeader = "УПРАВЛЕНИЕ",
    SmoothHeader = "ПЛАВНОСТЬ",
    SpeedText = "Скорость",
    SlowText = "Замедление на Ctrl (×)",
    FastText = "Ускорение на Shift (×)",
    SensitivityText = "Чувствительность",
    MoveSmoothText = "Плавность движения",
    LookSmoothText = "Плавность поворота",
    FovSmoothText = "Плавность зума",
    Accent = Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235),
    Speed = 48,
    MinSpeed = 2,
    MaxSpeed = 300,
    SlowMultiplier = 0.25,
    MinSlow = 0.02,
    MaxSlow = 1,
    FastMultiplier = 3,
    MinFast = 1,
    MaxFast = 20,
    Sensitivity = 1,
    MinSensitivity = 0.1,
    MaxSensitivity = 3,
    LookBase = 0.0033,
    MoveSmooth = 0.5,
    LookSmooth = 0.5,
    FovSmooth = 0.5,
    OmegaFast = 40,
    OmegaSlow = 1.5,
    PitchLimit = math.rad(85),
    ReferenceFov = 70,
    WheelStep = 1.1,
    MinFov = 5,
    MaxFov = 120,
    FocusDistance = 16,
    MaxStep = 0.1,
    StreamInterval = 1.5,
    StreamDistance = 64,
    StreamTimeout = 1,
    RestoreDelay = 0.15,
    CursorOwner = "freecam",
    ZoomFloor = 0.5,
    ZoomLimits = nil,
    Axes = {
        [Enum.KeyCode.W] = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),
        [Enum.KeyCode.S] = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1),
        [Enum.KeyCode.A] = Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0),
        [Enum.KeyCode.D] = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0),
        [Enum.KeyCode.E] = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
        [Enum.KeyCode.Q] = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0),
        [Enum.KeyCode.Space] = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
        [Enum.KeyCode.LeftAlt] = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0),
        [Enum.KeyCode.RightAlt] = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0),
    },
    SlowKeys = {Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl, Enum.KeyCode.RightControl},
    FastKeys = {Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift, Enum.KeyCode.RightShift},
    LookButton = Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2,
    KeyAction = "FreecamKeys",
    WheelAction = "FreecamWheel",
    InputPriority = Enum.ContextActionPriority.High.Value,
    StepName = "FreecamStep",
    StepPriority = Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1,
    Saved = nil,
    Conns = {},
    Pressed = {},
    Position = Vector3.zero,
    Rotation = Vector2.zero,
    Fov = 70,
    FovTarget = 70,
    FovRate = 0,
    Move = Vector3.zero,
    MoveRate = Vector3.zero,
    RotationTarget = Vector2.zero,
    LookRate = Vector2.zero,
    MouseDelta = Vector2.zero,
    Looking = false,
    StreamClock = 0,
    StreamAt = nil,
    StreamBusy = false,
}

function freecamState.omega(smooth)
    local state = freecamState
    return state.OmegaFast * (state.OmegaSlow / state.OmegaFast) ^ smooth
end

function freecamState.spring(value, rate, target, omega, dt)
    local offset = value - target
    local carry = (rate + offset * omega) * dt
    local decay = math.exp(-omega * dt)
    return target + (offset + carry) * decay, (rate - carry * omega) * decay
end

function freecamState.onKey(_, inputState, input)
    freecamState.Pressed[input.KeyCode] = inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin
    return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
end

function freecamState.onWheel(_, inputState, input)
    local state = freecamState
    if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Change then
        state.FovTarget = math.clamp(state.FovTarget * state.WheelStep ^ (-input.Position.Z),
            state.MinFov, state.MaxFov)
    end
    return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
end

function freecamState.held(keys)
    local pressed = freecamState.Pressed
    for _, key in ipairs(keys) do
        if pressed[key] then return true end
    end
    return false
end

function freecamState.setLooking(on)
    local state = freecamState
    if state.Looking == on then return end
    state.Looking = on
    UserInputService.MouseBehavior = on and Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCurrentPosition or Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
end

function freecamState.stream()
    local state = freecamState
    if state.StreamBusy or not workspace.StreamingEnabled then return end
    if state.StreamAt and (state.Position - state.StreamAt).Magnitude < state.StreamDistance then return end
    state.StreamBusy = true
    state.StreamAt = state.Position
    local position = state.Position
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function() player:RequestStreamAroundAsync(position, state.StreamTimeout) end)
        state.StreamBusy = false
    end)
end

function freecamState.step(dt)
    local state = freecamState
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end
    if camera.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable end
    dt = math.min(dt, state.MaxStep)
    if dt <= 0 then return end
    if state.Looking then UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCurrentPosition end
    hideState.setCursor(state.CursorOwner, state.Looking or not Root.Visible)
    state.StreamClock = state.StreamClock + dt
    if state.StreamClock >= state.StreamInterval then
        state.StreamClock = 0
        state.stream()
    end
    local push = Vector3.zero
    for key, axis in pairs(state.Axes) do
        if state.Pressed[key] then push = push + axis end
    end
    if push.Magnitude > 1 then push = push.Unit end
    local speed = state.Speed
    if state.held(state.SlowKeys) then speed = speed * state.SlowMultiplier end
    if state.held(state.FastKeys) then speed = speed * state.FastMultiplier end
    state.Move, state.MoveRate = state.spring(state.Move, state.MoveRate, push * speed,
        state.omega(state.MoveSmooth), dt)
    local shownFov = uavState.zoomedFov(state.Fov)
    local zoomFactor = math.tan(math.rad(shownFov / 2)) / math.tan(math.rad(state.ReferenceFov / 2))
    local scale = state.LookBase * state.Sensitivity * zoomFactor
    local target = state.RotationTarget
    state.RotationTarget = Vector2.new(
        math.clamp(target.X - state.MouseDelta.Y * scale, -state.PitchLimit, state.PitchLimit),
        target.Y - state.MouseDelta.X * scale)
    state.MouseDelta = Vector2.zero
    state.Rotation, state.LookRate = state.spring(state.Rotation, state.LookRate, state.RotationTarget,
        state.omega(state.LookSmooth), dt)
    local turns = math.floor(state.Rotation.Y / (2 * math.pi)) * 2 * math.pi
    if turns ~= 0 then
        state.Rotation = state.Rotation - Vector2.new(0, turns)
        state.RotationTarget = state.RotationTarget - Vector2.new(0, turns)
    end
    local pitch = state.Rotation.X
    local yaw = state.Rotation.Y
    state.Fov, state.FovRate = state.spring(state.Fov, state.FovRate, state.FovTarget,
        state.omega(state.FovSmooth), dt)
    local orientation = CFrame.fromOrientation(pitch, yaw, 0)
    local flat = orientation:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(state.Move.X, 0, state.Move.Z))
    state.Position = state.Position + (flat + Vector3.new(0, state.Move.Y, 0)) * dt
    local cframe = CFrame.new(state.Position) * orientation
    camera.CFrame = cframe
    camera.Focus = cframe * CFrame.new(0, 0, -state.FocusDistance)
    camera.FieldOfView = uavState.zoomedFov(state.Fov)
end

function freecamState.start()
    local state = freecamState
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return false end
    local baseFov = uavState.ZoomApplied and uavState.BaseFov
        or (uavState.FovSource and uavState.FovSource()) or camera.FieldOfView
    state.Saved = {
        Type = camera.CameraType,
        CFrame = camera.CFrame,
        Focus = camera.Focus,
        Fov = baseFov,
        Distance = (camera.CFrame.Position - camera.Focus.Position).Magnitude,
    }
    local pitch, yaw = camera.CFrame:ToOrientation()
    state.Position = camera.CFrame.Position
    state.Rotation = Vector2.new(pitch, yaw)
    state.RotationTarget = state.Rotation
    state.Fov = baseFov
    state.FovTarget = baseFov
    state.FovRate = 0
    state.Move = Vector3.zero
    state.MoveRate = Vector3.zero
    state.LookRate = Vector2.zero
    state.MouseDelta = Vector2.zero
    state.Pressed = {}
    state.Looking = false
    state.StreamClock = 0
    state.StreamAt = nil
    camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
    uavState.ExternalCamera = true
    UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
    local actions = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
    local keys = {}
    for key in pairs(state.Axes) do keys[#keys + 1] = key end
    for _, key in ipairs(state.SlowKeys) do keys[#keys + 1] = key end
    for _, key in ipairs(state.FastKeys) do keys[#keys + 1] = key end
    pcall(function()
        actions:BindActionAtPriority(state.KeyAction, state.onKey, false, state.InputPriority, table.unpack(keys))
        actions:BindActionAtPriority(state.WheelAction, state.onWheel, false, state.InputPriority,
            Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel)
    end)
    state.Conns[#state.Conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
        if not processed and input.UserInputType == state.LookButton then state.setLooking(true) end
    end)
    state.Conns[#state.Conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == state.LookButton then state.setLooking(false) end
    end)
    state.Conns[#state.Conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if state.Looking and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            state.MouseDelta = state.MouseDelta + Vector2.new(input.Delta.X, input.Delta.Y)
        end
    end)
    state.Conns[#state.Conns + 1] = UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased:Connect(function()
        state.Pressed = {}
        state.setLooking(false)
    end)
    RunService:BindToRenderStep(state.StepName, state.StepPriority, state.step)
    return true
end

function freecamState.lockZoom(saved)
    local state = freecamState
    if saved.Type ~= Enum.CameraType.Custom then return end
    local distance = math.max(saved.Distance, state.ZoomFloor)
    if not state.ZoomLimits then
        state.ZoomLimits = {player.CameraMinZoomDistance, player.CameraMaxZoomDistance}
    end
    local limits = state.ZoomLimits
    pcall(function()
        player.CameraMinZoomDistance = math.min(limits[1], distance)
        player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = math.max(limits[2], distance)
        player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = distance
        player.CameraMinZoomDistance = distance
    end)
end

function freecamState.releaseZoom()
    local state = freecamState
    local limits = state.ZoomLimits
    if not limits then return end
    state.ZoomLimits = nil
    pcall(function()
        player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = limits[2]
        player.CameraMinZoomDistance = limits[1]
    end)
end

function freecamState.stop()
    local state = freecamState
    local saved = state.Saved
    if not saved then return end
    state.Saved = nil
    uavState.ExternalCamera = false
    state.setLooking(false)
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(state.StepName) end)
    local actions = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
    pcall(function()
        actions:UnbindAction(state.KeyAction)
        actions:UnbindAction(state.WheelAction)
    end)
    for _, conn in ipairs(state.Conns) do conn:Disconnect() end
    state.Conns = {}
    state.Pressed = {}
    hideState.clearCursor(state.CursorOwner)
    UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        camera.FieldOfView = saved.Fov
        camera.CameraType = saved.Type
        camera.CFrame = saved.CFrame
        camera.Focus = saved.Focus
    end
    state.lockZoom(saved)
    uavState.applyZoom()
    task.delay(state.RestoreDelay, function()
        state.releaseZoom()
        if state.Enabled then return end
        local current = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if current then current.FieldOfView = saved.Fov end
        uavState.applyZoom()
        if not uavState.firstPerson() then
            UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
        end
    end)
end

function freecamState.apply(enabled)
    local state = freecamState
    if enabled == state.Enabled then return end
    state.Enabled = enabled
    if enabled then
        if not state.start() then state.Enabled = false end
    else
        state.stop()
    end
end

function freecamState.buildPanel(Body)
    local state = freecamState
    addPanelHeader(Body, state.ControlHeader)
    addSlider(Body, state.SpeedText, state.MinSpeed, state.MaxSpeed, state.Speed, 0, function(v)
        state.Speed = v
    end, state.Accent, true)
    addSlider(Body, state.SlowText, state.MinSlow, state.MaxSlow, state.SlowMultiplier, 2, function(v)
        state.SlowMultiplier = v
    end, state.Accent, true)
    addSlider(Body, state.FastText, state.MinFast, state.MaxFast, state.FastMultiplier, 1, function(v)
        state.FastMultiplier = v
    end, state.Accent, true)
    addSlider(Body, state.SensitivityText, state.MinSensitivity, state.MaxSensitivity, state.Sensitivity, 2,
        function(v) state.Sensitivity = v end, state.Accent)
    addPanelHeader(Body, state.SmoothHeader)
    addSlider(Body, state.MoveSmoothText, 0, 1, state.MoveSmooth, 2, function(v)
        state.MoveSmooth = v
    end, state.Accent)
    addSlider(Body, state.LookSmoothText, 0, 1, state.LookSmooth, 2, function(v)
        state.LookSmooth = v
    end, state.Accent)
    addSlider(Body, state.FovSmoothText, 0, 1, state.FovSmooth, 2, function(v)
        state.FovSmooth = v
    end, state.Accent)
end

local cameraOffset = {
    StepName = "CameraOffsetStep",
    StepPriority = Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 2,
    MaxStep = 0.1,
    Sources = {},
    Bound = false,
    Humanoid = nil,
    Applied = Vector3.zero,
    Written = nil,
}

function cameraOffset.release()
    local state = cameraOffset
    local humanoid = state.Humanoid
    if humanoid and humanoid.Parent and state.Written ~= nil and humanoid.CameraOffset == state.Written then
        humanoid.CameraOffset = humanoid.CameraOffset - state.Applied
    end
    state.Humanoid = nil
    state.Applied = Vector3.zero
    state.Written = nil
end

function cameraOffset.step(dt)
    local state = cameraOffset
    dt = math.min(dt, state.MaxStep)
    local character = player.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = humanoid and humanoid.RootPart
    if not root then
        state.release()
        return
    end
    if state.Humanoid ~= humanoid then
        state.release()
        state.Humanoid = humanoid
    end
    local total = Vector3.zero
    for _, source in pairs(state.Sources) do
        total = total + source(dt, humanoid, root)
    end
    local current = humanoid.CameraOffset
    if state.Written == nil or current ~= state.Written then
        state.Applied = Vector3.zero
    end
    humanoid.CameraOffset = current - state.Applied + total
    state.Applied = total
    state.Written = humanoid.CameraOffset
end

function cameraOffset.refresh()
    local state = cameraOffset
    local used = next(state.Sources) ~= nil
    if used and not state.Bound then
        state.Bound = true
        RunService:BindToRenderStep(state.StepName, state.StepPriority, state.step)
    elseif not used and state.Bound then
        state.Bound = false
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(state.StepName) end)
        state.release()
    end
end

function cameraOffset.set(name, source)
    cameraOffset.Sources[name] = source
    cameraOffset.refresh()
end

local firstPersonState = {
    Enabled = false,
    PanelTitle = "Принудительное первое лицо",
    PanelHeight = 542,
    SectionTitle = "КАМЕРА",
    MainHeader = "ОСНОВНОЕ",
    HeadText = "Камера следит за головой",
    HeadVerticalText = "Слежение по высоте",
    HeadHorizontalText = "Слежение по горизонтали",
    CursorText = "Скрывать курсор",
    UnlockText = "Разблокировать курсор",
    UnlockTextSize = 12,
    PillWidth = 40,
    KeyWidth = 56,
    KeyHeight = 22,
    KeyRightInset = 14,
    KeyGap = 8,
    ZoomHeader = "ЗУМ",
    ZoomText = "Зум колёсиком",
    MaxZoomText = "Максимальный зум",
    Accent = Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235),
    ZoomEnabled = false,
    ZoomMax = 10,
    MinZoomLimit = 1,
    MaxZoomLimit = 100,
    ZoomStep = 1.2,
    ZoomSmooth = 10,
    ZoomEpsilon = 0.001,
    MinFov = 1,
    MaxFov = 120,
    WheelAction = "FirstPersonWheelZoom",
    WheelPriority = Enum.ContextActionPriority.High.Value,
    StepName = "FirstPersonZoomStep",
    StepPriority = Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 4,
    Mode = Enum.CameraMode.LockFirstPerson,
    Saved = nil,
    Conn = nil,
    Zoom = 1,
    ZoomTarget = 1,
    BaseFov = 70,
    WrittenFov = nil,
    ZoomApplied = false,
    WheelBound = false,
    StepBound = false,
    HeadFollow = false,
    HeadVertical = 1,
    HeadHorizontal = 1,
    MinHeadFollow = 0,
    MaxHeadFollow = 1,
    HideCursor = false,
    HideRow = nil,
    HideRefresh = nil,
    UnlockCursor = false,
    UnlockRow = nil,
    UnlockRefresh = nil,
    UnlockBound = false,
    UnlockStepName = "FirstPersonCursorUnlock",
    UnlockPriority = Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 6,
    UnlockBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default,
    CursorOwner = "firstperson",
    HeadPart = "Head",
    HeadStepName = "FirstPersonHeadStep",
    HeadPriority = Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1,
    HeadCameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom,
    HeadOmega = 14,
    HeadMaxShift = 4,
    HeadMaxStep = 0.1,
    HeadEpsilon = 0.001,
    HeadBlend = 0,
    HeadBlendRate = 0,
    HeadBound = false,
    ForwardText = "Смещение",
    Forward = 0.39,
    MinForward = -2,
    MaxForward = 2,
    OffsetName = "FirstPersonForward",
    BlendOmega = 12,
    Blend = 0,
    BlendRate = 0,
}

function firstPersonState.apply(enabled)
    local state = firstPersonState
    state.Enabled = enabled
    if state.Conn then
        state.Conn:Disconnect()
        state.Conn = nil
    end
    if enabled then
        if state.Saved == nil then state.Saved = player.CameraMode end
        pcall(function() player.CameraMode = state.Mode end)
        state.Conn = player:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CameraMode"):Connect(function()
            if state.Enabled and player.CameraMode ~= state.Mode then
                pcall(function() player.CameraMode = state.Mode end)
            end
        end)
    elseif state.Saved ~= nil then
        local saved = state.Saved
        state.Saved = nil
        pcall(function() player.CameraMode = saved end)
    end
    state.refreshZoom()
    state.refreshHead()
    state.refreshOffset()
    state.refreshCursor()
    state.refreshUnlock()
end

function firstPersonState.canEnable()
    if firstPersonState.Bind.Key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return true end
    hideState.showWarning()
    return false
end

function firstPersonState.fovFor(zoom, base)
    local state = firstPersonState
    local half = math.rad(base / 2)
    local fov = math.deg(2 * math.atan(math.tan(half) / zoom))
    return math.clamp(fov, state.MinFov, state.MaxFov)
end

function firstPersonState.zoomCeiling(base)
    local state = firstPersonState
    local ratio = math.tan(math.rad(base / 2)) / math.tan(math.rad(state.MinFov / 2))
    return math.max(state.MinZoomLimit, math.min(state.ZoomMax, ratio))
end

function firstPersonState.setMaxZoom(value)
    local state = firstPersonState
    state.ZoomMax = math.clamp(math.floor(value), state.MinZoomLimit, state.MaxZoomLimit)
    state.ZoomTarget = math.min(state.ZoomTarget, state.ZoomMax)
end

function firstPersonState.zoomActive()
    local state = firstPersonState
    return state.Enabled and state.ZoomEnabled and not freecamState.Enabled and not uavState.WheelBound
end

function firstPersonState.zoomBase()
    local state = firstPersonState
    if state.ZoomApplied then return state.BaseFov end
    return nil
end

uavState.FovSource = firstPersonState.zoomBase

function firstPersonState.resetZoom(camera)
    local state = firstPersonState
    if state.ZoomApplied and camera and camera.FieldOfView == state.WrittenFov then
        camera.FieldOfView = state.BaseFov
    end
    state.Zoom = 1
    state.ZoomTarget = 1
    state.ZoomApplied = false
    state.WrittenFov = nil
end

function firstPersonState.onWheel(_, inputState, input)
    local state = firstPersonState
    if not state.zoomActive() or not uavState.firstPerson() then
        return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
    end
    local delta = input.Position.Z
    if delta < 0 and state.ZoomTarget <= 1 then
        return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
    end
    if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Change then
        local base = state.zoomBase() or workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView
        state.ZoomTarget = math.clamp(state.ZoomTarget * state.ZoomStep ^ delta, 1, state.zoomCeiling(base))
    end
    return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
end

function firstPersonState.step(dt)
    local state = firstPersonState
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end
    if freecamState.Enabled then
        state.Zoom = 1
        state.ZoomTarget = 1
        state.ZoomApplied = false
        state.WrittenFov = nil
        return
    end
    if not state.zoomActive() then
        state.resetZoom(camera)
        return
    end
    if not uavState.firstPerson() then state.ZoomTarget = 1 end
    if state.Zoom ~= state.ZoomTarget then
        local gap = math.log(state.ZoomTarget) - math.log(state.Zoom)
        if math.abs(gap) < state.ZoomEpsilon then
            state.Zoom = state.ZoomTarget
        else
            state.Zoom = state.Zoom * math.exp(gap * (1 - math.exp(-state.ZoomSmooth * dt)))
        end
    end
    if state.Zoom > 1 + state.ZoomEpsilon then
        if not state.ZoomApplied or camera.FieldOfView ~= state.WrittenFov then
            state.BaseFov = camera.FieldOfView
            state.ZoomApplied = true
        end
        camera.FieldOfView = state.fovFor(state.Zoom, state.BaseFov)
        state.WrittenFov = camera.FieldOfView
    elseif state.ZoomApplied then
        if camera.FieldOfView == state.WrittenFov then
            camera.FieldOfView = state.BaseFov
        end
        state.ZoomApplied = false
        state.WrittenFov = nil
    end
end

function firstPersonState.refreshZoom()
    local state = firstPersonState
    local actions = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
    local wanted = state.Enabled and state.ZoomEnabled
    if wanted and not state.WheelBound then
        state.WheelBound = pcall(function()
            actions:BindActionAtPriority(state.WheelAction, state.onWheel, false, state.WheelPriority,
                Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel)
        end)
    elseif not wanted and state.WheelBound then
        pcall(function() actions:UnbindAction(state.WheelAction) end)
        state.WheelBound = false
    end
    if wanted and not state.StepBound then
        state.StepBound = pcall(function()
            RunService:BindToRenderStep(state.StepName, state.StepPriority, state.step)
        end)
    elseif not wanted and state.StepBound then
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(state.StepName) end)
        state.StepBound = false
    end
    if not wanted then state.resetZoom(workspace.CurrentCamera) end
end

function firstPersonState.headStep(dt)
    local state = firstPersonState
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local character = player.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = humanoid and humanoid.RootPart
    local head = character and character:FindFirstChild(state.HeadPart)
    local target = 0
    local shift = Vector3.zero
    if camera and root and head and head:IsA("BasePart") and not freecamState.Enabled
        and camera.CameraType == state.HeadCameraType and camera.CameraSubject == humanoid then
        target = uavState.firstPerson() and 1 or 0
        shift = head.Position - camera.Focus.Position + root.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(humanoid.CameraOffset)
        shift = Vector3.new(shift.X * state.HeadHorizontal, shift.Y * state.HeadVertical, shift.Z * state.HeadHorizontal)
        if shift.Magnitude > state.HeadMaxShift then
            shift = shift.Unit * state.HeadMaxShift
        end
    end
    state.HeadBlend, state.HeadBlendRate = freecamState.spring(state.HeadBlend, state.HeadBlendRate, target,
        state.HeadOmega, math.min(dt, state.HeadMaxStep))
    if camera and state.HeadBlend > state.HeadEpsilon then
        local applied = shift * state.HeadBlend
        camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame + applied
        camera.Focus = camera.Focus + applied
    end
end

function firstPersonState.refreshHead()
    local state = firstPersonState
    local wanted = state.Enabled and state.HeadFollow
    if wanted and not state.HeadBound then
        state.HeadBlend = 0
        state.HeadBlendRate = 0
        state.HeadBound = pcall(function()
            RunService:BindToRenderStep(state.HeadStepName, state.HeadPriority, state.headStep)
        end)
    elseif not wanted and state.HeadBound then
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(state.HeadStepName) end)
        state.HeadBound = false
        state.HeadBlend = 0
        state.HeadBlendRate = 0
    end
end

function firstPersonState.offset(dt)
    local state = firstPersonState
    local target = (state.Enabled and uavState.firstPerson()) and 1 or 0
    state.Blend, state.BlendRate = freecamState.spring(state.Blend, state.BlendRate, target, state.BlendOmega, dt)
    return Vector3.new(0, 0, -state.Forward * state.Blend)
end

function firstPersonState.refreshOffset()
    local state = firstPersonState
    if state.Enabled then
        cameraOffset.set(state.OffsetName, state.offset)
    else
        state.Blend = 0
        state.BlendRate = 0
        cameraOffset.set(state.OffsetName, nil)
    end
end

function firstPersonState.refreshCursor()
    local state = firstPersonState
    if state.Enabled and state.HideCursor then
        hideState.setCursor(state.CursorOwner, true)
    else
        hideState.clearCursor(state.CursorOwner)
    end
end

function firstPersonState.repaint(row, refresh)
    if refresh and row and row.Parent then refresh() end
end

function firstPersonState.unlockStep()
    if freecamState.Enabled then return end
    UserInputService.MouseBehavior = firstPersonState.UnlockBehavior
end

function firstPersonState.refreshUnlock()
    local state = firstPersonState
    local wanted = state.Enabled and state.UnlockCursor
    if wanted and not state.UnlockBound then
        state.UnlockBound = pcall(function()
            RunService:BindToRenderStep(state.UnlockStepName, state.UnlockPriority, state.unlockStep)
        end)
    elseif not wanted and state.UnlockBound then
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(state.UnlockStepName) end)
        state.UnlockBound = false
    end
end

function firstPersonState.setHide(value)
    local state = firstPersonState
    state.HideCursor = value
    if value and state.UnlockCursor then
        state.UnlockCursor = false
        state.refreshUnlock()
        state.repaint(state.UnlockRow, state.UnlockRefresh)
    end
    state.refreshCursor()
end

function firstPersonState.setUnlock(value)
    local state = firstPersonState
    state.UnlockCursor = value
    if value and state.HideCursor then
        state.HideCursor = false
        state.refreshCursor()
        state.repaint(state.HideRow, state.HideRefresh)
    end
    state.refreshUnlock()
end

function firstPersonState.pressUnlock()
    local state = firstPersonState
    if not state.Enabled then return end
    state.setUnlock(not state.UnlockCursor)
    state.repaint(state.UnlockRow, state.UnlockRefresh)
end

function firstPersonState.buildPanel(Body)
    local state = firstPersonState
    addPanelHeader(Body, state.MainHeader)
    terrainState.addPill(Body, state.HeadText, function() return state.HeadFollow end, function(value)
        state.HeadFollow = value
        state.refreshHead()
    end)
    addSlider(Body, state.HeadVerticalText, state.MinHeadFollow, state.MaxHeadFollow, state.HeadVertical, 2,
        function(v) state.HeadVertical = v end, state.Accent)
    addSlider(Body, state.HeadHorizontalText, state.MinHeadFollow, state.MaxHeadFollow, state.HeadHorizontal, 2,
        function(v) state.HeadHorizontal = v end, state.Accent)
    addSlider(Body, state.ForwardText, state.MinForward, state.MaxForward, state.Forward, 2, function(v)
        state.Forward = v
    end, state.Accent)
    local unlockRow, unlockRefresh = terrainState.addPill(Body, state.UnlockText,
        function() return state.UnlockCursor end, function(value)
            state.setUnlock(value)
        end)
    state.UnlockRow = unlockRow
    state.UnlockRefresh = unlockRefresh
    local unlockLabel = unlockRow:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
    unlockLabel.TextSize = state.UnlockTextSize
    unlockLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(14 + state.KeyRightInset + state.KeyWidth + state.KeyGap
        + state.PillWidth + state.KeyGap), 1, 0)
    local unlockPill = unlockRow:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextButton")
    unlockPill.Position = UDim2.new(1, -(state.KeyRightInset + state.KeyWidth + state.KeyGap), 0.5, 0)
    local keyButton = mk("TextButton", {Parent = unlockRow, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -state.KeyRightInset, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, state.KeyWidth, 0, state.KeyHeight),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "", TextSize = 11,
        TextColor3 = COL.textMuted, AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = keyButton, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)})
    mk("UIStroke", {Parent = keyButton, Thickness = 1.5, Color = Color3.fromRGB(48, 42, 64),
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
    keyBinds.attacher(state.UnlockBind)(keyButton)
    local hideRow, hideRefresh = terrainState.addPill(Body, state.CursorText,
        function() return state.HideCursor end, function(value)
            state.setHide(value)
        end)
    state.HideRow = hideRow
    state.HideRefresh = hideRefresh
    addPanelHeader(Body, state.ZoomHeader)
    terrainState.addPill(Body, state.ZoomText, function() return state.ZoomEnabled end, function(value)
        state.ZoomEnabled = value
        state.refreshZoom()
    end)
    addSlider(Body, state.MaxZoomText, state.MinZoomLimit, state.MaxZoomLimit, state.ZoomMax, 0, function(v)
        state.setMaxZoom(v)
    end, state.Accent, true)
end

local smoothCameraState = {
    Enabled = false,
    PanelTitle = "Плавная камера",
    PanelHeight = 190,
    SmoothHeader = "ПЛАВНОСТЬ",
    SmoothText = "Плавность камеры",
    TiltText = "Крен при повороте",
    Accent = Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235),
    Smooth = 0.5,
    MinSmooth = 0,
    MaxSmooth = 1,
    Tilt = 0.5,
    MinTilt = 0,
    MaxTilt = 1,
    TiltGain = 0.015,
    TiltLimit = 0.08,
    TiltOmega = 12,
    OmegaFast = 40,
    OmegaSlow = 1.5,
    FirstPersonDistance = 1,
    RestoreAngle = 0.1,
    PitchLimit = math.rad(89),
    PivotTolerance = 0.05,
    PivotFloor = 0.02,
    MaxStep = 0.1,
    PreName = "SmoothCameraPre",
    PostName = "SmoothCameraPost",
    PrePriority = Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1,
    PostPriority = Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 2,
    Ready = false,
    Applied = false,
    Raw = nil,
    Written = nil,
    Yaw = 0,
    Pitch = 0,
    YawRate = 0,
    PitchRate = 0,
    TargetYaw = 0,
    RawYaw = 0,
    Roll = 0,
    RollRate = 0,
}

function smoothCameraState.omega()
    local state = smoothCameraState
    return state.OmegaFast * (state.OmegaSlow / state.OmegaFast) ^ state.Smooth
end

function smoothCameraState.wrap(angle)
    return (angle + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi
end

function smoothCameraState.reset()
    local state = smoothCameraState
    state.Ready = false
    state.Applied = false
    state.Raw = nil
    state.Written = nil
    state.YawRate = 0
    state.PitchRate = 0
    state.Roll = 0
    state.RollRate = 0
end

function smoothCameraState.pre()
    local state = smoothCameraState
    if not state.Applied then return end
    state.Applied = false
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end
    local alignment = math.clamp(camera.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(state.Written.LookVector), -1, 1)
    if math.acos(alignment) < state.RestoreAngle then
        camera.CFrame = state.Raw
    end
end

function smoothCameraState.post(dt)
    local state = smoothCameraState
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera or freecamState.Enabled then
        state.Ready = false
        return
    end
    dt = math.min(dt, state.MaxStep)
    local raw = camera.CFrame
    local pitch, yaw, roll = raw:ToOrientation()
    if not state.Ready then
        state.Pitch = pitch
        state.Yaw = yaw
        state.TargetYaw = yaw
        state.RawYaw = yaw
        state.PitchRate = 0
        state.YawRate = 0
        state.Roll = 0
        state.RollRate = 0
        state.Ready = true
        return
    end
    local focus = camera.Focus.Position
    local firstPerson = (raw.Position - focus).Magnitude < state.FirstPersonDistance
    local omega = state.omega()
    state.TargetYaw = state.TargetYaw + state.wrap(yaw - state.RawYaw)
    state.RawYaw = yaw
    local turns = math.floor(state.TargetYaw / (2 * math.pi)) * 2 * math.pi
    state.TargetYaw = state.TargetYaw - turns
    state.Yaw = state.Yaw - turns
    local pitchTarget = math.clamp(pitch, -state.PitchLimit, state.PitchLimit)
    state.Yaw, state.YawRate = freecamState.spring(state.Yaw, state.YawRate, state.TargetYaw, omega, dt)
    state.Pitch, state.PitchRate = freecamState.spring(state.Pitch, state.PitchRate, pitchTarget, omega, dt)
    state.Pitch = math.clamp(state.Pitch, -state.PitchLimit, state.PitchLimit)
    local tiltTarget = 0
    if firstPerson and camera.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Custom then
        tiltTarget = math.clamp(state.YawRate * state.TiltGain * state.Tilt, -state.TiltLimit, state.TiltLimit)
    end
    state.Roll, state.RollRate = freecamState.spring(state.Roll, state.RollRate, tiltTarget, state.TiltOmega, dt)
    local rotation = CFrame.fromOrientation(state.Pitch, state.Yaw, roll + state.Roll)
    local position = raw.Position
    if camera.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Custom then
        local toFocus = raw:PointToObjectSpace(focus)
        local offAxis = math.sqrt(toFocus.X * toFocus.X + toFocus.Y * toFocus.Y)
        if offAxis <= math.max(state.PivotFloor, math.abs(toFocus.Z) * state.PivotTolerance) then
            position = focus - rotation:VectorToWorldSpace(toFocus)
        end
    end
    state.Raw = raw
    camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(position) * rotation
    state.Written = camera.CFrame
    state.Applied = true
end

function smoothCameraState.apply(enabled)
    local state = smoothCameraState
    if enabled == state.Enabled then return end
    state.Enabled = enabled
    state.reset()
    if enabled then
        RunService:BindToRenderStep(state.PreName, state.PrePriority, state.pre)
        RunService:BindToRenderStep(state.PostName, state.PostPriority, state.post)
    else
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(state.PreName) end)
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(state.PostName) end)
    end
end

function smoothCameraState.buildPanel(Body)
    local state = smoothCameraState
    addPanelHeader(Body, state.SmoothHeader)
    addSlider(Body, state.SmoothText, state.MinSmooth, state.MaxSmooth, state.Smooth, 2, function(v)
        state.Smooth = v
    end, state.Accent)
    addSlider(Body, state.TiltText, state.MinTilt, state.MaxTilt, state.Tilt, 2, function(v)
        state.Tilt = v
    end, state.Accent)
end

local bodyState = {
    Enabled = false,
    PanelTitle = "Видимое тело от первого лица",

    RefreshText = "Обновить список",

    ToolClass = "Tool",

    AccessoryPrefix = "^Accessory %((.+)%)$",

        RightFootAttachment = "Правая стопа",
    },
    Regions = {
        {Text = "Правая рука", Hide = false, HideBody = false,
            Parts = {"Right Arm", "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand"}},
        {Text = "Левая рука", Hide = false, HideBody = false,
            Parts = {"Left Arm", "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand"}},
        {Text = "Торс", Hide = false, HideBody = false, Parts = {"Torso", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"}},
        {Text = "Левая нога", Hide = false, HideBody = false,
            Parts = {"Left Leg", "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"}},
        {Text = "Правая нога", Hide = false, HideBody = false,
            Parts = {"Right Leg", "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"}},
        {Text = "Голова", Hide = true, HideBody = true, Parts = {"Head"}},

    StepName = "VisibleBodyStep",
    StepPriority = Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 3,

    Body = {},

    Accessories = {},
    AccessoryRegions = {},
    AccessoryUnits = {},
    Entries = {},
    ListUnits = {},
    UnitKeys = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
    Picked = {},
    RegionHost = nil,
    ListHost = nil,
    Anchors = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
    MaxDepth = 2,
    ForeignDepth = 3,
    ForeignInterval = 0.2,
    ForeignAt = 0,
    ForeignCharacter = nil,
    Foreign = {},
    Extra = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
    BodyIgnore = {HumanoidRootPart = true},
    SeatClasses = {Seat = true, VehicleSeat = true},
    Saved = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
    HiddenTransparency = 1,
