function bodyState.parts()
    local state = bodyState
    local character = player.Character
    local shown = {}
    local concealed = {}
    if not character then return shown, concealed end
    for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") and not state.Extra[part] then
            local index = state.regionOfName(part.Name)
            if index and state.Regions[index].HideBody then
                concealed[#concealed + 1] = part
            else
                shown[#shown + 1] = part
            end
        end
    end
    return shown, concealed
end

function bodyState.regionOfName(name)
    for index, region in ipairs(bodyState.Regions) do
        if table.find(region.Parts, name) then return index end
    end
    return nil
end

function bodyState.otherSide(joint, part)
    if joint.Part0 == part then return joint.Part1 end
    if joint.Part1 == part then return joint.Part0 end
    return nil
end

function bodyState.joints(part)
    local list = {}
    for _, child in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("JointInstance") or child:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
            list[#list + 1] = child
        end
    end
    local ok, connected = pcall(function() return part:GetJoints() end)
    if ok then
        for _, joint in ipairs(connected) do
            if joint:IsA("JointInstance") or joint:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
                list[#list + 1] = joint
            end
        end
    end
    return list
end

function bodyState.resolve(part, character, depth)
    local state = bodyState
    local cached = state.Anchors[part]
    if cached then return cached, true end
    local joints = state.joints(part)
    for _, joint in ipairs(joints) do
        local other = state.otherSide(joint, part)
        if other and other.Parent == character then
            local index = state.regionOfName(other.Name)
            if index then
                state.Anchors[part] = index
                return index, true
            end
        end
    end
    for _, child in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Attachment") then
            for _, body in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                if body:IsA("BasePart") and body:FindFirstChild(child.Name) then
                    local index = state.regionOfName(body.Name)
                    if index then
                        state.Anchors[part] = index
                        return index, true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if depth < state.MaxDepth then
        for _, joint in ipairs(joints) do
            local other = state.otherSide(joint, part)
            if other and other.Parent ~= character then
                local index, sure = state.resolve(other, character, depth + 1)
                if index and sure then
                    state.Anchors[part] = index
                    return index, true
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local nearest = math.huge
    local found = nil
    for _, body in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
        local index = body:IsA("BasePart") and state.regionOfName(body.Name)
        if index then
            local distance = (body.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
            if distance < nearest then
                nearest = distance
                found = index
            end
        end
    end
    return found, false
end

function bodyState.isExtra(part)
    local state = bodyState
    return not state.regionOfName(part.Name) and not state.BodyIgnore[part.Name]
end

function bodyState.foreign(character)
    local state = bodyState
    local now = os.clock()
    if state.ForeignCharacter == character and now - state.ForeignAt < state.ForeignInterval then
        return state.Foreign
    end
    local found = {}
    local seen = {}
    local function walk(part, depth)
        for _, joint in ipairs(state.joints(part)) do
            local other = state.otherSide(joint, part)
            if other and other:IsA("BasePart") and not seen[other] and not other:IsDescendantOf(character)
                and not state.SeatClasses[other.ClassName] then
                seen[other] = true
                found[#found + 1] = other
                if depth < state.ForeignDepth then walk(other, depth + 1) end
            end
        end
    end
    for _, body in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
        if body:IsA("BasePart") and state.regionOfName(body.Name) then walk(body, 1) end
    end
    state.Foreign = found
    state.ForeignAt = now
    state.ForeignCharacter = character
    return found
end

function bodyState.collectAccessories()
    local state = bodyState
    local character = player.Character
    local parts = {}
    local regions = {}
    local units = {}
    if not character then return parts, regions, units end
    local function add(part, extra)
        local index = state.resolve(part, character, 0)
        if index then
            if extra then state.Extra[part] = true end
            parts[#parts + 1] = part
            regions[#regions + 1] = index
            units[#units + 1] = state.unitOf(part)
        end
    end
    for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") and not part:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA(state.ToolClass) then
            if part.Parent ~= character then
                add(part, false)
            elseif state.isExtra(part) then
                add(part, true)
            end
        end
    end
    for _, part in ipairs(state.foreign(character)) do
        if part.Parent then add(part, true) end
    end
    return parts, regions, units
end

function bodyState.release(parts)
    local state = bodyState
    if next(state.Saved) == nil then return end
    local current = {}
    for _, part in ipairs(parts) do current[part] = true end
    for part, value in pairs(state.Saved) do
        if not current[part] then
            part.Transparency = value
            part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
            state.Saved[part] = nil
        end
    end
end

function bodyState.unitOf(part)
    local state = bodyState
    local reference = part:FindFirstChild(state.ReferenceName)
    if reference and reference:IsA("ObjectValue") and typeof(reference.Value) == "Instance" then
        return reference.Value
    end
    local accessory = part:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA(state.AccessoryClass)
    if accessory then return accessory end
    if state.Extra[part] then
        local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        if model and model ~= player.Character then return model end
    end
    return part
end

function bodyState.labelOf(unit, part)
    local state = bodyState
    local catalog = unit:GetAttribute(state.CatalogAttribute)
    if type(catalog) == "string" and catalog ~= "" then return catalog end
    if unit ~= part then
        return unit.Name:match(state.AccessoryPrefix) or unit.Name
    end
    if state.Extra[part] or not state.GenericNames[part.Name] then return part.Name end
    local first = nil
    for _, child in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Attachment") then
            if state.Slots[child.Name] then return state.Slots[child.Name] end
            first = first or child.Name
        end
    end
    if first then
        local cleaned = first:gsub(state.AttachmentSuffix, "")
        if cleaned ~= "" then return cleaned end
    end
    return state.UnknownText
end

function bodyState.changed(units)
    local last = bodyState.ListUnits
    if #last ~= #units then return true end
    local same = true
    for index, unit in ipairs(units) do
        if last[index] ~= unit then
            same = false
            break
        end
    end
    if same then return false end
    local counts = {}
    for _, unit in ipairs(last) do
        counts[unit] = (counts[unit] or 0) + 1
    end
    for _, unit in ipairs(units) do
        local left = counts[unit]
        if not left or left == 0 then return true end
        counts[unit] = left - 1
    end
    return false
end

function bodyState.rebuildEntries(parts, units)
    local state = bodyState
    local entries = {}
    local seen = {}
    for index, part in ipairs(parts) do
        local unit = units[index]
        if not seen[unit] then
            seen[unit] = true
            local base = state.labelOf(unit, part)
            entries[#entries + 1] = {Unit = unit, Base = base, Sort = base:lower(), Order = #entries + 1}
        end
    end
    table.sort(entries, function(left, right)
        if left.Sort ~= right.Sort then return left.Sort < right.Sort end
        if left.Base ~= right.Base then return left.Base < right.Base end
        return left.Order < right.Order
    end)
    local totals = {}
    for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
        totals[entry.Base] = (totals[entry.Base] or 0) + 1
    end
    local counted = {}
    local keys = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
        local number = (counted[entry.Base] or 0) + 1
        counted[entry.Base] = number
        entry.Label = totals[entry.Base] > 1 and (entry.Base .. " " .. number) or entry.Base
        keys[entry.Unit] = entry.Label
    end
    state.Entries = entries
    state.UnitKeys = keys
    state.ListUnits = units
end

function bodyState.hides(index)
    local state = bodyState
    if not state.HideAccessories then return false end
    if state.Mode == 1 then
        return state.Regions[state.AccessoryRegions[index]].Hide
    end
    return state.Picked[state.UnitKeys[state.AccessoryUnits[index]]] == true
end

function bodyState.close()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return false end
    return (camera.CFrame.Position - camera.Focus.Position).Magnitude < bodyState.HideDistance
end

function bodyState.write()
    local state = bodyState
    for _, part in ipairs(state.Body) do
        part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
    end
    local close = state.close()
    if close then
        for _, part in ipairs(state.HiddenBody) do
            part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
        end
    end
    for index, part in ipairs(state.Accessories) do
        if close and state.hides(index) then
            if state.Saved[part] == nil then state.Saved[part] = part.Transparency end
            if part.Transparency ~= state.HiddenTransparency then part.Transparency = state.HiddenTransparency end
            part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
        else
            local saved = state.Saved[part]
            if saved ~= nil then
                part.Transparency = saved
                state.Saved[part] = nil
            end
            if close then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end
        end
    end
end

function bodyState.step()
    local state = bodyState
    state.Body, state.HiddenBody = state.parts()
    local parts, regions, units = state.collectAccessories()
    state.Accessories = parts
    state.AccessoryRegions = regions
    state.AccessoryUnits = units
    state.release(parts)
    if state.changed(units) then
        state.rebuildEntries(parts, units)
        state.refreshList()
    end
    state.write()
end

function bodyState.restore()
    local state = bodyState
    for part, value in pairs(state.Saved) do
        part.Transparency = value
        state.Saved[part] = nil
    end
    if not workspace.CurrentCamera then return end
    local hidden = state.close() and 1 or 0
    local shown, concealed = state.parts()
    for _, part in ipairs(shown) do
        part.LocalTransparencyModifier = hidden
    end
    for _, part in ipairs(concealed) do
        part.LocalTransparencyModifier = hidden
    end
    for _, part in ipairs((state.collectAccessories())) do
        part.LocalTransparencyModifier = hidden
    end
end

function bodyState.apply(enabled)
    local state = bodyState
    if enabled == state.Enabled then return end
    state.Enabled = enabled
    if enabled then
        RunService:BindToRenderStep(state.StepName, state.StepPriority, state.step)
        local ok, conn = pcall(function() return RunService.PreRender:Connect(state.write) end)
        state.LateConn = ok and conn or nil
    else
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(state.StepName) end)
        if state.LateConn then
            state.LateConn:Disconnect()
            state.LateConn = nil
        end
        state.Body = {}
        state.HiddenBody = {}
        state.Accessories = {}
        state.AccessoryRegions = {}
        state.AccessoryUnits = {}
        state.restore()
    end
end

function bodyState.addHost(parent)
    local state = bodyState
    local host = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UIListLayout", {Parent = host, Padding = UDim.new(0, state.HostPadding), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder})
    return host
end

function bodyState.refreshList()
    local state = bodyState
    local host = state.ListHost
    if not host or not host.Parent then return end
    for _, child in ipairs(host:GetChildren()) do
        if not child:IsA("UIListLayout") then child:Destroy() end
    end
    addPanelHeader(host, state.ListHeader)
    tvState.Logo.addButton(host, state.RefreshText, COL.off, state.reload)
    if #state.Entries == 0 then
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = host, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, state.EmptyHeight),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = state.EmptyText, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    end
    for _, entry in ipairs(state.Entries) do
        local key = entry.Label
        local row = terrainState.addPill(host, key, function() return state.Picked[key] == true end, function(value)
            state.Picked[key] = value or nil
        end)
        local text = row:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
        if text then
            text.TextWrapped = true
            text.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
            text.TextSize = state.ListTextSize
        end
    end
end

function bodyState.reload()
    local state = bodyState
    local parts, _, units = state.collectAccessories()
    state.rebuildEntries(parts, units)
    state.refreshList()
end

function bodyState.showMode()
    local state = bodyState
    if state.RegionHost then state.RegionHost.Visible = state.Mode == 1 end
    if state.ListHost then state.ListHost.Visible = state.Mode == 2 end
    if state.Mode == 2 then state.reload() end
end

function bodyState.buildPanel(Body)
    local state = bodyState
    addPanelHeader(Body, state.BodyHeader)
    for _, region in ipairs(state.Regions) do
        terrainState.addPill(Body, region.Text, function() return region.HideBody end, function(value)
            region.HideBody = value
        end)
    end
    addPanelHeader(Body, state.AccessoryHeader)
    terrainState.addPill(Body, state.HideText, function() return state.HideAccessories end, function(value)
        state.HideAccessories = value
    end)
    tvState.addSwitch(Body, state.ModeLabels, state.Mode, function(index)
        state.Mode = index
        state.showMode()
    end)
    state.RegionHost = state.addHost(Body)
    addPanelHeader(state.RegionHost, state.VisibilityHeader)
    for _, region in ipairs(state.Regions) do
        terrainState.addPill(state.RegionHost, region.Text, function() return region.Hide end, function(value)
            region.Hide = value
        end)
    end
    state.ListHost = state.addHost(Body)
    state.showMode()
end

local bobState = {
    Enabled = false,
    PanelTitle = "Покачивание камеры",
    PanelHeight = 190,
    BobHeader = "ПОКАЧИВАНИЕ",
    StrengthText = "Сила покачивания",
    RateText = "Частота шагов",
    Accent = Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 235),
    Strength = 1,
    MinStrength = 0,
    MaxStrength = 2,
    Rate = 1,
    MinRate = 0.5,
    MaxRate = 2,
    Height = 0.09,
    Sway = 0.05,
    StepDistance = 7,
    ReferenceSpeed = 16,
    MaxSpeedFactor = 1.25,
    MinSpeed = 1,
    MinMove = 0.1,
    FadeOmega = 10,
    OffsetName = "CameraBob",
    Phase = 0,
    Amount = 0,
    AmountRate = 0,
}

function bobState.offset(dt, humanoid, root)
    local state = bobState
    local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local speed = Vector3.new(velocity.X, 0, velocity.Z).Magnitude
    local moving = humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air
        and humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > state.MinMove
        and speed > state.MinSpeed
    local target = moving and math.min(speed / state.ReferenceSpeed, state.MaxSpeedFactor) or 0
    state.Amount, state.AmountRate = freecamState.spring(state.Amount, state.AmountRate, target,
        state.FadeOmega, dt)
    state.Amount = math.max(state.Amount, 0)
    if moving then
        state.Phase = (state.Phase + speed * dt * state.Rate / state.StepDistance) % 2
    end
    local scale = state.Amount * state.Strength
    return Vector3.new(
        math.cos(state.Phase * math.pi) * state.Sway * scale,
        math.sin(state.Phase * 2 * math.pi) * state.Height * scale,
        0)
end

function bobState.apply(enabled)
    local state = bobState
    if enabled == state.Enabled then return end
    state.Enabled = enabled
    if enabled then
        state.Phase = 0
        state.Amount = 0
        state.AmountRate = 0
        cameraOffset.set(state.OffsetName, state.offset)
    else
        cameraOffset.set(state.OffsetName, nil)
    end
end

function bobState.buildPanel(Body)
    local state = bobState
    addPanelHeader(Body, state.BobHeader)
    addSlider(Body, state.StrengthText, state.MinStrength, state.MaxStrength, state.Strength, 2, function(v)
        state.Strength = v
    end, state.Accent)
    addSlider(Body, state.RateText, state.MinRate, state.MaxRate, state.Rate, 2, function(v)
        state.Rate = v
    end, state.Accent)
end

;(function()
    local atmoState = {
        SectionTitle = "ЗВУКИ",
        RowLabel = "Атмосферные звуки",
        PanelHeader = "ЗВУКИ",
        PanelHeight = 520,
        SoundPrefix = "CustomAtmoSoundPreview",
        SliderLabel = "Громкость",
        SliderMin = 0,
        SliderMax = 10,
        SliderDecimals = 2,
        SliderColor = Color3.fromRGB(120, 195, 215),
        LoadTimeout = 10,
        LoadStep = 0.1,
        FailText = "Не удалось загрузить звук: ",
        Master = false,
        Sounds = {
            {Text = "Ветер на улице", Id = "rbxassetid://5799870105", Volume = 0.5, On = true, Sound = nil, Token = 0, Refresh = nil},
            {Text = "Город V1", Id = "rbxassetid://32011937", Volume = 0.5, On = false, Sound = nil, Token = 0, Refresh = nil},
            {Text = "Небольшой ветер на улице", Id = "rbxassetid://9112757705", Volume = 0.5, On = false, Sound = nil, Token = 0, Refresh = nil},
            {Text = "Ночь V1", Id = "rbxassetid://97376915761088", Volume = 0.5, On = false, Sound = nil, Token = 0, Refresh = nil},
        },
    }

    function atmoState.stop(entry)
        entry.Token = entry.Token + 1
        local sound = entry.Sound
        entry.Sound = nil
        if sound then pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end) end
    end

    function atmoState.fail(entry)
        local state = atmoState
        state.stop(entry)
        entry.On = false
        if entry.Refresh then pcall(entry.Refresh) end
        warn(state.FailText .. entry.Text .. " (" .. entry.Id .. ")")
    end

    function atmoState.play(entry, index)
        local state = atmoState
        if entry.Sound then return end
        entry.Token = entry.Token + 1
        local token = entry.Token
        local sound = terrainState.loopSound(state.SoundPrefix .. index, entry.Id)
        sound.Volume = entry.Volume
        entry.Sound = sound
        sound.Parent = SoundService
        sound:Play()
        task.spawn(function()
            local started = os.clock()
            while token == entry.Token and not sound.IsLoaded and os.clock() - started < state.LoadTimeout do
                task.wait(state.LoadStep)
            end
            if token == entry.Token and not sound.IsLoaded then state.fail(entry) end
        end)
    end

    function atmoState.sync()
        local state = atmoState
        for index, entry in ipairs(state.Sounds) do
            if state.Master and entry.On then state.play(entry, index) else state.stop(entry) end
        end
    end

    function atmoState.buildPanel(Body)
        local state = atmoState
        addPanelHeader(Body, state.PanelHeader)
        for _, entry in ipairs(state.Sounds) do
            local _, refresh = terrainState.addPill(Body, entry.Text, function() return entry.On end, function(value)
                entry.On = value
                state.sync()
            end)
            entry.Refresh = refresh
            addSlider(Body, state.SliderLabel, state.SliderMin, state.SliderMax, entry.Volume, state.SliderDecimals, function(value)
                entry.Volume = value
                if entry.Sound then entry.Sound.Volume = value end
            end, state.SliderColor)
        end
    end

    function atmoState.build(page)
        local state = atmoState
        addSectionHeader(page, state.SectionTitle)
        addToggleRow(page, state.RowLabel, true, function(enabled)
            state.Master = enabled
            state.sync()
        end, state.buildPanel, state.PanelHeight)
    end

    function atmoState.destroy()
        atmoState.Master = false
        atmoState.sync()
    end

    toggleRegistry.AtmoSounds = atmoState
    keyBinds.Cleanups[#keyBinds.Cleanups + 1] = atmoState.destroy
end)()

;(function()
    local micState = {
        PanelTitle = "Микро по горячей клавише",
        PanelHeight = 170,
        ModeHeader = "РЕЖИМ",
        StatusHeader = "СОСТОЯНИЕ",
        ModeLabels = {"Переключатель", "Пока держишь"},
        OpenText = "Микрофон открыт",
        ClosedText = "Микрофон закрыт",
        InactiveText = "Микрофон не активен в Roblox",
        MissingText = "Микрофон не найден",
        OpenColor = Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 140),
        ErrorColor = Color3.fromRGB(230, 110, 110),
        DeviceClass = "AudioDeviceInput",
        WatchedProperties = {"Muted", "Active"},
        PollSeconds = 0.5,
        ModeHeight = 28,
        StatusHeight = 34,
        Mode = 1,
        Held = false,
        HoldApplied = false,
        Dead = false,
        Original = nil,
        Device = nil,
        Links = {},
        Conns = {},
        Bind = nil,
        StatusLabel = nil,
    }

    function micState.find()
        local device = micState.Device
        if device and device.Parent then return device end
        for _, link in ipairs(micState.Links) do link:Disconnect() end
        micState.Links = {}
        micState.Device = nil
        micState.Original = nil
        micState.HoldApplied = false
        local ok, found = pcall(player.FindFirstChildOfClass, player, micState.DeviceClass)
        if not (ok and found) then return nil end
        micState.Device = found
        for _, property in ipairs(micState.WatchedProperties) do
            local linked, link = pcall(function()
                return found:GetPropertyChangedSignal(property):Connect(micState.refresh)
            end)
            if linked then micState.Links[#micState.Links + 1] = link end
        end
        return found
    end

    function micState.write(device, muted)
        if micState.Original == nil then micState.Original = device.Muted end
        device.Muted = muted
    end

    function micState.restore()
        local device = micState.Device
        if device and device.Parent and micState.Original ~= nil then
            device.Muted = micState.Original
        end
        micState.Original = nil
    end

    function micState.keyBound()
        return micState.Bind ~= nil and micState.Bind.Key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
    end

    function micState.sync()
        local device = micState.Device
        if not (device and device.Parent) then return end
        if micState.Mode == 2 and micState.keyBound() then
            micState.HoldApplied = true
            local wanted = not micState.Held
            if device.Muted ~= wanted then micState.write(device, wanted) end
        elseif micState.HoldApplied then
            micState.HoldApplied = false
            micState.restore()
        end
    end

    function micState.refresh()
        local status = micState.StatusLabel
        if not status or not status.Parent then return end
        local device = micState.Device
        local text, color
        if not (device and device.Parent) then
            text, color = micState.MissingText, micState.ErrorColor
        elseif not device.Active then
            text, color = micState.InactiveText, micState.ErrorColor
        elseif device.Muted then
            text, color = micState.ClosedText, COL.textMuted
        else
            text, color = micState.OpenText, micState.OpenColor
        end
        status.Text = text
        status.TextColor3 = color
    end

    function micState.press()
        local device = micState.find()
        if not device then
            micState.refresh()
            return
        end
        if micState.Mode == 1 then
            micState.write(device, not device.Muted)
        else
            micState.Held = true
            micState.sync()
        end
        micState.refresh()
    end

    function micState.release()
        if not micState.Held then return end
        micState.Held = false
        micState.sync()
        micState.refresh()
    end

    function micState.keyChanged()
        micState.Held = false
        micState.sync()
        micState.refresh()
    end

    function micState.setMode(index)
        micState.Mode = index
        micState.Held = false
        micState.sync()
        micState.refresh()
    end

    function micState.tick()
        micState.find()
        micState.sync()
        micState.refresh()
    end

    function micState.destroy()
        micState.Dead = true
        micState.Held = false
        micState.restore()
        for _, link in ipairs(micState.Links) do link:Disconnect() end
        micState.Links = {}
        for _, conn in ipairs(micState.Conns) do conn:Disconnect() end
        micState.Conns = {}
    end

    function micState.buildPanel(Body)
        local state = micState
        addPanelHeader(Body, state.ModeHeader)
        local wrap = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, state.ModeHeight),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = wrap, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)})
        local count = #state.ModeLabels
        local indicator = mk("Frame", {Parent = wrap, Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2),
            Size = UDim2.new(1 / count, -4, 1, -4), BackgroundColor3 = COL.pinkOn, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = indicator, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)})
        local buttons = {}
        for index, text in ipairs(state.ModeLabels) do
            buttons[index] = mk("TextButton", {Parent = wrap, Position = UDim2.new((index - 1) / count, 0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1 / count, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text,
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted, AutoButtonColor = false})
        end
        local function paint()
            for index, button in ipairs(buttons) do
                button.TextColor3 = index == state.Mode and COL.text or COL.textMuted
            end
            TweenService:Create(indicator, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                Position = UDim2.new((state.Mode - 1) / count, 2, 0, 2)}):Play()
        end
        for index, button in ipairs(buttons) do
            toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(button, "select")
            button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                state.setMode(index)
                paint()
            end)
        end
        paint()
        addPanelHeader(Body, state.StatusHeader)
        local status = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, state.StatusHeight),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 12,
            TextColor3 = COL.textMuted, Text = ""})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = status, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
        state.StatusLabel = status
        state.refresh()
    end

    micState.Bind = keyBinds.new(micState.PanelTitle, micState.keyChanged, micState.press)

    micState.Conns[1] = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and input.KeyCode == micState.Bind.Key then
            micState.release()
        end
    end)
    micState.Conns[2] = UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased:Connect(micState.release)

    addToggleRow(tabPages["other"], micState.PanelTitle, true, nil, micState.buildPanel, micState.PanelHeight, nil,
        keyBinds.attacher(micState.Bind), nil, true)

    task.spawn(function()
        while not micState.Dead do
            pcall(micState.tick)
            task.wait(micState.PollSeconds)
        end
    end)

        Sounds = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
        Shared = {},

        RollModeItems = {},
        Equalizer = {Enabled = false, Low = 0, Mid = 0, High = 0},
        Tuning = {Enabled = false, Speed = 1, AmbientIndex = 0, FromPlayer = false, DistanceCurve = 0,
            DistanceMin = 0, DistanceMax = 0, Effects = {}, Apply = {}},
