    function volumeState.settings()
        if not volumeState.Settings then
            local ok, service = pcall(function()
                return UserSettings():GetService(volumeState.SettingsService)
            end)
            if ok then volumeState.Settings = service end
        end
        return volumeState.Settings
    end

    function volumeState.read()
        local settings = volumeState.settings()
        if not settings then return nil end
        local ok, value = pcall(function() return settings[volumeState.Property] end)
        if ok and type(value) == "number" then return value end
        return nil
    end

    function volumeState.write(value)
        local settings = volumeState.settings()
        if not settings then return false end
        return (pcall(function() settings[volumeState.Property] = value end))
    end

    function volumeState.initialPercent()
        local value = volumeState.read()
        if value == nil then return volumeState.DefaultPercent end
        local master = volumeState.MasterPercent
        return math.clamp(math.floor(value / volumeState.MaxValue * master + 0.5), 0, master)
    end

    function volumeState.showStatus(ok)
        for _, label in ipairs(volumeState.Labels) do
            label.Text = ok and volumeState.LabelText or volumeState.UnavailableText
            label.TextColor3 = ok and COL.text or volumeState.ErrorColor
        end
    end

    function volumeState.setProp(instance, name, value)
        instance[name] = value
    end

    function volumeState.getProp(instance, name)
        return instance[name]
    end

    function volumeState.same(first, second)
        if type(first) == "number" and type(second) == "number" then
            return math.abs(first - second) <= volumeState.Tolerance
        end
        return first == second
    end

    function volumeState.rollMode(index)
        local state = volumeState
        local item = state.RollModeItems[index]
        if item == nil then
            local ok, found = pcall(function() return Enum[state.DistanceEnum][state.DistanceCurves[index].Mode] end)
            item = (ok and found) or false
            state.RollModeItems[index] = item
        end
        return item
    end

    function volumeState.readRoll(sound)
        local values = {}
        for _, name in ipairs(volumeState.RollProps) do
            values[name] = sound[name]
        end
        return values
    end

    function volumeState.rollRestore(sound, record)
        local state = volumeState
        local targets = {}
        for _, name in ipairs(state.RollProps) do
            local slot = record.Roll[name]
            if slot and slot.Written ~= nil then
                local ok, current = pcall(state.getProp, sound, name)
                if ok and state.same(current, slot.Written) then targets[name] = slot.Original end
                slot.Written = nil
            end
        end
        return targets
    end

    function volumeState.applyRoll(sound, targets)
        local state = volumeState
        for _ = 1, state.RollPasses do
            local changed = false
            for _, name in ipairs(state.RollProps) do
                local wanted = targets[name]
                if wanted ~= nil then
                    local ok, current = pcall(state.getProp, sound, name)
                    if not (ok and state.same(current, wanted)) then
                        pcall(state.setProp, sound, name, wanted)
                        changed = true
                    end
                end
            end
            if not changed then return end
        end
    end

    function volumeState.writeRoll(sound, record)
        local state = volumeState
        local targets = {}
        if state.Roll then
            for _, name in ipairs(state.RollProps) do
                if record.Roll[name] then targets[name] = state.Roll[name] end
            end
        else
            targets = state.rollRestore(sound, record)
        end
        state.applyRoll(sound, targets)
        if state.Roll then
            for name, wanted in pairs(targets) do
                local ok, actual = pcall(state.getProp, sound, name)
                if ok and actual ~= nil then record.Roll[name].Written = actual else record.Roll[name].Written = wanted end
            end
        end
    end

    function volumeState.readVolume(sound)
        return sound.Volume
    end

    function volumeState.writeVolume(sound, value)
        sound.Volume = value
    end

    function volumeState.restoreVolume(sound, written, original)
        if math.abs(sound.Volume - written) <= volumeState.Tolerance then sound.Volume = original end
    end

    function volumeState.readSpeed(sound)
        return sound.PlaybackSpeed
    end

    function volumeState.writeSpeed(sound, value)
        sound.PlaybackSpeed = value
    end

    function volumeState.restoreSpeed(sound, written, original)
        if math.abs(sound.PlaybackSpeed - written) <= volumeState.Tolerance then sound.PlaybackSpeed = original end
    end

    function volumeState.watchProp(sound, name, handler)
        return sound:GetPropertyChangedSignal(name):Connect(handler)
    end

    function volumeState.watchDestroying(sound, handler)
        return sound.Destroying:Connect(handler)
    end

    function volumeState.gain(ratio)
        return 10 ^ (volumeState.DecibelsPerDoubling * math.log(ratio, 2) / 20)
    end

    function volumeState.quantize(value, decimals)
        if decimals == 0 then return math.floor(value) end
        local scale = 10 ^ decimals
        return math.floor(value * scale + 0.5) / scale
    end

    function volumeState.initDistance()
        local state = volumeState
        for _, def in ipairs(state.DistanceSliders) do state.Tuning[def.Key] = def.Initial end
    end

    function volumeState.initEffects()
        local state = volumeState
        local effects = state.Tuning.Effects
        for _, def in ipairs(state.SoundEffects) do
            local values = {}
            for _, prop in ipairs(def.Props) do values[prop.Name] = prop.Initial end
            effects[def.Key] = values
        end
    end

    function volumeState.initApply()
        local state = volumeState
        local apply = state.Tuning.Apply
        for key in pairs(state.SettingsHeaders) do apply[key] = state.ApplyDefault end
        for _, def in ipairs(state.SoundEffects) do apply[def.Key] = state.ApplyDefault end
    end

    function volumeState.effectActive(def, values)
        for name, neutral in pairs(def.Neutral) do
            if math.abs(values[name] - neutral) > volumeState.NeutralTolerance then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    function volumeState.refreshShared()
        local state = volumeState
        local shared = {}
        local equalizer = state.Equalizer
        if equalizer.Enabled and (equalizer.Low ~= 0 or equalizer.Mid ~= 0 or equalizer.High ~= 0) then
            shared.Equalizer = {LowGain = equalizer.Low, MidGain = equalizer.Mid, HighGain = equalizer.High}
        end
        local tuning = state.Tuning
        local apply = tuning.Apply
        if tuning.Enabled then
            for _, def in ipairs(state.SoundEffects) do
                local values = tuning.Effects[def.Key]
                if apply[def.Key] and state.effectActive(def, values) then shared[def.Key] = values end
            end
            local stun = state.Stun
            if apply.Ambient and stun.Index > 0 and tuning.AmbientIndex == stun.Index then
                shared.Stun = {LowGain = stun.LowGain, MidGain = stun.MidGain, HighGain = stun.HighGain}
            end
        end
        state.Shared = shared
        state.SpeedFactor = tuning.Enabled and apply.Speed and tuning.Speed or 1
        state.Roll = nil
        if tuning.Enabled and apply.Distance and tuning.DistanceCurve > 0 then
            local mode = state.rollMode(tuning.DistanceCurve)
            if mode then
                state.Roll = {RollOffMode = mode, RollOffMinDistance = tuning.DistanceMin,
                    RollOffMaxDistance = math.max(tuning.DistanceMax, tuning.DistanceMin + state.RollGap)}
            end
        end
    end

    function volumeState.needed()
        local state = volumeState
        return state.Boost > 1 or state.SpeedFactor ~= 1 or next(state.Shared) ~= nil or state.Roll ~= nil
    end

    function volumeState.target(record)
        local state = volumeState
        return math.min(record.Original * state.Boost, math.max(state.SoundVolumeCap, record.Original))
    end

    function volumeState.effectProps(key, record, target)
        local state = volumeState
        if key == "Boost" then
            local wanted = record.Original * state.Boost
            if target <= 0 or wanted <= target then return nil end
            return {Threshold = state.BoostThreshold, Ratio = state.BoostRatio,
                GainMakeup = math.min(20 * math.log10(wanted / target), state.EffectMaxDecibels)}
        end
        return state.Shared[key]
    end

    function volumeState.createEffect(sound, spec, props)
        local made = Instance.new(spec.Class)
        made.Name = spec.Name
        made.Priority = spec.Priority
        for name, value in pairs(props) do made[name] = value end
        made.Parent = sound
        return made
    end

    function volumeState.updateEffect(effect, props)
        if not effect.Enabled then effect.Enabled = true end
        for name, value in pairs(props) do
            if math.abs(effect[name] - value) > volumeState.EffectTolerance then effect[name] = value end
        end
    end

    function volumeState.destroyEffect(effect)
        effect:Destroy()
    end

    function volumeState.setEffect(sound, record, key, props)
        local state = volumeState
        local effect = record.Effects[key]
        if effect and effect.Parent ~= sound then
            effect = nil
            record.Effects[key] = nil
        end
        if not props then
            if effect then pcall(state.setProp, effect, "Enabled", false) end
            return
        end
        if not effect then
            local ok, created = pcall(state.createEffect, sound, state.EffectSpecs[key], props)
            if ok then record.Effects[key] = created end
            return
        end
        pcall(state.updateEffect, effect, props)
    end

    function volumeState.writeSound(sound, record)
        local state = volumeState
        record.Writing = true
        local target = state.target(record)
        pcall(state.writeVolume, sound, target)
        local ok, actual = pcall(state.readVolume, sound)
        record.Written = (ok and type(actual) == "number") and actual or target
        local speed = record.SpeedOriginal * state.SpeedFactor
        pcall(state.writeSpeed, sound, speed)
        local okSpeed, actualSpeed = pcall(state.readSpeed, sound)
        record.SpeedWritten = (okSpeed and type(actualSpeed) == "number") and actualSpeed or speed
        pcall(state.writeRoll, sound, record)
        record.Writing = false
        for _, key in ipairs(state.EffectOrder) do
            state.setEffect(sound, record, key, state.effectProps(key, record, target))
        end
    end

    function volumeState.sync(sound, record)
        local state = volumeState
        local changed = false
        local ok, now = pcall(state.readVolume, sound)
        if ok and type(now) == "number" and math.abs(now - record.Written) > state.Tolerance then
            record.Original = now
            changed = true
        end
        local okSpeed, nowSpeed = pcall(state.readSpeed, sound)
        if okSpeed and type(nowSpeed) == "number" and math.abs(nowSpeed - record.SpeedWritten) > state.Tolerance then
            record.SpeedOriginal = nowSpeed
            changed = true
        end
        for _, name in ipairs(state.RollProps) do
            local slot = record.Roll[name]
            if slot then
                local okRoll, nowRoll = pcall(state.getProp, sound, name)
                if okRoll and not state.same(nowRoll, slot.Written ~= nil and slot.Written or slot.Original) then
                    slot.Original = nowRoll
                    changed = true
                end
            end
        end
        return changed
    end

    function volumeState.track(sound)
        local state = volumeState
        if state.Sounds[sound] then return end
        local ok, original = pcall(state.readVolume, sound)
        if not ok or type(original) ~= "number" then return end
        local okSpeed, speed = pcall(state.readSpeed, sound)
        if not okSpeed or type(speed) ~= "number" then speed = 1 end
        local roll = {}
        local okRoll, currentRoll = pcall(state.readRoll, sound)
        if okRoll then
            for _, name in ipairs(state.RollProps) do roll[name] = {Original = currentRoll[name], Written = nil} end
        end
        local record = {Original = original, Written = original, SpeedOriginal = speed, SpeedWritten = speed,
            Writing = false, Conns = {}, Effects = {}, Roll = roll}
        state.Sounds[sound] = record
        local function onChanged()
            if record.Writing or state.Sounds[sound] ~= record then return end
            if state.sync(sound, record) then state.writeSound(sound, record) end
        end
        for _, name in ipairs(state.WatchedProperties) do
            local watched, watchConn = pcall(state.watchProp, sound, name, onChanged)
            if watched then record.Conns[#record.Conns + 1] = watchConn end
        end
        local gone, goneConn = pcall(state.watchDestroying, sound, function()
            if state.Sounds[sound] == record then state.Sounds[sound] = nil end
        end)
        if gone then record.Conns[#record.Conns + 1] = goneConn end
        state.writeSound(sound, record)
    end

    function volumeState.release(sound, record)
        local state = volumeState
        for _, conn in ipairs(record.Conns) do conn:Disconnect() end
        record.Conns = {}
        for _, effect in pairs(record.Effects) do pcall(state.destroyEffect, effect) end
        record.Effects = {}
        pcall(state.restoreVolume, sound, record.Written, record.Original)
        pcall(state.restoreSpeed, sound, record.SpeedWritten, record.SpeedOriginal)
        pcall(state.applyRoll, sound, state.rollRestore(sound, record))
    end

    function volumeState.rescale()
        local state = volumeState
        for sound, record in pairs(state.Sounds) do
            state.sync(sound, record)
            state.writeSound(sound, record)
        end
    end

    function volumeState.schedule()
        local state = volumeState
        if state.Pending then return end
        state.Pending = true
        task.defer(function()
            state.Pending = false
            if state.Engaged then pcall(state.rescale) end
        end)
    end

    function volumeState.engage()
        local state = volumeState
        state.Engaged = true
        local connected, conn = pcall(function()
            return game.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
                if state.Engaged and descendant:IsA(state.SoundClass) then state.track(descendant) end
            end)
        end)
        if connected then state.AddedConn = conn end
        local scanned, list = pcall(function() return game:GetDescendants() end)
        if scanned then
            for _, descendant in ipairs(list) do
                if descendant:IsA(state.SoundClass) then state.track(descendant) end
            end
        end
    end

    function volumeState.disengage()
        local state = volumeState
        state.Engaged = false
        if state.AddedConn then
            state.AddedConn:Disconnect()
            state.AddedConn = nil
        end
        for sound, record in pairs(state.Sounds) do
            state.release(sound, record)
        end
        table.clear(state.Sounds)
    end

    function volumeState.update()
        local state = volumeState
        state.refreshShared()
        if state.needed() then
            if state.Engaged then state.schedule() else state.engage() end
        elseif state.Engaged then
            state.disengage()
        end
    end

    function volumeState.changed()
        local state = volumeState
        state.update()
        if state.Ambient then state.Ambient.apply() end
    end

    function volumeState.setBoost(boost)
        volumeState.Boost = boost
        volumeState.update()
    end

    function volumeState.setFeature(feature, key, value)
        volumeState[feature][key] = value
        volumeState.changed()
    end

    function volumeState.setEffectValue(effectKey, name, value)
        volumeState.Tuning.Effects[effectKey][name] = value
        volumeState.changed()
    end

    function volumeState.setApply(key, on)
        volumeState.Tuning.Apply[key] = on
        volumeState.changed()
    end

    function volumeState.apply(percent)
        local state = volumeState
        local master = state.MasterPercent
        state.Percent = percent
        local ok = state.write(math.min(percent, master) / master * state.MaxValue)
        if ok then state.Written = true end
        local boost = 1
        if percent > master then boost = state.gain(percent / master) end
        state.setBoost(boost)
        state.showStatus(ok)
    end

    function volumeState.destroy()
        local state = volumeState
        if state.Engaged then state.disengage() end
        if state.Written and state.Original ~= nil then state.write(state.Original) end
        state.Written = false
    end

    function volumeState.build(parent, mirror)
        local state = volumeState
        local pad = state.SidePadding
        local percent = state.Percent
        if not mirror then
            state.Original = state.read()
            percent = state.initialPercent()
            state.Percent = percent
        end
        local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, Name = "Row_" .. state.LabelText,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, state.RowHeight), BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
        local statusLabel = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, pad, 0, state.TextTop), Size = UDim2.new(0.6, -pad, 0, state.TextHeight),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = state.LabelText, TextSize = state.LabelTextSize, TextColor3 = COL.text,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
        state.Labels[#state.Labels + 1] = statusLabel
        if mirror then
            local grab = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
                BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, state.TrackTop)})
            grab.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    toggleRegistry.Favorites.press(state.LabelText, input)
                end
            end)
        end
        local valueBox = mk("TextBox", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, state.TextTop), Size = UDim2.new(0.4, -pad, 0, state.TextHeight),
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = state.ValueTextSize, TextColor3 = COL.accent,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, ClearTextOnFocus = false, Text = ""})
        local hit = mk("Frame", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, pad, 0, state.TrackTop), Size = UDim2.new(1, -2 * pad, 0, state.HandleSize)})
        local track = mk("Frame", {Parent = hit, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, state.TrackHeight), BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = track, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)})
        local fill = mk("Frame", {Parent = track, Size = UDim2.new(percent / state.MaxPercent, 0, 1, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.accent, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = fill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)})
        local handle = mk("Frame", {Parent = track, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(percent / state.MaxPercent, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, state.HandleSize, 0, state.HandleSize),
            BackgroundColor3 = state.HandleColor, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = handle, CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)})
        local current = percent
        local function show(value)
            current = value
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(value / state.MaxPercent, 0, 1, 0)
            handle.Position = UDim2.new(value / state.MaxPercent, 0, 0.5, 0)
            valueBox.Text = value .. "%"
        end
        state.Views[show] = true
        local function commit(value, force)
            value = math.clamp(math.floor(value + 0.5), 0, state.MaxPercent)
            local changed = value ~= current
            for view in pairs(state.Views) do view(value) end
            if changed or force then state.apply(value) end
        end
        local dragging = false
        local function update(inputPos)
            commit(math.clamp((inputPos.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / track.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1) * state.MaxPercent)
        end
        valueBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            local number = tonumber((valueBox.Text:gsub("%%", "")))
            if number then commit(number, true) else show(current) end
        end)
        hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                dragging = true
                update(input.Position)
            end
        end)
        local moveConn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then update(input.Position) end
        end)
        local endConn = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then dragging = false end
        end)
        row.Destroying:Connect(function()
            moveConn:Disconnect()
            endConn:Disconnect()
            state.Views[show] = nil
            local at = table.find(state.Labels, statusLabel)
            if at then table.remove(state.Labels, at) end
        end)
        show(percent)
        state.showStatus(state.Original ~= nil)
        toggleRegistry.attachStar(row, state.LabelText)
        return row
    end

    function volumeState.addChips(Body, labels, selected, columns, onSelect)
        local state = volumeState
        local rows = math.ceil(#labels / columns)
        local inset = state.ChipInset
        local height = rows * state.ChipHeight + (rows - 1) * state.ChipGapY
        local wrap = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height + inset * 2)})
        local holder = mk("Frame", {Parent = wrap, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, inset, 0, inset), Size = UDim2.new(1, -inset * 2, 1, -inset * 2)})
        mk("UIGridLayout", {Parent = holder, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            CellSize = UDim2.new(1 / columns, -(math.floor(state.ChipGapX * (columns - 1) / columns) + 1), 0, state.ChipHeight),
            CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, state.ChipGapX, 0, state.ChipGapY)})
        local strokes = {}
        for index, text in ipairs(labels) do
            local chip = mk("TextButton", {Parent = holder, LayoutOrder = index, BackgroundColor3 = COL.row,
                BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, Text = ""})
            mk("UICorner", {Parent = chip, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)})
            strokes[index] = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = chip, Thickness = 1.5, Color = COL.pinkOn,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Transparency = index == selected and 0 or 1})
            mk("TextLabel", {Parent = chip, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.new(0, state.ChipTextPadding, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -2 * state.ChipTextPadding, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = text,
                TextSize = state.ChipTextSize, TextColor3 = COL.text, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
            toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(chip, "select")
            chip.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                for strokeIndex, stroke in ipairs(strokes) do
                    stroke.Transparency = strokeIndex == index and 0 or 1
                end
                onSelect(index)
            end)
        end
        return wrap
    end

    function volumeState.addCategory(parent, key, text, withoutBox)
        local state = volumeState
        local box = state.ApplyBox
        local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = parent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, box.Size)})
        mk("UIListLayout", {Parent = row, FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
            Padding = UDim.new(0, box.Gap), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center})
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, LayoutOrder = 1, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
            Text = text, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = COL.textDim})
        if withoutBox then return row end
        local button = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, LayoutOrder = 2, Size = UDim2.new(0, box.Size, 0, box.Size),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.stroke, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = button, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, box.Corner)})
        local inner = mk("Frame", {Parent = button, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -2 * box.Border, 1, -2 * box.Border),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = inner, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, box.Corner - box.Border)})
        local check = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = button, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = box.CheckText, TextSize = box.CheckSize,
            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)})
        local function paint(on)
            inner.BackgroundColor3 = on and COL.accent or COL.off
            check.TextTransparency = on and 0 or 1
        end
        paint(state.Tuning.Apply[key])
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(button, "toggle")
        button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local on = not state.Tuning.Apply[key]
            paint(on)
            state.setApply(key, on)
        end)
        return row
    end

    function volumeState.buildSettingsPanel(Body)
        local state = volumeState
        local ambient = state.Ambient
        local tuning = state.Tuning
        local headers = state.SettingsHeaders

        state.addCategory(Body, "Speed", headers.Speed)
        local speed = state.SpeedSlider
        addSlider(Body, speed.Label, speed.Min, speed.Max, tuning.Speed, speed.Decimals, function(value)
            state.setFeature("Tuning", "Speed", state.quantize(value, speed.Decimals))
        end, nil, true)

        state.addCategory(Body, "Ambient", headers.Ambient, true)
        local reverbLabels = ambient.reverbLabels()
        if #reverbLabels > 1 then
            state.addChips(Body, reverbLabels, tuning.AmbientIndex + 1, state.ReverbColumns, function(index)
                state.setFeature("Tuning", "AmbientIndex", index - 1)
            end)
        else
            addPanelHeader(Body, state.NoPresetsText)
        end

        state.addCategory(Body, "Listener", headers.Listener)
        state.addChips(Body, state.ListenerChips, tuning.FromPlayer and 2 or 1, state.ListenerColumns, function(index)
            state.setFeature("Tuning", "FromPlayer", index == 2)
        end)

        state.addCategory(Body, "Distance", headers.Distance)
        local curveLabels = {state.KeepText}
        for _, curve in ipairs(state.DistanceCurves) do curveLabels[#curveLabels + 1] = curve.Text end
        state.addChips(Body, curveLabels, tuning.DistanceCurve + 1, state.DistanceColumns, function(index)
            state.setFeature("Tuning", "DistanceCurve", index - 1)
        end)
        for _, def in ipairs(state.DistanceSliders) do
            addSlider(Body, def.Label, def.Min, def.Max, tuning[def.Key], def.Decimals, function(value)
                state.setFeature("Tuning", def.Key, state.quantize(value, def.Decimals))
            end, nil, def.Log)
        end

        for _, def in ipairs(state.SoundEffects) do
            state.addCategory(Body, def.Key, def.Title)
            for _, prop in ipairs(def.Props) do
                addSlider(Body, prop.Label, prop.Min, prop.Max, tuning.Effects[def.Key][prop.Name], prop.Decimals,
                    function(value)
                        state.setEffectValue(def.Key, prop.Name, state.quantize(value, prop.Decimals))
                    end, nil, prop.Log)
            end
        end
    end

    local ambientState = {
        ReverbEnum = "ReverbType",
        ListenerEnum = "ListenerType",
        ListenerItemName = "ObjectPosition",
        RootNames = {"HumanoidRootPart", "Head"},
        ListenerNames = {"ListenerType", "ListenerObject"},
        ReverbPresets = {
            {Name = "NoReverb", Text = "Без эха"},
            {Name = "PaddedCell", Text = "Эхо едва слышно"},
            {Name = "CarpettedHallway", Text = "Эхо очень слабое"},
            {Name = "Room", Text = "Эхо слабое"},
            {Name = "LivingRoom", Text = "Эхо мягкое"},
            {Name = "GenericReverb", Text = "Эхо среднее"},
            {Name = "Bathroom", Text = "Эхо звонкое"},
            {Name = "StoneRoom", Text = "Эхо каменное"},
            {Name = "ConcertHall", Text = "Эхо концертное"},
            {Name = "Auditorium", Text = "Эхо сильное"},
            {Name = "Arena", Text = "Эхо очень сильное"},
            {Name = "Hangar", Text = "Эхо огромное"},
            {Name = "Hallway", Text = "Коридор"},
            {Name = "StoneCorridor", Text = "Тоннель"},
            {Name = "Cave", Text = "Пещера"},
            {Name = "Alley", Text = "Переулок"},
            {Name = "Forest", Text = "Лес"},
            {Name = "City", Text = "Город"},
            {Name = "Mountains", Text = "Горы"},
            {Name = "Quarry", Text = "Карьер"},
            {Name = "Plain", Text = "Равнина"},
            {Name = "ParkingLot", Text = "Парковка"},
            {Name = "SewerPipe", Text = "Труба"},
            {Name = "UnderWater", Text = "Под водой"},
        },
        Originals = {},
        Remembered = {},
        ReverbItems = {},
        ReverbTexts = {},
        FollowConn = nil,
        AppliedPlayer = false,
        Item = nil,
        Current = nil,
        TypeWritten = false,
    }

    function ambientState.get(name)
        return SoundService[name]
    end

    function ambientState.set(name, value)
        SoundService[name] = value
    end

    function ambientState.enumItem(enumName, itemName)
        return Enum[enumName][itemName]
    end

    function ambientState.remember(name)
        local state = ambientState
        if state.Remembered[name] then return end
        local ok, value = pcall(state.get, name)
        if ok then
            state.Remembered[name] = true
            state.Originals[name] = value
        end
    end

    function ambientState.write(name, value)
        local state = ambientState
        state.remember(name)
        return (pcall(state.set, name, value))
    end

    function ambientState.restore(name)
        local state = ambientState
        if not state.Remembered[name] then return end
        pcall(state.set, name, state.Originals[name])
        state.Remembered[name] = nil
        state.Originals[name] = nil
    end

    function ambientState.reverbList()
        local state = ambientState
        if #state.ReverbItems == 0 then
            for _, preset in ipairs(state.ReverbPresets) do
                local ok, item = pcall(state.enumItem, state.ReverbEnum, preset.Name)
                if ok and item then
                    state.ReverbItems[#state.ReverbItems + 1] = item
                    state.ReverbTexts[#state.ReverbTexts + 1] = preset.Text
                end
            end
            local stun = volumeState.Stun
            local okStun, stunItem = pcall(state.enumItem, state.ReverbEnum, stun.Reverb)
            if okStun and stunItem then
                state.ReverbItems[#state.ReverbItems + 1] = stunItem
                state.ReverbTexts[#state.ReverbTexts + 1] = stun.Text
                stun.Index = #state.ReverbItems
            end
        end
        return state.ReverbItems
    end

    function ambientState.reverbLabels()
        local state = ambientState
        state.reverbList()
        local labels = {volumeState.KeepText}
        for _, text in ipairs(state.ReverbTexts) do labels[#labels + 1] = text end
        return labels
    end

    function ambientState.root()
        local state = ambientState
        local character = player.Character
        if not character then return nil end
        for _, name in ipairs(state.RootNames) do
            local part = character:FindFirstChild(name)
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return part end
        end
        return nil
    end

    function ambientState.step()
        local state = ambientState
        local root = state.root()
        if not root then return end
        if state.Current ~= root then
            state.Current = root
            pcall(state.set, "ListenerObject", root)
        end
        if not state.TypeWritten then
            state.TypeWritten = true
            pcall(state.set, "ListenerType", state.Item)
        end
    end

    function ambientState.startFollow()
        local state = ambientState
        if state.FollowConn then return end
        state.FollowConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            pcall(state.step)
        end)
    end

    function ambientState.stopFollow()
        local state = ambientState
        if state.FollowConn then
            state.FollowConn:Disconnect()
            state.FollowConn = nil
        end
    end

    function ambientState.restoreListener()
        local state = ambientState
        for _, name in ipairs(state.ListenerNames) do state.restore(name) end
    end

    function ambientState.applyReverb()
        local state = ambientState
        local tuning = volumeState.Tuning
        local item = nil
        if tuning.Enabled and tuning.Apply.Ambient and tuning.AmbientIndex > 0 then
            item = state.reverbList()[tuning.AmbientIndex]
        end
        if item then state.write("AmbientReverb", item) else state.restore("AmbientReverb") end
    end

    function ambientState.applyListener()
        local state = ambientState
        local tuning = volumeState.Tuning
        local active = tuning.Enabled and tuning.Apply.Listener and tuning.FromPlayer
        if active == state.AppliedPlayer then return end
        state.AppliedPlayer = active
        state.stopFollow()
        state.restoreListener()
        if not active then return end
        local ok, item = pcall(state.enumItem, state.ListenerEnum, state.ListenerItemName)
        if not ok or not item then return end
        state.Item = item
        for _, name in ipairs(state.ListenerNames) do state.remember(name) end
        state.Current = nil
        state.TypeWritten = false
        state.startFollow()
        pcall(state.step)
    end

    function ambientState.apply()
        ambientState.applyReverb()
        ambientState.applyListener()
    end

    function ambientState.destroy()
        local state = ambientState
        state.stopFollow()
        state.restoreListener()
        state.restore("AmbientReverb")
        state.AppliedPlayer = false
    end

    local function addFeatureRow(parent, spec)
        local builder = nil
        if spec.Builder then
            builder = volumeState[spec.Builder]
        else
            builder = function(Body)
                for _, slider in ipairs(spec.Sliders) do
                    addSlider(Body, slider.Label, slider.Min, slider.Max, slider.Initial, slider.Decimals,
                        function(value)
                            volumeState.setFeature(spec.Feature, slider.Key, volumeState.quantize(value, slider.Decimals))
                        end)
                end
            end
        end
        addToggleRow(parent, spec.Label, true, function(on)
            volumeState.setFeature(spec.Feature, "Enabled", on)
        end, builder, spec.PanelHeight)
    end

    volumeState.Ambient = ambientState
    volumeState.initEffects()
    volumeState.initDistance()
    volumeState.initApply()

    addSectionHeader(tabPages["sound"], volumeState.SectionTitle)

    toggleRegistry[volumeState.LabelText] = {Listeners = {}, Stars = {}, Spec = {
        mirror = function(page) return volumeState.build(page, true) end,
    }}
    volumeState.build(tabPages["sound"])

    toggleRegistry.AtmoSounds.build(tabPages["sound"])

    addSectionHeader(tabPages["sound"], volumeState.FeaturesTitle)

    for _, spec in ipairs(volumeState.FeatureRows) do
        addFeatureRow(tabPages["sound"], spec)
    end

    keyBinds.Cleanups[#keyBinds.Cleanups + 1] = micState.destroy
    keyBinds.Cleanups[#keyBinds.Cleanups + 1] = volumeState.destroy
    keyBinds.Cleanups[#keyBinds.Cleanups + 1] = ambientState.destroy
end)()

terrainState.NightVision = {
    Enabled = false,
    Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 255, 60),
    Brightness = 2,
    MinBrightness = 0.1,
    MaxBrightness = 5,
    AmbientPerBrightness = 0.25,
    EffectPerBrightness = 0.03,
    Saturation = -1,
    Contrast = 0,
    GrainEnabled = true,
    Grain = 0.35,
    MinGrain = 0,
    MaxGrain = 1,
    GrainTextures = {268592485, 268592462, 268592427, 268590007},
    GrainInterval = 0.06,
    GrainTileMin = 400,
    GrainTileMax = 600,
    GrainDisplayOrder = -4,
    GrainGuiName = "CustomNightVisionGrainPreview",
    EffectName = "CustomNightVisionPreview",
    PageKey = "feel",
    SectionTitle = "УТИЛИТЫ",
    RowTitle = "Ночное зрение",
    RowGlyph = "🌙",
    PanelHeight = 430,
    AccentColor = Color3.fromRGB(110, 255, 130),
    GrainAccentColor = Color3.fromRGB(150, 255, 160),
    Properties = {"Ambient", "OutdoorAmbient"},
    Effect = nil,
    Conn = nil,
    Row = nil,
    Gui = nil,
    GrainFrames = {},
    GrainIndex = 1,
    GrainTimer = 0,
    Base = {},
    Written = {},
}

function terrainState.NightVision.floor()
    local night = terrainState.NightVision
    return math.clamp(night.AmbientPerBrightness * night.Brightness, 0, 1)
end

function terrainState.NightVision.lift(color)
    local floor = terrainState.NightVision.floor()
    return Color3.new(math.max(color.R, floor), math.max(color.G, floor), math.max(color.B, floor))
end

function terrainState.NightVision.release()
    local night = terrainState.NightVision
    for _, property in ipairs(night.Properties) do
        local written = night.Written[property]
        local base = night.Base[property]
        if written ~= nil and base ~= nil and Lighting[property] == written then
            Lighting[property] = base
        end
        night.Written[property] = nil
        night.Base[property] = nil
    end
end

function terrainState.NightVision.stepLight()
    local night = terrainState.NightVision
    if shadowsEnabled or moonState.Enabled then
        night.Written.Ambient = nil
        night.Written.OutdoorAmbient = nil
        return
    end
    for _, property in ipairs(night.Properties) do
        local current = Lighting[property]
        if current ~= night.Written[property] then night.Base[property] = current end
        local target = night.lift(night.Base[property])
        if not shadowsLoop.same(current, target) then Lighting[property] = target end
        night.Written[property] = Lighting[property]
    end
end

function terrainState.NightVision.stepGrain(dt)
    local night = terrainState.NightVision
    local frames = night.GrainFrames
    if #frames == 0 then return end
    if night.Grain <= 0 then
        for _, frame in ipairs(frames) do
            frame.Visible = false
        end
        return
    end
    night.GrainTimer = night.GrainTimer + dt
    if night.GrainTimer < night.GrainInterval then return end
    night.GrainTimer = 0
    for _, frame in ipairs(frames) do
        frame.Visible = false
    end
    local frame = frames[night.GrainIndex]
    frame.ImageTransparency = 1 - night.Grain
    frame.TileSize = UDim2.new(0, math.random(night.GrainTileMin, night.GrainTileMax),
        0, math.random(night.GrainTileMin, night.GrainTileMax))
    frame.Visible = true
    night.GrainIndex = night.GrainIndex % #frames + 1
end

function terrainState.NightVision.step(dt)
    local night = terrainState.NightVision
    night.stepLight()
    night.stepGrain(dt or 0)
end

function terrainState.NightVision.destroyGrain()
    local night = terrainState.NightVision
    if night.Gui then
        night.Gui:Destroy()
        night.Gui = nil
    end
    night.GrainFrames = {}
end

function terrainState.NightVision.buildGrain()
    local night = terrainState.NightVision
    night.destroyGrain()
    local gui = mk("ScreenGui", {
        Name = night.GrainGuiName, ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
        DisplayOrder = night.GrainDisplayOrder, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling})
    hideState.track(gui)
    local frames = {}
    for index, id in ipairs(night.GrainTextures) do
        frames[index] = mk("ImageLabel", {Parent = gui, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Image = "rbxassetid://" .. id,
            ImageColor3 = night.Color, ImageTransparency = 1, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Tile,
            TileSize = UDim2.new(0, night.GrainTileMax, 0, night.GrainTileMax), Visible = false})
    end
    night.GrainFrames = frames
    night.GrainIndex = 1
    night.GrainTimer = 0
    night.Gui = gui
    gui.Parent = guiParent
end

function terrainState.NightVision.setGrain(value)
    local night = terrainState.NightVision
    night.GrainEnabled = value
    if night.Enabled and value then
        night.buildGrain()
    else
        night.destroyGrain()
    end
end

function terrainState.NightVision.setColor(color)
    local night = terrainState.NightVision
    night.Color = color
    if night.Effect then night.Effect.TintColor = color end
    for _, frame in ipairs(night.GrainFrames) do
        frame.ImageColor3 = color
    end
end

function terrainState.NightVision.apply(enabled)
    local night = terrainState.NightVision
    night.Enabled = enabled
    if night.Conn then night.Conn:Disconnect(); night.Conn = nil end
    if night.Effect then night.Effect:Destroy(); night.Effect = nil end
    night.destroyGrain()
    night.release()
    if enabled then
        local effect = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
        effect.Name = night.EffectName
        effect.TintColor = night.Color
        effect.Saturation = night.Saturation
        effect.Contrast = night.Contrast
        effect.Brightness = night.EffectPerBrightness * night.Brightness
        effect.Parent = Lighting
        night.Effect = effect
        effectPriority.Pinned.ColorCorrectionEffect = true
        effectPriority.set("ColorCorrectionEffect", true)
        shadowsLoop.Lift = night.lift
        shadowsLoop.Release = night.release
        if night.GrainEnabled then night.buildGrain() end
        night.Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(night.step)
        night.step()
    else
        shadowsLoop.Lift = nil
        shadowsLoop.Release = nil
        effectPriority.Pinned.ColorCorrectionEffect = nil
        effectPriority.set("ColorCorrectionEffect", ccEffect ~= nil)
    end
end

do
    local night = terrainState.NightVision
    addSectionHeader(tabPages[night.PageKey], night.SectionTitle)
    night.Row = addToggleRow(tabPages[night.PageKey], night.RowTitle, true, night.apply, function(Body)
        addPanelHeader(Body, "ОСНОВНОЕ")
        addSlider(Body, "Яркость", night.MinBrightness, night.MaxBrightness, night.Brightness, 1, function(v)
            night.Brightness = math.floor(v * 10 + 0.5) / 10
            if night.Effect then night.Effect.Brightness = night.EffectPerBrightness * night.Brightness end
        end, night.AccentColor)
        addPanelHeader(Body, "ЗЕРНИСТОСТЬ")
        terrainState.addPill(Body, "Зернистость", function() return night.GrainEnabled end, night.setGrain)
        addSlider(Body, "Интенсивность", night.MinGrain, night.MaxGrain, night.Grain, 2, function(v)
            night.Grain = math.floor(v * 100 + 0.5) / 100
        end, night.GrainAccentColor)
        addPanelHeader(Body, "ЦВЕТ")
        addColorPicker(Body, "Цвет ночного зрения", night.Color, night.setColor)
    end, night.PanelHeight, night.RowGlyph)
end

keyBinds.bindInput()
hideState.bindToolInput()

addSectionHeader(tabPages["presets"], "ВСТРОЕННЫЕ ПРЕСЕТЫ")

local presetButtonRefs = {}
local currentGraphicsPreset = nil

local presetsWrap = mk("Frame", {Parent = tabPages["presets"], Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,192), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
mk("UIGridLayout", {Parent = presetsWrap, CellSize = UDim2.new(0.48,0,0,42),
    CellPadding = UDim2.new(0.04,0,0,8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder})

local presetDefs = {
    {name="None", emoji="❔", accent=Color3.fromRGB(150,150,170)},
    {name="Low", emoji="⚡", accent=Color3.fromRGB(75,155,255)},
    {name="Medium", emoji="⚙️", accent=Color3.fromRGB(75,190,85)},
    {name="High", emoji="✨", accent=Color3.fromRGB(240,175,60)},
    {name="Ultra", emoji="🔥", accent=Color3.fromRGB(235,75,120)},
    {name="Ultra+", emoji="🌈", accent=Color3.fromRGB(155,95,255)},
    {name="Hyper Ultra", emoji="🚀", accent=Color3.fromRGB(70,220,215)},
}

for i, p in ipairs(presetDefs) do
    local btn = mk("TextButton", {Parent = presetsWrap, BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Text = p.emoji.." "..p.name, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 13,
        TextColor3 = COL.text, AutoButtonColor = false, LayoutOrder = i})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = btn, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,8)})
    local stroke = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = btn, Color = p.accent, Thickness = 1.5, Transparency = 0.5})
    presetButtonRefs[p.name] = {btn = btn, stroke = stroke, accent = p.accent}
end

local function applyGraphicsPreset(name)
    for _, ref in pairs(presetButtonRefs) do
        TweenService:Create(ref.btn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = COL.row}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(ref.stroke, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {Transparency = 0.5}):Play()
    end
    if presetButtonRefs[name] then
        TweenService:Create(presetButtonRefs[name].btn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = presetButtonRefs[name].accent}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(presetButtonRefs[name].stroke, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
    end
    currentGraphicsPreset = name

    local hyper = name == "Hyper Ultra"
    local ultraPlus = name == "Ultra+" or hyper

    if name == "None" then
        ToggleSetters.shadows(false)
        ToggleSetters.cc(false)
        ToggleSetters.atmo(false)
        ToggleSetters.distBlur(false)
        ToggleSetters.blur(false)
        ToggleSetters.bloom(false)
        ToggleSetters.sunRays(false)
    elseif name == "Low" then
        shadowDarkness = 50; shadowIntensity = 0.50; shadowSoftness = 1.0; shadowSpecular = 0
        ToggleSetters.shadows(true)
        ToggleSetters.cc(false)
        ToggleSetters.atmo(false)
        ToggleSetters.distBlur(false)
        ToggleSetters.blur(false)
        bloomIntensity = 0.5; bloomSize = 21; bloomThreshold = 2.0
        ToggleSetters.bloom(true)
        sunRaysIntensity = 0.03; sunRaysSpread = 0.25
        ToggleSetters.sunRays(true)
    elseif name == "Medium" then
        ToggleSetters.cc(false)
        ToggleSetters.atmo(false)
        ToggleSetters.distBlur(false)
        ToggleSetters.blur(false)
        bloomIntensity = 1.5; bloomSize = 43; bloomThreshold = 2.0
        ToggleSetters.bloom(true)
        sunRaysIntensity = 0.15; sunRaysSpread = 1.0
        ToggleSetters.sunRays(true)
        shadowDarkness = 50; shadowIntensity = 0.80; shadowSoftness = 1.0; shadowSpecular = 0
        ToggleSetters.shadows(true)
    elseif name == "High" then
        ToggleSetters.cc(false)
        ToggleSetters.atmo(false)
        ToggleSetters.distBlur(false)
        bloomIntensity = 2.0; bloomSize = 56; bloomThreshold = 2.0
        ToggleSetters.bloom(true)
        sunRaysIntensity = 0.30; sunRaysSpread = 2.0
        ToggleSetters.sunRays(true)
        shadowDarkness = 50; shadowIntensity = 1.0; shadowSoftness = 1.0; shadowSpecular = 0
        ToggleSetters.shadows(true)
        blurSize = 1.0
        ToggleSetters.blur(true)
    elseif name == "Ultra" then
        ToggleSetters.cc(false)
        ToggleSetters.atmo(false)
        bloomIntensity = 2.0; bloomSize = 56; bloomThreshold = 2.0
        ToggleSetters.bloom(true)
        sunRaysIntensity = 0.30; sunRaysSpread = 2.0
        ToggleSetters.sunRays(true)
        shadowDarkness = 50; shadowIntensity = 1.0; shadowSoftness = 1.0; shadowSpecular = 0
        ToggleSetters.shadows(true)
        blurSize = 3.0
        ToggleSetters.blur(true)
        distBlurFocus = 115; distBlurRadius = 80; distBlurIntensity = 0.30
        ToggleSetters.distBlur(true)
    elseif ultraPlus then
        ToggleSetters.atmo(false)
        bloomIntensity = 2.0; bloomSize = 56; bloomThreshold = 2.0
        ToggleSetters.bloom(true)
        sunRaysIntensity = hyper and 0.40 or 0.30; sunRaysSpread = hyper and 1 or 2.0
        ToggleSetters.sunRays(true)
        shadowDarkness = 50; shadowIntensity = 1.0; shadowSoftness = 1.0; shadowSpecular = 0
        ToggleSetters.shadows(true)
        blurSize = 3.0
        ToggleSetters.blur(true)
        distBlurFocus = 115; distBlurRadius = 80; distBlurIntensity = 0.30
        ToggleSetters.distBlur(true)
        ccBrightness = 0; ccContrast = 0; ccSaturation = hyper and 1 or 0.50
        ToggleSetters.cc(true)
    end
    if name ~= "None" then shadowsLoop.OutdoorDarkness = shadowDarkness end

    local moonRegistry = toggleRegistry["Лунный свет"]
    if moonRegistry then
        if ultraPlus then
            moonState.Strength = 0
            moonState.Reach = 100000
            moonState.LightBrightness = 0.10
            if not moonRegistry.isOn() then moonRegistry.setState(true) end
            if moonRegistry.rebuild then moonRegistry.rebuild() end
        elseif moonRegistry.isOn() then
            moonRegistry.setState(false)
        end
    end

    local skyRegistry = toggleRegistry["Небо"]
    if skyRegistry and not ultraPlus then
        if skyRegistry.isOn() then skyRegistry.setState(false) end
    elseif skyRegistry then
        skyState.StarCount = 5000
        if not skyRegistry.isOn() then skyRegistry.setState(true) end
        if skyRegistry.rebuild then skyRegistry.rebuild() end
    end

    local cloudsRegistry = toggleRegistry["Облака"]
    if cloudsRegistry and not hyper then
        if cloudsRegistry.isOn() then cloudsRegistry.setState(false) end
    elseif cloudsRegistry then
        cloudCover = 0.65
        cloudDensity = 0.40
        if cloudsRegistry.isOn() then cloudsRegistry.setState(false) end
        cloudsRegistry.setState(true)
        if cloudsRegistry.rebuild then cloudsRegistry.rebuild() end
    end

    local windRegistry = toggleRegistry["Ветер"]
    if windRegistry and not hyper then
        if windRegistry.isOn() then windRegistry.setState(false) end
    elseif windRegistry then
        windState.Strength = 10
        windState.SpawnRate = 600
        windState.Lifetime = 2
        windState.Direction = 13
        windState.MaxDistance = 3000
        windState.PassThroughWalls = false
        windState.SoundEnabled = false
        windState.SoundVolume = 1
        windState.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        windState.ColorSeq = ColorSequence.new(windState.Color)
        if windRegistry.isOn() then windRegistry.setState(false) end
        windRegistry.setState(true)
        if windRegistry.rebuild then windRegistry.rebuild() end
    end

    local waterRegistry = toggleRegistry["Улучшение воды"]
    if waterRegistry then
        local water = terrainState.Water
        if water.Preset.Names[name] then
            water.Color = water.Preset.Color
            water.Transparency = water.Preset.Transparency
            water.Reflectance = water.Preset.Reflectance
            water.WaveSize = water.Preset.WaveSize
            water.WaveSpeed = water.Preset.WaveSpeed
            local under = terrainState.Underwater
            under.Tint = water.Preset.UnderwaterTint
            under.Sound = water.Preset.UnderwaterSound
            under.TintIntensity = water.Preset.TintIntensity
            under.SoundIntensity = water.Preset.SoundIntensity
            if not waterRegistry.isOn() then waterRegistry.setState(true) end
            under.refresh()
            if waterRegistry.rebuild then waterRegistry.rebuild() end
        elseif waterRegistry.isOn() then
            waterRegistry.setState(false)
        end
    end

    local dayAmount = ({High = 25, Ultra = 25, ["Ultra+"] = 25, ["Hyper Ultra"] = 25})[name]
    shadowDay.Enabled = dayAmount ~= nil
    if dayAmount then shadowDay.Amount = dayAmount end
    local shadowsRegistry = toggleRegistry["Улучшение теней"]
    if shadowsRegistry and shadowsRegistry.rebuild then shadowsRegistry.rebuild() end

    local syncValues = {
        shadowDarkness = shadowDarkness, shadowOutdoor = shadowsLoop.OutdoorDarkness,
        shadowIntensity = shadowIntensity,
        shadowSoftness = shadowSoftness, shadowSpecular = shadowSpecular,
        sunRaysIntensity = sunRaysIntensity, sunRaysSpread = sunRaysSpread,
        bloomIntensity = bloomIntensity, bloomSize = bloomSize, bloomThreshold = bloomThreshold,
        ccBrightness = ccBrightness, ccContrast = ccContrast, ccSaturation = ccSaturation,
        blurSize = blurSize,
        distBlurFocus = distBlurFocus, distBlurRadius = distBlurRadius, distBlurIntensity = distBlurIntensity,
    }
    for key, value in pairs(syncValues) do
        local setDisplay = sliderSync[key]
        if setDisplay then setDisplay(value) end
    end
end

for _, p in ipairs(presetDefs) do
    toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(presetButtonRefs[p.name].btn, "select")
    presetButtonRefs[p.name].btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        applyGraphicsPreset(p.name)
    end)
end
