    local function defaultParts()
        local parts = {}
        for _, def in ipairs(PART_DEFS) do parts[def.key] = true end
        return parts
    end

    local function readParts(raw)
        local parts = defaultParts()
        if type(raw) == "table" then
            for _, def in ipairs(PART_DEFS) do
                if raw[def.key] == false then parts[def.key] = false end
            end
        end
        return parts
    end

    local function anyPart(parts)
        for _, def in ipairs(PART_DEFS) do
            if parts[def.key] then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function collectConfig(parts)
        local config = {}
        if parts.values then
            local values = {}
            for _, field in ipairs(fields) do
                local value = field.get()
                if field.kind == "c" then
                    values[field.key] = {value.R, value.G, value.B}
                else
                    values[field.key] = value
                end
            end
            config.values = values
        end
        if parts.toggles then
            local toggles = {}
            for _, entry in ipairs(toggleKeys) do
                local reg = toggleRegistry[entry.label]
                toggles[entry.key] = reg ~= nil and reg.isOn()
            end
            config.toggles = toggles
        end
        if parts.keys then
            local keys = {}
            for _, bind in ipairs(keyBinds.List) do
                if not bind.SkipPresets then keys[bind.Title] = bind.Key.Name end
            end
            config.keys = keys
        end
        return config
    end

    local function readField(field, raw)
        if field.kind == "b" then
            if type(raw) == "boolean" then return raw end
        elseif field.kind == "c" then
            if type(raw) == "table" and type(raw[1]) == "number" and type(raw[2]) == "number" and type(raw[3]) == "number" then
                return Color3.new(math.clamp(raw[1], 0, 1), math.clamp(raw[2], 0, 1), math.clamp(raw[3], 0, 1))
            end
        elseif field.kind == "s" then
            if type(raw) == "string" then return string.sub(raw, 1, 200) end
        elseif field.kind == "t" then
            if type(raw) == "table" then return raw end
        elseif type(raw) == "number" and raw == raw then
            local value = math.clamp(raw, field.min, field.max)
            if field.kind == "i" then value = math.floor(value + 0.5) end
            return value
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function applyKeys(keys)
        keyBinds.stop()
        for _, bind in ipairs(keyBinds.List) do
            local name = keys[bind.Title]
            if type(name) == "string" then
                local ok, code = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[name] end)
                if ok and typeof(code) == "EnumItem" and code.EnumType == Enum.KeyCode then
                    bind.Key = code
                    keyBinds.paint(bind)
                    if bind.OnChange then pcall(bind.OnChange) end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function applyConfig(config)
        local hasValues = type(config.values) == "table"
        local hasToggles = type(config.toggles) == "table"
        if hasValues then
            for _, field in ipairs(fields) do
                local value = readField(field, config.values[field.key])
                if value ~= nil then field.set(value) end
            end
            local legacyPercent = config.values.shadowDayPercent
            if config.values.shadowDayAmount == nil and type(legacyPercent) == "number" and legacyPercent == legacyPercent then
                local share = math.clamp(legacyPercent, 0, 100) / 100
                shadowDay.Amount = math.floor(share * shadowsLoop.OutdoorDarkness + 0.5)
            end
        end
        if hasValues or hasToggles then
            for _, entry in ipairs(toggleKeys) do
                local reg = toggleRegistry[entry.label]
                if reg then
                    local want = nil
                    if hasToggles then want = config.toggles[entry.key] == true else want = reg.isOn() end
                    if reg.isOn() then reg.setState(false) end
                    if want then reg.setState(true) end
                end
            end
            for _, entry in ipairs(toggleKeys) do
                local reg = toggleRegistry[entry.label]
                if reg and reg.rebuild then reg.rebuild() end
            end
        end
        if type(config.keys) == "table" then applyKeys(config.keys) end
    end

    local slots = {}
    for i = 1, BASE_SLOTS do
        slots[i] = {name = "", config = nil, parts = defaultParts()}
    end

    local function cleanName(text)
        local trimmed = string.match(tostring(text or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        local okCut, cut = pcall(utf8.offset, trimmed, NAME_MAX_LENGTH + 1)
        if okCut and cut then trimmed = string.sub(trimmed, 1, cut - 1) end
        return trimmed
    end

    local function loadFromFile()
        if not hasFS then return end
        pcall(function()
            if not isfile(SAVE_FILE) then return end
            local decoded = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(SAVE_FILE))
            if type(decoded) ~= "table" or type(decoded.slots) ~= "table" then return end
            local count = type(decoded.count) == "number" and math.floor(decoded.count) or 0
            for key in pairs(decoded.slots) do
                local index = tonumber(key)
                if index and index == math.floor(index) and index > count then count = index end
            end
            count = math.min(count, LOAD_LIMIT)
            local loaded = {}
            for i = 1, count do
                loaded[i] = {name = "", config = nil, parts = defaultParts()}
                local raw = decoded.slots[tostring(i)]
                if type(raw) == "table" then
                    loaded[i].name = type(raw.name) == "string" and cleanName(raw.name) or ""
                    if type(raw.config) == "table" then loaded[i].config = raw.config end
                    loaded[i].parts = readParts(raw.parts)
                end
            end
            table.clear(slots)
            for i = 1, count do
                slots[i] = loaded[i]
            end
        end)
    end

    local function persist()
        if not hasFS then return false end
        local out = {version = 1, count = #slots, slots = {}}
        for i = 1, #slots do
            out.slots[tostring(i)] = {name = slots[i].name, config = slots[i].config, parts = slots[i].parts}
        end
        return (pcall(function()
            writefile(SAVE_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(out))
        end))
    end

    loadFromFile()

    local page = tabPages["presets"]
    local headerRow = addSectionHeader(page, "КАСТОМНЫЕ ПРЕСЕТЫ")

    local defaultStatus = hasFS and "" or "Файлы недоступны: слоты до перезапуска."
    local statusLabel = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = headerRow, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 170, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -174, 1, 0),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11,
        Text = defaultStatus, TextColor3 = COL.textDim, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right})
    local statusToken = 0
    local function setStatus(text, color)
        statusToken = statusToken + 1
        local myToken = statusToken
        statusLabel.Text = text
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = color or COL.textMuted
        task.delay(STATUS_SECONDS, function()
            if myToken == statusToken then
                statusLabel.Text = defaultStatus
                statusLabel.TextColor3 = COL.textDim
            end
        end)
    end

    local rows = {}
    local function slotTitle(i)
        if slots[i].name ~= "" then return slots[i].name end
        return "Пресет " .. i
    end

    local function refreshRow(i)
        local ui = rows[i]
        local slot = slots[i]
        local filled = slot.config ~= nil
        ui.nameBox.Text = slot.name
        ui.nameBox.PlaceholderText = filled and ("Пресет " .. i) or ("Пустой слот " .. i)
        ui.nameBox.TextColor3 = filled and COL.text or COL.textMuted
        ui.stroke.Color = filled and COL.pinkOn or COL.stroke
        ui.stroke.Transparency = filled and 0.25 or 0.8
        ui.loadBtn.BackgroundColor3 = filled and COL.pinkOn or COL.off
        ui.loadBtn.TextColor3 = filled and COL.text or COL.textDim
        ui.clearBtn.TextColor3 = (filled or slot.name ~= "") and COL.text or COL.textDim
    end

    local function bindConfirm(button, idleText, needsConfirm, action, confirmText, confirmBackground)
        if confirmText == nil then confirmText = CONFIRM_TEXT end
        local idleBackground = button.BackgroundColor3
        local armed = false
        local token = 0
        local function reset()
            armed = false
            if idleText ~= false then button.Text = idleText end
            button.TextColor3 = COL.text
            button.BackgroundColor3 = idleBackground
        end
        button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if needsConfirm() and not armed then
                armed = true
                token = token + 1
                local myToken = token
                if confirmText ~= false then button.Text = confirmText end
                if confirmBackground then
                    button.TextColor3 = COL.text
                    button.BackgroundColor3 = confirmBackground
                else
                    button.TextColor3 = COLOR_ERROR
                end
                task.delay(CONFIRM_SECONDS, function()
                    if myToken == token then reset() end
                end)
                return
            end
            token = token + 1
            reset()
            action()
        end)
    end

    local function makeButton(row, width, rightOffset, text)
        local button = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -rightOffset, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, width, 0, 26),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            Text = text, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = COL.text, AutoButtonColor = false})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = button, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)})
        return button
    end

    local function buildPartsPanel(Body, i)
        Body.Position = UDim2.new(Body.Position.X.Scale, Body.Position.X.Offset, 0, PANEL_BODY_TOP)
        Body.Size = UDim2.new(Body.Size.X.Scale, Body.Size.X.Offset, 1, -(PANEL_BODY_TOP + PANEL_BODY_BOTTOM))
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, PANEL_HINT_HEIGHT),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = PANEL_HINT, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = COL.textDim,
            TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top})
        for _, def in ipairs(PART_DEFS) do
            local partRow = mk("Frame", {Parent = Body, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, PART_ROW_HEIGHT),
                BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
            mk("UICorner", {Parent = partRow, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
            mk("TextLabel", {Parent = partRow, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = def.label, TextSize = 13,
                TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
            local box = mk("TextButton", {Parent = partRow, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, PART_BOX_SIZE, 0, PART_BOX_SIZE),
                BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
            mk("UICorner", {Parent = box, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)})
            local boxStroke = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = box, Thickness = 1.5, Color = COL.stroke,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
            local check = mk("TextLabel", {Parent = box, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "✓", TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)})
            local function paint(on)
                box.BackgroundColor3 = on and COL.accent or COL.off
                boxStroke.Transparency = on and 1 or 0
                check.TextTransparency = on and 0 or 1
            end
            paint(slots[i].parts[def.key])
            toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(box, "toggle")
            box.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local parts = slots[i].parts
                parts[def.key] = not parts[def.key]
                paint(parts[def.key])
                persist()
            end)
        end
    end

    local removeSlot

    local function buildRow(i)
        local shift = DELETE_WIDTH + DELETE_GAP
        local gearShift = GEAR_WIDTH + GEAR_GAP
        local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = page, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ROW_HEIGHT),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
        local stroke = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = row, Thickness = 1.5, Color = COL.stroke, Transparency = 0.8,
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
        local nameBox = mk("TextBox", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -(242 + shift + gearShift), 1, 0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 13, Text = "", PlaceholderColor3 = COL.textDim,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            ClearTextOnFocus = false, ClipsDescendants = true})
        pcall(function() nameBox.MaxLength = NAME_MAX_LENGTH end)
        local deleteBtn = makeButton(row, DELETE_WIDTH, 10, "")
        toggleRegistry.Icons.outline(deleteBtn)
        toggleRegistry.Icons.drawCross(deleteBtn, toggleRegistry.Icons.DeleteCrossGlyph)
        local clearBtn = makeButton(row, 60, 10 + shift, "Очистить")
        local saveBtn = makeButton(row, 74, 76 + shift, "Сохранить")
        local loadBtn = makeButton(row, 66, 156 + shift + gearShift, "Загрузить")
        local gear, _, _, dispose = toggleRegistry.attachGear(row, 156 + shift, GEAR_WIDTH,
            string.format(PANEL_TITLE, i), function(Body) buildPartsPanel(Body, i) end, PANEL_HEIGHT)
        rows[i] = {frame = row, nameBox = nameBox, stroke = stroke, loadBtn = loadBtn, saveBtn = saveBtn,
            clearBtn = clearBtn, deleteBtn = deleteBtn, gear = gear, dispose = dispose}

        nameBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            slots[i].name = cleanName(nameBox.Text)
            refreshRow(i)
            persist()
        end)

        loadBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local slot = slots[i]
            if not slot.config then
                setStatus("Слот " .. i .. " пуст. Сначала сохраните.", COL.textMuted)
                return
            end
            toggleRegistry.Sounds.play("toggle")
            local ok = pcall(applyConfig, slot.config)
            if ok then
                for _, ref in pairs(presetButtonRefs) do
                    ref.btn.BackgroundColor3 = COL.row
                    ref.stroke.Transparency = 0.5
                end
                currentGraphicsPreset = nil
                setStatus("Загружен: " .. slotTitle(i), COLOR_OK)
            else
                setStatus("Ошибка загрузки слота " .. i .. ".", COLOR_ERROR)
            end
        end)

        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(saveBtn, "toggle")
        bindConfirm(saveBtn, "Сохранить", function()
            return slots[i].config ~= nil and anyPart(slots[i].parts)
        end, function()
            local slot = slots[i]
            if not anyPart(slot.parts) then
                setStatus(string.format(NO_PARTS_TEXT, i), COLOR_ERROR)
                return
            end
            slot.config = collectConfig(slot.parts)
            if slot.name == "" then slot.name = "Пресет " .. i end
            refreshRow(i)
            if persist() then
                setStatus("Слот " .. i .. " сохранён: " .. slotTitle(i), COLOR_OK)
            elseif hasFS then
                setStatus("Слот " .. i .. ": файл не записан.", COLOR_ERROR)
            else
                setStatus("Слот " .. i .. " сохранён (без файла).", COLOR_OK)
            end
        end)

        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(clearBtn, "toggle")
        bindConfirm(clearBtn, "Очистить", function() return slots[i].config ~= nil end, function()
            slots[i].name = ""
            slots[i].config = nil
            refreshRow(i)
            persist()
            setStatus("Слот " .. i .. " очищен.", COL.textMuted)
        end)

        bindConfirm(deleteBtn, false, function()
            return slots[i].config ~= nil or slots[i].name ~= ""
        end, function()
            toggleRegistry.Sounds.play("close")
            removeSlot(i)
        end, false, DELETE_CONFIRM_COLOR)

        refreshRow(i)
    end

    removeSlot = function(index)
        table.remove(slots, index)
        for k = index, #slots do
            if slots[k].name == "Пресет " .. (k + 1) then slots[k].name = "Пресет " .. k end
        end
        for _, ui in ipairs(rows) do
            ui.dispose()
            ui.frame:Destroy()
        end
        rows = {}
        for k = 1, #slots do
            buildRow(k)
        end
        if persist() or not hasFS then
            setStatus("Слот " .. index .. " удалён.", COL.textMuted)
        else
            setStatus("Слот " .. index .. " удалён, файл не записан.", COLOR_ERROR)
        end
    end

    for i = 1, #slots do
        buildRow(i)
    end

    local addButton = mk("TextButton", {Parent = page, LayoutOrder = ADD_ORDER,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ROW_HEIGHT), BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = ADD_TEXT, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted,
        AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = addButton, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
    mk("UIStroke", {Parent = addButton, Thickness = 1.5, Color = COL.stroke, Transparency = 0.8,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
    addButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local index = #slots + 1
        slots[index] = {name = "", config = nil, parts = defaultParts()}
        buildRow(index)
        persist()
        setStatus("Добавлен слот " .. index .. ".", COLOR_OK)
        task.delay(SCROLL_DELAY, function()
            page.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, page.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y)
        end)
    end)
end)()

toggleRegistry.Dropdown = {
    Keep = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
    RowHeight = 44,
    RowTextX = 14,
    RowTextSize = 13,
    LabelWidth = 80,
    RightInset = 10,
    ButtonWidth = 150,
    ButtonHeight = 26,
    ButtonTextSize = 11,
    ButtonCorner = 7,
    ItemHeight = 28,
    ItemGap = 2,
    ItemTextSize = 12,
    ItemCorner = 7,
    ListPad = 5,
    ListGap = 4,
    ListCorner = 10,
    ScrollBar = 3,
    SelectedStrokeThickness = 2,
    GearSize = 26,
    GearGap = 8,
    HolderCorner = 10,
}

function toggleRegistry.Dropdown.build(page, spec)
    local config = toggleRegistry.Dropdown
    local values = spec.Values
    local picked = spec.Index
    local entry = spec.Label and toggleRegistry[spec.Label] or nil
    local gearInset = config.RightInset + config.ButtonWidth + config.GearGap
    local fullHeight = #values * config.ItemHeight + (#values - 1) * config.ItemGap + 2 * config.ListPad
    local listHeight = spec.MaxListHeight and math.min(fullHeight, spec.MaxListHeight) or fullHeight
    local holder = mk("Frame", {Parent = page, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, config.RowHeight), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = holder, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, config.HolderCorner)})
    mk("UIListLayout", {Parent = holder, Padding = UDim.new(0, config.ListGap), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder})
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = holder, LayoutOrder = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, config.RowHeight),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, config.RowTextX, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, spec.LabelWidth or config.LabelWidth, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = spec.Text,
        TextSize = config.RowTextSize, TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
    local button = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -config.RightInset, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, config.ButtonWidth, 0, config.ButtonHeight),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = "",
        TextSize = config.ButtonTextSize, TextColor3 = COL.text, AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = button, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, config.ButtonCorner)})
    if entry then
        if spec.Mirror then
            local mirrorGear = toggleRegistry.attachGear(row, gearInset, config.GearSize, spec.Label, nil, nil, entry.openPanel)
            if entry.gearMirror then entry.gearMirror(mirrorGear) end
            local grab = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
                BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, config.RowTextX + (spec.LabelWidth or config.LabelWidth), 1, 0)})
            grab.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    toggleRegistry.Favorites.press(spec.Label, input)
                end
            end)
        else
            local _, _, _, _, togglePanel, addMirror = toggleRegistry.attachGear(row, gearInset, config.GearSize,
                spec.Label, spec.PanelBuilder, spec.PanelHeight)
            entry.openPanel = togglePanel
            entry.gearMirror = addMirror
        end
        toggleRegistry.attachStar(row, spec.Label)
    end
    local list = mk("ScrollingFrame", {Parent = holder, LayoutOrder = 2, Visible = false,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, listHeight), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = config.ScrollBar,
        ScrollBarImageColor3 = COL.accentDim, BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = list, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, config.ListCorner)})
    mk("UIPadding", {Parent = list, PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, config.ListPad), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, config.ListPad),
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, config.ListPad), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, config.ListPad)})
    mk("UIListLayout", {Parent = list, Padding = UDim.new(0, config.ItemGap), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder})
    local items = {}
    local function paint()
        if spec.Get then picked = spec.Get() end
        button.Text = values[picked].Name
        for index, item in ipairs(items) do
            local selected = index == picked
            item.Stroke.Thickness = selected and config.SelectedStrokeThickness or 0
            item.Button.TextColor3 = selected and COL.text or COL.textMuted
        end
    end
    for index, choice in ipairs(values) do
        local item = mk("TextButton", {Parent = list, LayoutOrder = index,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, config.ItemHeight), BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            FontFace = Font.fromEnum(choice.Enum or Enum.Font.Gotham), Text = choice.Name,
            TextSize = config.ItemTextSize, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted, AutoButtonColor = false})
        config.Keep[item] = true
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = item, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, config.ItemCorner)})
        local stroke = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = item, Thickness = 0, Color = COL.accent,
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round})
        items[index] = {Button = item, Stroke = stroke}
        toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(item, "select")
        item.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            picked = index
            list.Visible = false
            spec.OnPick(index)
            if spec.Views then
                for repaint in pairs(spec.Views) do repaint() end
            else
                paint()
            end
        end)
    end
    toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(button, "select")
    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        list.Visible = not list.Visible
    end)
    if spec.Views then
        spec.Views[paint] = true
        holder.Destroying:Connect(function() spec.Views[paint] = nil end)
    end
    paint()
    return holder
end

toggleRegistry.GameFont = {
    Text = "Шрифт плейса",
    LabelWidth = 100,
    ListHeight = 250,
    PanelHeader = "ЧТО ТРОГАТЬ",
    PanelHeight = 175,
    IconPattern = "[Ii]con",
    StandardRoot = "rbxasset://fonts/families/",
    Index = 1,
    Default = 1,
    Classes = {TextLabel = true, TextButton = true, TextBox = true},
    Scopes = {
        {Label = "Трогать плейс", Enabled = true, Standard = false, Service = nil, Touched = false, Watch = nil},
        {Label = "Трогать Roblox", Enabled = false, Standard = true, Service = "CoreGui", Touched = false, Watch = nil},
    },
    Values = {
        {Name = "Как в плейсе"},
        {Name = "Legacy", Enum = Enum.Font.Legacy},
        {Name = "Arial", Enum = Enum.Font.Arial},
        {Name = "Arimo", Enum = Enum.Font.Arimo},
        {Name = "Source Sans", Enum = Enum.Font.SourceSans},

        {Name = "Special Elite", Enum = Enum.Font.SpecialElite},

    Faces = {},
    Views = {},
    Original = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
}

function toggleRegistry.GameFont.root(scope)
    if scope.Service then
        local found, service = pcall(game.GetService, game, scope.Service)
        if not found or not service then return nil end
        local readable = pcall(service.GetChildren, service)
        return readable and service or nil
    end
    return player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
end

function toggleRegistry.GameFont.ours(instance)
    local gui = instance:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScreenGui") or instance
    return gui == ScreenGui or hideState.Tracked[gui] == true or gui == hideState.WarnGui
end

function toggleRegistry.GameFont.face(original)
    local state = toggleRegistry.GameFont
    local key = tostring(original.Weight) .. tostring(original.Style)
    local face = state.Faces[key]
    if not face then
        face = Font.new(Font.fromEnum(state.Values[state.Index].Enum).Family, original.Weight, original.Style)
        state.Faces[key] = face
    end
    return face
end

function toggleRegistry.GameFont.restyle(instance, active, scope)
    local state = toggleRegistry.GameFont
    if not state.Classes[instance.ClassName] then return end
    local record = state.Original[instance]
    if not active then
        if record then
            if instance.FontFace == record.Set then
                pcall(function() instance.FontFace = record.Face end)
            end
            state.Original[instance] = nil
        end
        return
    end
    if state.ours(instance) then return end
    local current = instance.FontFace
    if record and current ~= record.Set then
        state.Original[instance] = nil
        record = nil
    end
    if record == nil then
        local family = current.Family
        if string.find(family, state.IconPattern) then return end
        if scope.Standard and string.sub(family, 1, #state.StandardRoot) ~= state.StandardRoot then return end
        record = {Face = current}
        state.Original[instance] = record
    end
    record.Set = state.face(record.Face)
    pcall(function() instance.FontFace = record.Set end)
end

function toggleRegistry.GameFont.setScope(scope, enabled)
    local state = toggleRegistry.GameFont
    if enabled and not state.root(scope) then return end
    scope.Enabled = enabled
    state.apply()
end

function toggleRegistry.GameFont.buildPanel(Body)
    local state = toggleRegistry.GameFont
    addPanelHeader(Body, state.PanelHeader)
    for _, scope in ipairs(state.Scopes) do
        terrainState.addPill(Body, scope.Label, function() return scope.Enabled end, function(value)
            state.setScope(scope, value)
        end)
    end
end

function toggleRegistry.GameFont.apply()
    local state = toggleRegistry.GameFont
    state.Faces = {}
    for _, scope in ipairs(state.Scopes) do
        local active = scope.Enabled and state.Index ~= state.Default
        local root = state.root(scope)
        if root and (active or scope.Touched) then
            local listed, list = pcall(root.GetDescendants, root)
            if listed then
                for _, instance in ipairs(list) do
                    state.restyle(instance, active, scope)
                end
            end
        end
        scope.Touched = active and root ~= nil
        if scope.Watch and not scope.Touched then
            scope.Watch:Disconnect()
            scope.Watch = nil
        elseif scope.Touched and not scope.Watch then
            local watched, connection = pcall(function()
                return root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
                    if state.Classes[instance.ClassName] then task.defer(state.restyle, instance, true, scope) end
                end)
            end)
            scope.Watch = watched and connection or nil
        end
    end
end

function toggleRegistry.GameFont.destroy()
    local state = toggleRegistry.GameFont
    state.Index = state.Default
    state.apply()
end

function toggleRegistry.GameFont.build(page, mirror)
    local state = toggleRegistry.GameFont
    return toggleRegistry.Dropdown.build(page, {
        Text = state.Text,
        Label = state.Text,
        LabelWidth = state.LabelWidth,
        Values = state.Values,
        Index = state.Index,
        Get = function() return state.Index end,
        Views = state.Views,
        Mirror = mirror,
        MaxListHeight = state.ListHeight,
        PanelBuilder = state.buildPanel,
        PanelHeight = state.PanelHeight,
        OnPick = function(index)
            state.Index = index
            state.apply()
        end,
    })
end

toggleRegistry[toggleRegistry.GameFont.Text] = {Listeners = {}, Stars = {}, Spec = {
    mirror = function(page) return toggleRegistry.GameFont.build(page, true) end,
}}
toggleRegistry.GameFont.build(tabPages["custom"], false)

;(function()
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local hasFS = type(writefile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" and type(isfile) == "function"

    local settingsState = {

        Font = {Text = "Шрифт", Index = 1, Values = {
            {Name = "Gotham", Enum = Enum.Font.Gotham},

            {Name = "Arial", Enum = Enum.Font.Arial},

            {Name = "Roboto Condensed", Enum = Enum.Font.RobotoCondensed},
            {Name = "Ubuntu", Enum = Enum.Font.Ubuntu},
            {Name = "Nunito", Enum = Enum.Font.Nunito},
            {Name = "Merriweather", Enum = Enum.Font.Merriweather},
            {Name = "Oswald", Enum = Enum.Font.Oswald},
            {Name = "Jura", Enum = Enum.Font.Jura},
            {Name = "Roboto Mono", Enum = Enum.Font.RobotoMono},
        }},
        FontDefault = 1,
        FontSource = {
            [Enum.Font.Gotham] = true,
            [Enum.Font.GothamMedium] = true,
            [Enum.Font.GothamSemibold] = true,
            [Enum.Font.GothamBold] = true,
            [Enum.Font.GothamBlack] = true,
        },
        FontClasses = {TextLabel = true, TextButton = true, TextBox = true},
        FontSymbolPattern = "[%w\208\209]",
        RowHeight = 44,
        RowTextX = 14,
        RowTextSize = 13,
        SizeLabelWidth = 80,
        SizeButtonWidth = 34,
        SizeButtonHeight = 26,
        SizeButtonGap = 5,
        SizeRightInset = 10,
        SizeButtonTextSize = 11,
        SelectedStrokeColor = COL.accent,
        SelectedStrokeThickness = 2,
        KeyHolderHeight = 52,
        KeyCardScale = 0.5,
        KeyCardOffset = -4,
        KeyLabelX = 6,
        KeyLabelY = 4,
        KeyLabelHeight = 18,
        KeyLabelSize = 14,
        KeyButtonInset = 10,
        KeyButtonBottom = 6,
        KeyButtonHeight = 22,
        KeyButtonTextSize = 11,
        KeyButtonCorner = 7,
        SoundText = "Звуки интерфейса",
        VolumeText = "Громкость",
        VolumeMax = 100,
        PillWidth = 62,
        PillHeight = 26,
        PillCorner = 9,
        PillGap = 8,
        PillTextSize = 11,
        PillStrokeThickness = 1.5,
        PillTween = 0.15,
        OnText = "ВКЛ",
        OffText = "ВЫКЛ",
        SaveDelay = 0.4,
        SaveToken = 0,
        Previous = nil,
        OpenBind = nil,
        FontFaces = {},
        FontApplied = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
        FontOrigin = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
        FontWatch = nil,
    }

    function settingsState.read()
        local state = settingsState
        if not hasFS then return nil end
        local data = nil
        pcall(function()
            if not isfile(state.File) then return end
            local decoded = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(state.File))
            if type(decoded) == "table" then data = decoded end
        end)
        return data
    end

    function settingsState.save()
        local state = settingsState
        if not hasFS then return end
        pcall(function()
            writefile(state.File, HttpService:JSONEncode({
                widthIndex = state.Width.Index,
                heightIndex = state.Height.Index,
                styleIndex = state.Style.Index,
                fontIndex = state.Font.Index,
                soundEnabled = toggleRegistry.Sounds.Enabled,
                soundVolume = toggleRegistry.Sounds.Volume,
                openKey = state.OpenBind.Key.Name,
            }))
        end)
    end

    function settingsState.applySize()
        local state = settingsState
        Root.Size = UDim2.new(0, state.Width.Values[state.Width.Index], 0, state.Height.Values[state.Height.Index])
        toggleRegistry.Layout.Epoch = toggleRegistry.Layout.Epoch + 1
    end

    function settingsState.pickSize(dimension, index)
        if closeCurrentPanel then closeCurrentPanel() end
        dimension.Index = index
        settingsState.applySize()
        settingsState.save()
    end

    function settingsState.applyStyle()
        local state = settingsState
        toggleRegistry.Icons.setStyle(state.Style.Values[state.Style.Index])
    end

    function settingsState.pickStyle(dimension, index)
        dimension.Index = index
        settingsState.applyStyle()
        settingsState.save()
    end

    function settingsState.readIndex(dimension, raw)
        if type(raw) ~= "number" or raw ~= raw then return end
        dimension.Index = math.clamp(math.floor(raw), 1, #dimension.Values)
    end

    function settingsState.addSizeRow(page, dimension, onPick)
        local state = settingsState
        local names = dimension.Names or state.SizeNames
        local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = page, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, state.RowHeight),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, state.RowTextX, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, state.SizeLabelWidth, 1, 0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = dimension.Text, TextSize = state.RowTextSize,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left})
        local count = #names
        local holderWidth = count * state.SizeButtonWidth + (count - 1) * state.SizeButtonGap
        local holder = mk("Frame", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -state.SizeRightInset, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, holderWidth, 0, state.SizeButtonHeight)})
        mk("UIListLayout", {Parent = holder, FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
            Padding = UDim.new(0, state.SizeButtonGap), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center})
        local buttons = {}
        local function paint()
            for index, entry in ipairs(buttons) do
                local picked = index == dimension.Index
                entry.Stroke.Thickness = picked and state.SelectedStrokeThickness or 0
                entry.Button.TextColor3 = picked and COL.text or COL.textMuted
            end
        end
        for index, name in ipairs(names) do
            local button = mk("TextButton", {Parent = holder, LayoutOrder = index,
                Size = UDim2.new(0, state.SizeButtonWidth, 0, state.SizeButtonHeight),
                BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = name,
                TextSize = state.SizeButtonTextSize, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted, AutoButtonColor = false})
            mk("UICorner", {Parent = button, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)})
            local stroke = mk("UIStroke", {Parent = button, Thickness = 0, Color = state.SelectedStrokeColor,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round})
            buttons[index] = {Button = button, Stroke = stroke}
            toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(button, "select")
            button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                onPick(dimension, index)
                paint()
            end)
        end
        paint()
    end

    function settingsState.isIcon(instance)
        for entry in pairs(toggleRegistry.Icons.Entries) do
            if entry.Button == instance then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    function settingsState.face(origin)
        local state = settingsState
        local face = state.FontFaces[origin]
        if not face then
            local choice = state.Font.Values[state.Font.Index]
            face = Font.new(Font.fromEnum(choice.Enum).Family, Font.fromEnum(origin).Weight, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
            state.FontFaces[origin] = face
        end
        return face
    end

    function settingsState.restyle(instance)
        local state = settingsState
        if not state.FontClasses[instance.ClassName] or toggleRegistry.Dropdown.Keep[instance] then return end
        local origin = state.FontOrigin[instance]
        if state.Font.Index == state.FontDefault then
            if origin and state.FontApplied[instance] then
                instance.Font = origin
                state.FontApplied[instance] = nil
            end
            return
        end
        if origin == nil then
            origin = instance.Font
            state.FontOrigin[instance] = origin
        end
        if not state.FontSource[origin] or state.isIcon(instance) then return end
        local text = instance.Text
        if text ~= "" and not text:find(state.FontSymbolPattern) then return end
        instance.FontFace = state.face(origin)
        state.FontApplied[instance] = true
    end

    function settingsState.applyFont()
        local state = settingsState
        state.FontFaces = {}
        for _, instance in ipairs(ScreenGui:GetDescendants()) do
            state.restyle(instance)
        end
        if state.Font.Index ~= state.FontDefault and not state.FontWatch then
            state.FontWatch = ScreenGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
                if state.FontClasses[instance.ClassName] then task.defer(state.restyle, instance) end
            end)
        end
    end

    function settingsState.addFontRow(page)
        local state = settingsState
        local dimension = state.Font
        toggleRegistry.Dropdown.build(page, {
            Text = dimension.Text,
            Values = dimension.Values,
            Index = dimension.Index,
            OnPick = function(index)
                dimension.Index = index
                state.applyFont()
                state.save()
            end,
        })
    end

    function settingsState.queueSave()
        local state = settingsState
        state.SaveToken = state.SaveToken + 1
        local token = state.SaveToken
        task.delay(state.SaveDelay, function()
            if token == state.SaveToken then state.save() end
        end)
    end

    function settingsState.readSounds(data)
        local sounds = toggleRegistry.Sounds
        if type(data.soundEnabled) == "boolean" then sounds.Enabled = data.soundEnabled end
        local volume = data.soundVolume
        if type(volume) == "number" and volume == volume then sounds.setVolume(math.clamp(volume, 0, 1)) end
    end

    function settingsState.addSoundRow(page)
        local state = settingsState
        local sounds = toggleRegistry.Sounds
        local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = page, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, state.RowHeight),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
        local reserve = state.PillWidth + state.SizeRightInset + state.PillGap
        local clickZone = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -reserve, 1, 0)})
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, state.RowTextX, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -(state.RowTextX + reserve), 1, 0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = state.SoundText, TextSize = state.RowTextSize,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
        local pill = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -state.SizeRightInset, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, state.PillWidth, 0, state.PillHeight), BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = state.PillTextSize, AutoButtonColor = false,
            BackgroundColor3 = sounds.Enabled and COL.pinkOn or COL.off,
            TextColor3 = sounds.Enabled and COL.text or COL.textMuted,
            Text = sounds.Enabled and state.OnText or state.OffText})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = pill, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, state.PillCorner)})
        mk("UIStroke", {Parent = pill, Thickness = state.PillStrokeThickness, Color = COL.stroke,
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
        local function press()
            if sounds.Enabled then
                sounds.play("toggle")
                sounds.Enabled = false
            else
                sounds.Enabled = true
                sounds.play("toggle")
            end
            TweenService:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(state.PillTween), {
                BackgroundColor3 = sounds.Enabled and COL.pinkOn or COL.off,
                TextColor3 = sounds.Enabled and COL.text or COL.textMuted}):Play()
            pill.Text = sounds.Enabled and state.OnText or state.OffText
            state.save()
        end
        clickZone.MouseButton1Click:Connect(press)
        pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(press)
    end

    function settingsState.addVolumeRow(page)
        local state = settingsState
        local sounds = toggleRegistry.Sounds
        addSlider(page, state.VolumeText, 0, state.VolumeMax, math.floor(sounds.Volume * state.VolumeMax + 0.5), 0,
            function(value)
                sounds.setVolume(value / state.VolumeMax)
                state.queueSave()
            end)
    end

    function settingsState.toggleMenu()
        if Root.Visible then closeMenu() else openMenu() end
    end

    local bind = keyBinds.new(settingsState.OpenText, settingsState.save, settingsState.toggleMenu)
    bind.SkipPresets = true
    bind.FullName = true
    bind.BoundColor = COL.accent
    settingsState.OpenBind = bind

    local saved = settingsState.read()
    if saved then
        settingsState.readIndex(settingsState.Width, saved.widthIndex)
        settingsState.readIndex(settingsState.Height, saved.heightIndex)
        settingsState.readIndex(settingsState.Style, saved.styleIndex)
        settingsState.readIndex(settingsState.Font, saved.fontIndex)
        settingsState.readSounds(saved)
        if type(saved.openKey) == "string" then
            local valid, code = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[saved.openKey] end)
            if valid and typeof(code) == "EnumItem" and code.EnumType == Enum.KeyCode then bind.Key = code end
        end
    end

    local page = makePage()
    tabPages[settingsState.PageKey] = page

    addSectionHeader(page, settingsState.SizeHeader)
    settingsState.addSizeRow(page, settingsState.Width, settingsState.pickSize)
    settingsState.addSizeRow(page, settingsState.Height, settingsState.pickSize)
    settingsState.addSizeRow(page, settingsState.Style, settingsState.pickStyle)
    settingsState.addFontRow(page)
    settingsState.addSoundRow(page)
    settingsState.addVolumeRow(page)

    addSectionHeader(page, settingsState.KeysHeader)
    local keyHolder = mk("Frame", {Parent = page, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, settingsState.KeyHolderHeight),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1})
    local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = keyHolder,
        Size = UDim2.new(settingsState.KeyCardScale, settingsState.KeyCardOffset, 1, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)})
    mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, settingsState.KeyLabelX, 0, settingsState.KeyLabelY),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -2 * settingsState.KeyLabelX, 0, settingsState.KeyLabelHeight),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = settingsState.OpenText, TextSize = settingsState.KeyLabelSize,
        TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center})
    local keyButton = mk("TextButton", {Parent = row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -settingsState.KeyButtonBottom),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -2 * settingsState.KeyButtonInset, 0, settingsState.KeyButtonHeight),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = "",
        TextSize = settingsState.KeyButtonTextSize, TextColor3 = COL.textMuted, AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = keyButton, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, settingsState.KeyButtonCorner)})
    keyBinds.attacher(bind)(keyButton)

    local gearRight = CloseBtn.Position.X.Offset - CloseBtn.Size.X.Offset - settingsState.GearGap
    local gear = mk("TextButton", {Parent = Header, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, gearRight, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, settingsState.GearSize, 0, settingsState.GearSize),
        BackgroundColor3 = COL.off, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false})
    mk("UICorner", {Parent = gear, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)})
    toggleRegistry.Icons.outline(gear)
    toggleRegistry.Icons.outline(CloseBtn)
    toggleRegistry.Icons.drawGear(gear)
    toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(gear, "gear")
    toggleRegistry.Icons.drawCross(CloseBtn, toggleRegistry.Icons.HeaderCrossGlyph)
    toggleRegistry.Sounds.attach(CloseBtn, "close")
    dragZone.Size = UDim2.new(1, gearRight - settingsState.GearSize - settingsState.DragGap, 1, 0)
    gear.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local state = settingsState
        if activeTab == state.PageKey then
            switchTab(state.Previous or state.FallbackTab)
        else
            state.Previous = activeTab
            switchTab(state.PageKey)
        end
    end)
    page:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(gear, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            BackgroundColor3 = page.Visible and COL.pinkOn or COL.off}):Play()
    end)

    settingsState.applySize()
    settingsState.applyStyle()
    settingsState.applyFont()
end)()

;(function()
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local hasFS = type(writefile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" and type(isfile) == "function"

    local usageState = {
        Version = "3.0 DEMO",
        LegacyVersion = "2.0",
        File = "GraphicsMenu_Usage.json",
        SaveSeconds = 15,
        MaxStepSeconds = 120,
        Precision = 1000,
        PageKey = "settings",
        Header = "СТАТИСТИКА",
        TimeText = "Время использования",
        VersionText = "Версия, с которой впервые был загружен",
        TimeFormat = "%d ч %02d мин %02d с",
        RefreshSeconds = 1,
        RowHeight = 44,
        RowTextX = 14,
        RowTextSize = 13,
        RowCorner = 10,
        FirstVersion = nil,
        TotalSeconds = 0,
        LastClock = 0,
        Alive = true,
    }

    function usageState.read()
        local state = usageState
        if not hasFS then return nil end
        local data = nil
        pcall(function()
            if not isfile(state.File) then return end
            local decoded = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(state.File))
            if type(decoded) == "table" and type(decoded.firstVersion) == "string"
                and type(decoded.totalSeconds) == "number" and decoded.totalSeconds >= 0 then
                local first = decoded.firstVersion
                if string.find(first, state.LegacyVersion, 1, true) then first = state.Version end
                data = {firstVersion = first, totalSeconds = decoded.totalSeconds}
            end
        end)
        return data
    end

    function usageState.write(data)
        local state = usageState
        if not hasFS then return end
        pcall(function()
            writefile(state.File, HttpService:JSONEncode(data))
        end)
    end

    function usageState.record()
        local state = usageState
        local now = os.clock()
        local step = now - state.LastClock
        state.LastClock = now
        if step < 0 or step > state.MaxStepSeconds then step = 0 end
        local data = state.read()
            or {firstVersion = state.FirstVersion or state.Version, totalSeconds = state.TotalSeconds}
        data.totalSeconds = math.floor((data.totalSeconds + step) * state.Precision + 0.5) / state.Precision
        state.FirstVersion = data.firstVersion
        state.TotalSeconds = data.totalSeconds
        state.write(data)
    end

    function usageState.destroy()
        local state = usageState
        if not state.Alive then return end
        state.Alive = false
        state.record()
    end

    function usageState.live()
        local state = usageState
        local pending = math.clamp(os.clock() - state.LastClock, 0, state.MaxStepSeconds)
        return state.TotalSeconds + pending
    end

    function usageState.format(total)
        local seconds = math.floor(total)
        return string.format(usageState.TimeFormat, math.floor(seconds / 3600), math.floor(seconds % 3600 / 60), seconds % 60)
    end

    function usageState.addRow(page, label, value)
        local state = usageState
        local row = mk("Frame", {Parent = page, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, state.RowHeight),
            BackgroundColor3 = COL.row, BorderSizePixel = 0})
        mk("UICorner", {Parent = row, CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, state.RowCorner)})
        mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, state.RowTextX, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -state.RowTextX, 1, 0),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = label, TextSize = state.RowTextSize,
            TextColor3 = COL.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextWrapped = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
        return mk("TextLabel", {Parent = row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -state.RowTextX, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -state.RowTextX, 1, 0),
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = value, TextSize = state.RowTextSize,
            TextColor3 = COL.accent, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
    end

    usageState.LastClock = os.clock()
    usageState.record()
    task.spawn(function()
        while usageState.Alive do
            task.wait(usageState.SaveSeconds)
            if usageState.Alive then usageState.record() end
        end
    end)
    keyBinds.Cleanups[#keyBinds.Cleanups + 1] = usageState.destroy

    local page = tabPages[usageState.PageKey]
    if page then
        addSectionHeader(page, usageState.Header)
        local timeLabel = usageState.addRow(page, usageState.TimeText, usageState.format(usageState.live()))
        usageState.addRow(page, usageState.VersionText, tostring(usageState.FirstVersion))
        page:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
            if page.Visible then timeLabel.Text = usageState.format(usageState.live()) end
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            while usageState.Alive do
                task.wait(usageState.RefreshSeconds)
                if page.Visible then timeLabel.Text = usageState.format(usageState.live()) end
            end
        end)
    end
end)()

do
    local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    env.__GfxPreviewUnload = function()
        weatherCycle.Enabled = false
        weatherCycle.Token = weatherCycle.Token + 1
        if lightningState.Enabled then pcall(applyLightning, false) end
        if rainState.EnabledState then pcall(applyRain, false) end
        pcall(rainState.Puddles.destroy)
        pcall(toggleRegistry.GameFont.destroy)
        if snowState.EnabledState then pcall(applySnow, false) end
        if windState.Enabled then pcall(applyWind, false) end
        if newFogEnabled then pcall(applyNewFog, false) end
        if terrainState.Water.Enabled then pcall(terrainState.apply, terrainState.Water, false) end
        pcall(terrainState.Underwater.refresh)
        if terrainState.Materials.Enabled then pcall(terrainState.Materials.apply, false) end
        if terrainState.Pack.Enabled then pcall(terrainState.Pack.apply, false) end
        if terrainState.Fire.Enabled then
            pcall(terrainState.Fire.apply, "Fire", false)
            pcall(terrainState.Fire.apply, "Smoke", false)
        end
        if terrainState.Glare.Enabled then pcall(terrainState.Glare.apply, false) end
        if terrainState.NightVision.Enabled then pcall(terrainState.NightVision.apply, false) end
        pcall(hideState.destroy)
        pcall(keyBinds.destroy)
        if tvState.Enabled then pcall(tvState.apply, false) end
        if tvState.Logo.Enabled then pcall(tvState.Logo.apply, false) end
        if tvState.News.Enabled then pcall(tvState.News.apply, false) end
        if uavState.Enabled then pcall(uavState.apply, false) end
        if firstPersonState.Enabled then pcall(firstPersonState.apply, false) end
        if freecamState.Enabled then pcall(freecamState.apply, false) end
        if smoothCameraState.Enabled then pcall(smoothCameraState.apply, false) end
        if bodyState.Enabled then pcall(bodyState.apply, false) end
        if bobState.Enabled then pcall(bobState.apply, false) end
        if cloudsConn then pcall(applyClouds, false) end
        if skyState.Enabled then pcall(skyState.apply, false) end
        if moonState.Enabled then pcall(moonState.apply, false) end
        if auroraState.Enabled then pcall(auroraState.apply, false) end
        if rainbowState.Enabled then pcall(rainbowState.apply, false) end
        if meteorState.Enabled then pcall(meteorState.apply, false) end
        if fireflyState.Enabled then pcall(fireflyState.apply, false) end
        if fogAtmosphere then pcall(applyFog, false) end
        if clockTimeEnabled then pcall(applyClockTime, false) end
        if exposureEnabled then pcall(applyExposure, false) end
        if colorShiftEnabled then pcall(applyColorShift, false) end
        if distBlurEffect then pcall(applyDistanceBlur, false) end
        if blurEffect then pcall(applyBlur, false) end
        if ccEffect then pcall(applyColorCorrection, false) end
        if bloomEffect then pcall(applyBloom, false) end
        if sunRaysEffect then pcall(applySunRays, false) end
        if brightnessEnabled then pcall(applyBrightness, false) end
        if lightingStyleEnabled then pcall(applyLightingStyle, false) end
        if shadowsEnabled then pcall(applyShadows, false) end
        cameraZoom.restore()
        pcall(toggleRegistry.Sounds.destroy)
        pcall(function() ScreenGui:Destroy() end)
    end
end
